# 最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法》的解释

**分类**：司法解释  
**法律部门**：诉讼与非诉讼程序法  
**发布机关**：最高人民法院  
**发文字号**：法释〔2021〕1号  
**公布日期**：2021-01-26  
**生效日期**：2021-03-01

> 《最高人民法院关于适用〈中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法〉的解释》已于2020年12月7日由最高人民法院审判委员会第1820次会议通过，现予公布，自2021年3月1日起施行。 最高人民法院 2021年1月26日 法释〔2021〕1号 最高人民法院 关于适用《中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法》的解释 （2020年12月7日最高人民法院审判委员会 第1820次会议通过，自2021年3月1日起施行）

---

### 第一章 管辖

<a id="art-1"></a>第一条 人民法院直接受理的自诉案件包括：  
（一）告诉才处理的案件：  
⒈侮辱、诽谤案（[刑法第二百四十六条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法.md#art-246)规定的，但严重危害社会秩序和国家利益的除外）；  
⒉暴力干涉婚姻自由案（[刑法第二百五十七条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法.md#art-257)第一款规定的）；  
⒊虐待案（[刑法第二百六十条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法.md#art-260)第一款规定的，但被害人没有能力告诉或者因受到强制、威吓无法告诉的除外）；  
⒋侵占案（[刑法第二百七十条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法.md#art-270)规定的）。  
（二）人民检察院没有提起公诉，被害人有证据证明的轻微刑事案件：  
⒈故意伤害案（[刑法第二百三十四条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法.md#art-234)第一款规定的）；  
⒉非法侵入住宅案（[刑法第二百四十五条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法.md#art-245)规定的）；  
⒊侵犯通信自由案（[刑法第二百五十二条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法.md#art-252)规定的）；  
⒋重婚案（[刑法第二百五十八条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法.md#art-258)规定的）；  
⒌遗弃案（[刑法第二百六十一条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法.md#art-261)规定的）；  
⒍生产、销售伪劣商品案（刑法分则第三章第一节规定的，但严重危害社会秩序和国家利益的除外）；  
⒎侵犯知识产权案（刑法分则第三章第七节规定的，但严重危害社会秩序和国家利益的除外）；  
⒏刑法分则第四章、第五章规定的，可能判处三年有期徒刑以下刑罚的案件。  
本项规定的案件，被害人直接向人民法院起诉的，人民法院应当依法受理。对其中证据不足，可以由公安机关受理的，或者认为对被告人可能判处三年有期徒刑以上刑罚的，应当告知被害人向公安机关报案，或者移送公安机关立案侦查。  
（三）被害人有证据证明对被告人侵犯自己人身、财产权利的行为应当依法追究刑事责任，且有证据证明曾经提出控告，而公安机关或者人民检察院不予追究被告人刑事责任的案件。

**Article 1** Private prosecution cases directly accepted by the People's Court include:  
(1) Cases to be handled only upon complaint:  
1. Cases of insult and defamation (as provided for in Article 246 of the Criminal Law, except where such conduct seriously endangers social order and national interests);  
2. Cases of violent interference with the freedom of marriage (as provided for in Paragraph 1 of Article 257 of the Criminal Law);  
3. Cases of maltreatment (as provided for in Paragraph 1 of Article 260 of the Criminal Law, except where the victim is unable to file a complaint, or is unable to file a complaint due to coercion or intimidation);  
4. Cases of unlawful possession (as provided for in Article 270 of the Criminal Law).  
(2) Minor criminal cases for which the People's Procuratorate has not initiated public prosecution and the victim has evidence to prove:  
1. Cases of intentional injury (as provided for in Paragraph 1 of Article 234 of the Criminal Law);  
2. Cases of unlawful intrusion into a residence (as provided for in Article 245 of the Criminal Law);  
3. Cases of infringement upon freedom of correspondence (as provided for in Article 252 of the Criminal Law);  
4. Cases of bigamy (as provided for in Article 258 of the Criminal Law);  
5. Cases of abandonment (as provided for in Article 261 of the Criminal Law);  
6. Cases of producing and selling fake or substandard commodities (as provided for in Section 1 of Chapter III of the Specific Provisions of the Criminal Law, except where such conduct seriously endangers social order and national interests);  
7. Cases of infringing intellectual property rights (as provided for in Section 7 of Chapter III of the Specific Provisions of the Criminal Law, except where such conduct seriously endangers social order and national interests);  
8. Cases provided for in Chapters IV and V of the Specific Provisions of the Criminal Law for which a sentence of fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years may be imposed.  
For the cases provided for in this item, where the victim directly initiates a lawsuit with the People's Court, the People's Court shall accept the case according to law. For any of them with insufficient evidence that may be accepted by the public security organ, or where the defendant may be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than three years, the People's Court shall inform the victim to report the case to the public security organ, or transfer the case to the public security organ for filing and investigation.  
(3) Cases in which the victim has evidence to prove that the defendant's infringement upon the victim's personal or property rights shall be investigated for criminal liability according to law, and has evidence to prove that the victim once filed a complaint, but the public security organ or the People's Procuratorate has not investigated the defendant's criminal liability.

<a id="art-2"></a>第二条 犯罪地包括犯罪行为地和犯罪结果地。  
针对或者主要利用计算机网络实施的犯罪，犯罪地包括用于实施犯罪行为的网络服务使用的服务器所在地，网络服务提供者所在地，被侵害的信息网络系统及其管理者所在地，犯罪过程中被告人、被害人使用的信息网络系统所在地，以及被害人被侵害时所在地和被害人财产遭受损失地等。

**Article 2** The place of the crime includes the place where the criminal act is committed and the place where the criminal result occurs.  
For a crime committed against or primarily through a computer network, the place of the crime includes the location of the server used by the network service used to commit the criminal act, the location of the network service provider, the location of the infringed information network system and its administrator, the location of the information network systems used by the defendant or the victim during the crime, and the location where the victim was located at the time of infringement and the location where the victim's property sustained loss, among others.

<a id="art-3"></a>第三条 被告人的户籍地为其居住地。经常居住地与户籍地不一致的，经常居住地为其居住地。经常居住地为被告人被追诉前已连续居住一年以上的地方，但住院就医的除外。  
被告单位登记的住所地为其居住地。主要营业地或者主要办事机构所在地与登记的住所地不一致的，主要营业地或者主要办事机构所在地为其居住地。

**Article 3** The place of registered residence of a defendant is his or her place of residence. Where the habitual residence is inconsistent with the place of registered residence, the habitual residence shall be his or her place of residence. The habitual residence is a place where the defendant has continuously resided for more than one year before being prosecuted, except where he or she is hospitalized for medical treatment.  
The registered domicile of a defendant unit is its place of residence. Where the principal place of business or the location of the principal office is inconsistent with the registered domicile, the principal place of business or the location of the principal office shall be its place of residence.

<a id="art-4"></a>第四条 在中华人民共和国内水、领海发生的刑事案件，由犯罪地或者被告人登陆地的人民法院管辖。由被告人居住地的人民法院审判更为适宜的，可以由被告人居住地的人民法院管辖。

**Article 4** Criminal cases occurring in the internal waters or territorial sea of the People's Republic of China shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court of the place where the crime is committed or the place where the defendant lands. Where trial by the People's Court of the defendant's place of residence is more appropriate, the case may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court of the defendant's place of residence.

<a id="art-5"></a>第五条 在列车上的犯罪，被告人在列车运行途中被抓获的，由前方停靠站所在地负责审判铁路运输刑事案件的人民法院管辖。必要时，也可以由始发站或者终点站所在地负责审判铁路运输刑事案件的人民法院管辖。  
被告人不是在列车运行途中被抓获的，由负责该列车乘务的铁路公安机关对应的审判铁路运输刑事案件的人民法院管辖；被告人在列车运行途经车站被抓获的，也可以由该车站所在地负责审判铁路运输刑事案件的人民法院管辖。

**Article 5** For a crime committed on a train, where the defendant is captured while the train is in operation, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court in charge of adjudicating railway transport criminal cases at the place of the forward stop. Where necessary, the case may also be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court in charge of adjudicating railway transport criminal cases at the place of the originating station or the terminal station.  
Where the defendant is not captured while the train is in operation, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court for adjudicating railway transport criminal cases corresponding to the railway public security organ responsible for the service of the train; where the defendant is captured at a station passed by the train, the case may also be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court for adjudicating railway transport criminal cases at the place of that station.

<a id="art-6"></a>第六条 在国际列车上的犯罪，根据我国与相关国家签订的协定确定管辖；没有协定的，由该列车始发或者前方停靠的中国车站所在地负责审判铁路运输刑事案件的人民法院管辖。

**Article 6** For crimes committed on an international train, jurisdiction shall be determined according to the agreements concluded between China and the relevant countries; where there is no agreement, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court in charge of adjudicating railway transport criminal cases at the place of the Chinese station where the train starts or makes a forward stop.

<a id="art-7"></a>第七条 在中华人民共和国领域外的中国船舶内的犯罪，由该船舶最初停泊的中国口岸所在地或者被告人登陆地、入境地的人民法院管辖。

**Article 7** For a crime committed on a Chinese vessel outside the territory of the People's Republic of China, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place of the Chinese port where the vessel first berths, or at the place where the defendant lands or enters the country.

<a id="art-8"></a>第八条 在中华人民共和国领域外的中国航空器内的犯罪，由该航空器在中国最初降落地的人民法院管辖。

**Article 8** For a crime committed on a Chinese aircraft outside the territory of the People's Republic of China, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place where the aircraft first lands in China.

<a id="art-9"></a>第九条 中国公民在中国驻外使领馆内的犯罪，由其主管单位所在地或者原户籍地的人民法院管辖。

**Article 9** For a crime committed by a Chinese citizen within a Chinese embassy or consulate abroad, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place where the competent authority in charge of the embassy or consulate is located, or at the place of the person's former registered residence.

<a id="art-10"></a>第十条 中国公民在中华人民共和国领域外的犯罪，由其登陆地、入境地、离境前居住地或者现居住地的人民法院管辖；被害人是中国公民的，也可以由被害人离境前居住地或者现居住地的人民法院管辖。

**Article 10** For a crime committed by a Chinese citizen outside the territory of the People's Republic of China, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place where the person lands, enters the country, resided before leaving the country, or currently resides; where the victim is a Chinese citizen, the case may also be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place where the victim resided before leaving the country or currently resides.

<a id="art-11"></a>第十一条 外国人在中华人民共和国领域外对中华人民共和国国家或者公民犯罪，根据《中华人民共和国刑法》应当受处罚的，由该外国人登陆地、入境地或者入境后居住地的人民法院管辖，也可以由被害人离境前居住地或者现居住地的人民法院管辖。

**Article 11** Where a foreigner commits a crime against the state or a citizen of the People's Republic of China outside the territory of the People's Republic of China, and such crime shall be punishable in accordance with the Criminal Law of the People's Republic of China, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place where the foreigner lands, enters the country, or resides after entering the country, and may also be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place where the victim resided before leaving the country or currently resides.

<a id="art-12"></a>第十二条 对中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约所规定的罪行，中华人民共和国在所承担条约义务的范围内行使刑事管辖权的，由被告人被抓获地、登陆地或者入境地的人民法院管辖。

**Article 12** For crimes provided for in international treaties concluded or acceded to by the People's Republic of China, where the People's Republic of China exercises criminal jurisdiction within the scope of the treaty obligations it has undertaken, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the place where the defendant is captured, lands, or enters the country.

<a id="art-13"></a>第十三条 正在服刑的罪犯在判决宣告前还有其他罪没有判决的，由原审地人民法院管辖；由罪犯服刑地或者犯罪地的人民法院审判更为适宜的，可以由罪犯服刑地或者犯罪地的人民法院管辖。  
罪犯在服刑期间又犯罪的，由服刑地的人民法院管辖。  
罪犯在脱逃期间又犯罪的，由服刑地的人民法院管辖。但是，在犯罪地抓获罪犯并发现其在脱逃期间犯罪的，由犯罪地的人民法院管辖。

**Article 13** Where a criminal currently serving a sentence has other crimes that were not adjudicated before the judgment was pronounced, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court of the place of the original trial; where trial by the People's Court of the place where the criminal is serving his sentence or the place of the crime is more appropriate, the case may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court of that place.  
Where a criminal commits another crime during the period of serving a sentence, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court of the place where the sentence is being served.  
Where a criminal commits another crime during escape from custody, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court of the place where the sentence is being served. However, where the criminal is captured at the place of the crime and it is discovered that he committed the crime during escape, the case shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court of the place of the crime.

<a id="art-14"></a>第十四条 人民检察院认为可能判处无期徒刑、死刑，向中级人民法院提起公诉的案件，中级人民法院受理后，认为不需要判处无期徒刑、死刑的，应当依法审判，不再交基层人民法院审判。

**Article 14** Where the People's Procuratorate initiates public prosecution with an Intermediate People's Court with respect to a case in which it considers that a sentence of life imprisonment or the death penalty may be imposed, and, after accepting the case, the Intermediate People's Court considers that the imposition of life imprisonment or the death penalty is unnecessary, it shall adjudicate the case according to law and shall not transfer the case to a Primary People's Court for adjudication.

<a id="art-15"></a>第十五条 一人犯数罪、共同犯罪或者其他需要并案审理的案件，其中一人或者一罪属于上级人民法院管辖的，全案由上级人民法院管辖。

**Article 15** Where a person commits multiple crimes, a joint crime, or other cases that need to be tried in a consolidated manner, and one of the persons or one of the crimes falls within the jurisdiction of a higher People's Court, the entire case shall be under the jurisdiction of the higher People's Court.

<a id="art-16"></a>第十六条 上级人民法院决定审判下级人民法院管辖的第一审刑事案件的，应当向下级人民法院下达改变管辖决定书，并书面通知同级人民检察院。

**Article 16** Where a higher People's Court decides to adjudicate a criminal case of first instance within the jurisdiction of a lower People's Court, it shall issue a written decision on changing jurisdiction to the lower People's Court and notify the People's Procuratorate at the same level in writing.

<a id="art-17"></a>第十七条 基层人民法院对可能判处无期徒刑、死刑的第一审刑事案件，应当移送中级人民法院审判。  
基层人民法院对下列第一审刑事案件，可以请求移送中级人民法院审判：  
（一）重大、复杂案件；  
（二）新类型的疑难案件；  
（三）在法律适用上具有普遍指导意义的案件。  
需要将案件移送中级人民法院审判的，应当在报请院长决定后，至迟于案件审理期限届满十五日以前书面请求移送。中级人民法院应当在接到申请后十日以内作出决定。不同意移送的，应当下达不同意移送决定书，由请求移送的人民法院依法审判；同意移送的，应当下达同意移送决定书，并书面通知同级人民检察院。

**Article 17** A Primary People's Court shall transfer to an Intermediate People's Court for trial a criminal case of first instance for which a sentence of life imprisonment or the death penalty may be imposed.  
A Primary People's Court may request to transfer the following criminal cases of first instance to an Intermediate People's Court for trial:  
(1) major and complex cases;  
(2) novel and difficult cases;  
(3) cases with universal guiding significance in the application of law.  
Where a case needs to be transferred to an Intermediate People's Court for trial, the Primary People's Court shall, after submitting the matter to the president of the court for decision, file a written request for transfer no later than fifteen days before the expiration of the case trial period. The Intermediate People's Court shall make a decision within ten days after receiving the application. If the transfer is not approved, it shall issue a written decision disapproving the transfer, and the case shall be tried according to law by the People's Court that requested the transfer; if the transfer is approved, it shall issue a written decision approving the transfer and notify the People's Procuratorate at the same level in writing.

<a id="art-18"></a>第十八条 有管辖权的人民法院因案件涉及本院院长需要回避或者其他原因，不宜行使管辖权的，可以请求移送上一级人民法院管辖。上一级人民法院可以管辖，也可以指定与提出请求的人民法院同级的其他人民法院管辖。

**Article 18** Where a People's Court with jurisdiction is not suitable to exercise jurisdiction because the case involves the president of the court and requires the president's recusal, or for other reasons, it may request to transfer the case to a People's Court at the next higher level for jurisdiction. The People's Court at the next higher level may take jurisdiction itself, or may designate another People's Court at the same level as the court that made the request to have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-19"></a>第十九条 两个以上同级人民法院都有管辖权的案件，由最初受理的人民法院审判。必要时，可以移送主要犯罪地的人民法院审判。  
管辖权发生争议的，应当在审理期限内协商解决；协商不成的，由争议的人民法院分别层报共同的上级人民法院指定管辖。

**Article 19** Where two or more People's Courts at the same level all have jurisdiction over a case, the case shall be tried by the People's Court that first accepted it. Where necessary, the case may be transferred to the People's Court at the principal place of the crime for trial.  
Where a dispute over jurisdiction arises, the courts concerned shall resolve it through consultation within the trial period; if consultation fails, the courts involved in the dispute shall report level by level to their common higher People's Court for designation of jurisdiction.

<a id="art-20"></a>第二十条 管辖不明的案件，上级人民法院可以指定下级人民法院审判。  
有关案件，由犯罪地、被告人居住地以外的人民法院审判更为适宜的，上级人民法院可以指定下级人民法院管辖。

**Article 20** For a case with unclear jurisdiction, the higher People's Court may designate a lower People's Court to adjudicate the case.  
Where trial of a relevant case by a People's Court other than that of the place of the crime or the defendant's place of residence is more appropriate, the higher People's Court may designate a lower People's Court to have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-21"></a>第二十一条 上级人民法院指定管辖，应当将指定管辖决定书送达被指定管辖的人民法院和其他有关的人民法院。

**Article 21** Where a higher People's Court designates jurisdiction, it shall serve the written decision on designation of jurisdiction on the designated People's Court and other relevant People's Courts.

<a id="art-22"></a>第二十二条 原受理案件的人民法院在收到上级人民法院改变管辖决定书、同意移送决定书或者指定其他人民法院管辖的决定书后，对公诉案件，应当书面通知同级人民检察院，并将案卷材料退回，同时书面通知当事人；对自诉案件，应当将案卷材料移送被指定管辖的人民法院，并书面通知当事人。

**Article 22** After receiving the written decision on changing jurisdiction, the written decision approving the transfer, or the written decision designating another People's Court to have jurisdiction issued by the higher People's Court, the People's Court that originally accepted the case shall, for a public prosecution case, notify the People's Procuratorate at the same level in writing, return the case file materials, and simultaneously notify the parties in writing; for a private prosecution case, it shall transfer the case file materials to the People's Court designated to have jurisdiction and notify the parties in writing.

<a id="art-23"></a>第二十三条 第二审人民法院发回重新审判的案件，人民检察院撤回起诉后，又向原第一审人民法院的下级人民法院重新提起公诉的，下级人民法院应当将有关情况层报原第二审人民法院。原第二审人民法院根据具体情况，可以决定将案件移送原第一审人民法院或者其他人民法院审判。

**Article 23** Where, in a case remanded for retrial by the People's Court of Second Instance, the People's Procuratorate withdraws the prosecution and then re-institutes public prosecution with a People's Court lower than the original first-instance People's Court, the lower People's Court shall report the relevant circumstances level by level to the original People's Court of Second Instance. The original People's Court of Second Instance may, in light of the specific circumstances, decide to transfer the case to the original first-instance People's Court or another People's Court for trial.

<a id="art-24"></a>第二十四条 人民法院发现被告人还有其他犯罪被起诉的，可以并案审理；涉及同种犯罪的，一般应当并案审理。  
人民法院发现被告人还有其他犯罪被审查起诉、立案侦查、立案调查的，可以参照前款规定协商人民检察院、公安机关、监察机关并案处理，但可能造成审判过分迟延的除外。  
根据前两款规定并案处理的案件，由最初受理地的人民法院审判。必要时，可以由主要犯罪地的人民法院审判。

**Article 24** Where a People's Court discovers that the defendant has been prosecuted for other crimes, it may consolidate the cases for trial; where the same kind of crime is involved, the cases shall generally be consolidated for trial.  
Where a People's Court discovers that the defendant has other crimes that are being examined for prosecution, placed on file for investigation, or placed on file for investigation by a supervisory organ, it may, by reference to the preceding paragraph, consult with the People's Procuratorate, the public security organ, or the supervisory organ for consolidated handling, except where such consolidation may cause excessive delay of trial.  
Cases handled in a consolidated manner pursuant to the preceding two paragraphs shall be tried by the People's Court at the place that first accepted the case. Where necessary, they may be tried by the People's Court at the principal place of the crime.

<a id="art-25"></a>第二十五条 第二审人民法院在审理过程中，发现被告人还有其他犯罪没有判决的，参照前条规定处理。第二审人民法院决定并案审理的，应当发回第一审人民法院，由第一审人民法院作出处理。

**Article 25** Where the People's Court of Second Instance, in the course of adjudication, discovers that the defendant has other crimes that have not been adjudicated, it shall handle the matter by reference to the preceding article. If the People's Court of Second Instance decides to consolidate the cases for trial, it shall remand the case to the first-instance People's Court, and the first-instance People's Court shall make the handling.

<a id="art-26"></a>第二十六条 军队和地方互涉刑事案件，按照有关规定确定管辖。

**Article 26** Criminal cases involving both the military and local authorities shall have jurisdiction determined in accordance with the relevant provisions.

### 第二章 回避

<a id="art-27"></a>第二十七条 审判人员具有下列情形之一的，应当自行回避，当事人及其法定代理人有权申请其回避：  
（一）是本案的当事人或者是当事人的近亲属的；  
（二）本人或者其近亲属与本案有利害关系的；  
（三）担任过本案的证人、鉴定人、辩护人、诉讼代理人、翻译人员的；  
（四）与本案的辩护人、诉讼代理人有近亲属关系的；  
（五）与本案当事人有其他利害关系，可能影响公正审判的。

**Article 27** Where judicial personnel fall under any of the following circumstances, they shall withdraw on their own initiative, and the party and his or her legal representative have the right to apply for their withdrawal:  
(1) being a party to the case or a close relative of a party;  
(2) having a personal interest in the case themselves or through their close relatives;  
(3) having served as a witness, expert witness, defender, litigation agent, or interpreter in the case;  
(4) having a close relative relationship with the defender or litigation agent in the case;  
(5) having other interests with the parties to the case that may affect impartial adjudication.

<a id="art-28"></a>第二十八条 审判人员具有下列情形之一的，当事人及其法定代理人有权申请其回避：  
（一）违反规定会见本案当事人、辩护人、诉讼代理人的；  
（二）为本案当事人推荐、介绍辩护人、诉讼代理人，或者为律师、其他人员介绍办理本案的；  
（三）索取、接受本案当事人及其委托的人的财物或者其他利益的；  
（四）接受本案当事人及其委托的人的宴请，或者参加由其支付费用的活动的；  
（五）向本案当事人及其委托的人借用款物的；  
（六）有其他不正当行为，可能影响公正审判的。

**Article 28** Where judicial personnel fall under any of the following circumstances, the party and his or her legal representative have the right to apply for their withdrawal:  
(1) meeting with the parties, defenders, or litigation agents of the case in violation of provisions;  
(2) recommending or introducing defenders or litigation agents for the parties to the case, or introducing lawyers or other persons to handle the case;  
(3) demanding or accepting property or other benefits from the parties to the case or the persons entrusted by them;  
(4) accepting banquet invitations from the parties to the case or the persons entrusted by them, or participating in activities paid for by them;  
(5) borrowing money or property from the parties to the case or the persons entrusted by them;  
(6) committing other improper acts that may affect impartial adjudication.

<a id="art-29"></a>第二十九条 参与过本案调查、侦查、审查起诉工作的监察、侦查、检察人员，调至人民法院工作的，不得担任本案的审判人员。  
在一个审判程序中参与过本案审判工作的合议庭组成人员或者独任审判员，不得再参与本案其他程序的审判。但是，发回重新审判的案件，在第一审人民法院作出裁判后又进入第二审程序、在法定刑以下判处刑罚的复核程序或者死刑复核程序的，原第二审程序、在法定刑以下判处刑罚的复核程序或者死刑复核程序中的合议庭组成人员不受本款规定的限制。

**Article 29** Personnel of supervisory, investigative, and procuratorial organs who participated in the supervisory investigation, criminal investigation, or examination and prosecution of the case and have been transferred to a People's Court shall not serve as judicial personnel for the case.  
Members of a collegial panel or a sole adjudicator who have participated in the adjudication of the case in one adjudication procedure shall not participate in the adjudication of the case in any other procedure. However, in a case remanded for retrial, where, after the first-instance People's Court renders a judgment, the case enters the second-instance procedure, the review procedure for a sentence below the statutory punishment, or the death penalty review procedure, the members of the collegial panel in the original second-instance procedure, review procedure for a sentence below the statutory punishment, or death penalty review procedure are not subject to the restriction of this paragraph.

<a id="art-30"></a>第三十条 依照法律和有关规定应当实行任职回避的，不得担任案件的审判人员。

**Article 30** Those who shall be subject to recusal from office in accordance with law and relevant provisions shall not serve as judicial personnel for a case.

<a id="art-31"></a>第三十一条 人民法院应当依法告知当事人及其法定代理人有权申请回避，并告知其合议庭组成人员、独任审判员、法官助理、书记员等人员的名单。

**Article 31** A People's Court shall, according to law, inform the party and his or her legal representative of the right to apply for withdrawal, and inform them of the list of members of the collegial panel, the sole adjudicator, judge assistant, court clerk, and other personnel.

<a id="art-32"></a>第三十二条 审判人员自行申请回避，或者当事人及其法定代理人申请审判人员回避的，可以口头或者书面提出，并说明理由，由院长决定。  
院长自行申请回避，或者当事人及其法定代理人申请院长回避的，由审判委员会讨论决定。审判委员会讨论时，由副院长主持，院长不得参加。

**Article 32** Where judicial personnel apply for withdrawal on their own initiative, or the party and his or her legal representative apply for the withdrawal of judicial personnel, the application may be made orally or in writing with reasons stated, and the decision shall be made by the president of the court.  
Where the president applies for withdrawal on his or her own initiative, or the party and his or her legal representative apply for the withdrawal of the president, the matter shall be discussed and decided by the Judicial Committee. When the Judicial Committee discusses the matter, it shall be presided over by the vice president, and the president shall not participate.

<a id="art-33"></a>第三十三条 当事人及其法定代理人依照[刑事诉讼法第三十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-30)和[本解释第二十八条](#art-28)的规定申请回避的，应当提供证明材料。

**Article 33** Where the party and his or her legal representative apply for withdrawal in accordance with Article 30 of the Criminal Procedure Law and Article 28 of this Interpretation, they shall provide evidentiary materials.

<a id="art-34"></a>第三十四条 应当回避的审判人员没有自行回避，当事人及其法定代理人也没有申请其回避的，院长或者审判委员会应当决定其回避。

**Article 34** Where judicial personnel who should withdraw do not withdraw on their own initiative, and the party and his or her legal representative also do not apply for their withdrawal, the president of the court or the Judicial Committee shall decide that they withdraw.

<a id="art-35"></a>第三十五条 对当事人及其法定代理人提出的回避申请，人民法院可以口头或者书面作出决定，并将决定告知申请人。  
当事人及其法定代理人申请回避被驳回的，可以在接到决定时申请复议一次。不属于[刑事诉讼法第二十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-29)、第三十条规定情形的回避申请，由法庭当庭驳回，并不得申请复议。

**Article 35** A People's Court may make a decision orally or in writing on an application for withdrawal filed by the party and his or her legal representative, and shall notify the applicant of the decision.  
Where the application for withdrawal filed by the party and his or her legal representative is rejected, they may apply for reconsideration once at the time of receiving the decision. An application for withdrawal that does not fall under the circumstances provided for in Articles 29 and 30 of the Criminal Procedure Law shall be rejected by the court in court, and no reconsideration may be applied for.

<a id="art-36"></a>第三十六条 当事人及其法定代理人申请出庭的检察人员回避的，人民法院应当区分情况作出处理：  
（一）属于[刑事诉讼法第二十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-29)、第三十条规定情形的回避申请，应当决定休庭，并通知人民检察院尽快作出决定；  
（二）不属于[刑事诉讼法第二十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-29)、第三十条规定情形的回避申请，应当当庭驳回，并不得申请复议。

**Article 36** Where the party and his or her legal representative apply for the withdrawal of procuratorial personnel appearing in court, the People's Court shall handle the matter according to the circumstances:  
(1) Where the application for withdrawal falls under the circumstances provided for in Articles 29 and 30 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the court shall decide to adjourn and notify the People's Procuratorate to make a decision as soon as possible;  
(2) Where the application for withdrawal does not fall under the circumstances provided for in Articles 29 and 30 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the court shall reject it in court, and no reconsideration may be applied for.

<a id="art-37"></a>第三十七条 本章所称的审判人员，包括人民法院院长、副院长、审判委员会委员、庭长、副庭长、审判员和人民陪审员。

**Article 37** The judicial personnel mentioned in this Chapter include the president, vice-presidents, members of the Judicial Committee, chief judges, deputy chief judges, judges, and people's assessors of the People's Court.

<a id="art-38"></a>第三十八条 法官助理、书记员、翻译人员和鉴定人适用审判人员回避的有关规定，其回避问题由院长决定。

**Article 38** The relevant provisions on withdrawal of judicial personnel shall apply to judge assistants, court clerks, interpreters, and expert witnesses, and their withdrawal shall be decided by the president of the court.

<a id="art-39"></a>第三十九条 辩护人、诉讼代理人可以依照本章的有关规定要求回避、申请复议。

**Article 39** Defenders and litigation agents may request withdrawal and apply for reconsideration in accordance with the relevant provisions of this Chapter.

### 第三章 辩护与代理

<a id="art-40"></a>第四十条 人民法院审判案件，应当充分保障被告人依法享有的辩护权利。  
被告人除自己行使辩护权以外，还可以委托辩护人辩护。下列人员不得担任辩护人：  
（一）正在被执行刑罚或者处于缓刑、假释考验期间的人；  
（二）依法被剥夺、限制人身自由的人；  
（三）被开除公职或者被吊销律师、公证员执业证书的人；  
（四）人民法院、人民检察院、监察机关、公安机关、国家安全机关、监狱的现职人员；  
（五）人民陪审员；  
（六）与本案审理结果有利害关系的人；  
（七）外国人或者无国籍人；  
（八）无行为能力或者限制行为能力的人。  
前款第三项至第七项规定的人员，如果是被告人的监护人、近亲属，由被告人委托担任辩护人的，可以准许。

**Article 40** In adjudicating cases, a People's Court shall fully protect the right to defense enjoyed by defendants according to law.  
In addition to exercising the right to defense himself or herself, a defendant may also retain a defender to defend. The following persons shall not serve as defenders:  
(1) persons currently serving criminal punishments or under probation or parole;  
(2) persons whose personal freedom is deprived or restricted according to law;  
(3) persons who have been dismissed from public office or whose lawyer's or notary's practice certificates have been revoked;  
(4) current staff members of People's Courts, People's Procuratorates, supervisory organs, public security organs, state security organs, or prisons;  
(5) people's assessors;  
(6) persons who have an interest in the adjudication result of the case;  
(7) foreigners or stateless persons;  
(8) persons without or with limited capacity for conduct.  
Where a person specified in items (3) to (7) of the preceding paragraph is a guardian or close relative of the defendant and is retained by the defendant to serve as a defender, the retention may be permitted.

<a id="art-41"></a>第四十一条 审判人员和人民法院其他工作人员从人民法院离任后二年内，不得以律师身份担任辩护人。  
审判人员和人民法院其他工作人员从人民法院离任后，不得担任原任职法院所审理案件的辩护人，但系被告人的监护人、近亲属的除外。  
审判人员和人民法院其他工作人员的配偶、子女或者父母不得担任其任职法院所审理案件的辩护人，但系被告人的监护人、近亲属的除外。

**Article 41** Judicial personnel and other staff members of a People's Court shall not act as defenders in the capacity of lawyers within two years after leaving the People's Court.  
After leaving the People's Court, judicial personnel and other staff members of a People's Court shall not act as defenders in cases adjudicated by the court where they previously served, except where they are guardians or close relatives of the defendant.  
The spouses, children, or parents of judicial personnel and other staff members of a People's Court shall not act as defenders in cases adjudicated by the court where such personnel serve, except where they are guardians or close relatives of the defendant.

<a id="art-42"></a>第四十二条 对接受委托担任辩护人的，人民法院应当核实其身份证明和授权委托书。

**Article 42** Where a person accepts retention to serve as a defender, the People's Court shall verify his or her identification documents and the power of attorney.

<a id="art-43"></a>第四十三条 一名被告人可以委托一至二人作为辩护人。  
一名辩护人不得为两名以上的同案被告人，或者未同案处理但犯罪事实存在关联的被告人辩护。

**Article 43** A defendant may retain one or two persons as defenders.  
A defender shall not defend more than two co-defendants in the same case, or defendants who, although not handled in the same case, have related criminal facts.

<a id="art-44"></a>第四十四条 被告人没有委托辩护人的，人民法院自受理案件之日起三日以内，应当告知其有权委托辩护人；被告人因经济困难或者其他原因没有委托辩护人的，应当告知其可以申请法律援助；被告人属于应当提供法律援助情形的，应当告知其将依法通知法律援助机构指派律师为其提供辩护。  
被告人没有委托辩护人，法律援助机构也没有指派律师为其提供辩护的，人民法院应当告知被告人有权约见值班律师，并为被告人约见值班律师提供便利。  
告知可以采取口头或者书面方式。

**Article 44** Where a defendant has not retained a defender, the People's Court shall, within three days from the date of accepting the case, inform him or her of the right to retain a defender; where the defendant has not retained a defender due to financial difficulties or other reasons, the court shall inform him or her that he or she may apply for legal aid; where the defendant falls under the circumstances where legal aid shall be provided, the court shall inform him or her that it will notify the legal aid institution according to law to assign a lawyer to provide defense for him or her.  
Where the defendant has not retained a defender and the legal aid institution has not assigned a lawyer to provide defense for him or her, the People's Court shall inform the defendant of the right to meet with a duty lawyer, and shall provide convenience for the defendant to meet with the duty lawyer.  
The notification may be made orally or in writing.

<a id="art-45"></a>第四十五条 审判期间，在押的被告人要求委托辩护人的，人民法院应当在三日以内向其监护人、近亲属或者其指定的人员转达要求。被告人应当提供有关人员的联系方式。有关人员无法通知的，应当告知被告人。

**Article 45** During adjudication, where a detained defendant requests to retain a defender, the People's Court shall, within three days, convey the request to his or her guardian, close relatives, or the persons designated by him or her. The defendant shall provide the contact information of the relevant persons. Where the relevant persons cannot be notified, the defendant shall be informed.

<a id="art-46"></a>第四十六条 人民法院收到在押被告人提出的法律援助或者法律帮助申请，应当依照有关规定及时转交法律援助机构或者通知值班律师。

**Article 46** Where a People's Court receives an application for legal aid or legal assistance filed by a detained defendant, it shall, in accordance with the relevant provisions, promptly forward the application to the legal aid institution or notify the duty lawyer.

<a id="art-47"></a>第四十七条 对下列没有委托辩护人的被告人，人民法院应当通知法律援助机构指派律师为其提供辩护：  
（一）盲、聋、哑人；  
（二）尚未完全丧失辨认或者控制自己行为能力的精神病人；  
（三）可能被判处无期徒刑、死刑的人。  
高级人民法院复核死刑案件，被告人没有委托辩护人的，应当通知法律援助机构指派律师为其提供辩护。  
死刑缓期执行期间故意犯罪的案件，适用前两款规定。

**Article 47** With respect to the following defendants who have not retained defenders, the People's Court shall notify the legal aid institution to assign a lawyer to provide defense for them:  
(1) blind, deaf, or mute persons;  
(2) mental patients who have not completely lost the ability to recognize or control their own conduct;  
(3) persons who may be sentenced to life imprisonment or the death penalty.  
Where, in reviewing a death penalty case, the Higher People's Court finds that the defendant has not retained a defender, it shall notify the legal aid institution to assign a lawyer to provide defense for him or her.  
The provisions of the preceding two paragraphs shall apply to cases of intentional crimes committed during the period of death sentence with a two-year reprieve.

<a id="art-48"></a>第四十八条 具有下列情形之一，被告人没有委托辩护人的，人民法院可以通知法律援助机构指派律师为其提供辩护：  
（一）共同犯罪案件中，其他被告人已经委托辩护人的；  
（二）案件有重大社会影响的；  
（三）人民检察院抗诉的；  
（四）被告人的行为可能不构成犯罪的；  
（五）有必要指派律师提供辩护的其他情形。

**Article 48** Under any of the following circumstances, where the defendant has not retained a defender, the People's Court may notify the legal aid institution to assign a lawyer to provide defense for him or her:  
(1) in a joint crime case, other defendants have already retained defenders;  
(2) the case has major social impact;  
(3) the People's Procuratorate has filed a protest;  
(4) the defendant's conduct may not constitute a crime;  
(5) other circumstances where it is necessary to assign a lawyer to provide defense.

<a id="art-49"></a>第四十九条 人民法院通知法律援助机构指派律师提供辩护的，应当将法律援助通知书、起诉书副本或者判决书送达法律援助机构；决定开庭审理的，除适用简易程序或者速裁程序审理的以外，应当在开庭十五日以前将上述材料送达法律援助机构。  
法律援助通知书应当写明案由、被告人姓名、提供法律援助的理由、审判人员的姓名和联系方式；已确定开庭审理的，应当写明开庭的时间、地点。

**Article 49** Where a People's Court notifies a legal aid institution to assign a lawyer to provide defense, it shall serve the legal aid notice, the copy of the indictment, or the judgment on the legal aid institution; if it decides to hold a court session, unless the case is tried under the summary procedure or the expedited procedure, the above materials shall be served on the legal aid institution 15 days before the court session.  
The legal aid notice shall state the nature of the case, the name of the defendant, the reasons for providing legal aid, and the names and contact information of the adjudicators; if a court session has been scheduled, the time and place of the court session shall be stated.

<a id="art-50"></a>第五十条 被告人拒绝法律援助机构指派的律师为其辩护，坚持自己行使辩护权的，人民法院应当准许。  
属于应当提供法律援助的情形，被告人拒绝指派的律师为其辩护的，人民法院应当查明原因。理由正当的，应当准许，但被告人应当在五日以内另行委托辩护人；被告人未另行委托辩护人的，人民法院应当在三日以内通知法律援助机构另行指派律师为其提供辩护。

**Article 50** Where a defendant refuses the lawyer assigned by a legal aid institution to defend him and insists on exercising the right to defend himself, the People's Court shall permit it.  
Where the case falls under the circumstances in which legal aid shall be provided, and the defendant refuses the assigned lawyer to defend him, the People's Court shall ascertain the reasons. If the reasons are justified, it shall permit the refusal, but the defendant shall separately retain a defender within five days; if the defendant fails to separately retain a defender, the People's Court shall, within three days, notify the legal aid institution to assign another lawyer to provide defense for him.

<a id="art-51"></a>第五十一条 对法律援助机构指派律师为被告人提供辩护，被告人的监护人、近亲属又代为委托辩护人的，应当听取被告人的意见，由其确定辩护人人选。

**Article 51** Where a lawyer assigned by a legal aid institution provides defense for a defendant, and the defendant's guardian or close relatives also entrust a defender on behalf of the defendant, the defendant's opinion shall be heard, and the defendant shall determine the defender.

<a id="art-52"></a>第五十二条 审判期间，辩护人接受被告人委托的，应当在接受委托之日起三日以内，将委托手续提交人民法院。  
接受法律援助机构指派为被告人提供辩护的，适用前款规定。

**Article 52** Where a defender accepts the entrustment of a defendant during the trial, he shall submit the entrustment formalities to the People's Court within three days from the date of acceptance.  
Where a defender accepts an assignment by a legal aid institution to provide defense for a defendant, the preceding paragraph shall apply.

<a id="art-53"></a>第五十三条 辩护律师可以查阅、摘抄、复制案卷材料。其他辩护人经人民法院许可，也可以查阅、摘抄、复制案卷材料。合议庭、审判委员会的讨论记录以及其他依法不公开的材料不得查阅、摘抄、复制。  
辩护人查阅、摘抄、复制案卷材料的，人民法院应当提供便利，并保证必要的时间。  
值班律师查阅案卷材料的，适用前两款规定。  
复制案卷材料可以采用复印、拍照、扫描、电子数据拷贝等方式。

**Article 53** Defense lawyers may consult, extract, and copy case file materials. Other defenders may also consult, extract, and copy case file materials with the permission of the People's Court. The discussion records of the collegiate bench and the adjudication committee, as well as other materials not publicly disclosed according to law, shall not be consulted, extracted, or copied.  
Where defenders consult, extract, or copy case file materials, the People's Court shall provide convenience and ensure necessary time.  
Where duty lawyers consult case file materials, the preceding two paragraphs shall apply.  
Copying case file materials may be done by photocopying, photographing, scanning, electronic data copying, or other means.

<a id="art-54"></a>第五十四条 对作为证据材料向人民法院移送的讯问录音录像，辩护律师申请查阅的，人民法院应当准许。

**Article 54** Where a defense lawyer applies to consult the audio and video recordings of interrogations transferred to the People's Court as evidence materials, the People's Court shall permit it.

<a id="art-55"></a>第五十五条 查阅、摘抄、复制案卷材料，涉及国家秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私的，应当保密；对不公开审理案件的信息、材料，或者在办案过程中获悉的案件重要信息、证据材料，不得违反规定泄露、披露，不得用于办案以外的用途。人民法院可以要求相关人员出具承诺书。  
违反前款规定的，人民法院可以通报司法行政机关或者有关部门，建议给予相应处罚；构成犯罪的，依法追究刑事责任。

**Article 55** Where consulting, extracting, or copying case file materials involves state secrets, trade secrets, or personal privacy, confidentiality shall be maintained; information and materials of cases tried in camera, or important case information and evidence materials learned in the course of handling the case, shall not be divulged or disclosed in violation of regulations, and shall not be used for purposes other than case handling. The People's Court may require relevant personnel to issue a letter of commitment.  
Where the preceding paragraph is violated, the People's Court may notify the judicial administrative organ or the relevant department and recommend corresponding penalties; if a crime is constituted, criminal liability shall be investigated according to law.

<a id="art-56"></a>第五十六条 辩护律师可以同在押的或者被监视居住的被告人会见和通信。其他辩护人经人民法院许可，也可以同在押的或者被监视居住的被告人会见和通信。

**Article 56** Defense lawyers may meet and correspond with defendants in custody or under residential surveillance. Other defenders may also meet and correspond with defendants in custody or under residential surveillance with the permission of the People's Court.

<a id="art-57"></a>第五十七条 辩护人认为在调查、侦查、审查起诉期间监察机关、公安机关、人民检察院收集的证明被告人无罪或者罪轻的证据材料未随案移送，申请人民法院调取的，应当以书面形式提出，并提供相关线索或者材料。人民法院接受申请后，应当向人民检察院调取。人民检察院移送相关证据材料后，人民法院应当及时通知辩护人。

**Article 57** Where a defender believes that evidence materials proving the defendant's innocence or minor guilt, collected by a supervisory organ, a public security organ, or a People's Procuratorate during investigation and examination and prosecution, were not transferred with the case, and applies to the People's Court to obtain them, the application shall be made in writing and provide relevant clues or materials. After accepting the application, the People's Court shall obtain them from the People's Procuratorate. After the People's Procuratorate transfers the relevant evidence materials, the People's Court shall promptly notify the defender.

<a id="art-58"></a>第五十八条 辩护律师申请向被害人及其近亲属、被害人提供的证人收集与本案有关的材料，人民法院认为确有必要的，应当签发准许调查书。

**Article 58** Where a defense lawyer applies to collect materials related to the case from the victim, the victim's close relatives, or witnesses provided by the victim, the People's Court shall issue a written permission for investigation if it considers it truly necessary.

<a id="art-59"></a>第五十九条 辩护律师向证人或者有关单位、个人收集、调取与本案有关的证据材料，因证人或者有关单位、个人不同意，申请人民法院收集、调取，或者申请通知证人出庭作证，人民法院认为确有必要的，应当同意。

**Article 59** Where a defense lawyer collects or obtains evidence materials related to the case from witnesses or relevant entities or individuals, and because the witnesses or relevant entities or individuals do not consent, the defense lawyer applies to the People's Court to collect or obtain them, or applies to notify the witnesses to appear in court to testify, the People's Court shall agree if it considers it truly necessary.

<a id="art-60"></a>第六十条 辩护律师直接申请人民法院向证人或者有关单位、个人收集、调取证据材料，人民法院认为确有必要，且不宜或者不能由辩护律师收集、调取的，应当同意。  
人民法院向有关单位收集、调取的书面证据材料，必须由提供人签名，并加盖单位印章；向个人收集、调取的书面证据材料，必须由提供人签名。  
人民法院对有关单位、个人提供的证据材料，应当出具收据，写明证据材料的名称、收到的时间、件数、页数以及是否为原件等，由书记员、法官助理或者审判人员签名。  
收集、调取证据材料后，应当及时通知辩护律师查阅、摘抄、复制，并告知人民检察院。

**Article 60** Where a defense lawyer directly applies to the People's Court to collect or obtain evidence materials from witnesses or relevant entities or individuals, the People's Court shall agree if it considers it truly necessary and that it is inappropriate or impossible for the defense lawyer to collect or obtain them.  
Written evidence materials collected or obtained by the People's Court from relevant entities must be signed by the provider and affixed with the entity's seal; written evidence materials collected or obtained from individuals must be signed by the provider.  
The People's Court shall issue a receipt for the evidence materials provided by relevant entities or individuals, stating the names of the evidence materials, the time of receipt, the number of items, the number of pages, and whether they are originals, and the receipt shall be signed by the court clerk, judge assistant, or adjudicator.  
After collecting or obtaining the evidence materials, the People's Court shall promptly notify the defense lawyer to consult, extract, and copy them, and inform the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-61"></a>第六十一条 [本解释第五十八条](#art-58)至第六十条规定的申请，应当以书面形式提出，并说明理由，写明需要收集、调取证据材料的内容或者需要调查问题的提纲。  
对辩护律师的申请，人民法院应当在五日以内作出是否准许、同意的决定，并通知申请人；决定不准许、不同意的，应当说明理由。

**Article 61** The applications provided in Articles 58 through 60 of this Interpretation shall be made in writing, state the reasons, and specify the contents of the evidence materials to be collected or obtained, or the outline of the issues to be investigated.  
With respect to a defense lawyer's application, the People's Court shall, within five days, make a decision on whether to permit or agree, and notify the applicant; where it decides not to permit or agree, the reasons shall be stated.

<a id="art-62"></a>第六十二条 人民法院自受理自诉案件之日起三日以内，应当告知自诉人及其法定代理人、附带民事诉讼当事人及其法定代理人，有权委托诉讼代理人，并告知其如果经济困难，可以申请法律援助。

**Article 62** Within three days from the date of accepting a private prosecution case, the People's Court shall inform the private prosecutor and his legal representative, and the parties to the incidental civil action and their legal representatives, that they have the right to entrust litigation agents, and shall inform them that if they have financial difficulties, they may apply for legal aid.

<a id="art-63"></a>第六十三条 当事人委托诉讼代理人的，参照适用[刑事诉讼法第三十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-33)和本解释的有关规定。

**Article 63** Where a party entrusts a litigation agent, Article 33 of the Criminal Procedure Law and the relevant provisions of this Interpretation shall apply mutatis mutandis.

<a id="art-64"></a>第六十四条 诉讼代理人有权根据事实和法律，维护被害人、自诉人或者附带民事诉讼当事人的诉讼权利和其他合法权益。

**Article 64** A litigation agent has the right, in accordance with the facts and the law, to safeguard the litigation rights and other lawful rights and interests of the victim, the private prosecutor, or the party to the incidental civil action.

<a id="art-65"></a>第六十五条 律师担任诉讼代理人的，可以查阅、摘抄、复制案卷材料。其他诉讼代理人经人民法院许可，也可以查阅、摘抄、复制案卷材料。  
律师担任诉讼代理人，需要收集、调取与本案有关的证据材料的，参照适用[本解释第五十九条](#art-59)至第六十一条的规定。

**Article 65** Where a lawyer serves as a litigation agent, he may consult, extract, and copy case file materials. Other litigation agents may also consult, extract, and copy case file materials with the permission of the People's Court.  
Where a lawyer serving as a litigation agent needs to collect or obtain evidence materials related to the case, the provisions of Articles 59 through 61 of this Interpretation shall apply mutatis mutandis.

<a id="art-66"></a>第六十六条 诉讼代理人接受当事人委托或者法律援助机构指派后，应当在三日以内将委托手续或者法律援助手续提交人民法院。

**Article 66** After accepting a party's entrustment or a legal aid institution's assignment, a litigation agent shall submit the entrustment formalities or legal aid formalities to the People's Court within three days.

<a id="art-67"></a>第六十七条 辩护律师向人民法院告知其委托人或者其他人准备实施、正在实施危害国家安全、公共安全以及严重危害他人人身安全犯罪的，人民法院应当记录在案，立即转告主管机关依法处理，并为反映有关情况的辩护律师保密。

**Article 67** Where a defense lawyer informs the People's Court that his principal or another person is preparing to commit or is committing a crime endangering national security, public security, or seriously endangering the personal safety of others, the People's Court shall record it in the case file, immediately notify the competent authorities to handle it according to law, and maintain confidentiality for the defense lawyer who reported the relevant circumstances.

<a id="art-68"></a>第六十八条 律师担任辩护人、诉讼代理人，经人民法院准许，可以带一名助理参加庭审。律师助理参加庭审的，可以从事辅助工作，但不得发表辩护、代理意见。

**Article 68** Where a lawyer serves as a defender or litigation agent, he may, with the permission of the People's Court, bring one assistant to participate in the court session. If the lawyer's assistant participates in the court session, he may perform auxiliary work but shall not present defense or representation opinions.

### 第四章 证据

#### 第一节 一般规定

<a id="art-69"></a>第六十九条 认定案件事实，必须以证据为根据。

**Article 69** The facts of a case must be determined on the basis of evidence.

<a id="art-70"></a>第七十条 审判人员应当依照法定程序收集、审查、核实、认定证据。

**Article 70** Judicial personnel shall collect, examine, verify, and determine evidence in accordance with statutory procedures.

<a id="art-71"></a>第七十一条 证据未经当庭出示、辨认、质证等法庭调查程序查证属实，不得作为定案的根据。

**Article 71** Evidence that has not been verified to be true through courtroom investigation procedures such as presentation in court, identification, and cross-examination shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case.

<a id="art-72"></a>第七十二条 应当运用证据证明的案件事实包括：  
（一）被告人、被害人的身份；  
（二）被指控的犯罪是否存在；  
（三）被指控的犯罪是否为被告人所实施；  
（四）被告人有无刑事责任能力，有无罪过，实施犯罪的动机、目的；  
（五）实施犯罪的时间、地点、手段、后果以及案件起因等；  
（六）是否系共同犯罪或者犯罪事实存在关联，以及被告人在犯罪中的地位、作用；  
（七）被告人有无从重、从轻、减轻、免除处罚情节；  
（八）有关涉案财物处理的事实；  
（九）有关附带民事诉讼的事实；  
（十）有关管辖、回避、延期审理等的程序事实；  
（十一）与定罪量刑有关的其他事实。  
认定被告人有罪和对被告人从重处罚，适用证据确实、充分的证明标准。

**Article 72** The facts of a case to be proved by evidence include:  
(1) the identities of the defendant and the victim;  
(2) whether the alleged crime exists;  
(3) whether the alleged crime was committed by the defendant;  
(4) whether the defendant has criminal liability capacity, whether there is culpability, and the motive and purpose of committing the crime;  
(5) the time, place, means, consequences, and causes of the crime, etc.;  
(6) whether it is a joint crime or whether the criminal facts are related, and the defendant's status and role in the crime;  
(7) whether the defendant has circumstances for severe punishment, light punishment, mitigated punishment, or exemption from punishment;  
(8) the facts concerning the disposition of property involved in the case;  
(9) the facts concerning the incidental civil action;  
(10) procedural facts such as those concerning jurisdiction, withdrawal, and adjournment;  
(11) other facts related to conviction and sentencing.  
The standard of proof of reliable and sufficient evidence shall apply to finding the defendant guilty and imposing severe punishment on the defendant.

<a id="art-73"></a>第七十三条 对提起公诉的案件，人民法院应当审查证明被告人有罪、无罪、罪重、罪轻的证据材料是否全部随案移送；未随案移送的，应当通知人民检察院在指定时间内移送。人民检察院未移送的，人民法院应当根据在案证据对案件事实作出认定。

**Article 73** With respect to a case prosecuted by the People's Procuratorate, the People's Court shall review whether all evidence materials proving the defendant's guilt, innocence, serious guilt, or minor guilt have been transferred with the case; where they have not been transferred with the case, it shall notify the People's Procuratorate to transfer them within a specified time. If the People's Procuratorate fails to transfer them, the People's Court shall determine the facts of the case based on the evidence on file.

<a id="art-74"></a>第七十四条 依法应当对讯问过程录音录像的案件，相关录音录像未随案移送的，必要时，人民法院可以通知人民检察院在指定时间内移送。人民检察院未移送，导致不能排除属于[刑事诉讼法第五十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-56)规定的以非法方法收集证据情形的，对有关证据应当依法排除；导致有关证据的真实性无法确认的，不得作为定案的根据。

**Article 74** With respect to a case in which audio and video recording of the interrogation process shall be made according to law, if the relevant audio and video recordings are not transferred with the case, the People's Court may, when necessary, notify the People's Procuratorate to transfer them within a specified time. If the People's Procuratorate fails to transfer them, resulting in the inability to exclude the circumstance that the evidence was collected by illegal methods as provided in Article 56 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the relevant evidence shall be excluded according to law; if the authenticity of the relevant evidence cannot be confirmed, it shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case.

<a id="art-75"></a>第七十五条 行政机关在行政执法和查办案件过程中收集的物证、书证、视听资料、电子数据等证据材料，经法庭查证属实，且收集程序符合有关法律、行政法规规定的，可以作为定案的根据。  
根据法律、行政法规规定行使国家行政管理职权的组织，在行政执法和查办案件过程中收集的证据材料，视为行政机关收集的证据材料。

**Article 75** Evidence materials such as physical evidence, documentary evidence, audio-visual materials, and electronic data collected by administrative organs in the course of administrative law enforcement and case investigation, after being verified to be true by the court, and if the collection procedures comply with relevant laws and administrative regulations, may be used as the basis for deciding the case.  
Evidence materials collected by organizations exercising state administrative management functions in accordance with laws and administrative regulations during administrative law enforcement and case investigation shall be deemed evidence materials collected by administrative organs.

<a id="art-76"></a>第七十六条 监察机关依法收集的证据材料，在刑事诉讼中可以作为证据使用。  
对前款规定证据的审查判断，适用刑事审判关于证据的要求和标准。

**Article 76** Evidence materials lawfully collected by supervisory organs may be used as evidence in criminal proceedings.  
The examination and determination of the evidence provided in the preceding paragraph shall apply the requirements and standards for evidence in criminal trials.

<a id="art-77"></a>第七十七条 对来自境外的证据材料，人民检察院应当随案移送有关材料来源、提供人、提取人、提取时间等情况的说明。经人民法院审查，相关证据材料能够证明案件事实且符合刑事诉讼法规定的，可以作为证据使用，但提供人或者我国与有关国家签订的双边条约对材料的使用范围有明确限制的除外；材料来源不明或者真实性无法确认的，不得作为定案的根据。  
当事人及其辩护人、诉讼代理人提供来自境外的证据材料的，该证据材料应当经所在国公证机关证明，所在国中央外交主管机关或者其授权机关认证，并经中华人民共和国驻该国使领馆认证，或者履行中华人民共和国与该所在国订立的有关条约中规定的证明手续，但我国与该国之间有互免认证协定的除外。

**Article 77** With respect to evidence materials from outside the territory, the People's Procuratorate shall transfer with the case a statement of the source of the materials, the provider, the extractor, the time of extraction, and other circumstances. After examination by the People's Court, if the relevant evidence materials can prove the facts of the case and comply with the provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law, they may be used as evidence, except where the provider or a bilateral treaty concluded between China and the relevant country clearly restricts the scope of use of the materials; if the source of the materials is unknown or their authenticity cannot be confirmed, they shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case.  
Where a party and his defender or litigation agent provide evidence materials from outside the territory, such evidence materials shall be certified by the notary authority of the country where they are located, authenticated by the central diplomatic authorities of that country or their authorized organs, and authenticated by the embassy or consulate of the People's Republic of China in that country, or go through the certification formalities prescribed in the relevant treaty concluded between the People's Republic of China and that country, except where there is an agreement on mutual exemption of authentication between China and that country.

<a id="art-78"></a>第七十八条 控辩双方提供的证据材料涉及外国语言、文字的，应当附中文译本。

**Article 78** Where evidence materials provided by the prosecution and the defense involve foreign languages, a Chinese translation shall be attached.

<a id="art-79"></a>第七十九条 人民法院依照[刑事诉讼法第一百九十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-196)的规定调查核实证据，必要时，可以通知检察人员、辩护人、自诉人及其法定代理人到场。上述人员未到场的，应当记录在案。  
人民法院调查核实证据时，发现对定罪量刑有重大影响的新的证据材料的，应当告知检察人员、辩护人、自诉人及其法定代理人。必要时，也可以直接提取，并及时通知检察人员、辩护人、自诉人及其法定代理人查阅、摘抄、复制。

**Article 79** Where the People's Court investigates and verifies evidence in accordance with Article 196 of the Criminal Procedure Law, it may, when necessary, notify prosecutors, defenders, private prosecutors, and their legal representatives to be present. If the above-mentioned persons are not present, this shall be recorded in the case file.  
When the People's Court investigates and verifies evidence and discovers new evidence materials that have a significant impact on conviction and sentencing, it shall inform prosecutors, defenders, private prosecutors, and their legal representatives. When necessary, it may also directly extract them and promptly notify prosecutors, defenders, private prosecutors, and their legal representatives to consult, extract, and copy them.

<a id="art-80"></a>第八十条 下列人员不得担任见证人：  
（一）生理上、精神上有缺陷或者年幼，不具有相应辨别能力或者不能正确表达的人；  
（二）与案件有利害关系，可能影响案件公正处理的人；  
（三）行使勘验、检查、搜查、扣押、组织辨认等监察调查、刑事诉讼职权的监察、公安、司法机关的工作人员或者其聘用的人员。  
对见证人是否属于前款规定的人员，人民法院可以通过相关笔录载明的见证人的姓名、身份证件种类及号码、联系方式以及常住人口信息登记表等材料进行审查。  
由于客观原因无法由符合条件的人员担任见证人的，应当在笔录材料中注明情况，并对相关活动进行全程录音录像。

**Article 80** The following persons shall not serve as witnesses:  
(1) persons who have physiological or mental defects or are young, lacking the corresponding ability to discern or the ability to express correctly;  
(2) persons who have an interest in the case and may affect the fair handling of the case;  
(3) staff members of supervisory, public security, or judicial organs, or persons employed by them, who exercise supervisory investigation or criminal litigation functions such as inspection, examination, search, seizure, or organizing identification.  
The People's Court may review whether a witness falls under the categories in the preceding paragraph by examining the witness's name, type and number of identification document, contact information, permanent residence information registration form, and other materials recorded in the relevant records.  
Where due to objective reasons no qualified person can serve as a witness, the circumstances shall be noted in the record materials, and the relevant activities shall be recorded in full by audio and video.

<a id="art-81"></a>第八十一条 公开审理案件时，公诉人、诉讼参与人提出涉及国家秘密、商业秘密或者个人隐私的证据的，法庭应当制止；确与本案有关的，可以根据具体情况，决定将案件转为不公开审理，或者对相关证据的法庭调查不公开进行。

**Article 81** In a case tried in public, if the public prosecutor or a participant in the proceedings presents evidence involving state secrets, trade secrets, or personal privacy, the court shall stop it; if it is indeed related to the case, the court may, according to the specific circumstances, decide to convert the case into a non-public trial, or conduct the courtroom investigation of the relevant evidence in camera.

#### 第二节 物证、书证的审查与认定

<a id="art-82"></a>第八十二条 对物证、书证应当着重审查以下内容：  
（一）物证、书证是否为原物、原件，是否经过辨认、鉴定；物证的照片、录像、复制品或者书证的副本、复制件是否与原物、原件相符，是否由二人以上制作，有无制作人关于制作过程以及原物、原件存放于何处的文字说明和签名；  
（二）物证、书证的收集程序、方式是否符合法律、有关规定；经勘验、检查、搜查提取、扣押的物证、书证，是否附有相关笔录、清单，笔录、清单是否经调查人员或者侦查人员、物品持有人、见证人签名，没有签名的，是否注明原因；物品的名称、特征、数量、质量等是否注明清楚；  
（三）物证、书证在收集、保管、鉴定过程中是否受损或者改变；  
（四）物证、书证与案件事实有无关联；对现场遗留与犯罪有关的具备鉴定条件的血迹、体液、毛发、指纹等生物样本、痕迹、物品，是否已作DNA鉴定、指纹鉴定等，并与被告人或者被害人的相应生物特征、物品等比对；  
（五）与案件事实有关联的物证、书证是否全面收集。

**Article 82** The following contents shall be examined with emphasis with respect to physical evidence and documentary evidence:  
(1) whether the physical or documentary evidence is the original object or original document, and whether it has been identified or appraised; whether photographs, videos, or reproductions of physical evidence or copies or duplicates of documentary evidence are consistent with the original object or original document, whether they were made by two or more persons, and whether there are written explanations and signatures of the makers regarding the production process and the place where the original object or original document is stored;  
(2) whether the collection procedures and methods of the physical and documentary evidence comply with laws and relevant regulations; whether physical and documentary evidence extracted or seized through inspection, examination, search, and seizure is accompanied by relevant records and lists; whether the records and lists are signed by the investigators, the holders of the items, and witnesses; if not signed, whether the reasons are stated; whether the names, characteristics, quantities, quality, etc., of the items are clearly stated;  
(3) whether the physical and documentary evidence was damaged or altered during collection, custody, or appraisal;  
(4) whether the physical and documentary evidence is related to the facts of the case; with respect to biological samples, traces, and items such as bloodstains, body fluids, hair, and fingerprints left at the scene that are related to the crime and meet the conditions for identification, whether DNA identification, fingerprint identification, etc., have been conducted, and whether they have been compared with the corresponding biological characteristics or items of the defendant or the victim;  
(5) whether all physical and documentary evidence related to the facts of the case has been comprehensively collected.

<a id="art-83"></a>第八十三条 据以定案的物证应当是原物。原物不便搬运、不易保存、依法应当返还或者依法应当由有关部门保管、处理的，可以拍摄、制作足以反映原物外形和特征的照片、录像、复制品。必要时，审判人员可以前往保管场所查看原物。  
物证的照片、录像、复制品，不能反映原物的外形和特征的，不得作为定案的根据。  
物证的照片、录像、复制品，经与原物核对无误、经鉴定或者以其他方式确认真实的，可以作为定案的根据。

**Article 83** The physical evidence on which a case is decided shall be the original object. Where the original object is inconvenient to move, not easy to preserve, shall be returned according to law, or shall be kept or disposed of by the relevant department according to law, photographs, videos, or reproductions that sufficiently reflect the shape and characteristics of the original object may be taken or made. When necessary, judicial personnel may go to the place of custody to inspect the original object.  
Where photographs, videos, or reproductions of physical evidence cannot reflect the shape and characteristics of the original object, they shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case.  
Where photographs, videos, or reproductions of physical evidence have been verified to be accurate against the original object, confirmed by appraisal, or confirmed to be authentic by other means, they may be used as the basis for deciding the case.

<a id="art-84"></a>第八十四条 据以定案的书证应当是原件。取得原件确有困难的，可以使用副本、复制件。  
对书证的更改或者更改迹象不能作出合理解释，或者书证的副本、复制件不能反映原件及其内容的，不得作为定案的根据。  
书证的副本、复制件，经与原件核对无误、经鉴定或者以其他方式确认真实的，可以作为定案的根据。

**Article 84** The documentary evidence on which a case is decided shall be the original document. Where obtaining the original document is truly difficult, copies or duplicates may be used.  
Where a reasonable explanation cannot be given for an alteration to documentary evidence or signs of alteration, or where copies or duplicates of documentary evidence cannot reflect the original document and its content, they shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case.  
Where copies or duplicates of documentary evidence have been verified to be accurate against the original document, confirmed by appraisal, or confirmed to be authentic by other means, they may be used as the basis for deciding the case.

<a id="art-85"></a>第八十五条 对与案件事实可能有关联的血迹、体液、毛发、人体组织、指纹、足迹、字迹等生物样本、痕迹和物品，应当提取而没有提取，应当鉴定而没有鉴定，应当移送鉴定意见而没有移送，导致案件事实存疑的，人民法院应当通知人民检察院依法补充收集、调取、移送证据。

**Article 85** Where biological samples, traces, and items such as bloodstains, body fluids, hair, human tissue, fingerprints, footprints, and handwriting that may be related to the facts of the case should have been extracted but were not, should have been appraised but were not, or should have had appraisal opinions transferred but were not, resulting in doubt about the facts of the case, the People's Court shall notify the People's Procuratorate to supplement the collection, obtaining, and transfer of evidence according to law.

<a id="art-86"></a>第八十六条 在勘验、检查、搜查过程中提取、扣押的物证、书证，未附笔录或者清单，不能证明物证、书证来源的，不得作为定案的根据。  
物证、书证的收集程序、方式有下列瑕疵，经补正或者作出合理解释的，可以采用：  
（一）勘验、检查、搜查、提取笔录或者扣押清单上没有调查人员或者侦查人员、物品持有人、见证人签名，或者对物品的名称、特征、数量、质量等注明不详的；  
（二）物证的照片、录像、复制品，书证的副本、复制件未注明与原件核对无异，无复制时间，或者无被收集、调取人签名的；  
（三）物证的照片、录像、复制品，书证的副本、复制件没有制作人关于制作过程和原物、原件存放地点的说明，或者说明中无签名的；  
（四）有其他瑕疵的。  
物证、书证的来源、收集程序有疑问，不能作出合理解释的，不得作为定案的根据。

**Article 86** Physical or documentary evidence extracted or seized during inspection, examination, or search, if not accompanied by records or lists and the source of the physical or documentary evidence cannot be proved, shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case.  
Where the collection procedures or methods of physical or documentary evidence have the following defects, the evidence may be admitted after correction or a reasonable explanation:  
(1) the inspection, examination, search, or extraction records or seizure lists are not signed by the investigators, the holders of the items, or witnesses, or the names, characteristics, quantities, quality, etc., of the items are not clearly stated;  
(2) photographs, videos, or reproductions of physical evidence, or copies or duplicates of documentary evidence, do not state that they are verified to be identical to the originals, do not state the reproduction time, or are not signed by the persons from whom they were collected or obtained;  
(3) photographs, videos, or reproductions of physical evidence, or copies or duplicates of documentary evidence, do not have the producers' explanations of the production process and the storage location of the original object or document, or the explanations do not contain signatures;  
(4) there are other defects.  
Where there is doubt about the source or collection procedures of physical or documentary evidence and a reasonable explanation cannot be given, it shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case.

#### 第三节 证人证言、被害人陈述的审查与认定

<a id="art-87"></a>第八十七条 对证人证言应当着重审查以下内容：  
（一）证言的内容是否为证人直接感知；  
（二）证人作证时的年龄，认知、记忆和表达能力，生理和精神状态是否影响作证；  
（三）证人与案件当事人、案件处理结果有无利害关系；  
（四）询问证人是否个别进行；  
（五）询问笔录的制作、修改是否符合法律、有关规定，是否注明询问的起止时间和地点，首次询问时是否告知证人有关权利义务和法律责任，证人对询问笔录是否核对确认；  
（六）询问未成年证人时，是否通知其法定代理人或者[刑事诉讼法第二百八十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-281)第一款规定的合适成年人到场，有关人员是否到场；  
（七）有无以暴力、威胁等非法方法收集证人证言的情形；  
（八）证言之间以及与其他证据之间能否相互印证，有无矛盾；存在矛盾的，能否得到合理解释。

**Article 87** With respect to witness testimony, the following contents shall be examined with emphasis:  
(1) whether the content of the testimony was directly perceived by the witness;  
(2) whether the witness's age at the time of testifying, cognitive, memory, and expressive abilities, and physiological and mental state affect testifying;  
(3) whether the witness has an interest in the parties or the outcome of the case;  
(4) whether witnesses were questioned individually;  
(5) whether the making and revision of the interrogation record comply with laws and relevant regulations, whether the starting and ending times and place of the interrogation are stated, whether the witness was informed of relevant rights, obligations, and legal liabilities at the first interrogation, and whether the witness checked and confirmed the interrogation record;  
(6) when questioning a minor witness, whether his legal representative or the appropriate adult as provided in Article 281, paragraph 1, of the Criminal Procedure Law was notified to be present, and whether the relevant person was present;  
(7) whether there are circumstances of collecting witness testimony by illegal methods such as violence or threats;  
(8) whether the testimony can corroborate each other and with other evidence, and whether there are contradictions; if there are contradictions, whether a reasonable explanation can be given.

<a id="art-88"></a>第八十八条 处于明显醉酒、中毒或者麻醉等状态，不能正常感知或者正确表达的证人所提供的证言，不得作为证据使用。  
证人的猜测性、评论性、推断性的证言，不得作为证据使用，但根据一般生活经验判断符合事实的除外。

**Article 88** Testimony provided by a witness who, being in a state of obvious drunkenness, poisoning, or anesthesia, etc., cannot perceive normally or express correctly shall not be used as evidence.  
Speculative, evaluative, or inferential testimony by a witness shall not be used as evidence, except where it is determined to be consistent with the facts based on general life experience.

<a id="art-89"></a>第八十九条 证人证言具有下列情形之一的，不得作为定案的根据：  
（一）询问证人没有个别进行的；  
（二）书面证言没有经证人核对确认的；  
（三）询问聋、哑人，应当提供通晓聋、哑手势的人员而未提供的；  
（四）询问不通晓当地通用语言、文字的证人，应当提供翻译人员而未提供的。

**Article 89** Witness testimony under any of the following circumstances shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case:  
(1) witnesses were not questioned individually;  
(2) written testimony was not checked and confirmed by the witness;  
(3) when questioning a deaf or mute person, a person who knows sign language should have been provided but was not;  
(4) when questioning a witness who does not understand the local commonly used language or script, an interpreter should have been provided but was not.

<a id="art-90"></a>第九十条 证人证言的收集程序、方式有下列瑕疵，经补正或者作出合理解释的，可以采用；不能补正或者作出合理解释的，不得作为定案的根据：  
（一）询问笔录没有填写询问人、记录人、法定代理人姓名以及询问的起止时间、地点的；  
（二）询问地点不符合规定的；  
（三）询问笔录没有记录告知证人有关权利义务和法律责任的；  
（四）询问笔录反映出在同一时段，同一询问人员询问不同证人的；  
（五）询问未成年人，其法定代理人或者合适成年人不在场的。

**Article 90** Where the collection procedures or methods of witness testimony have the following defects, the testimony may be admitted after correction or a reasonable explanation; if it cannot be corrected or reasonably explained, it shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case:  
(1) the interrogation record does not state the names of the interrogator, the recorder, and the legal representative, or the starting and ending times and place of the interrogation;  
(2) the place of interrogation does not comply with regulations;  
(3) the interrogation record does not record that the witness was informed of relevant rights, obligations, and legal liabilities;  
(4) the interrogation record reflects that the same interrogator questioned different witnesses during the same time period;  
(5) when questioning a minor, his legal representative or an appropriate adult was not present.

<a id="art-91"></a>第九十一条 证人当庭作出的证言，经控辩双方质证、法庭查证属实的，应当作为定案的根据。  
证人当庭作出的证言与其庭前证言矛盾，证人能够作出合理解释，并有其他证据印证的，应当采信其庭审证言；不能作出合理解释，而其庭前证言有其他证据印证的，可以采信其庭前证言。  
经人民法院通知，证人没有正当理由拒绝出庭或者出庭后拒绝作证，法庭对其证言的真实性无法确认的，该证人证言不得作为定案的根据。

**Article 91** : Testimony given by a witness in court, after being cross-examined by both the prosecution and the defense and verified by the court to be true, shall be used as the basis for deciding the case.  
If the testimony given by a witness in court contradicts the witness's pre-trial testimony, and the witness can provide a reasonable explanation and is corroborated by other evidence, the in-court testimony shall be admitted; if the witness cannot provide a reasonable explanation, but the pre-trial testimony is corroborated by other evidence, the pre-trial testimony may be admitted.  
If, after being notified by the People's Court, a witness refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons or refuses to testify after appearing in court, and the court cannot confirm the authenticity of the testimony, such witness testimony shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case.

<a id="art-92"></a>第九十二条 对被害人陈述的审查与认定，参照适用本节的有关规定。

**Article 92** : The review and determination of victims' statements shall be conducted by reference to the relevant provisions of this Section.

#### 第四节 被告人供述和辩解的审查与认定

<a id="art-93"></a>第九十三条 对被告人供述和辩解应当着重审查以下内容：  
（一）讯问的时间、地点，讯问人的身份、人数以及讯问方式等是否符合法律、有关规定；  
（二）讯问笔录的制作、修改是否符合法律、有关规定，是否注明讯问的具体起止时间和地点，首次讯问时是否告知被告人有关权利和法律规定，被告人是否核对确认；  
（三）讯问未成年被告人时，是否通知其法定代理人或者合适成年人到场，有关人员是否到场；  
（四）讯问女性未成年被告人时，是否有女性工作人员在场；  
（五）有无以刑讯逼供等非法方法收集被告人供述的情形；  
（六）被告人的供述是否前后一致，有无反复以及出现反复的原因；  
（七）被告人的供述和辩解是否全部随案移送；  
（八）被告人的辩解内容是否符合案情和常理，有无矛盾；  
（九）被告人的供述和辩解与同案被告人的供述和辩解以及其他证据能否相互印证，有无矛盾；存在矛盾的，能否得到合理解释。  
必要时，可以结合现场执法音视频记录、讯问录音录像、被告人进出看守所的健康检查记录、笔录等，对被告人的供述和辩解进行审查。

**Article 93** : For a defendant's confession and defense, the following contents shall be reviewed with emphasis:  
(1) Whether the time, place, identity and number of interrogators, and the methods of interrogation, etc. conform to the law and relevant provisions;  
(2) Whether the production and revision of the interrogation record conform to the law and relevant provisions, whether the specific start and end times and location of the interrogation are indicated, whether the defendant was informed of relevant rights and legal provisions at the first interrogation, and whether the defendant checked and confirmed the record;  
(3) When interrogating a minor defendant, whether the legal representative or an appropriate adult was notified to be present, and whether the relevant persons were present;  
(4) When interrogating a female minor defendant, whether female staff members were present;  
(5) Whether there are circumstances of collecting the defendant's confession by illegal methods such as extorting confession by torture;  
(6) Whether the defendant's confessions are consistent, whether there are repetitions, and the reasons for such repetitions;  
(7) Whether all of the defendant's confessions and defenses are transferred with the case;  
(8) Whether the content of the defendant's defense conforms to the facts of the case and common sense, and whether there are contradictions;  
(9) Whether the defendant's confessions and defenses can corroborate with the confessions and defenses of co-defendants and other evidence, and whether there are contradictions; if there are contradictions, whether they can be reasonably explained.  
When necessary, the defendant's confessions and defenses may be reviewed in light of on-site law enforcement audio-video records, interrogation audio-video recordings, health examination records and records of the defendant entering and leaving the detention center, etc.

<a id="art-94"></a>第九十四条 被告人供述具有下列情形之一的，不得作为定案的根据：  
（一）讯问笔录没有经被告人核对确认的；  
（二）讯问聋、哑人，应当提供通晓聋、哑手势的人员而未提供的；  
（三）讯问不通晓当地通用语言、文字的被告人，应当提供翻译人员而未提供的；  
（四）讯问未成年人，其法定代理人或者合适成年人不在场的。

**Article 94** : Where a defendant's confession falls under any of the following circumstances, it shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case:  
(1) The interrogation record has not been checked and confirmed by the defendant;  
(2) When interrogating a deaf or mute person, a person proficient in sign language was required to be provided but was not provided;  
(3) When interrogating a defendant who does not understand the local commonly used language or script, an interpreter was required to be provided but was not provided;  
(4) When interrogating a minor, the legal representative or an appropriate adult was not present.

<a id="art-95"></a>第九十五条 讯问笔录有下列瑕疵，经补正或者作出合理解释的，可以采用；不能补正或者作出合理解释的，不得作为定案的根据：  
（一）讯问笔录填写的讯问时间、讯问地点、讯问人、记录人、法定代理人等有误或者存在矛盾的；  
（二）讯问人没有签名的；  
（三）首次讯问笔录没有记录告知被讯问人有关权利和法律规定的。

**Article 95** : Where an interrogation record has the following defects, it may be adopted if corrections are made or a reasonable explanation is provided; if corrections cannot be made or a reasonable explanation cannot be provided, it shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case:  
(1) The interrogation time, interrogation place, interrogator, recorder, legal representative, etc. filled in the interrogation record are erroneous or contradictory;  
(2) The interrogator has not signed;  
(3) The first interrogation record does not record that the interrogated person was informed of relevant rights and legal provisions.

<a id="art-96"></a>第九十六条 审查被告人供述和辩解，应当结合控辩双方提供的所有证据以及被告人的全部供述和辩解进行。  
被告人庭审中翻供，但不能合理说明翻供原因或者其辩解与全案证据矛盾，而其庭前供述与其他证据相互印证的，可以采信其庭前供述。  
被告人庭前供述和辩解存在反复，但庭审中供认，且与其他证据相互印证的，可以采信其庭审供述；被告人庭前供述和辩解存在反复，庭审中不供认，且无其他证据与庭前供述印证的，不得采信其庭前供述。

**Article 96** : The review of a defendant's confessions and defenses shall be conducted in light of all evidence provided by both the prosecution and the defense, as well as all of the defendant's confessions and defenses.  
If a defendant retracts his confession during the court session but cannot reasonably explain the reason for the retraction, or his defense contradicts the evidence in the entire case, and his pre-trial confession is corroborated by other evidence, the pre-trial confession may be admitted.  
If the defendant's pre-trial confessions and defenses are inconsistent, but he confesses during the court session and such confession is corroborated by other evidence, the in-court confession may be admitted; if the defendant's pre-trial confessions and defenses are inconsistent, he does not confess during the court session, and there is no other evidence corroborating the pre-trial confession, the pre-trial confession shall not be admitted.

#### 第五节 鉴定意见的审查与认定

<a id="art-97"></a>第九十七条 对鉴定意见应当着重审查以下内容：  
（一）鉴定机构和鉴定人是否具有法定资质；  
（二）鉴定人是否存在应当回避的情形；  
（三）检材的来源、取得、保管、送检是否符合法律、有关规定，与相关提取笔录、扣押清单等记载的内容是否相符，检材是否可靠；  
（四）鉴定意见的形式要件是否完备，是否注明提起鉴定的事由、鉴定委托人、鉴定机构、鉴定要求、鉴定过程、鉴定方法、鉴定日期等相关内容，是否由鉴定机构盖章并由鉴定人签名；  
（五）鉴定程序是否符合法律、有关规定；  
（六）鉴定的过程和方法是否符合相关专业的规范要求；  
（七）鉴定意见是否明确；  
（八）鉴定意见与案件事实有无关联；  
（九）鉴定意见与勘验、检查笔录及相关照片等其他证据是否矛盾；存在矛盾的，能否得到合理解释；  
（十）鉴定意见是否依法及时告知相关人员，当事人对鉴定意见有无异议。

**Article 97** : The following contents regarding an expert opinion shall be reviewed with emphasis:  
(1) Whether the expert institution and the expert have legal qualifications;  
(2) Whether the expert is under circumstances requiring recusal;  
(3) Whether the source, acquisition, storage, and submission for examination of the sample materials conform to the law and relevant provisions, whether they are consistent with the contents recorded in relevant extraction records and seizure lists, and whether the sample materials are reliable;  
(4) Whether the formal requirements of the expert opinion are complete, whether the reason for initiating the expert evaluation, the commissioning party, the expert institution, the requirements, process, method, date, and other relevant contents of the expert evaluation are indicated, and whether the expert institution affixed its seal and the expert signed;  
(5) Whether the expert evaluation procedure conforms to the law and relevant provisions;  
(6) Whether the process and method of the expert evaluation conform to the specification requirements of relevant professions;  
(7) Whether the expert opinion is clear;  
(8) Whether the expert opinion is relevant to the facts of the case;  
(9) Whether the expert opinion contradicts other evidence such as inspection and examination records and related photographs; if there are contradictions, whether they can be reasonably explained;  
(10) Whether the expert opinion was timely informed to relevant persons according to law, and whether the parties have objections to the expert opinion.

<a id="art-98"></a>第九十八条 鉴定意见具有下列情形之一的，不得作为定案的根据：  
（一）鉴定机构不具备法定资质，或者鉴定事项超出该鉴定机构业务范围、技术条件的；  
（二）鉴定人不具备法定资质，不具有相关专业技术或者职称，或者违反回避规定的；  
（三）送检材料、样本来源不明，或者因污染不具备鉴定条件的；  
（四）鉴定对象与送检材料、样本不一致的；  
（五）鉴定程序违反规定的；  
（六）鉴定过程和方法不符合相关专业的规范要求的；  
（七）鉴定文书缺少签名、盖章的；  
（八）鉴定意见与案件事实没有关联的；  
（九）违反有关规定的其他情形。

**Article 98** : Where an expert opinion falls under any of the following circumstances, it shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case:  
(1) The expert institution does not have legal qualifications, or the matter of expert evaluation exceeds the business scope or technical conditions of the expert institution;  
(2) The expert does not have legal qualifications, does not have relevant professional technology or professional title, or violates recusal provisions;  
(3) The source of the submitted materials or samples is unknown, or due to contamination, the conditions for expert evaluation are not met;  
(4) The object of expert evaluation is inconsistent with the submitted materials or samples;  
(5) The expert evaluation procedure violates provisions;  
(6) The process and method of the expert evaluation do not conform to the specification requirements of relevant professions;  
(7) The expert evaluation document lacks a signature or seal;  
(8) The expert opinion is not relevant to the facts of the case;  
(9) Other circumstances that violate relevant provisions.

<a id="art-99"></a>第九十九条 经人民法院通知，鉴定人拒不出庭作证的，鉴定意见不得作为定案的根据。  
鉴定人由于不能抗拒的原因或者有其他正当理由无法出庭的，人民法院可以根据情况决定延期审理或者重新鉴定。  
鉴定人无正当理由拒不出庭作证的，人民法院应当通报司法行政机关或者有关部门。

**Article 99** : Where an expert, after being notified by the People's Court, refuses to appear in court to testify, the expert opinion shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case.  
Where an expert is unable to appear in court due to irresistible causes or has other justifiable reasons for being unable to appear, the People's Court may decide to adjourn the trial or conduct a new expert evaluation according to the circumstances.  
Where an expert refuses to appear in court to testify without justifiable reasons, the People's Court shall notify the judicial administrative organ or relevant department.

<a id="art-100"></a>第一百条 因无鉴定机构，或者根据法律、司法解释的规定，指派、聘请有专门知识的人就案件的专门性问题出具的报告，可以作为证据使用。  
对前款规定的报告的审查与认定，参照适用本节的有关规定。  
经人民法院通知，出具报告的人拒不出庭作证的，有关报告不得作为定案的根据。
