**Article 100** : Where there is no expert institution, or where, in accordance with the provisions of laws or judicial interpretations, a person with specialized knowledge is designated or retained to issue a report on the specialized issues of a case, such report may be used as evidence.  
The review and determination of the report specified in the preceding paragraph shall be conducted by reference to the relevant provisions of this Section.  
Where a person who issued the report, after being notified by the People's Court, refuses to appear in court to testify, the relevant report shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case.

<a id="art-101"></a>第一百零一条 有关部门对事故进行调查形成的报告，在刑事诉讼中可以作为证据使用；报告中涉及专门性问题的意见，经法庭查证属实，且调查程序符合法律、有关规定的，可以作为定案的根据。

**Article 101** : Reports formed by relevant departments in investigating an accident may be used as evidence in criminal proceedings; opinions in the report concerning specialized issues, after being verified to be true by the court and where the investigation procedure conforms to the law and relevant provisions, may be used as the basis for deciding the case.

#### 第六节 勘验、检查、辨认、侦查实验等笔录的审查与认定

<a id="art-102"></a>第一百零二条 对勘验、检查笔录应当着重审查以下内容：  
（一）勘验、检查是否依法进行，笔录制作是否符合法律、有关规定，勘验、检查人员和见证人是否签名或者盖章；  
（二）勘验、检查笔录是否记录了提起勘验、检查的事由，勘验、检查的时间、地点，在场人员、现场方位、周围环境等，现场的物品、人身、尸体等的位置、特征等情况，以及勘验、检查的过程；文字记录与实物或者绘图、照片、录像是否相符；现场、物品、痕迹等是否伪造、有无破坏；人身特征、伤害情况、生理状态有无伪装或者变化等；  
（三）补充进行勘验、检查的，是否说明了再次勘验、检查的原由，前后勘验、检查的情况是否矛盾。

**Article 102** : The inspection and examination records shall be reviewed with emphasis on the following contents:  
(1) Whether the inspection and examination were conducted according to law, whether the production of the records conforms to the law and relevant provisions, and whether the persons conducting the inspection and examination and the witnesses present signed or affixed their seals;  
(2) Whether the inspection and examination records recorded the reasons for initiating the inspection and examination, the time and place of the inspection and examination, the persons present, the orientation of the scene, the surrounding environment, etc., and the positions and characteristics of objects, persons, corpses, etc. at the scene, as well as the process of the inspection and examination; whether the written records are consistent with the physical objects or drawings, photographs, and videos; whether the scene, objects, traces, etc. are forged or damaged; whether personal characteristics, injuries, and physiological states are disguised or changed, etc.;  
(3) If supplementary inspection and examination are conducted, whether the reasons for the re-inspection and re-examination are stated, and whether the circumstances before and after the inspections and examinations are contradictory.

<a id="art-103"></a>第一百零三条 勘验、检查笔录存在明显不符合法律、有关规定的情形，不能作出合理解释的，不得作为定案的根据。

**Article 103** : Where inspection and examination records contain obvious non-conformity with the law and relevant provisions and cannot be reasonably explained, they shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case.

<a id="art-104"></a>第一百零四条 对辨认笔录应当着重审查辨认的过程、方法，以及辨认笔录的制作是否符合有关规定。

**Article 104** : With respect to identification records, the process and methods of the identification, and whether the production of the identification records conform to relevant provisions shall be reviewed with emphasis.

<a id="art-105"></a>第一百零五条 辨认笔录具有下列情形之一的，不得作为定案的根据：  
（一）辨认不是在调查人员、侦查人员主持下进行的；  
（二）辨认前使辨认人见到辨认对象的；  
（三）辨认活动没有个别进行的；  
（四）辨认对象没有混杂在具有类似特征的其他对象中，或者供辨认的对象数量不符合规定的；  
（五）辨认中给辨认人明显暗示或者明显有指认嫌疑的；  
（六）违反有关规定，不能确定辨认笔录真实性的其他情形。

**Article 105** : Where an identification record falls under any of the following circumstances, it shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case:  
(1) The identification was not conducted under the auspices of investigation personnel or investigators;  
(2) The identifying person saw the identification target before the identification;  
(3) The identification activities were not conducted separately;  
(4) The identification target was not mixed with other objects with similar characteristics, or the number of objects provided for identification does not conform to the provisions;  
(5) During the identification, the identifying person was given obvious suggestions or there was obvious suspicion of indicating the target;  
(6) Other circumstances where relevant provisions are violated and the authenticity of the identification record cannot be confirmed.

<a id="art-106"></a>第一百零六条 对侦查实验笔录应当着重审查实验的过程、方法，以及笔录的制作是否符合有关规定。

**Article 106** : The record of investigation experiment shall be reviewed with emphasis on the process and methods of the experiment, and whether the production of the record conforms to relevant provisions.

<a id="art-107"></a>第一百零七条 侦查实验的条件与事件发生时的条件有明显差异，或者存在影响实验结论科学性的其他情形的，侦查实验笔录不得作为定案的根据。

**Article 107** : Where the conditions of the investigation experiment are significantly different from the conditions at the time of the incident, or there are other circumstances affecting the scientific nature of the experimental conclusion, the record of the investigation experiment shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case.

#### 第七节 视听资料、电子数据的审查与认定

<a id="art-108"></a>第一百零八条 对视听资料应当着重审查以下内容：  
（一）是否附有提取过程的说明，来源是否合法；  
（二）是否为原件，有无复制及复制份数；是复制件的，是否附有无法调取原件的原因、复制件制作过程和原件存放地点的说明，制作人、原视听资料持有人是否签名；  
（三）制作过程中是否存在威胁、引诱当事人等违反法律、有关规定的情形；  
（四）是否写明制作人、持有人的身份，制作的时间、地点、条件和方法；  
（五）内容和制作过程是否真实，有无剪辑、增加、删改等情形；  
（六）内容与案件事实有无关联。  
对视听资料有疑问的，应当进行鉴定。

**Article 108** : With respect to audio-visual materials, the following contents shall be reviewed with emphasis:  
(1) Whether a description of the extraction process is attached, and whether the source is lawful;  
(2) Whether they are originals, whether there are copies and the number of copies; if they are copies, whether a description of the reasons why the originals could not be obtained, the production process of the copies, and the location where the originals are stored is attached, and whether the producer and the original holder of the audio-visual materials signed;  
(3) Whether there are circumstances during the production process that violate the law and relevant provisions, such as threatening or inducing the parties;  
(4) Whether the identities of the producer and holder, and the time, place, conditions, and methods of production are clearly stated;  
(5) Whether the content and production process are authentic, and whether there are circumstances such as editing, addition, or alteration;  
(6) Whether the content is relevant to the facts of the case.  
Where there is doubt about audio-visual materials, an expert evaluation shall be conducted.

<a id="art-109"></a>第一百零九条 视听资料具有下列情形之一的，不得作为定案的根据：  
（一）系篡改、伪造或者无法确定真伪的；  
（二）制作、取得的时间、地点、方式等有疑问，不能作出合理解释的。

**Article 109** : Where audio-visual materials fall under any of the following circumstances, they shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case:  
(1) They have been tampered with, forged, or their authenticity cannot be determined;  
(2) There are doubts about the time, place, method, etc. of their production or acquisition, and a reasonable explanation cannot be provided.

<a id="art-110"></a>第一百一十条 对电子数据是否真实，应当着重审查以下内容：  
（一）是否移送原始存储介质；在原始存储介质无法封存、不便移动时，有无说明原因，并注明收集、提取过程及原始存储介质的存放地点或者电子数据的来源等情况；  
（二）是否具有数字签名、数字证书等特殊标识；  
（三）收集、提取的过程是否可以重现；  
（四）如有增加、删除、修改等情形的，是否附有说明；  
（五）完整性是否可以保证。

**Article 110** : With respect to whether electronic data is authentic, the following contents shall be reviewed with emphasis:  
(1) Whether the original storage medium is transferred; when the original storage medium cannot be sealed or is inconvenient to move, whether the reasons are stated, and whether the collection and extraction process, the storage location of the original storage medium, or the source of the electronic data, etc. are indicated;  
(2) Whether it has special identifiers such as digital signatures or digital certificates;  
(3) Whether the collection and extraction process can be reproduced;  
(4) If there are circumstances such as addition, deletion, or modification, whether a description is attached;  
(5) Whether the integrity can be guaranteed.

<a id="art-111"></a>第一百一十一条 对电子数据是否完整，应当根据保护电子数据完整性的相应方法进行审查、验证：  
（一）审查原始存储介质的扣押、封存状态；  
（二）审查电子数据的收集、提取过程，查看录像；  
（三）比对电子数据完整性校验值；  
（四）与备份的电子数据进行比较；  
（五）审查冻结后的访问操作日志；  
（六）其他方法。

**Article 111** : With respect to whether electronic data is complete, review and verification shall be conducted according to the corresponding methods for protecting the integrity of electronic data:  
(1) Reviewing the seizure and sealing status of the original storage medium;  
(2) Reviewing the collection and extraction process of the electronic data and viewing the video recordings;  
(3) Comparing the integrity check values of the electronic data;  
(4) Comparing it with the backup electronic data;  
(5) Reviewing the access operation logs after freezing;  
(6) Other methods.

<a id="art-112"></a>第一百一十二条 对收集、提取电子数据是否合法，应当着重审查以下内容：  
（一）收集、提取电子数据是否由二名以上调查人员、侦查人员进行，取证方法是否符合相关技术标准；  
（二）收集、提取电子数据，是否附有笔录、清单，并经调查人员、侦查人员、电子数据持有人、提供人、见证人签名或者盖章；没有签名或者盖章的，是否注明原因；对电子数据的类别、文件格式等是否注明清楚；  
（三）是否依照有关规定由符合条件的人员担任见证人，是否对相关活动进行录像；  
（四）采用技术调查、侦查措施收集、提取电子数据的，是否依法经过严格的批准手续；  
（五）进行电子数据检查的，检查程序是否符合有关规定。

**Article 112** : With respect to whether the collection and extraction of electronic data is lawful, the following contents shall be reviewed with emphasis:  
(1) Whether the collection and extraction of electronic data is conducted by no fewer than two investigation personnel or investigators, and whether the evidence collection methods conform to relevant technical standards;  
(2) Whether the collection and extraction of electronic data is accompanied by records and lists, and signed or sealed by the investigation personnel, investigators, the holder of the electronic data, the provider, and the witnesses present; if there is no signature or seal, whether the reasons are indicated; whether the category, file format, etc. of the electronic data are clearly indicated;  
(3) Whether qualified persons serve as witnesses present in accordance with relevant provisions, and whether the relevant activities are videotaped;  
(4) Where electronic data is collected and extracted by technical investigation or investigative measures, whether strict approval procedures have been followed according to law;  
(5) Where an examination of electronic data is conducted, whether the examination procedure conforms to relevant provisions.

<a id="art-113"></a>第一百一十三条 电子数据的收集、提取程序有下列瑕疵，经补正或者作出合理解释的，可以采用；不能补正或者作出合理解释的，不得作为定案的根据：  
（一）未以封存状态移送的；  
（二）笔录或者清单上没有调查人员或者侦查人员、电子数据持有人、提供人、见证人签名或者盖章的；  
（三）对电子数据的名称、类别、格式等注明不清的；  
（四）有其他瑕疵的。

**Article 113** : Where the collection and extraction procedure for electronic data has the following defects, it may be adopted if corrections are made or a reasonable explanation is provided; if corrections cannot be made or a reasonable explanation cannot be provided, it shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case:  
(1) The electronic data is not transferred in a sealed state;  
(2) The records or lists are not signed or sealed by the investigation personnel or investigators, the holder of the electronic data, the provider, or the witnesses present;  
(3) The name, category, format, etc. of the electronic data are not clearly indicated;  
(4) There are other defects.

<a id="art-114"></a>第一百一十四条 电子数据具有下列情形之一的，不得作为定案的根据：  
（一）系篡改、伪造或者无法确定真伪的；  
（二）有增加、删除、修改等情形，影响电子数据真实性的；  
（三）其他无法保证电子数据真实性的情形。

**Article 114** : Where electronic data falls under any of the following circumstances, it shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case:  
(1) It has been tampered with, forged, or its authenticity cannot be determined;  
(2) There are circumstances such as addition, deletion, or modification that affect the authenticity of the electronic data;  
(3) Other circumstances where the authenticity of the electronic data cannot be guaranteed.

<a id="art-115"></a>第一百一十五条 对视听资料、电子数据，还应当审查是否移送文字抄清材料以及对绰号、暗语、俗语、方言等不易理解内容的说明。未移送的，必要时，可以要求人民检察院移送。

**Article 115** : With respect to audio-visual materials and electronic data, it shall also be reviewed whether transcribed text materials and explanations of content that is not easily understood, such as nicknames, coded language, colloquial expressions, and dialects, are transferred. If they are not transferred, the People's Procuratorate may be requested to transfer them when necessary.

#### 第八节 技术调查、侦查证据的审查与认定

<a id="art-116"></a>第一百一十六条 依法采取技术调查、侦查措施收集的材料在刑事诉讼中可以作为证据使用。  
采取技术调查、侦查措施收集的材料，作为证据使用的，应当随案移送。

**Article 116** : Materials collected by law through technical investigation or investigative measures may be used as evidence in criminal proceedings.  
Where materials collected through technical investigation or investigative measures are used as evidence, they shall be transferred with the case.

<a id="art-117"></a>第一百一十七条 使用采取技术调查、侦查措施收集的证据材料可能危及有关人员的人身安全，或者可能产生其他严重后果的，可以采取下列保护措施：  
（一）使用化名等代替调查、侦查人员及有关人员的个人信息；  
（二）不具体写明技术调查、侦查措施使用的技术设备和技术方法；  
（三）其他必要的保护措施。

**Article 117** : Where the use of evidence materials collected through technical investigation or investigative measures may endanger the personal safety of relevant persons, or may produce other serious consequences, the following protective measures may be adopted:  
(1) Using pseudonyms and other means to replace the personal information of investigation personnel, investigators, and relevant persons;  
(2) Not specifically stating the technical equipment and technical methods used in the technical investigation or investigative measures;  
(3) Other necessary protective measures.

<a id="art-118"></a>第一百一十八条 移送技术调查、侦查证据材料的，应当附采取技术调查、侦查措施的法律文书、技术调查、侦查证据材料清单和有关说明材料。  
移送采用技术调查、侦查措施收集的视听资料、电子数据的，应当制作新的存储介质，并附制作说明，写明原始证据材料、原始存储介质的存放地点等信息，由制作人签名，并加盖单位印章。

**Article 118** : Where technical investigation or investigative evidence materials are transferred, the legal documents for taking technical investigation or investigative measures, a list of the technical investigation or investigative evidence materials, and relevant explanatory materials shall be attached.  
Where audio-visual materials or electronic data collected through technical investigation or investigative measures are transferred, a new storage medium shall be made, with a description of production attached, stating the location where the original evidence materials and the original storage medium are stored, and other information, signed by the producer and affixed with the seal of the unit.

<a id="art-119"></a>第一百一十九条 对采取技术调查、侦查措施收集的证据材料，除根据相关证据材料所属的证据种类，依照本章第二节至第七节的相应规定进行审查外，还应当着重审查以下内容：  
（一）技术调查、侦查措施所针对的案件是否符合法律规定；  
（二）技术调查措施是否经过严格的批准手续，按照规定交有关机关执行；技术侦查措施是否在刑事立案后，经过严格的批准手续；  
（三）采取技术调查、侦查措施的种类、适用对象和期限是否按照批准决定载明的内容执行；  
（四）采取技术调查、侦查措施收集的证据材料与其他证据是否矛盾；存在矛盾的，能否得到合理解释。

**Article 119** : With respect to evidence materials collected through technical investigation or investigative measures, in addition to reviewing them according to the corresponding provisions in Sections 2 to 7 of this Chapter based on the type of evidence to which the relevant evidence materials belong, the following contents shall also be reviewed with emphasis:  
(1) Whether the case targeted by the technical investigation or investigative measures conforms to the provisions of the law;  
(2) Whether the technical investigation measures have undergone strict approval procedures and were submitted to the relevant organ for execution according to the provisions; whether the technical investigative measures have undergone strict approval procedures after the criminal case was filed;  
(3) Whether the types, applicable objects, and time limits of the technical investigation or investigative measures taken are executed in accordance with the contents stated in the approval decision;  
(4) Whether the evidence materials collected through technical investigation or investigative measures contradict other evidence; if there are contradictions, whether they can be reasonably explained.

<a id="art-120"></a>第一百二十条 采取技术调查、侦查措施收集的证据材料，应当经过当庭出示、辨认、质证等法庭调查程序查证。  
当庭调查技术调查、侦查证据材料可能危及有关人员的人身安全，或者可能产生其他严重后果的，法庭应当采取不暴露有关人员身份和技术调查、侦查措施使用的技术设备、技术方法等保护措施。必要时，审判人员可以在庭外对证据进行核实。

**Article 120** : Evidence materials collected through technical investigation or investigative measures shall be examined through court investigation procedures such as presentation in court, identification, and cross-examination.  
Where in-court examination of technical investigation or investigative evidence materials may endanger the personal safety of relevant persons, or may produce other serious consequences, the court shall take protective measures such as not disclosing the identities of relevant persons and the technical equipment and technical methods used in the technical investigation or investigative measures. When necessary, judicial personnel may verify the evidence outside the courtroom.

<a id="art-121"></a>第一百二十一条 采用技术调查、侦查证据作为定案根据的，人民法院在裁判文书中可以表述相关证据的名称、证据种类和证明对象，但不得表述有关人员身份和技术调查、侦查措施使用的技术设备、技术方法等。

**Article 121** : Where technical investigation or investigative evidence is used as the basis for deciding the case, the People's Court may state in the judgment document the name, type of evidence, and object of proof of the relevant evidence, but shall not state the identities of relevant persons or the technical equipment and technical methods used in the technical investigation or investigative measures.

<a id="art-122"></a>第一百二十二条 人民法院认为应当移送的技术调查、侦查证据材料未随案移送的，应当通知人民检察院在指定时间内移送。人民检察院未移送的，人民法院应当根据在案证据对案件事实作出认定。

**Article 122** : Where the People's Court determines that technical investigation or investigative evidence materials required to be transferred have not been transferred with the case, it shall notify the People's Procuratorate to transfer them within a specified time. If the People's Procuratorate fails to transfer them, the People's Court shall make a determination of the facts of the case based on the evidence on file.

#### 第九节 非法证据排除

<a id="art-123"></a>第一百二十三条 采用下列非法方法收集的被告人供述，应当予以排除：  
（一）采用殴打、违法使用戒具等暴力方法或者变相肉刑的恶劣手段，使被告人遭受难以忍受的痛苦而违背意愿作出的供述；  
（二）采用以暴力或者严重损害本人及其近亲属合法权益等相威胁的方法，使被告人遭受难以忍受的痛苦而违背意愿作出的供述；  
（三）采用非法拘禁等非法限制人身自由的方法收集的被告人供述。

**Article 123** : Confessions of a defendant collected by the following illegal methods shall be excluded:  
(1) Confessions made against the defendant's will by causing the defendant unbearable pain through violent methods such as beating or illegal use of restraints, or through despicable means such as disguised corporal punishment;  
(2) Confessions made against the defendant's will by causing the defendant unbearable pain through methods such as threatening with violence or seriously harming the lawful rights and interests of the defendant or his close relatives;  
(3) Confessions of a defendant collected by illegal restriction of personal freedom such as unlawful detention.

<a id="art-124"></a>第一百二十四条 采用刑讯逼供方法使被告人作出供述，之后被告人受该刑讯逼供行为影响而作出的与该供述相同的重复性供述，应当一并排除，但下列情形除外：  
（一）调查、侦查期间，监察机关、侦查机关根据控告、举报或者自己发现等，确认或者不能排除以非法方法收集证据而更换调查、侦查人员，其他调查、侦查人员再次讯问时告知有关权利和认罪的法律后果，被告人自愿供述的；  
（二）审查逮捕、审查起诉和审判期间，检察人员、审判人员讯问时告知诉讼权利和认罪的法律后果，被告人自愿供述的。

**Article 124** : Where a defendant is made to make a confession by means of extorting confession by torture, and thereafter, under the influence of such torture, makes repeated confessions identical to that confession, such repeated confessions shall also be excluded, except under the following circumstances:  
(1) During the investigation period, the supervisory organ or the investigating organ, based on a complaint, report, or its own discovery, confirms or cannot exclude that evidence was collected by illegal methods and accordingly replaces the investigation personnel or investigators, and when other investigation personnel or investigators interrogate the defendant again, they inform him of the relevant rights and the legal consequences of pleading guilty, and the defendant voluntarily confesses;  
(2) During the period of examination for arrest, review for prosecution, and adjudication, when procuratorial personnel or judicial personnel interrogate the defendant, they inform him of litigation rights and the legal consequences of pleading guilty, and the defendant voluntarily confesses.

<a id="art-125"></a>第一百二十五条 采用暴力、威胁以及非法限制人身自由等非法方法收集的证人证言、被害人陈述，应当予以排除。

**Article 125** : Witness testimony and victim statements collected by illegal methods such as violence, threats, and illegal restriction of personal freedom shall be excluded.

<a id="art-126"></a>第一百二十六条 收集物证、书证不符合法定程序，可能严重影响司法公正的，应当予以补正或者作出合理解释；不能补正或者作出合理解释的，对该证据应当予以排除。  
认定“可能严重影响司法公正”，应当综合考虑收集证据违反法定程序以及所造成后果的严重程度等情况。

**Article 126** : Where physical evidence and documentary evidence are collected in a manner that does not conform to legal procedures and may seriously affect judicial fairness, corrections shall be made or a reasonable explanation shall be provided; if corrections cannot be made or a reasonable explanation cannot be provided, such evidence shall be excluded.  
In determining "may seriously affect judicial fairness", the circumstances such as the severity of the violation of legal procedures in collecting the evidence and the consequences caused shall be comprehensively considered.

<a id="art-127"></a>第一百二十七条 当事人及其辩护人、诉讼代理人申请人民法院排除以非法方法收集的证据的，应当提供涉嫌非法取证的人员、时间、地点、方式、内容等相关线索或者材料。

**Article 127** : Where a party and his defender or litigation agent apply to the People's Court to exclude evidence collected by illegal methods, they shall provide relevant clues or materials such as the personnel suspected of illegal evidence collection, the time, place, method, and content.

<a id="art-128"></a>第一百二十八条 人民法院向被告人及其辩护人送达起诉书副本时，应当告知其申请排除非法证据的，应当在开庭审理前提出，但庭审期间才发现相关线索或者材料的除外。

**Article 128** : When serving a copy of the indictment on the defendant and his defender, the People's Court shall inform them that any application to exclude illegal evidence shall be made before the court session begins, except where the relevant clues or materials are only discovered during the court session.

<a id="art-129"></a>第一百二十九条 开庭审理前，当事人及其辩护人、诉讼代理人申请人民法院排除非法证据的，人民法院应当在开庭前及时将申请书或者申请笔录及相关线索、材料的复制件送交人民检察院。

**Article 129** : Before the commencement of the court session, where a party and his defender or litigation agent apply to the People's Court to exclude illegal evidence, the People's Court shall promptly deliver a copy of the application or application record and the relevant clues and materials to the People's Procuratorate before the court session.

<a id="art-130"></a>第一百三十条 开庭审理前，人民法院可以召开庭前会议，就非法证据排除等问题了解情况，听取意见。  
在庭前会议中，人民检察院可以通过出示有关证据材料等方式，对证据收集的合法性加以说明。必要时，可以通知调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员参加庭前会议，说明情况。

**Article 130** : Before the commencement of the court session, the People's Court may convene a pre-trial conference to learn about the situation and hear opinions on issues such as the exclusion of illegal evidence.  
At the pre-trial conference, the People's Procuratorate may explain the lawfulness of evidence collection by presenting relevant evidence materials and other means. When necessary, investigation personnel, investigators, or other persons may be notified to participate in the pre-trial conference to explain the situation.

<a id="art-131"></a>第一百三十一条 在庭前会议中，人民检察院可以撤回有关证据。撤回的证据，没有新的理由，不得在庭审中出示。  
当事人及其辩护人、诉讼代理人可以撤回排除非法证据的申请。撤回申请后，没有新的线索或者材料，不得再次对有关证据提出排除申请。

**Article 131** : At the pre-trial conference, the People's Procuratorate may withdraw relevant evidence. Withdrawn evidence shall not be presented at a court hearing without new reasons.  
A party and his defender or litigation agent may withdraw the application for exclusion of illegal evidence. After the application is withdrawn, without new clues or materials, no application for exclusion of the relevant evidence shall be filed again.

<a id="art-132"></a>第一百三十二条 当事人及其辩护人、诉讼代理人在开庭审理前未申请排除非法证据，在庭审过程中提出申请的，应当说明理由。人民法院经审查，对证据收集的合法性有疑问的，应当进行调查；没有疑问的，驳回申请。  
驳回排除非法证据的申请后，当事人及其辩护人、诉讼代理人没有新的线索或者材料，以相同理由再次提出申请的，人民法院不再审查。

**Article 132** : Where a party and his defender or litigation agent do not apply to exclude illegal evidence before the hearing commences but file the application during the court hearing, they shall state the reasons. After review, if the People's Court has doubts about the lawfulness of evidence collection, it shall conduct an investigation; if there are no doubts, it shall reject the application.  
After the application for exclusion of illegal evidence is rejected, if the party and his defender or litigation agent, without new clues or materials, file the application again on the same grounds, the People's Court shall not review it again.

<a id="art-133"></a>第一百三十三条 控辩双方在庭前会议中对证据收集是否合法未达成一致意见，人民法院对证据收集的合法性有疑问的，应当在庭审中进行调查；对证据收集的合法性没有疑问，且无新的线索或者材料表明可能存在非法取证的，可以决定不再进行调查并说明理由。

**Article 133** : Where the prosecution and the defense fail to reach an agreement at the pre-trial conference on whether evidence collection is lawful, and the People's Court has doubts about the lawfulness of evidence collection, it shall conduct an investigation during the court hearing; where there are no doubts about the lawfulness of evidence collection and no new clues or materials indicate that illegal evidence collection may exist, the People's Court may decide not to conduct an investigation and shall explain the reasons.

<a id="art-134"></a>第一百三十四条 庭审期间，法庭决定对证据收集的合法性进行调查的，应当先行当庭调查。但为防止庭审过分迟延，也可以在法庭调查结束前调查。

**Article 134** : During the court hearing, where the court decides to investigate the lawfulness of evidence collection, it shall first conduct the investigation during the court session. However, to prevent excessive delay of the hearing, the investigation may also be conducted before the conclusion of the court investigation.

<a id="art-135"></a>第一百三十五条 法庭决定对证据收集的合法性进行调查的，由公诉人通过宣读调查、侦查讯问笔录、出示提讯登记、体检记录、对讯问合法性的核查材料等证据材料，有针对性地播放讯问录音录像，提请法庭通知有关调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员出庭说明情况等方式，证明证据收集的合法性。  
讯问录音录像涉及国家秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私或者其他不宜公开内容的，法庭可以决定对讯问录音录像不公开播放、质证。  
公诉人提交的取证过程合法的说明材料，应当经有关调查人员、侦查人员签名，并加盖单位印章。未经签名或者盖章的，不得作为证据使用。上述说明材料不能单独作为证明取证过程合法的根据。

**Article 135** : Where the court decides to investigate the lawfulness of evidence collection, the public prosecutor shall prove the lawfulness of evidence collection by reading investigation and interrogation transcripts, presenting evidence materials such as interrogation registration records, physical examination records, and verification materials concerning the lawfulness of interrogation, purposefully playing audio or video recordings of interrogation, and requesting the court to notify relevant investigation personnel, investigative personnel, or other personnel to appear in court to explain the circumstances.  
Where audio or video recordings of interrogation involve state secrets, trade secrets, personal privacy, or other content unsuitable for public disclosure, the court may decide not to play such recordings publicly or to subject them to cross-examination.  
Written explanatory materials submitted by the public prosecutor to prove the lawfulness of the evidence collection process shall be signed by the relevant investigation personnel and investigative personnel and affixed with the seal of the unit. Without a signature or seal, such materials shall not be used as evidence. The aforesaid explanatory materials may not independently serve as the basis for proving the lawfulness of the evidence collection process.

<a id="art-136"></a>第一百三十六条 控辩双方申请法庭通知调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员出庭说明情况，法庭认为有必要的，应当通知有关人员出庭。  
根据案件情况，法庭可以依职权通知调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员出庭说明情况。  
调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员出庭的，应当向法庭说明证据收集过程，并就相关情况接受控辩双方和法庭的询问。

**Article 136** : Where the prosecution and the defense request the court to notify investigation personnel, investigative personnel, or other personnel to appear in court to explain the circumstances, and the court considers it necessary, it shall notify the relevant personnel to appear in court.  
Based on the circumstances of the case, the court may, on its own authority, notify investigation personnel, investigative personnel, or other personnel to appear in court to explain the circumstances.  
Where investigation personnel, investigative personnel, or other personnel appear in court, they shall explain the evidence collection process to the court and accept questioning by the prosecution, the defense, and the court on relevant matters.

<a id="art-137"></a>第一百三十七条 法庭对证据收集的合法性进行调查后，确认或者不能排除存在[刑事诉讼法第五十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-56)规定的以非法方法收集证据情形的，对有关证据应当排除。

**Article 137** : After the court investigates the lawfulness of evidence collection, where it confirms or cannot exclude the existence of circumstances in which evidence was collected by illegal methods as provided in Article 56 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the relevant evidence shall be excluded.

<a id="art-138"></a>第一百三十八条 具有下列情形之一的，第二审人民法院应当对证据收集的合法性进行审查，并根据刑事诉讼法和本解释的有关规定作出处理：  
（一）第一审人民法院对当事人及其辩护人、诉讼代理人排除非法证据的申请没有审查，且以该证据作为定案根据的；  
（二）人民检察院或者被告人、自诉人及其法定代理人不服第一审人民法院作出的有关证据收集合法性的调查结论，提出抗诉、上诉的；  
（三）当事人及其辩护人、诉讼代理人在第一审结束后才发现相关线索或者材料，申请人民法院排除非法证据的。

**Article 138** : Under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court of second instance shall review the lawfulness of evidence collection and handle the matter in accordance with the Criminal Procedure Law and the relevant provisions of this Interpretation:  
(1) The People's Court of first instance did not review the application by the party and his defender or litigation agent for exclusion of illegal evidence and used the evidence as the basis for deciding the case;  
(2) The People's Procuratorate, or the defendant, the private prosecutor, or their legal representatives, dissatisfied with the conclusion of the investigation concerning the lawfulness of evidence collection made by the People's Court of first instance, file a protest or an appeal;  
(3) The party and his defender or litigation agent discover relevant clues or materials only after the conclusion of the first instance and apply to the People's Court for exclusion of illegal evidence.

#### 第十节 证据的综合审查与运用

<a id="art-139"></a>第一百三十九条 对证据的真实性，应当综合全案证据进行审查。  
对证据的证明力，应当根据具体情况，从证据与案件事实的关联程度、证据之间的联系等方面进行审查判断。

**Article 139** : The authenticity of evidence shall be reviewed comprehensively in light of all evidence in the case.  
The probative force of evidence shall be reviewed and determined based on the specific circumstances, including the degree of relevance between the evidence and the facts of the case and the connections among the evidence.

<a id="art-140"></a>第一百四十条 没有直接证据，但间接证据同时符合下列条件的，可以认定被告人有罪：  
（一）证据已经查证属实；  
（二）证据之间相互印证，不存在无法排除的矛盾和无法解释的疑问；  
（三）全案证据形成完整的证据链；  
（四）根据证据认定案件事实足以排除合理怀疑，结论具有唯一性；  
（五）运用证据进行的推理符合逻辑和经验。

**Article 140** : Where there is no direct evidence, but the indirect evidence simultaneously meets the following conditions, the defendant may be found guilty:  
(1) The evidence has been verified to be true;  
(2) The evidence corroborates each other, and there are no irreconcilable contradictions or inexplicable doubts;  
(3) All evidence in the case forms a complete chain of evidence;  
(4) Determining the facts of the case based on the evidence is sufficient to exclude reasonable doubt, and the conclusion is exclusive;  
(5) The reasoning using the evidence conforms to logic and experience.

<a id="art-141"></a>第一百四十一条 根据被告人的供述、指认提取到了隐蔽性很强的物证、书证，且被告人的供述与其他证明犯罪事实发生的证据相互印证，并排除串供、逼供、诱供等可能性的，可以认定被告人有罪。

**Article 141** : Where, based on the defendant's confession and identification, physical evidence and documentary evidence of a highly concealed nature are obtained, the defendant's confession corroborates with other evidence proving the commission of the crime, and the possibilities of colluding to make consistent statements, extorting confessions, or inducing confessions are excluded, the defendant may be found guilty.

<a id="art-142"></a>第一百四十二条 对监察机关、侦查机关出具的被告人到案经过、抓获经过等材料，应当审查是否有出具该说明材料的办案人员、办案机关的签名、盖章。  
对到案经过、抓获经过或者确定被告人有重大嫌疑的根据有疑问的，应当通知人民检察院补充说明。

**Article 142** : For materials such as the process by which the defendant was brought to justice or captured and issued by supervisory organs or investigative organs, it shall be reviewed whether the case-handling personnel and case-handling organs that issued such explanatory materials have signed or affixed seals.  
Where there are doubts about the process of bringing the defendant to justice, the process of capture, or the basis for determining that the defendant has major suspicion, the People's Procuratorate shall be notified to provide supplementary explanations.

<a id="art-143"></a>第一百四十三条 下列证据应当慎重使用，有其他证据印证的，可以采信：  
（一）生理上、精神上有缺陷，对案件事实的认知和表达存在一定困难，但尚未丧失正确认知、表达能力的被害人、证人和被告人所作的陈述、证言和供述；  
（二）与被告人有亲属关系或者其他密切关系的证人所作的有利于被告人的证言，或者与被告人有利害冲突的证人所作的不利于被告人的证言。

**Article 143** : The following evidence shall be used with caution; where corroborated by other evidence, it may be admitted:  
(1) Statements, testimony, and confessions made by victims, witnesses, and defendants who have physical or mental defects, experience certain difficulty in perceiving and expressing the facts of the case, but have not yet lost the ability to correctly perceive and express;  
(2) Testimony favorable to the defendant given by witnesses who have a kinship or other close relationship with the defendant, or testimony unfavorable to the defendant given by witnesses who have an interest conflict with the defendant.

<a id="art-144"></a>第一百四十四条 证明被告人自首、坦白、立功的证据材料，没有加盖接受被告人投案、坦白、检举揭发等的单位的印章，或者接受人员没有签名的，不得作为定案的根据。  
对被告人及其辩护人提出有自首、坦白、立功的事实和理由，有关机关未予认定，或者有关机关提出被告人有自首、坦白、立功表现，但证据材料不全的，人民法院应当要求有关机关提供证明材料，或者要求有关人员作证，并结合其他证据作出认定。

**Article 144** : Evidence materials proving that the defendant voluntarily surrendered, made a truthful confession, or rendered meritorious service, without the seal of the unit accepting the defendant's surrender, confession, or reporting and disclosure, or without the signature of the accepting personnel, shall not be used as the basis for deciding the case.  
Where the defendant and his defender raise facts and reasons showing voluntary surrender, truthful confession, or meritorious service, but the relevant organ fails to recognize them, or where the relevant organ claims that the defendant had conduct of voluntary surrender, truthful confession, or meritorious service but the evidence materials are incomplete, the People's Court shall require the relevant organ to provide supporting materials or require relevant personnel to testify, and shall make a determination in light of other evidence.

<a id="art-145"></a>第一百四十五条 证明被告人具有累犯、毒品再犯情节等的证据材料，应当包括前罪的裁判文书、释放证明等材料；材料不全的，应当通知人民检察院提供。

**Article 145** : Evidence materials proving that the defendant is a recidivist or a drug recidivist shall include the judgment instruments of the previous crime, release certificates, and other materials; where the materials are incomplete, the People's Procuratorate shall be notified to provide them.

<a id="art-146"></a>第一百四十六条 审查被告人实施被指控的犯罪时或者审判时是否达到相应法定责任年龄，应当根据户籍证明、出生证明文件、学籍卡、人口普查登记、无利害关系人的证言等证据综合判断。  
证明被告人已满十二周岁、十四周岁、十六周岁、十八周岁或者不满七十五周岁的证据不足的，应当作出有利于被告人的认定。

**Article 146** : In reviewing whether the defendant has reached the corresponding statutory age of criminal responsibility when committing the alleged crime or at the time of trial, a comprehensive determination shall be made based on evidence such as household registration certificates, birth certificate documents, school registration cards, census registration, and testimony of disinterested persons.  
Where the evidence is insufficient to prove that the defendant has reached the age of twelve, fourteen, sixteen, or eighteen, or is under the age of seventy-five, a determination favorable to the defendant shall be made.

### 第五章 强制措施

<a id="art-147"></a>第一百四十七条 人民法院根据案件情况，可以决定对被告人拘传、取保候审、监视居住或者逮捕。  
对被告人采取、撤销或者变更强制措施的，由院长决定；决定继续取保候审、监视居住的，可以由合议庭或者独任审判员决定。

**Article 147** : The People's Court may, based on the circumstances of the case, decide to apply forcible summons, release on bail pending investigation or trial, residential surveillance, or arrest to the defendant.  
The adoption, revocation, or change of compulsory measures against the defendant shall be decided by the president of the court; the decision to continue release on bail pending investigation or trial or residential surveillance may be made by the collegiate bench or the sole judge.

<a id="art-148"></a>第一百四十八条 对经依法传唤拒不到庭的被告人，或者根据案件情况有必要拘传的被告人，可以拘传。  
拘传被告人，应当由院长签发拘传票，由司法警察执行，执行人员不得少于二人。  
拘传被告人，应当出示拘传票。对抗拒拘传的被告人，可以使用戒具。

**Article 148** : A defendant who, after being lawfully summoned, refuses to appear in court, or a defendant for whom forcible summons is necessary based on the circumstances of the case, may be forcibly summoned.  
For forcible summons of a defendant, the president of the court shall issue a forcible summons warrant, which shall be executed by judicial police, and there shall be no fewer than two executing officers.  
For forcible summons of a defendant, the forcible summons warrant shall be presented. For a defendant who resists forcible summons, restraints may be used.

<a id="art-149"></a>第一百四十九条 拘传被告人，持续的时间不得超过十二小时；案情特别重大、复杂，需要采取逮捕措施的，持续的时间不得超过二十四小时。不得以连续拘传的形式变相拘禁被告人。应当保证被拘传人的饮食和必要的休息时间。

**Article 149** : The duration of forcible summons of a defendant shall not exceed twelve hours; where the case is especially major and complex and arrest measures need to be taken, the duration shall not exceed twenty-four hours. It shall not use continuous forcible summons as a disguised form of detaining the defendant. The food and necessary rest time of the forcibly summoned person shall be guaranteed.

<a id="art-150"></a>第一百五十条 被告人具有[刑事诉讼法第六十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-67)第一款规定情形之一的，人民法院可以决定取保候审。  
对被告人决定取保候审的，应当责令其提出保证人或者交纳保证金，不得同时使用保证人保证与保证金保证。

**Article 150** : Where the defendant falls under any of the circumstances provided in paragraph 1 of Article 67 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the People's Court may decide to release him on bail pending investigation or trial.  
Where the People's Court decides to release the defendant on bail pending investigation or trial, it shall order him to provide a surety or pay bail money, and shall not simultaneously use surety guarantee and bail money guarantee.

<a id="art-151"></a>第一百五十一条 对下列被告人决定取保候审的，可以责令其提出一至二名保证人：  
（一）无力交纳保证金的；  
（二）未成年或者已满七十五周岁的；  
（三）不宜收取保证金的其他被告人。

**Article 151** : Where release on bail pending investigation or trial is decided for the following defendants, the defendants may be ordered to provide one or two sureties:  
(1) Those who are unable to pay the bail money;  
(2) Those who are minors or have reached the age of seventy-five;  
(3) Other defendants for whom it is inappropriate to collect bail money.

<a id="art-152"></a>第一百五十二条 人民法院应当审查保证人是否符合法定条件。符合条件的，应当告知其必须履行的保证义务，以及不履行义务的法律后果，并由其出具保证书。

**Article 152** : The People's Court shall review whether the surety meets the statutory conditions. Where the conditions are met, the surety shall be informed of the obligatory duties he must perform and the legal consequences of failing to perform such duties, and the surety shall issue a written guarantee.

<a id="art-153"></a>第一百五十三条 对决定取保候审的被告人使用保证金保证的，应当依照[刑事诉讼法第七十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-72)第一款的规定确定保证金的具体数额，并责令被告人或者为其提供保证金的单位、个人将保证金一次性存入公安机关指定银行的专门账户。

**Article 153** : Where bail money guarantee is used for a defendant released on bail pending investigation or trial, the specific amount of bail money shall be determined in accordance with paragraph 1 of Article 72 of the Criminal Procedure Law, and the defendant or the unit or individual providing bail money for him shall be ordered to deposit the bail money in a lump sum into the special account at the bank designated by the public security organ.

<a id="art-154"></a>第一百五十四条 人民法院向被告人宣布取保候审决定后，应当将取保候审决定书等相关材料送交当地公安机关。  
对被告人使用保证金保证的，应当在核实保证金已经存入公安机关指定银行的专门账户后，将银行出具的收款凭证一并送交公安机关。

**Article 154** : After the People's Court announces the decision on release on bail pending investigation or trial to the defendant, it shall deliver the decision on release on bail and other relevant materials to the local public security organ.  
Where bail money guarantee is used for the defendant, after verifying that the bail money has been deposited into the special account at the bank designated by the public security organ, the receipt issued by the bank shall be delivered to the public security organ together with the other materials.

<a id="art-155"></a>第一百五十五条 被告人被取保候审期间，保证人不愿继续履行保证义务或者丧失履行保证义务能力的，人民法院应当在收到保证人的申请或者公安机关的书面通知后三日以内，责令被告人重新提出保证人或者交纳保证金，或者变更强制措施，并通知公安机关。

**Article 155** : Where, during the period when the defendant is released on bail pending investigation or trial, the surety is unwilling to continue to perform the surety obligations or loses the ability to perform them, the People's Court shall, within three days after receiving the surety's application or the written notice of the public security organ, order the defendant to provide a new surety or pay bail money, or change the compulsory measures, and shall notify the public security organ.

<a id="art-156"></a>第一百五十六条 人民法院发现保证人未履行保证义务的，应当书面通知公安机关依法处理。

**Article 156** : Where the People's Court discovers that the surety fails to perform the surety obligations, it shall notify the public security organ in writing to handle the matter according to law.

<a id="art-157"></a>第一百五十七条 根据案件事实和法律规定，认为已经构成犯罪的被告人在取保候审期间逃匿的，如果系保证人协助被告人逃匿，或者保证人明知被告人藏匿地点但拒绝向司法机关提供，对保证人应当依法追究责任。

**Article 157** : Where a defendant who, according to the facts of the case and the provisions of law, is considered to have already constituted a crime escapes during the period of release on bail pending investigation or trial, if the surety assisted the defendant in escaping, or the surety clearly knew the defendant's hiding place but refused to provide it to the judicial organ, the surety shall be investigated for liability according to law.

<a id="art-158"></a>第一百五十八条 人民法院发现使用保证金保证的被取保候审人违反[刑事诉讼法第七十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-71)第一款、第二款规定的，应当书面通知公安机关依法处理。  
人民法院收到公安机关已经没收保证金的书面通知或者变更强制措施的建议后，应当区别情形，在五日以内责令被告人具结悔过，重新交纳保证金或者提出保证人，或者变更强制措施，并通知公安机关。  
人民法院决定对被依法没收保证金的被告人继续取保候审的，取保候审的期限连续计算。

**Article 158** : Where the People's Court discovers that a person released on bail pending investigation or trial using bail money guarantee violates the provisions of paragraphs 1 and 2 of Article 71 of the Criminal Procedure Law, it shall notify the public security organ in writing to handle the matter according to law.  
After the People's Court receives the written notice from the public security organ that the bail money has been confiscated or the suggestion to change compulsory measures, it shall, distinguishing the circumstances, order the defendant within five days to sign a statement of repentance, pay bail money anew or provide a surety, or change the compulsory measures, and shall notify the public security organ.  
Where the People's Court decides to continue release on bail pending investigation or trial for a defendant whose bail money has been confiscated according to law, the period of release on bail pending investigation or trial shall be calculated continuously.

<a id="art-159"></a>第一百五十九条 对被取保候审的被告人的判决、裁定生效后，如果保证金属于其个人财产，且需要用以退赔被害人、履行附带民事赔偿义务或者执行财产刑的，人民法院可以书面通知公安机关移交全部保证金，由人民法院作出处理，剩余部分退还被告人。

**Article 159** : After the judgment or order against a defendant released on bail pending investigation or trial takes effect, if the bail money belongs to his personal property and is needed to make restitution to the victim, to perform the obligation of incidental civil compensation, or to enforce a property penalty, the People's Court may notify the public security organ in writing to transfer all the bail money, and the People's Court shall dispose of it, with the remaining portion returned to the defendant.

<a id="art-160"></a>第一百六十条 对具有[刑事诉讼法第七十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-74)第一款、第二款规定情形的被告人，人民法院可以决定监视居住。  
人民法院决定对被告人监视居住的，应当核实其住处；没有固定住处的，应当为其指定居所。

**Article 160** : For a defendant with the circumstances provided in paragraphs 1 and 2 of Article 74 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the People's Court may decide to place him under residential surveillance.  
Where the People's Court decides to place the defendant under residential surveillance, it shall verify his residence; if he has no fixed residence, it shall designate a dwelling for him.

<a id="art-161"></a>第一百六十一条 人民法院向被告人宣布监视居住决定后，应当将监视居住决定书等相关材料送交被告人住处或者指定居所所在地的公安机关执行。  
对被告人指定居所监视居住后，人民法院应当在二十四小时以内，将监视居住的原因和处所通知其家属；确实无法通知的，应当记录在案。

**Article 161** : After the People's Court announces the decision on residential surveillance to the defendant, it shall deliver the decision on residential surveillance and other relevant materials to the public security organ at the place of the defendant's residence or designated dwelling for execution.  
After the defendant is placed under residential surveillance at a designated dwelling, the People's Court shall, within twenty-four hours, notify his family members of the reasons for and the place of residential surveillance; where it is truly impossible to notify, this shall be recorded in the case file.

<a id="art-162"></a>第一百六十二条 人民检察院、公安机关已经对犯罪嫌疑人取保候审、监视居住，案件起诉至人民法院后，需要继续取保候审、监视居住或者变更强制措施的，人民法院应当在七日以内作出决定，并通知人民检察院、公安机关。  
决定继续取保候审、监视居住的，应当重新办理手续，期限重新计算；继续使用保证金保证的，不再收取保证金。

**Article 162** : Where the People's Procuratorate or the public security organ has already released a criminal suspect on bail pending investigation or trial or placed him under residential surveillance, and after the case is prosecuted to the People's Court, it is necessary to continue release on bail pending investigation or trial or residential surveillance or to change compulsory measures, the People's Court shall make a decision within seven days and notify the People's Procuratorate and the public security organ.  
Where a decision is made to continue release on bail pending investigation or trial or residential surveillance, the procedures shall be gone through anew, and the period shall be recalculated; where bail money guarantee continues to be used, bail money shall no longer be collected.

<a id="art-163"></a>第一百六十三条 对具有[刑事诉讼法第八十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-81)第一款、第三款规定情形的被告人，人民法院应当决定逮捕。

**Article 163** : For a defendant with the circumstances provided in paragraphs 1 and 3 of Article 81 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the People's Court shall decide to arrest him.

<a id="art-164"></a>第一百六十四条 被取保候审的被告人具有下列情形之一的，人民法院应当决定逮捕：  
（一）故意实施新的犯罪的；  
（二）企图自杀或者逃跑的；  
（三）毁灭、伪造证据，干扰证人作证或者串供的；  
（四）打击报复、恐吓滋扰被害人、证人、鉴定人、举报人、控告人等的；  
（五）经传唤，无正当理由不到案，影响审判活动正常进行的；  
（六）擅自改变联系方式或者居住地，导致无法传唤，影响审判活动正常进行的；  
（七）未经批准，擅自离开所居住的市、县，影响审判活动正常进行，或者两次未经批准，擅自离开所居住的市、县的；  
（八）违反规定进入特定场所、与特定人员会见或者通信、从事特定活动，影响审判活动正常进行，或者两次违反有关规定的；  
（九）依法应当决定逮捕的其他情形。

**Article 164** : Where a defendant released on bail pending investigation or trial falls under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall decide to arrest him:  
(1) Intentionally committing a new crime;  
(2) Attempting to commit suicide or escape;  
(3) Destroying or falsifying evidence, interfering with witnesses' testimony, or colluding to make consistent statements;  
(4) Retaliating against, intimidating, or harassing victims, witnesses, expert witnesses, informants, or accusers;  
(5) After summons, failing to appear without justifiable reasons, thereby affecting the normal conduct of trial activities;  
(6) Without authorization changing contact information or residence, rendering summons impossible and affecting the normal conduct of trial activities;  
(7) Without approval leaving the city or county where he resides, affecting the normal conduct of trial activities, or twice without approval leaving the city or county where he resides;  
(8) In violation of regulations entering specific places, meeting or communicating with specific persons, or engaging in specific activities, affecting the normal conduct of trial activities, or twice violating the relevant regulations;  
(9) Other circumstances under which arrest shall be decided according to law.

<a id="art-165"></a>第一百六十五条 被监视居住的被告人具有下列情形之一的，人民法院应当决定逮捕：  
（一）具有前条第一项至第五项规定情形之一的；  
（二）未经批准，擅自离开执行监视居住的处所，影响审判活动正常进行，或者两次未经批准，擅自离开执行监视居住的处所的；  
（三）未经批准，擅自会见他人或者通信，影响审判活动正常进行，或者两次未经批准，擅自会见他人或者通信的；  
（四）对因患有严重疾病、生活不能自理，或者因怀孕、正在哺乳自己婴儿而未予逮捕的被告人，疾病痊愈或者哺乳期已满的；  
（五）依法应当决定逮捕的其他情形。

**Article 165** : Where a defendant under residential surveillance falls under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall decide to arrest him:  
(1) Having any of the circumstances provided in items (1) through (5) of the preceding article;  
(2) Without approval leaving the place where residential surveillance is executed, affecting the normal conduct of trial activities, or twice without approval leaving the place where residential surveillance is executed;  
(3) Without approval meeting others or communicating, affecting the normal conduct of trial activities, or twice without approval meeting others or communicating;  
(4) Where the defendant was not arrested because of suffering from a serious disease, being unable to take care of himself, or because of pregnancy or nursing his or her own infant, and the disease has been cured or the nursing period has expired;  
(5) Other circumstances under which arrest shall be decided according to law.

<a id="art-166"></a>第一百六十六条 对可能判处徒刑以下刑罚的被告人，违反取保候审、监视居住规定，严重影响诉讼活动正常进行的，可以决定逮捕。

**Article 166** : Where a defendant who may be sentenced to a punishment below fixed-term imprisonment violates the provisions on release on bail pending investigation or trial or residential surveillance, seriously affecting the normal conduct of litigation activities, an arrest may be decided.

<a id="art-167"></a>第一百六十七条 人民法院作出逮捕决定后，应当将逮捕决定书等相关材料送交公安机关执行，并将逮捕决定书抄送人民检察院。逮捕被告人后，人民法院应当将逮捕的原因和羁押的处所，在二十四小时以内通知其家属；确实无法通知的，应当记录在案。

**Article 167** : After the People's Court makes an arrest decision, it shall deliver the arrest decision and other relevant materials to the public security organ for execution, and send a copy of the arrest decision to the People's Procuratorate. After arresting the defendant, the People's Court shall, within twenty-four hours, notify his family members of the reasons for the arrest and the place of custody; where it is truly impossible to notify, this shall be recorded in the case file.

<a id="art-168"></a>第一百六十八条 人民法院对决定逮捕的被告人，应当在逮捕后二十四小时以内讯问。发现不应当逮捕的，应当立即释放。必要时，可以依法变更强制措施。

**Article 168** : The People's Court shall interrogate a defendant for whom arrest is decided within twenty-four hours after the arrest. If it is found that the defendant should not have been arrested, he shall be released immediately. Where necessary, compulsory measures may be changed according to law.

<a id="art-169"></a>第一百六十九条 被逮捕的被告人具有下列情形之一的，人民法院可以变更强制措施：  
（一）患有严重疾病、生活不能自理的；  
（二）怀孕或者正在哺乳自己婴儿的；  
（三）系生活不能自理的人的唯一扶养人。

**Article 169** : Where an arrested defendant falls under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court may change the compulsory measures:  
(1) Suffering from a serious disease and being unable to take care of himself;  
(2) Being pregnant or nursing his or her own infant;  
(3) Being the sole supporter of a person who is unable to take care of himself.

<a id="art-170"></a>第一百七十条 被逮捕的被告人具有下列情形之一的，人民法院应当立即释放；必要时，可以依法变更强制措施：  
（一）第一审人民法院判决被告人无罪、不负刑事责任或者免予刑事处罚的；  
（二）第一审人民法院判处管制、宣告缓刑、单独适用附加刑，判决尚未发生法律效力的；  
（三）被告人被羁押的时间已到第一审人民法院对其判处的刑期期限的；  
（四）案件不能在法律规定的期限内审结的。

**Article 170** : Where an arrested defendant falls under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall release him immediately; where necessary, compulsory measures may be changed according to law:  
(1) The People's Court of first instance has sentenced the defendant to be innocent, not criminally responsible, or exempt from criminal punishment;  
(2) The People's Court of first instance has sentenced the defendant to public surveillance, declared probation, or solely applied supplementary punishment, and the judgment has not yet taken legal effect;  
(3) The period of custody of the defendant has already reached the term of sentence imposed on him by the People's Court of first instance;  
(4) The case cannot be concluded within the time limit prescribed by law.

<a id="art-171"></a>第一百七十一条 人民法院决定释放被告人的，应当立即将释放通知书送交公安机关执行。

**Article 171** : Where the People's Court decides to release a defendant, it shall immediately deliver the release notice to the public security organ for execution.

<a id="art-172"></a>第一百七十二条 被采取强制措施的被告人，被判处管制、缓刑的，在社区矫正开始后，强制措施自动解除；被单处附加刑的，在判决、裁定发生法律效力后，强制措施自动解除；被判处监禁刑的，在刑罚开始执行后，强制措施自动解除。

**Article 172** : Where a defendant subjected to compulsory measures is sentenced to public surveillance or probation, the compulsory measures shall be automatically lifted after community correction begins; where he is solely sentenced to supplementary punishment, the compulsory measures shall be automatically lifted after the judgment or order takes legal effect; where he is sentenced to a custodial penalty, the compulsory measures shall be automatically lifted after the punishment begins to be executed.

<a id="art-173"></a>第一百七十三条 对人民法院决定逮捕的被告人，人民检察院建议释放或者变更强制措施的，人民法院应当在收到建议后十日以内将处理情况通知人民检察院。

**Article 173** : Where, for a defendant arrested upon the decision of the People's Court, the People's Procuratorate suggests release or change of compulsory measures, the People's Court shall, within ten days after receiving the suggestion, notify the People's Procuratorate of the handling.

<a id="art-174"></a>第一百七十四条 被告人及其法定代理人、近亲属或者辩护人申请变更、解除强制措施的，应当说明理由。人民法院收到申请后，应当在三日以内作出决定。同意变更、解除强制措施的，应当依照本解释规定处理；不同意的，应当告知申请人，并说明理由。

**Article 174** : Where the defendant and his legal representatives, close relatives, or defenders apply to change or remove compulsory measures, they shall state the reasons. After receiving the application, the People's Court shall make a decision within three days. Where the application to change or remove compulsory measures is approved, the matter shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of this Interpretation; where it is not approved, the applicant shall be informed and the reasons stated.

### 第六章 附带民事诉讼

<a id="art-175"></a>第一百七十五条 被害人因人身权利受到犯罪侵犯或者财物被犯罪分子毁坏而遭受物质损失的，有权在刑事诉讼过程中提起附带民事诉讼；被害人死亡或者丧失行为能力的，其法定代理人、近亲属有权提起附带民事诉讼。  
因受到犯罪侵犯，提起附带民事诉讼或者单独提起民事诉讼要求赔偿精神损失的，人民法院一般不予受理。

**Article 175** : A victim who suffers material loss because his personal rights are infringed by a crime or his property is destroyed by a criminal has the right to bring an incidental civil action during the criminal proceedings; where the victim dies or loses capacity, his legal representatives or close relatives have the right to bring an incidental civil action.  
Where, due to infringement by a crime, an incidental civil action is brought or an independent civil action is brought to claim compensation for mental loss, the People's Court shall generally not accept the case.

<a id="art-176"></a>第一百七十六条 被告人非法占有、处置被害人财产的，应当依法予以追缴或者责令退赔。被害人提起附带民事诉讼的，人民法院不予受理。追缴、退赔的情况，可以作为量刑情节考虑。

**Article 176** : Where the defendant illegally occupies or disposes of the victim's property, such property shall be recovered or the defendant shall be ordered to make restitution according to law. Where the victim brings an incidental civil action, the People's Court shall not accept it. The circumstances of recovery and restitution may be considered as a sentencing circumstance.

<a id="art-177"></a>第一百七十七条 国家机关工作人员在行使职权时，侵犯他人人身、财产权利构成犯罪，被害人或者其法定代理人、近亲属提起附带民事诉讼的，人民法院不予受理，但应当告知其可以依法申请国家赔偿。

**Article 177** : Where a functionary of a state organ, while exercising his authority, infringes upon another person's personal or property rights and constitutes a crime, and the victim or his legal representatives or close relatives bring an incidental civil action, the People's Court shall not accept it, but shall inform them that they may apply for state compensation according to law.

<a id="art-178"></a>第一百七十八条 人民法院受理刑事案件后，对符合[刑事诉讼法第一百零一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-101)和[本解释第一百七十五条](#art-175)第一款规定的，可以告知被害人或者其法定代理人、近亲属有权提起附带民事诉讼。  
有权提起附带民事诉讼的人放弃诉讼权利的，应当准许，并记录在案。

**Article 178** : After the People's Court accepts a criminal case, where the case meets the provisions of Article 101 of the Criminal Procedure Law and paragraph 1 of Article 175 of this Interpretation, the victim or his legal representatives or close relatives may be informed of their right to bring an incidental civil action.  
Where a person entitled to bring an incidental civil action waives the right of action, the waiver shall be permitted and recorded in the case file.

<a id="art-179"></a>第一百七十九条 国家财产、集体财产遭受损失，受损失的单位未提起附带民事诉讼，人民检察院在提起公诉时提起附带民事诉讼的，人民法院应当受理。  
人民检察院提起附带民事诉讼的，应当列为附带民事诉讼原告人。  
被告人非法占有、处置国家财产、集体财产的，依照[本解释第一百七十六条](#art-176)的规定处理。

**Article 179** : Where state property or collective property suffers losses, and the injured unit does not bring an incidental civil action, if the People's Procuratorate brings an incidental civil action when initiating public prosecution, the People's Court shall accept it.  
Where the People's Procuratorate brings an incidental civil action, it shall be listed as the plaintiff in the incidental civil action.  
Where the defendant illegally occupies or disposes of state property or collective property, the matter shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of Article 176 of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-180"></a>第一百八十条 附带民事诉讼中依法负有赔偿责任的人包括：  
（一）刑事被告人以及未被追究刑事责任的其他共同侵害人；  
（二）刑事被告人的监护人；  
（三）死刑罪犯的遗产继承人；  
（四）共同犯罪案件中，案件审结前死亡的被告人的遗产继承人；  
（五）对被害人的物质损失依法应当承担赔偿责任的其他单位和个人。  
附带民事诉讼被告人的亲友自愿代为赔偿的，可以准许。

**Article 180** : In an incidental civil action, the persons who bear compensation liability according to law include:  
(1) the criminal defendant and other joint infringers who have not been investigated for criminal liability;  
(2) the guardians of the criminal defendant;  
(3) the heirs of the estate of a criminal sentenced to death;  
(4) in a joint crime case, the heirs of the estate of a defendant who dies before the conclusion of the case;  
(5) other units and individuals who shall bear compensation liability according to law for the victim's material losses.  
Where relatives or friends of the defendant in the incidental civil action voluntarily compensate on his or her behalf, it may be permitted.

<a id="art-181"></a>第一百八十一条 被害人或者其法定代理人、近亲属仅对部分共同侵害人提起附带民事诉讼的，人民法院应当告知其可以对其他共同侵害人，包括没有被追究刑事责任的共同侵害人,一并提起附带民事诉讼，但共同犯罪案件中同案犯在逃的除外。  
被害人或者其法定代理人、近亲属放弃对其他共同侵害人的诉讼权利的，人民法院应当告知其相应法律后果，并在裁判文书中说明其放弃诉讼请求的情况。

**Article 181** : Where the victim or his or her legal representative or close relatives initiate an incidental civil action only against some of the joint infringers, the People's Court shall inform them that they may initiate an incidental civil action against the other joint infringers, including those not investigated for criminal liability, except where co-defendants in a joint crime case are at large.  
Where the victim or his or her legal representative or close relatives waive their litigation rights against other joint infringers, the People's Court shall inform them of the corresponding legal consequences and explain in the judgment document the circumstances of their waiver of claims.

<a id="art-182"></a>第一百八十二条 附带民事诉讼的起诉条件是：  
（一）起诉人符合法定条件；  
（二）有明确的被告人；  
（三）有请求赔偿的具体要求和事实、理由；  
（四）属于人民法院受理附带民事诉讼的范围。

**Article 182** : The conditions for initiating an incidental civil action are:  
(1) the plaintiff meets the statutory conditions;  
(2) there is a definite defendant;  
(3) there are specific claims for compensation and facts and reasons; and  
(4) the case falls within the scope of incidental civil actions accepted by the People's Court.

<a id="art-183"></a>第一百八十三条 共同犯罪案件，同案犯在逃的，不应列为附带民事诉讼被告人。逃跑的同案犯到案后，被害人或者其法定代理人、近亲属可以对其提起附带民事诉讼，但已经从其他共同犯罪人处获得足额赔偿的除外。

**Article 183** : In a joint crime case, a co-defendant who is at large shall not be listed as a defendant in the incidental civil action. After the escaped co-defendant has been brought to justice, the victim or his or her legal representative or close relatives may initiate an incidental civil action against him or her, except where full compensation has already been obtained from other joint offenders.

<a id="art-184"></a>第一百八十四条 附带民事诉讼应当在刑事案件立案后及时提起。  
提起附带民事诉讼应当提交附带民事起诉状。

**Article 184** : An incidental civil action shall be initiated promptly after the criminal case is placed on file.  
To initiate an incidental civil action, a written incidental civil complaint shall be submitted.

<a id="art-185"></a>第一百八十五条 侦查、审查起诉期间，有权提起附带民事诉讼的人提出赔偿要求，经公安机关、人民检察院调解，当事人双方已经达成协议并全部履行，被害人或者其法定代理人、近亲属又提起附带民事诉讼的，人民法院不予受理，但有证据证明调解违反自愿、合法原则的除外。

**Article 185** : Where, during investigation and review for prosecution, a person entitled to initiate an incidental civil action claims compensation, and after mediation by the public security organ or the People's Procuratorate, the parties have reached an agreement and fully performed it, and the victim or his or her legal representative or close relatives again initiate an incidental civil action, the People's Court shall not accept it, except where there is evidence proving that the mediation violated the principles of voluntariness and lawfulness.

<a id="art-186"></a>第一百八十六条 被害人或者其法定代理人、近亲属提起附带民事诉讼的，人民法院应当在七日以内决定是否受理。符合[刑事诉讼法第一百零一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-101)以及本解释有关规定的，应当受理；不符合的，裁定不予受理。

**Article 186** : Where the victim or his or her legal representative or close relatives initiate an incidental civil action, the People's Court shall decide whether to accept it within seven days. If it meets the requirements of Article 101 of the Criminal Procedure Law and the relevant provisions of this Interpretation, it shall be accepted; if not, a ruling of non-acceptance shall be made.

<a id="art-187"></a>第一百八十七条 人民法院受理附带民事诉讼后，应当在五日以内将附带民事起诉状副本送达附带民事诉讼被告人及其法定代理人，或者将口头起诉的内容及时通知附带民事诉讼被告人及其法定代理人，并制作笔录。  
人民法院送达附带民事起诉状副本时，应当根据刑事案件的审理期限，确定被告人及其法定代理人的答辩准备时间。

**Article 187** : After accepting an incidental civil action, the People's Court shall, within five days, serve a copy of the incidental civil complaint on the defendant in the incidental civil action and his or her legal representative, or promptly notify them of the contents of the oral complaint and make a record.  
When serving a copy of the incidental civil complaint, the People's Court shall, based on the trial period of the criminal case, determine the time for the defendant and his or her legal representative to prepare their defense.

<a id="art-188"></a>第一百八十八条 附带民事诉讼当事人对自己提出的主张，有责任提供证据。

**Article 188** : The parties to an incidental civil action have the duty to provide evidence for the propositions they assert.

<a id="art-189"></a>第一百八十九条 人民法院对可能因被告人的行为或者其他原因，使附带民事判决难以执行的案件，根据附带民事诉讼原告人的申请，可以裁定采取保全措施，查封、扣押或者冻结被告人的财产；附带民事诉讼原告人未提出申请的，必要时，人民法院也可以采取保全措施。  
有权提起附带民事诉讼的人因情况紧急，不立即申请保全将会使其合法权益受到难以弥补的损害的，可以在提起附带民事诉讼前，向被保全财产所在地、被申请人居住地或者对案件有管辖权的人民法院申请采取保全措施。申请人在人民法院受理刑事案件后十五日以内未提起附带民事诉讼的，人民法院应当解除保全措施。  
人民法院采取保全措施，适用[民事诉讼法第一百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-100)至第一百零五条的有关规定，但[民事诉讼法第一百零一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-101)第三款的规定除外。

**Article 189** : For a case in which enforcement of the incidental civil judgment may be difficult due to the defendant's conduct or other reasons, the People's Court may, upon application of the plaintiff in the incidental civil action, rule to adopt preservation measures, such as sealing, seizing, or freezing the defendant's property; where the plaintiff in the incidental civil action has not applied, the People's Court may also adopt preservation measures when necessary.  
Where a person entitled to initiate an incidental civil action, due to an emergency, would suffer irreparable damage to his or her lawful rights and interests if preservation were not applied for immediately, he or she may, before initiating the incidental civil action, apply to the People's Court at the place where the property to be preserved is located, at the place of residence of the respondent, or at the People's Court with jurisdiction over the case for preservation measures. If the applicant does not initiate an incidental civil action within fifteen days after the People's Court accepts the criminal case, the People's Court shall lift the preservation measures.  
When the People's Court adopts preservation measures, the relevant provisions of Articles 100 to 105 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply, except the provisions of Article 101, Paragraph 3 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-190"></a>第一百九十条 人民法院审理附带民事诉讼案件，可以根据自愿、合法的原则进行调解。经调解达成协议的，应当制作调解书。调解书经双方当事人签收后即具有法律效力。  
调解达成协议并即时履行完毕的，可以不制作调解书，但应当制作笔录，经双方当事人、审判人员、书记员签名后即发生法律效力。

**Article 190** : When hearing an incidental civil action case, the People's Court may conduct mediation based on the principles of voluntariness and lawfulness. If an agreement is reached through mediation, a mediation agreement shall be prepared. The mediation agreement shall become legally effective after it is signed by both parties.  
Where an agreement is reached through mediation and performed immediately in full, a mediation agreement may not be prepared, but a record shall be made, which becomes legally effective after it is signed by both parties, the judicial personnel, and the court clerk.

<a id="art-191"></a>第一百九十一条 调解未达成协议或者调解书签收前当事人反悔的，附带民事诉讼应当同刑事诉讼一并判决。

**Article 191** : If no mediation agreement is reached, or a party repudiates before signing the mediation agreement, the incidental civil action shall be adjudicated together with the criminal action.

<a id="art-192"></a>第一百九十二条 对附带民事诉讼作出判决，应当根据犯罪行为造成的物质损失，结合案件具体情况，确定被告人应当赔偿的数额。  
犯罪行为造成被害人人身损害的，应当赔偿医疗费、护理费、交通费等为治疗和康复支付的合理费用，以及因误工减少的收入。造成被害人残疾的，还应当赔偿残疾生活辅助器具费等费用；造成被害人死亡的，还应当赔偿丧葬费等费用。  
驾驶机动车致人伤亡或者造成公私财产重大损失，构成犯罪的，依照[《中华人民共和国道路交通安全法》第七十六条](../../经济、税务与金融/其他经济法规/中华人民共和国道路交通安全法.md#art-76)的规定确定赔偿责任。  
附带民事诉讼当事人就民事赔偿问题达成调解、和解协议的，赔偿范围、数额不受第二款、第三款规定的限制。

**Article 192** : When making a judgment on an incidental civil action, the amount of compensation to be paid by the defendant shall be determined based on the material losses caused by the criminal act and in light of the specific circumstances of the case.  
Where a criminal act causes personal injury to the victim, reasonable expenses paid for treatment and rehabilitation, such as medical expenses, nursing fees, and transportation expenses, as well as income reduced due to missed work, shall be compensated. Where the criminal act causes disability to the victim, expenses such as the cost of assistive devices for disabled living shall also be compensated; where the criminal act causes death of the victim, expenses such as funeral expenses shall also be compensated.  
Where a person drives a motor vehicle causing casualties or major losses to public or private property, constituting a crime, compensation liability shall be determined in accordance with Article 76 of the Road Traffic Safety Law of the People's Republic of China.  
Where the parties to an incidental civil action reach a mediation or settlement agreement on civil compensation, the scope and amount of compensation shall not be limited by the provisions of Paragraphs 2 and 3.

<a id="art-193"></a>第一百九十三条 人民检察院提起附带民事诉讼的，人民法院经审理，认为附带民事诉讼被告人依法应当承担赔偿责任的，应当判令附带民事诉讼被告人直接向遭受损失的单位作出赔偿；遭受损失的单位已经终止，有权利义务继受人的，应当判令其向继受人作出赔偿；没有权利义务继受人的，应当判令其向人民检察院交付赔偿款，由人民检察院上缴国库。

**Article 193** : Where the People's Procuratorate initiates an incidental civil action, and the People's Court after trial finds that the defendant in the incidental civil action shall bear compensation liability according to law, it shall order the defendant in the incidental civil action to directly compensate the unit that suffered losses; if the unit that suffered losses has terminated and there are successors to its rights and obligations, it shall order the defendant to compensate the successors; if there are no successors to its rights and obligations, it shall order the defendant to deliver the compensation to the People's Procuratorate, which shall turn it over to the State treasury.

<a id="art-194"></a>第一百九十四条 审理刑事附带民事诉讼案件，人民法院应当结合被告人赔偿被害人物质损失的情况认定其悔罪表现，并在量刑时予以考虑。

**Article 194** : When hearing a criminal case with an incidental civil action, the People's Court shall determine the defendant's repentance in light of the circumstances of the defendant's compensation for the victim's material losses and consider it in sentencing.

<a id="art-195"></a>第一百九十五条 附带民事诉讼原告人经传唤，无正当理由拒不到庭，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭的，应当按撤诉处理。  
刑事被告人以外的附带民事诉讼被告人经传唤，无正当理由拒不到庭，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭的，附带民事部分可以缺席判决。  
刑事被告人以外的附带民事诉讼被告人下落不明，或者用公告送达以外的其他方式无法送达，可能导致刑事案件审判过分迟延的，可以不将其列为附带民事诉讼被告人，告知附带民事诉讼原告人另行提起民事诉讼。

**Article 195** : Where the plaintiff in an incidental civil action, after being summoned, refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons, or leaves the court during proceedings without permission, the case shall be treated as withdrawn.  
Where a defendant in the incidental civil action other than the criminal defendant, after being summoned, refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons, or leaves the court during proceedings without permission, a default judgment may be made on the incidental civil part.  
Where a defendant in the incidental civil action other than the criminal defendant is missing, or cannot be served by means other than service by public announcement, which may cause excessive delay in the trial of the criminal case, he or she may not be listed as a defendant in the incidental civil action, and the plaintiff in the incidental civil action shall be informed to file a separate civil action.

<a id="art-196"></a>第一百九十六条 附带民事诉讼应当同刑事案件一并审判，只有为了防止刑事案件审判的过分迟延，才可以在刑事案件审判后，由同一审判组织继续审理附带民事诉讼；同一审判组织的成员确实不能继续参与审判的，可以更换。

**Article 196** : An incidental civil action shall be tried together with the criminal case. Only for the purpose of preventing excessive delay in the trial of the criminal case may the same adjudication organization continue to hear the incidental civil action after the trial of the criminal case; if members of the same adjudication organization are truly unable to continue participating in the trial, they may be replaced.

<a id="art-197"></a>第一百九十七条 人民法院认定公诉案件被告人的行为不构成犯罪，对已经提起的附带民事诉讼，经调解不能达成协议的，可以一并作出刑事附带民事判决，也可以告知附带民事原告人另行提起民事诉讼。  
人民法院准许人民检察院撤回起诉的公诉案件，对已经提起的附带民事诉讼，可以进行调解；不宜调解或者经调解不能达成协议的，应当裁定驳回起诉，并告知附带民事诉讼原告人可以另行提起民事诉讼。

**Article 197** : Where the People's Court determines that the conduct of the defendant in a public prosecution case does not constitute a crime, and for an incidental civil action already initiated, if no agreement can be reached through mediation, it may render a criminal incidental civil judgment together, or inform the plaintiff in the incidental civil action to file a separate civil action.  
Where the People's Court permits the People's Procuratorate to withdraw a public prosecution, it may conduct mediation for an incidental civil action already initiated; if mediation is inappropriate or no agreement can be reached through mediation, it shall rule to dismiss the action and inform the plaintiff in the incidental civil action that he or she may file a separate civil action.

<a id="art-198"></a>第一百九十八条 第一审期间未提起附带民事诉讼，在第二审期间提起的，第二审人民法院可以依法进行调解；调解不成的，告知当事人可以在刑事判决、裁定生效后另行提起民事诉讼。

**Article 198** : If an incidental civil action is not initiated during the first instance but is initiated during the second instance, the People's Court of second instance may conduct mediation according to law; if mediation fails, the parties shall be informed that they may file a separate civil action after the criminal judgment or ruling takes effect.

<a id="art-199"></a>第一百九十九条 人民法院审理附带民事诉讼案件，不收取诉讼费。

**Article 199** : The People's Court shall not collect litigation fees for hearing incidental civil action cases.

<a id="art-200"></a>第二百条 被害人或者其法定代理人、近亲属在刑事诉讼过程中未提起附带民事诉讼，另行提起民事诉讼的，人民法院可以进行调解，或者根据[本解释第一百九十二条](#art-192)第二款、第三款的规定作出判决。

**Article 200** : Where the victim or his or her legal representative or close relatives did not initiate an incidental civil action during the criminal proceedings and instead file a separate civil action, the People's Court may conduct mediation, or render a judgment in accordance with Article 192, Paragraphs 2 and 3 of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-201"></a>第二百零一条 人民法院审理附带民事诉讼案件，除刑法、刑事诉讼法以及刑事司法解释已有规定的以外，适用民事法律的有关规定。

**Article 201** : When hearing incidental civil action cases, except for matters already provided for by the Criminal Law, the Criminal Procedure Law, and criminal judicial interpretations, the relevant provisions of civil law shall apply.

### 第七章 期间、送达、审理期限

<a id="art-202"></a>第二百零二条 以月计算的期间，自本月某日至下月同日为一个月;期限起算日为本月最后一日的，至下月最后一日为一个月;下月同日不存在的，自本月某日至下月最后一日为一个月；半个月一律按十五日计算。  
以年计算的刑期，自本年本月某日至次年同月同日的前一日为一年；次年同月同日不存在的，自本年本月某日至次年同月最后一日的前一日为一年。以月计算的刑期，自本月某日至下月同日的前一日为一个月；刑期起算日为本月最后一日的，至下月最后一日的前一日为一个月；下月同日不存在的，自本月某日至下月最后一日的前一日为一个月；半个月一律按十五日计算。

**Article 202** : A period calculated by month runs from a certain day of the current month to the same day of the following month as one month; if the starting day of the period is the last day of the current month, it runs to the last day of the following month as one month; if the same day of the following month does not exist, it runs from the certain day of the current month to the last day of the following month as one month; half a month is uniformly calculated as fifteen days.  
A term of imprisonment calculated by year runs from a certain day of a month in the current year to the day before the same day of the same month in the following year as one year; if the same day of the same month in the following year does not exist, it runs from the certain day of the current month to the day before the last day of the same month in the following year as one year. A term of imprisonment calculated by month runs from a certain day of the current month to the day before the same day of the following month as one month; if the starting day of the term is the last day of the current month, it runs to the day before the last day of the following month as one month; if the same day of the following month does not exist, it runs from the certain day of the current month to the day before the last day of the following month as one month; half a month is uniformly calculated as fifteen days.

<a id="art-203"></a>第二百零三条 当事人由于不能抗拒的原因或者有其他正当理由而耽误期限，依法申请继续进行应当在期满前完成的诉讼活动的，人民法院查证属实后，应当裁定准许。

**Article 203** : Where a party fails to meet a time limit due to irresistible causes or other justifiable reasons and applies according to law to continue litigation activities that should have been completed before the expiration of the time limit, the People's Court, after verifying the facts, shall rule to permit it.

<a id="art-204"></a>第二百零四条 送达诉讼文书，应当由收件人签收。收件人不在的，可以由其成年家属或者所在单位负责收件的人员代收。收件人或者代收人在送达回证上签收的日期为送达日期。  
收件人或者代收人拒绝签收的，送达人可以邀请见证人到场，说明情况，在送达回证上注明拒收的事由和日期，由送达人、见证人签名或者盖章，将诉讼文书留在收件人、代收人的住处或者单位；也可以把诉讼文书留在受送达人的住处，并采用拍照、录像等方式记录送达过程，即视为送达。

**Article 204** : Service of litigation documents shall be signed for by the recipient. If the recipient is absent, an adult family member or the person in charge of receiving mail at the recipient's unit may accept service on his or her behalf. The date on which the recipient or the person accepting service signs the acknowledgement of service shall be the date of service.  
Where the recipient or the person accepting service refuses to sign, the server may invite witnesses to be present, explain the situation, note the reasons for refusal and the date on the acknowledgement of service, have the server and witnesses sign or affix seals, and leave the litigation documents at the residence or unit of the recipient or the person accepting service; alternatively, the server may leave the litigation documents at the residence of the person served and record the service process by taking photographs or videos, which shall be deemed service.

<a id="art-205"></a>第二百零五条 直接送达诉讼文书有困难的，可以委托收件人所在地的人民法院代为送达或者邮寄送达。

**Article 205** : If direct service of litigation documents is difficult, service may be entrusted to the People's Court at the place where the recipient is located, or service may be made by mail.

<a id="art-206"></a>第二百零六条 委托送达的，应当将委托函、委托送达的诉讼文书及送达回证寄送受托法院。受托法院收到后，应当登记，在十日以内送达收件人，并将送达回证寄送委托法院；无法送达的，应当告知委托法院，并将诉讼文书及送达回证退回。

**Article 206** : For service by entrustment, the letter of entrustment, the litigation documents to be served, and the acknowledgement of service shall be sent to the entrusted court. After receiving them, the entrusted court shall register them, serve them on the recipient within ten days, and send the acknowledgement of service back to the entrusting court; if service cannot be made, it shall inform the entrusting court and return the litigation documents and the acknowledgement of service.

<a id="art-207"></a>第二百零七条 邮寄送达的，应当将诉讼文书、送达回证邮寄给收件人。签收日期为送达日期。

**Article 207** : For service by mail, the litigation documents and the acknowledgement of service shall be mailed to the recipient. The date of signing shall be the date of service.

<a id="art-208"></a>第二百零八条 诉讼文书的收件人是军人的，可以通过其所在部队团级以上单位的政治部门转交。  
收件人正在服刑的，可以通过执行机关转交。  
收件人正在接受专门矫治教育等的，可以通过相关机构转交。  
由有关部门、单位代为转交诉讼文书的，应当请有关部门、单位收到后立即交收件人签收，并将送达回证及时寄送人民法院。

**Article 208** : If the recipient of litigation documents is a soldier, the documents may be forwarded through the political department of the unit at or above the regiment level.  
If the recipient is currently serving a sentence, the documents may be forwarded through the enforcement organ.  
If the recipient is currently receiving specialized correctional education or similar, the documents may be forwarded through the relevant institution.  
Where relevant departments or units forward litigation documents on behalf, they shall be requested to immediately deliver the documents to the recipient for signing after receipt and promptly send the acknowledgement of service to the People's Court.

<a id="art-209"></a>第二百零九条 指定管辖案件的审理期限，自被指定管辖的人民法院收到指定管辖决定书和案卷、证据材料之日起计算。

**Article 209** : The trial period for a case with designated jurisdiction shall be calculated from the date on which the People's Court designated to have jurisdiction receives the designation decision and the case files and evidence materials.

<a id="art-210"></a>第二百一十条 对可能判处死刑的案件或者附带民事诉讼的案件，以及有[刑事诉讼法第一百五十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-158)规定情形之一的案件，上一级人民法院可以批准延长审理期限一次，期限为三个月。因特殊情况还需要延长的，应当报请最高人民法院批准。  
申请批准延长审理期限的，应当在期限届满十五日以前层报。有权决定的人民法院不同意的，应当在审理期限届满五日以前作出决定。  
因特殊情况报请最高人民法院批准延长审理期限，最高人民法院经审查，予以批准的，可以延长审理期限一至三个月。期限届满案件仍然不能审结的，可以再次提出申请。

**Article 210** : For a case in which a death sentence may be imposed, or a case with an incidental civil action, or a case with one of the circumstances provided for in Article 158 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the next higher People's Court may approve one extension of the trial period, with the extension period being three months. If an extension is still needed due to special circumstances, the case shall be reported to the Supreme People's Court for approval.  
An application for approval of an extension of the trial period shall be submitted level by level fifteen days before the expiration of the period. If the court with authority to decide does not consent, it shall make a decision within five days before the expiration of the trial period.  
Where a case is reported to the Supreme People's Court for approval of an extension of the trial period due to special circumstances, and the Supreme People's Court, after review, approves it, the trial period may be extended for one to three months. If the case still cannot be concluded upon expiration of the period, an application may be made again.

<a id="art-211"></a>第二百一十一条 审判期间，对被告人作精神病鉴定的时间不计入审理期限。

**Article 211** : During trial, the time for conducting a psychiatric evaluation of the defendant shall not be included in the trial period.

### 第八章 审判组织

<a id="art-212"></a>第二百一十二条 合议庭由审判员担任审判长。院长或者庭长参加审理案件时，由其本人担任审判长。  
审判员依法独任审判时，行使与审判长相同的职权。
