**Article 212** : A collegial panel shall have a judge as the presiding judge. When the president or the division chief participates in the trial of a case, he or she shall serve as the presiding judge.  
When a judge conducts a sole-judge trial according to law, he or she shall exercise the same functions and powers as the presiding judge.

<a id="art-213"></a>第二百一十三条 基层人民法院、中级人民法院、高级人民法院审判下列第一审刑事案件，由审判员和人民陪审员组成合议庭进行：  
（一）涉及群体利益、公共利益的；  
（二）人民群众广泛关注或者其他社会影响较大的；  
（三）案情复杂或者有其他情形，需要由人民陪审员参加审判的。  
基层人民法院、中级人民法院、高级人民法院审判下列第一审刑事案件，由审判员和人民陪审员组成七人合议庭进行：  
（一）可能判处十年以上有期徒刑、无期徒刑、死刑，且社会影响重大的；  
（二）涉及征地拆迁、生态环境保护、食品药品安全，且社会影响重大的；  
（三）其他社会影响重大的。

**Article 213** : The Primary People's Court, the Intermediate People's Court, and the Higher People's Court shall form collegial panels composed of judges and people's assessors to adjudicate the following first-instance criminal cases:  
(1) cases involving group interests or public interests;  
(2) cases widely concerned by the people or otherwise having relatively major social impact;  
(3) cases with complex circumstances or other circumstances requiring the participation of people's assessors in adjudication.  
The Primary People's Court, the Intermediate People's Court, and the Higher People's Court shall form seven-person collegial panels composed of judges and people's assessors to adjudicate the following first-instance criminal cases:  
(1) cases in which a sentence of fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, life imprisonment, or death penalty may be imposed and which have major social impact;  
(2) cases involving land expropriation and demolition, ecological environment protection, food and drug safety, and which have major social impact;  
(3) other cases with major social impact.

<a id="art-214"></a>第二百一十四条 开庭审理和评议案件，应当由同一合议庭进行。合议庭成员在评议案件时，应当独立发表意见并说明理由。意见分歧的，应当按多数意见作出决定，但少数意见应当记入笔录。评议笔录由合议庭的组成人员在审阅确认无误后签名。评议情况应当保密。

**Article 214** : Hearing and deliberation of a case shall be conducted by the same collegial panel. When deliberating on a case, members of the collegial panel shall independently express opinions and state reasons. If opinions differ, a decision shall be made in accordance with the majority opinion, but the minority opinion shall be recorded in the record. The deliberation record shall be signed by the members of the collegial panel after they have reviewed and confirmed it to be correct. The deliberations shall be kept confidential.

<a id="art-215"></a>第二百一十五条 人民陪审员参加三人合议庭审判案件，应当对事实认定、法律适用独立发表意见，行使表决权。  
人民陪审员参加七人合议庭审判案件，应当对事实认定独立发表意见，并与审判员共同表决；对法律适用可以发表意见，但不参加表决。

**Article 215** : When participating in the adjudication of a case in a three-person collegial panel, a people's assessor shall independently express opinions on fact finding and application of law and exercise the right to vote.  
When participating in the adjudication of a case in a seven-person collegial panel, a people's assessor shall independently express opinions on fact finding and vote together with the judges; on application of law, he or she may express opinions but shall not participate in voting.

<a id="art-216"></a>第二百一十六条 合议庭审理、评议后,应当及时作出判决、裁定。  
对下列案件，合议庭应当提请院长决定提交审判委员会讨论决定：  
（一）高级人民法院、中级人民法院拟判处死刑立即执行的案件，以及中级人民法院拟判处死刑缓期执行的案件；  
（二）本院已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定确有错误需要再审的案件；  
（三）人民检察院依照审判监督程序提出抗诉的案件。  
对合议庭成员意见有重大分歧的案件、新类型案件、社会影响重大的案件以及其他疑难、复杂、重大的案件，合议庭认为难以作出决定的，可以提请院长决定提交审判委员会讨论决定。  
人民陪审员可以要求合议庭将案件提请院长决定是否提交审判委员会讨论决定。  
对提请院长决定提交审判委员会讨论决定的案件，院长认为不必要的，可以建议合议庭复议一次。  
独任审判的案件，审判员认为有必要的，也可以提请院长决定提交审判委员会讨论决定。

**Article 216** : After hearing and deliberation, the collegial panel shall promptly render a judgment or ruling.  
For the following cases, the collegial panel shall request the president to decide to submit them to the judicial committee for discussion and decision:  
(1) cases in which a Higher People's Court or an Intermediate People's Court intends to sentence an immediate execution of death penalty, and cases in which an Intermediate People's Court intends to sentence a death sentence with a two-year reprieve;  
(2) cases in which an effective judgment or ruling of this court is indeed erroneous and requires retrial;  
(3) cases in which the People's Procuratorate files a protest in accordance with the trial supervision procedure.  
For cases with major differences of opinion among members of the collegial panel, new-type cases, cases with major social impact, and other difficult, complex, or major cases, if the collegial panel considers it difficult to make a decision, it may request the president to decide to submit them to the judicial committee for discussion and decision.  
A people's assessor may require the collegial panel to submit the case to the president to decide whether to refer it to the judicial committee for discussion and decision.  
For a case requested to be submitted to the president for decision on whether to refer it to the judicial committee for discussion and decision, if the president considers it unnecessary, he or she may suggest that the collegial panel reconsider once.  
For a case tried by a single judge, if the judge considers it necessary, he or she may also request the president to decide to submit the case to the judicial committee for discussion and decision.

<a id="art-217"></a>第二百一十七条 审判委员会的决定，合议庭、独任审判员应当执行；有不同意见的，可以建议院长提交审判委员会复议。

**Article 217** : The collegial panel and the sole judge shall implement the decisions of the judicial committee; if there are different opinions, they may suggest that the president submit the matter to the judicial committee for reconsideration.

### 第九章 公诉案件第一审普通程序

#### 第一节 审查受理与庭前准备

<a id="art-218"></a>第二百一十八条 对提起公诉的案件，人民法院应当在收到起诉书（一式八份，每增加一名被告人，增加起诉书五份）和案卷、证据后，审查以下内容：  
（一）是否属于本院管辖；  
（二）起诉书是否写明被告人的身份，是否受过或者正在接受刑事处罚、行政处罚、处分，被采取留置措施的情况，被采取强制措施的时间、种类、羁押地点，犯罪的时间、地点、手段、后果以及其他可能影响定罪量刑的情节；有多起犯罪事实的，是否在起诉书中将事实分别列明；  
（三）是否移送证明指控犯罪事实及影响量刑的证据材料，包括采取技术调查、侦查措施的法律文书和所收集的证据材料；  
（四）是否查封、扣押、冻结被告人的违法所得或者其他涉案财物，查封、扣押、冻结是否逾期；是否随案移送涉案财物、附涉案财物清单；是否列明涉案财物权属情况；是否就涉案财物处理提供相关证据材料；  
（五）是否列明被害人的姓名、住址、联系方式；是否附有证人、鉴定人名单；是否申请法庭通知证人、鉴定人、有专门知识的人出庭，并列明有关人员的姓名、性别、年龄、职业、住址、联系方式；是否附有需要保护的证人、鉴定人、被害人名单；  
（六）当事人已委托辩护人、诉讼代理人或者已接受法律援助的，是否列明辩护人、诉讼代理人的姓名、住址、联系方式；  
（七）是否提起附带民事诉讼；提起附带民事诉讼的，是否列明附带民事诉讼当事人的姓名、住址、联系方式等，是否附有相关证据材料；  
（八）监察调查、侦查、审查起诉程序的各种法律手续和诉讼文书是否齐全；  
（九）被告人认罪认罚的，是否提出量刑建议、移送认罪认罚具结书等材料；  
（十）有无[刑事诉讼法第十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-16)第二项至第六项规定的不追究刑事责任的情形。

**Article 218** : For a case filed by public prosecution, the People's Court shall, after receiving the indictment (with eight copies, and five additional copies for each additional defendant) and the case files and evidence, examine the following contents:  
(1) whether the case falls within the jurisdiction of this court;  
(2) whether the indictment states the defendant's identity, whether he or she has been or is being subjected to criminal punishment, administrative penalty, or disciplinary action, the circumstances of being subjected to custody measures, the time, type, and place of detention of the compulsory measures taken, the time, place, means, and consequences of the crime, and other circumstances that may affect conviction and sentencing; if there are multiple criminal facts, whether the facts are separately listed in the indictment;  
(3) whether the evidence materials proving the charged criminal facts and affecting sentencing have been transferred, including the legal instruments for taking technical investigation and investigative measures and the evidence materials collected;  
(4) whether the defendant's illegal gains or other property involved in the case have been sealed, seized, or frozen, and whether the sealing, seizure, or freezing has exceeded the time limit; whether the property involved in the case has been transferred with the case and a list of the property involved has been attached; whether the ownership status of the property involved has been stated; whether relevant evidence materials have been provided regarding the disposal of the property involved;  
(5) whether the victim's name, address, and contact information are listed; whether a list of witnesses and expert witnesses is attached; whether an application is made for the court to notify witnesses, expert witnesses, or persons with specialized knowledge to appear in court, and whether the names, genders, ages, occupations, addresses, and contact information of the relevant persons are listed; whether a list of witnesses, expert witnesses, or victims requiring protection is attached;  
(6) if the parties have entrusted defenders or litigation agents or have received legal aid, whether the names, addresses, and contact information of the defenders or litigation agents are listed;  
(7) whether an incidental civil action is initiated; if so, whether the names, addresses, contact information, etc. of the parties to the incidental civil action are listed, and whether relevant evidence materials are attached;  
(8) whether all legal formalities and litigation documents of the supervision investigation, investigation, and review for prosecution procedures are complete;  
(9) if the defendant admits guilt and accepts punishment, whether a sentencing recommendation has been proposed and materials such as the written undertaking to admit guilt and accept punishment have been transferred;  
(10) whether there are circumstances provided for in Items 2 to 6 of Article 16 of the Criminal Procedure Law in which criminal liability shall not be investigated.

<a id="art-219"></a>第二百一十九条 人民法院对提起公诉的案件审查后，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）不属于本院管辖的，应当退回人民检察院；  
（二）属于[刑事诉讼法第十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-16)第二项至第六项规定情形的，应当退回人民检察院；属于告诉才处理的案件，应当同时告知被害人有权提起自诉；  
（三）被告人不在案的，应当退回人民检察院；但是，对人民检察院按照缺席审判程序提起公诉的，应当依照本解释第二十四章的规定作出处理；  
（四）不符合前条第二项至第九项规定之一，需要补充材料的，应当通知人民检察院在三日以内补送；  
（五）依照[刑事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-200)第三项规定宣告被告人无罪后，人民检察院根据新的事实、证据重新起诉的，应当依法受理；  
（六）依照[本解释第二百九十六条](#art-296)规定裁定准许撤诉的案件，没有新的影响定罪量刑的事实、证据，重新起诉的，应当退回人民检察院；  
（七）被告人真实身份不明，但符合[刑事诉讼法第一百六十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-160)第二款规定的，应当依法受理。  
对公诉案件是否受理，应当在七日以内审查完毕。

**Article 219** After reviewing a case prosecuted by the People's Procuratorate, the People's Court shall handle it separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the case is not within the jurisdiction of this court, it shall be returned to the People's Procuratorate;  
(2) If the case falls under the circumstances provided for in Items (2) to (6) of Article 16 of the Criminal Procedure Law, it shall be returned to the People's Procuratorate; if it is a case to be handled only upon complaint, the victim shall simultaneously be informed that he or she has the right to file a private prosecution;  
(3) If the defendant is not present, the case shall be returned to the People's Procuratorate; however, with respect to a case prosecuted by the People's Procuratorate in accordance with the trial in absentia procedure, a decision shall be made in accordance with the provisions of Chapter 24 of this Interpretation;  
(4) If the case does not conform to any of Items (2) to (9) of the preceding Article and supplementary materials are needed, the People's Procuratorate shall be notified to provide them within three days;  
(5) If, after a defendant is pronounced not guilty in accordance with Item (3) of Article 200 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the People's Procuratorate re-institutes a prosecution based on new facts or evidence, the case shall be accepted according to law;  
(6) If a case for which withdrawal of prosecution was permitted by ruling in accordance with Article 296 of this Interpretation is re-prosecuted without new facts or evidence affecting conviction and sentencing, the case shall be returned to the People's Procuratorate;  
(7) If the defendant's true identity is unknown, but the provision of Paragraph 2 of Article 160 of the Criminal Procedure Law is satisfied, the case shall be accepted according to law.  
Whether to accept a public prosecution case shall be reviewed and completed within seven days.

<a id="art-220"></a>第二百二十条 对一案起诉的共同犯罪或者关联犯罪案件，被告人人数众多、案情复杂，人民法院经审查认为，分案审理更有利于保障庭审质量和效率的，可以分案审理。分案审理不得影响当事人质证权等诉讼权利的行使。  
对分案起诉的共同犯罪或者关联犯罪案件，人民法院经审查认为，合并审理更有利于查明案件事实、保障诉讼权利、准确定罪量刑的，可以并案审理。

**Article 220** For a joint crime case or a related crime case prosecuted in one case, if the number of defendants is large and the case is complex, and the People's Court, after examination, considers that separate trial is more conducive to guaranteeing the quality and efficiency of the court hearing, it may try the cases separately. Separate trial shall not affect the exercise of litigation rights such as the parties' right to cross-examine evidence.  
For a joint crime case or a related crime case prosecuted in separate cases, if the People's Court, after examination, considers that consolidated trial is more conducive to ascertaining the facts of the case, safeguarding litigation rights, and accurately determining conviction and sentencing, it may try the cases jointly.

<a id="art-221"></a>第二百二十一条 开庭审理前，人民法院应当进行下列工作：  
（一）确定审判长及合议庭组成人员；  
（二）开庭十日以前将起诉书副本送达被告人、辩护人；  
（三）通知当事人、法定代理人、辩护人、诉讼代理人在开庭五日以前提供证人、鉴定人名单，以及拟当庭出示的证据；申请证人、鉴定人、有专门知识的人出庭的，应当列明有关人员的姓名、性别、年龄、职业、住址、联系方式；  
（四）开庭三日以前将开庭的时间、地点通知人民检察院；  
（五）开庭三日以前将传唤当事人的传票和通知辩护人、诉讼代理人、法定代理人、证人、鉴定人等出庭的通知书送达；通知有关人员出庭，也可以采取电话、短信、传真、电子邮件、即时通讯等能够确认对方收悉的方式；对被害人人数众多的涉众型犯罪案件，可以通过互联网公布相关文书，通知有关人员出庭；  
（六）公开审理的案件，在开庭三日以前公布案由、被告人姓名、开庭时间和地点。  
上述工作情况应当记录在案。

**Article 221** Before the court session, the People's Court shall carry out the following work:  
(1) Determine the presiding judge and the members of the collegial panel;  
(2) Serve a copy of the bill of indictment on the defendant and the defender ten days prior to the court session;  
(3) Notify the parties, legal representatives, defenders, and litigation agents to provide, five days prior to the court session, the list of witnesses and expert witnesses, and the evidence intended to be produced in court; where an application is made for witnesses, expert witnesses, or persons with specialized knowledge to appear in court, the names, genders, ages, occupations, addresses, and contact information of the relevant persons shall be listed;  
(4) Notify the People's Procuratorate of the time and place of the court session three days prior to the court session;  
(5) Serve, three days prior to the court session, the summons to summon the parties and the notices to notify the defenders, litigation agents, legal representatives, witnesses, expert witnesses, and other persons to appear in court; the notice to relevant persons to appear in court may also be made by telephone, short message, fax, email, instant messaging, or other means that can confirm receipt; for crimes involving a large number of victims, the relevant documents may be published on the Internet to notify relevant persons to appear in court;  
(6) For a case heard in public, announce the cause of action, the defendant's name, and the time and place of the court session three days prior to the court session.  
The above work shall be recorded in the case file.

<a id="art-222"></a>第二百二十二条 审判案件应当公开进行。  
案件涉及国家秘密或者个人隐私的，不公开审理；涉及商业秘密，当事人提出申请的，法庭可以决定不公开审理。  
不公开审理的案件，任何人不得旁听，但具有[刑事诉讼法第二百八十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-285)规定情形的除外。

**Article 222** Cases shall be tried in public.  
If a case involves State secrets or personal privacy, it shall not be heard in public; if it involves trade secrets and a party applies, the court may decide not to hear the case in public.  
No person shall attend the hearing of a case not heard in public, except under the circumstances provided for in Article 285 of the Criminal Procedure Law.

<a id="art-223"></a>第二百二十三条 精神病人、醉酒的人、未经人民法院批准的未成年人以及其他不宜旁听的人不得旁听案件审理。

**Article 223** Mentally ill persons, intoxicated persons, minors who have not been approved by the People's Court, and other persons who are not suitable to attend a hearing shall not attend the trial of a case.

<a id="art-224"></a>第二百二十四条 被害人人数众多，且案件不属于附带民事诉讼范围的，被害人可以推选若干代表人参加庭审。

**Article 224** If there are a large number of victims and the case is not within the scope of incidental civil action, the victims may elect several representatives to participate in the court proceedings.

<a id="art-225"></a>第二百二十五条 被害人、诉讼代理人经传唤或者通知未到庭，不影响开庭审理的，人民法院可以开庭审理。  
辩护人经通知未到庭，被告人同意的，人民法院可以开庭审理，但被告人属于应当提供法律援助情形的除外。

**Article 225** If the victim or the litigation agent fails to appear in court after being summoned or notified, and this does not affect the holding of the court session, the People's Court may proceed with the court session.  
If the defender fails to appear after being notified, and the defendant agrees, the People's Court may proceed with the court session, except where the defendant falls under the circumstances for which legal aid shall be provided.

#### 第二节 庭前会议与庭审衔接

<a id="art-226"></a>第二百二十六条 案件具有下列情形之一的，人民法院可以决定召开庭前会议：  
（一）证据材料较多、案情重大复杂的；  
（二）控辩双方对事实、证据存在较大争议的；  
（三）社会影响重大的；  
（四）需要召开庭前会议的其他情形。

**Article 226** Under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court may decide to convene a pre-trial conference:  
(1) The evidentiary materials are numerous and the case is major and complex;  
(2) The prosecution and the defense have major disputes over the facts and evidence;  
(3) The case has significant social impact;  
(4) Other circumstances requiring the convening of a pre-trial conference.

<a id="art-227"></a>第二百二十七条 控辩双方可以申请人民法院召开庭前会议，提出申请应当说明理由。人民法院经审查认为有必要的，应当召开庭前会议；决定不召开的，应当告知申请人。

**Article 227** The prosecution and the defense may apply to the People's Court for convening a pre-trial conference, and an application shall state the reasons. If the People's Court, after examination, considers it necessary, it shall convene a pre-trial conference; if it decides not to convene one, it shall notify the applicant.

<a id="art-228"></a>第二百二十八条 庭前会议可以就下列事项向控辩双方了解情况，听取意见：  
（一）是否对案件管辖有异议；  
（二）是否申请有关人员回避；  
（三）是否申请不公开审理；  
（四）是否申请排除非法证据；  
（五）是否提供新的证据材料；  
（六）是否申请重新鉴定或者勘验；  
（七）是否申请收集、调取证明被告人无罪或者罪轻的证据材料；  
（八）是否申请证人、鉴定人、有专门知识的人、调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员出庭，是否对出庭人员名单有异议；  
（九）是否对涉案财物的权属情况和人民检察院的处理建议有异议；  
（十）与审判相关的其他问题。  
庭前会议中，人民法院可以开展附带民事调解。  
对第一款规定中可能导致庭审中断的程序性事项，人民法院可以在庭前会议后依法作出处理，并在庭审中说明处理决定和理由。控辩双方没有新的理由，在庭审中再次提出有关申请或者异议的，法庭可以在说明庭前会议情况和处理决定理由后，依法予以驳回。  
庭前会议情况应当制作笔录，由参会人员核对后签名。

**Article 228** At a pre-trial conference, the People's Court may ascertain the circumstances and hear opinions from the prosecution and the defense on the following matters:  
(1) Whether there is any objection to the jurisdiction of the case;  
(2) Whether to apply for the withdrawal of relevant personnel;  
(3) Whether to apply for a non-public hearing;  
(4) Whether to apply for the exclusion of illegally obtained evidence;  
(5) Whether to provide new evidentiary materials;  
(6) Whether to apply for a new expert evaluation or inspection;  
(7) Whether to apply for the collection or obtaining of evidentiary materials proving the defendant's innocence or lesser guilt;  
(8) Whether to apply for witnesses, expert witnesses, persons with specialized knowledge, investigators, investigation personnel, or other persons to appear in court, and whether there is any objection to the list of persons appearing in court;  
(9) Whether there is any objection to the ownership of the property involved in the case and to the handling suggestions of the People's Procuratorate;  
(10) Other issues related to the trial.  
During the pre-trial conference, the People's Court may conduct mediation of the incidental civil action.  
With respect to the procedural matters specified in Paragraph 1 that may cause interruption of the trial, the People's Court may, after the pre-trial conference, handle them according to law and explain the handling decisions and reasons during the trial. If the prosecution or the defense, without new reasons, again raises relevant applications or objections during the trial, the court may, after explaining the circumstances of the pre-trial conference and the reasons for the handling decision, reject them according to law.  
The circumstances of the pre-trial conference shall be recorded in written minutes, which shall be checked and signed by the participants.

<a id="art-229"></a>第二百二十九条 庭前会议中，审判人员可以询问控辩双方对证据材料有无异议，对有异议的证据，应当在庭审时重点调查；无异议的，庭审时举证、质证可以简化。

**Article 229** At the pre-trial conference, the judicial personnel may inquire of the prosecution and the defense whether they have any objection to the evidentiary materials. Evidence to which there is an objection shall be examined emphatically during the trial; where there is no objection, the presentation and examination of evidence during the trial may be simplified.

<a id="art-230"></a>第二百三十条 庭前会议由审判长主持，合议庭其他审判员也可以主持庭前会议。  
召开庭前会议应当通知公诉人、辩护人到场。  
庭前会议准备就非法证据排除了解情况、听取意见，或者准备询问控辩双方对证据材料的意见的，应当通知被告人到场。有多名被告人的案件，可以根据情况确定参加庭前会议的被告人。

**Article 230** A pre-trial conference shall be presided over by the presiding judge; other judges of the collegial panel may also preside over the pre-trial conference.  
To convene a pre-trial conference, the public prosecutor and the defender shall be notified to be present.  
If the pre-trial conference is prepared to ascertain the circumstances and hear opinions regarding the exclusion of illegally obtained evidence, or to inquire into the opinions of the prosecution and the defense on the evidentiary materials, the defendant shall be notified to be present. In a case with multiple defendants, the defendants participating in the pre-trial conference may be determined according to the circumstances.

<a id="art-231"></a>第二百三十一条 庭前会议一般不公开进行。  
根据案件情况，庭前会议可以采用视频等方式进行。

**Article 231** A pre-trial conference shall generally not be held in public.  
Depending on the circumstances of the case, a pre-trial conference may be held by video or other means.

<a id="art-232"></a>第二百三十二条 人民法院在庭前会议中听取控辩双方对案件事实、证据材料的意见后，对明显事实不清、证据不足的案件，可以建议人民检察院补充材料或者撤回起诉。建议撤回起诉的案件，人民检察院不同意的，开庭审理后，没有新的事实和理由，一般不准许撤回起诉。

**Article 232** After hearing, at the pre-trial conference, the opinions of the prosecution and the defense on the facts and evidentiary materials of the case, the People's Court may, for a case with clearly unclear facts and insufficient evidence, suggest that the People's Procuratorate supplement materials or withdraw the prosecution. For a case for which withdrawal of prosecution is suggested, if the People's Procuratorate does not agree, after the trial begins, withdrawal of prosecution shall generally not be permitted without new facts or reasons.

<a id="art-233"></a>第二百三十三条 对召开庭前会议的案件，可以在开庭时告知庭前会议情况。对庭前会议中达成一致意见的事项，法庭在向控辩双方核实后，可以当庭予以确认；未达成一致意见的事项，法庭可以归纳控辩双方争议焦点，听取控辩双方意见，依法作出处理。  
控辩双方在庭前会议中就有关事项达成一致意见，在庭审中反悔的，除有正当理由外，法庭一般不再进行处理。

**Article 233** For a case in which a pre-trial conference has been convened, the circumstances of the pre-trial conference may be informed at the time of trial. With respect to matters on which an agreement has been reached at the pre-trial conference, the court may, after verifying with the prosecution and the defense, confirm them in court; with respect to matters on which no agreement has been reached, the court may summarize the disputed issues between the prosecution and the defense, hear their opinions, and handle them according to law.  
If the prosecution or the defense, having reached an agreement on relevant matters at the pre-trial conference, reneges during the trial, the court shall generally no longer handle it, except for justified reasons.

#### 第三节 宣布开庭与法庭调查

<a id="art-234"></a>第二百三十四条 开庭审理前，书记员应当依次进行下列工作：  
（一）受审判长委托，查明公诉人、当事人、辩护人、诉讼代理人、证人及其他诉讼参与人是否到庭；  
（二）核实旁听人员中是否有证人、鉴定人、有专门知识的人；  
（三）请公诉人、辩护人、诉讼代理人及其他诉讼参与人入庭；  
（四）宣读法庭规则；  
（五）请审判长、审判员、人民陪审员入庭；  
（六）审判人员就座后，向审判长报告开庭前的准备工作已经就绪。

**Article 234** Before the court session, the court clerk shall, in order, carry out the following work:  
(1) Upon the entrustment of the presiding judge, verify whether the public prosecutor, parties, defenders, litigation agents, witnesses, and other participants in the proceedings are present;  
(2) Verify whether any of the persons attending the hearing are witnesses, expert witnesses, or persons with specialized knowledge;  
(3) Invite the public prosecutor, defenders, litigation agents, and other participants in the proceedings to enter the courtroom;  
(4) Read out the courtroom rules;  
(5) Invite the presiding judge, judges, and people's assessors to enter the courtroom;  
(6) After the judicial personnel are seated, report to the presiding judge that the preparations before the trial have been completed.

<a id="art-235"></a>第二百三十五条 审判长宣布开庭，传被告人到庭后，应当查明被告人的下列情况：  
（一）姓名、出生日期、民族、出生地、文化程度、职业、住址，或者被告单位的名称、住所地、法定代表人、实际控制人以及诉讼代表人的姓名、职务；  
（二）是否受过刑事处罚、行政处罚、处分及其种类、时间；  
（三）是否被采取留置措施及留置的时间，是否被采取强制措施及强制措施的种类、时间；  
（四）收到起诉书副本的日期；有附带民事诉讼的，附带民事诉讼被告人收到附带民事起诉状的日期。  
被告人较多的，可以在开庭前查明上述情况，但开庭时审判长应当作出说明。

**Article 235** After the presiding judge announces the opening of the court session and summons the defendant into court, the presiding judge shall verify the following circumstances of the defendant:  
(1) Name, date of birth, ethnicity, place of birth, educational level, occupation, and address, or, for a defendant unit, its name, domicile, legal representative, actual controller, and the name and position of its litigation representative;  
(2) Whether the defendant has previously received criminal punishment, administrative penalty, or disciplinary sanction, and the types and times thereof;  
(3) Whether custody has been taken and the time of custody, whether compulsory measures have been taken and the types and times of the compulsory measures;  
(4) The date of receipt of the copy of the bill of indictment; if there is an incidental civil action, the date on which the defendant in the incidental civil action received the incidental civil complaint.  
If there are multiple defendants, the above circumstances may be verified before the court session, but the presiding judge shall make an explanation at the time of trial.

<a id="art-236"></a>第二百三十六条 审判长宣布案件的来源、起诉的案由、附带民事诉讼当事人的姓名及是否公开审理；不公开审理的，应当宣布理由。

**Article 236** The presiding judge shall announce the source of the case, the cause of action of the prosecution, the names of the parties to the incidental civil action, and whether the case is to be heard in public; if the case is not to be heard in public, the reasons shall be announced.

<a id="art-237"></a>第二百三十七条 审判长宣布合议庭组成人员、法官助理、书记员、公诉人的名单，以及辩护人、诉讼代理人、鉴定人、翻译人员等诉讼参与人的名单。

**Article 237** The presiding judge shall announce the list of the members of the collegial panel, judge assistants, court clerk, and public prosecutors, as well as the list of defenders, litigation agents, expert witnesses, interpreters, and other participants in the proceedings.

<a id="art-238"></a>第二百三十八条 审判长应当告知当事人及其法定代理人、辩护人、诉讼代理人在法庭审理过程中依法享有下列诉讼权利：  
（一）可以申请合议庭组成人员、法官助理、书记员、公诉人、鉴定人和翻译人员回避；  
（二）可以提出证据，申请通知新的证人到庭、调取新的证据，申请重新鉴定或者勘验；  
（三）被告人可以自行辩护；  
（四）被告人可以在法庭辩论终结后作最后陈述。

**Article 238** The presiding judge shall inform the parties, their legal representatives, defenders, and litigation agents that, during the court proceedings, they enjoy the following litigation rights according to law:  
(1) They may apply for the withdrawal of the members of the collegial panel, judge assistants, court clerk, public prosecutors, expert witnesses, and interpreters;  
(2) They may present evidence, apply for the notification of new witnesses to appear in court, apply for the obtaining of new evidence, and apply for a new expert evaluation or inspection;  
(3) The defendant may defend himself or herself;  
(4) The defendant may make a final statement after the conclusion of the court debate.

<a id="art-239"></a>第二百三十九条 审判长应当询问当事人及其法定代理人、辩护人、诉讼代理人是否申请回避、申请何人回避和申请回避的理由。  
当事人及其法定代理人、辩护人、诉讼代理人申请回避的，依照刑事诉讼法及本解释的有关规定处理。  
同意或者驳回回避申请的决定及复议决定，由审判长宣布，并说明理由。必要时，也可以由院长到庭宣布。

**Article 239** The presiding judge shall inquire of the parties, their legal representatives, defenders, and litigation agents whether they apply for withdrawal, against whom they apply for withdrawal, and the reasons for the application for withdrawal.  
If the parties, their legal representatives, defenders, or litigation agents apply for withdrawal, the matter shall be handled in accordance with the relevant provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law and this Interpretation.  
The decisions to agree to or reject an application for withdrawal and the decision on reconsideration shall be announced by the presiding judge, with reasons stated. When necessary, the president of the court may also announce them in court.

<a id="art-240"></a>第二百四十条 审判长宣布法庭调查开始后，应当先由公诉人宣读起诉书；公诉人宣读起诉书后，审判长应当询问被告人对起诉书指控的犯罪事实和罪名有无异议。  
有附带民事诉讼的，公诉人宣读起诉书后，由附带民事诉讼原告人或者其法定代理人、诉讼代理人宣读附带民事起诉状。

**Article 240** After the presiding judge announces the beginning of the court investigation, the public prosecutor shall first read out the bill of indictment; after the public prosecutor reads out the bill of indictment, the presiding judge shall inquire of the defendant whether he or she has any objection to the criminal facts and charges alleged in the bill of indictment.  
If there is an incidental civil action, after the public prosecutor reads out the bill of indictment, the plaintiff in the incidental civil action or his or her legal representative or litigation agent shall read out the incidental civil complaint.

<a id="art-241"></a>第二百四十一条 在审判长主持下，被告人、被害人可以就起诉书指控的犯罪事实分别陈述。

**Article 241** Under the direction of the presiding judge, the defendant and the victim may respectively make statements regarding the criminal facts alleged in the bill of indictment.

<a id="art-242"></a>第二百四十二条 在审判长主持下，公诉人可以就起诉书指控的犯罪事实讯问被告人。  
经审判长准许，被害人及其法定代理人、诉讼代理人可以就公诉人讯问的犯罪事实补充发问；附带民事诉讼原告人及其法定代理人、诉讼代理人可以就附带民事部分的事实向被告人发问；被告人的法定代理人、辩护人，附带民事诉讼被告人及其法定代理人、诉讼代理人可以在控诉方、附带民事诉讼原告方就某一问题讯问、发问完毕后向被告人发问。  
根据案件情况，就证据问题对被告人的讯问、发问可以在举证、质证环节进行。

**Article 242** Under the direction of the presiding judge, the public prosecutor may interrogate the defendant about the criminal facts alleged in the bill of indictment.  
With the permission of the presiding judge, the victim and his or her legal representative or litigation agent may make supplementary questioning about the criminal facts interrogated by the public prosecutor; the plaintiff in the incidental civil action and his or her legal representative or litigation agent may question the defendant about the facts of the incidental civil part; the defendant's legal representative or defender, and the defendant in the incidental civil action and his or her legal representative or litigation agent may question the defendant after the prosecuting party and the plaintiff party in the incidental civil action have finished interrogating and questioning on a particular issue.  
According to the circumstances of the case, the interrogation and questioning of the defendant on evidentiary issues may be conducted during the stage of presenting and examining evidence.

<a id="art-243"></a>第二百四十三条 讯问同案审理的被告人，应当分别进行。

**Article 243** The interrogation of co-defendants tried in the same case shall be conducted separately.

<a id="art-244"></a>第二百四十四条 经审判长准许，控辩双方可以向被害人、附带民事诉讼原告人发问。

**Article 244** With the permission of the presiding judge, the prosecution and the defense may question the victim and the plaintiff in the incidental civil action.

<a id="art-245"></a>第二百四十五条 必要时，审判人员可以讯问被告人，也可以向被害人、附带民事诉讼当事人发问。

**Article 245** When necessary, the judicial personnel may interrogate the defendant and may also question the victim or the parties to the incidental civil action.

<a id="art-246"></a>第二百四十六条 公诉人可以提请法庭通知证人、鉴定人、有专门知识的人、调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员出庭，或者出示证据。被害人及其法定代理人、诉讼代理人，附带民事诉讼原告人及其诉讼代理人也可以提出申请。  
在控诉方举证后，被告人及其法定代理人、辩护人可以提请法庭通知证人、鉴定人、有专门知识的人、调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员出庭，或者出示证据。

**Article 246** The public prosecutor may request the court to notify witnesses, expert witnesses, persons with specialized knowledge, investigators, investigation personnel, or other persons to appear in court, or to present evidence. The victim and his or her legal representative or litigation agent, and the plaintiff in the incidental civil action and his or her litigation agent may also make applications.  
After the prosecuting party has presented evidence, the defendant and his or her legal representative or defender may request the court to notify witnesses, expert witnesses, persons with specialized knowledge, investigators, investigation personnel, or other persons to appear in court, or to present evidence.

<a id="art-247"></a>第二百四十七条 控辩双方申请证人出庭作证，出示证据，应当说明证据的名称、来源和拟证明的事实。法庭认为有必要的，应当准许；对方提出异议，认为有关证据与案件无关或者明显重复、不必要，法庭经审查异议成立的，可以不予准许。

**Article 247** Where the prosecution or the defense applies for a witness to appear in court to testify or to produce evidence, it shall state the name, source, and facts intended to be proved of the evidence. If the court considers it necessary, it shall grant the application; if the opposing party raises an objection, considering that the relevant evidence is irrelevant to the case or is clearly duplicative or unnecessary, and the court, after examination, finds the objection to be established, it may refuse to grant the application.

<a id="art-248"></a>第二百四十八条 已经移送人民法院的案卷和证据材料，控辩双方需要出示的，可以向法庭提出申请，法庭可以准许。案卷和证据材料应当在质证后当庭归还。  
需要播放录音录像或者需要将证据材料交由法庭、公诉人或者诉讼参与人查看的，法庭可以指令值庭法警或者相关人员予以协助。

**Article 248** If the prosecution or the defense needs to present the case files and evidentiary materials that have already been transferred to the People's Court, it may apply to the court, and the court may grant the application. The case files and evidentiary materials shall be returned in court after examination.  
If it is necessary to play audio or video recordings or to make evidentiary materials available for inspection by the court, the public prosecutor, or participants in the proceedings, the court may instruct the judicial police officer on duty or relevant personnel to provide assistance.

<a id="art-249"></a>第二百四十九条 公诉人、当事人或者辩护人、诉讼代理人对证人证言有异议，且该证人证言对定罪量刑有重大影响，或者对鉴定意见有异议，人民法院认为证人、鉴定人有必要出庭作证的，应当通知证人、鉴定人出庭。  
控辩双方对侦破经过、证据来源、证据真实性或者合法性等有异议，申请调查人员、侦查人员或者有关人员出庭，人民法院认为有必要的，应当通知调查人员、侦查人员或者有关人员出庭。

**Article 249** If the public prosecutor, a party, or a defender or litigation agent has an objection to witness testimony, and the witness testimony has a major impact on conviction and sentencing, or has an objection to an expert evaluation opinion, and the People's Court considers it necessary for the witness or expert witness to appear in court to testify, the People's Court shall notify the witness or expert witness to appear in court.  
If the prosecution or the defense has any objection to the process of case solving, the source of evidence, the authenticity or legality of evidence, or other matters, and applies for investigators, investigation personnel, or relevant persons to appear in court, the People's Court shall, if it considers it necessary, notify the investigators, investigation personnel, or relevant persons to appear in court.

<a id="art-250"></a>第二百五十条 公诉人、当事人及其辩护人、诉讼代理人申请法庭通知有专门知识的人出庭，就鉴定意见提出意见的，应当说明理由。法庭认为有必要的，应当通知有专门知识的人出庭。  
申请有专门知识的人出庭，不得超过二人。有多种类鉴定意见的，可以相应增加人数。

**Article 250** Where the public prosecutor, a party, or his or her defender or litigation agent applies to the court to notify a person with specialized knowledge to appear in court to give opinions on an expert evaluation opinion, the reasons shall be stated. If the court considers it necessary, it shall notify the person with specialized knowledge to appear in court.  
The number of persons with specialized knowledge applying to appear in court shall not exceed two. Where there are multiple types of expert evaluation opinions, the number may be correspondingly increased.

<a id="art-251"></a>第二百五十一条 为查明案件事实、调查核实证据，人民法院可以依职权通知证人、鉴定人、有专门知识的人、调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员出庭。

**Article 251** For the purpose of ascertaining the facts of the case and investigating and verifying evidence, the People's Court may, on its own authority, notify witnesses, expert witnesses, persons with specialized knowledge, investigators, investigation personnel, or other persons to appear in court.

<a id="art-252"></a>第二百五十二条 人民法院通知有关人员出庭的，可以要求控辩双方予以协助。

**Article 252** Where the People's Court notifies relevant persons to appear in court, it may request the prosecution and the defense to provide assistance.

<a id="art-253"></a>第二百五十三条 证人具有下列情形之一，无法出庭作证的，人民法院可以准许其不出庭：  
（一）庭审期间身患严重疾病或者行动极为不便的；  
（二）居所远离开庭地点且交通极为不便的；  
（三）身处国外短期无法回国的；  
（四）有其他客观原因，确实无法出庭的。  
具有前款规定情形的，可以通过视频等方式作证。

**Article 253** If a witness is unable to appear in court to testify under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court may permit him or her not to appear in court:  
(1) He or she suffers from a serious illness or has great difficulty in moving during the period of the trial;  
(2) His or her residence is far from the place of hearing and transportation is extremely inconvenient;  
(3) He or she is abroad and cannot return to the country within a short period;  
(4) There are other objective reasons that truly make it impossible to appear in court.  
Under the circumstances provided in the preceding paragraph, testimony may be given by video or other means.

<a id="art-254"></a>第二百五十四条 证人出庭作证所支出的交通、住宿、就餐等费用，人民法院应当给予补助。

**Article 254** The People's Court shall provide subsidies for the expenses such as transportation, accommodation, and meals incurred by a witness for appearing in court to testify.

<a id="art-255"></a>第二百五十五条 强制证人出庭的，应当由院长签发强制证人出庭令，由法警执行。必要时，可以商请公安机关协助。

**Article 255** Where a witness is compelled to appear in court, an order for compelling the witness to appear in court shall be issued by the president of the court and executed by judicial police. When necessary, the public security organ may be consulted to provide assistance.

<a id="art-256"></a>第二百五十六条 证人、鉴定人、被害人因出庭作证，本人或者其近亲属的人身安全面临危险的，人民法院应当采取不公开其真实姓名、住址和工作单位等个人信息，或者不暴露其外貌、真实声音等保护措施。辩护律师经法庭许可，查阅对证人、鉴定人、被害人使用化名情况的，应当签署保密承诺书。  
审判期间，证人、鉴定人、被害人提出保护请求的，人民法院应当立即审查；认为确有保护必要的，应当及时决定采取相应保护措施。必要时，可以商请公安机关协助。

**Article 256** If a witness, expert witness, or victim faces danger to the personal safety of himself or herself or his or her close relatives due to appearing in court to testify, the People's Court shall adopt protective measures such as not disclosing his or her true name, address, work unit, and other personal information, or not exposing his or her appearance, true voice, etc. If a defense lawyer, with the permission of the court, inspects the use of pseudonyms for witnesses, expert witnesses, or victims, he or she shall sign a confidentiality undertaking.  
During the period of trial, if a witness, expert witness, or victim makes a request for protection, the People's Court shall immediately examine it; if it considers that protection is indeed necessary, it shall promptly decide to adopt corresponding protective measures. When necessary, it may consult the public security organ to provide assistance.

<a id="art-257"></a>第二百五十七条 决定对出庭作证的证人、鉴定人、被害人采取不公开个人信息的保护措施的，审判人员应当在开庭前核实其身份，对证人、鉴定人如实作证的保证书不得公开，在判决书、裁定书等法律文书中可以使用化名等代替其个人信息。

**Article 257** Where a decision is made to adopt protective measures of not disclosing personal information for a witness, expert witness, or victim appearing in court to testify, the judicial personnel shall verify his or her identity before the court session. The written assurance of truthful testimony given by a witness or expert witness shall not be made public, and pseudonyms may be used in judgments, rulings, and other legal documents to replace his or her personal information.

<a id="art-258"></a>第二百五十八条 证人出庭的，法庭应当核实其身份、与当事人以及本案的关系，并告知其有关权利义务和法律责任。证人应当保证向法庭如实提供证言，并在保证书上签名。

**Article 258** Where a witness appears in court, the court shall verify his or her identity and his or her relationship with the parties and with the case, and inform him or her of the relevant rights, obligations, and legal liability. The witness shall guarantee to provide testimony truthfully to the court and sign the written assurance.

<a id="art-259"></a>第二百五十九条 证人出庭后，一般先向法庭陈述证言；其后，经审判长许可，由申请通知证人出庭的一方发问，发问完毕后，对方也可以发问。  
法庭依职权通知证人出庭的，发问顺序由审判长根据案件情况确定。

**Article 259** After a witness appears in court, he or she shall generally first state his or her testimony to the court; thereafter, with the permission of the presiding judge, the party that applied to have the witness notified to appear in court shall ask questions, and after the questioning is completed, the opposing party may also ask questions.  
Where the court notifies a witness to appear in court on its own authority, the order of questioning shall be determined by the presiding judge according to the circumstances of the case.

<a id="art-260"></a>第二百六十条 鉴定人、有专门知识的人、调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员出庭的，参照适用前两条规定。

**Article 260** Where an expert witness, a person with specialized knowledge, an investigator, investigation personnel, or other person appears in court, the provisions of the preceding two Articles shall apply mutatis mutandis.

<a id="art-261"></a>第二百六十一条 向证人发问应当遵循以下规则：  
（一）发问的内容应当与本案事实有关；  
（二）不得以诱导方式发问；  
（三）不得威胁证人；  
（四）不得损害证人的人格尊严。  
对被告人、被害人、附带民事诉讼当事人、鉴定人、有专门知识的人、调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员的讯问、发问，适用前款规定。

**Article 261** Questioning of witnesses shall follow the following rules:  
(1) The content of the questioning shall be related to the facts of the case;  
(2) Questioning shall not be conducted in a leading manner;  
(3) Witnesses shall not be threatened;  
(4) The personal dignity of witnesses shall not be impaired.  
The provisions of the preceding paragraph apply to the interrogation and questioning of defendants, victims, parties to incidental civil actions, expert witnesses, persons with specialized knowledge, investigators, investigation personnel, or other persons.

<a id="art-262"></a>第二百六十二条 控辩双方的讯问、发问方式不当或者内容与本案无关的，对方可以提出异议，申请审判长制止，审判长应当判明情况予以支持或者驳回；对方未提出异议的，审判长也可以根据情况予以制止。

**Article 262** Where the manner of interrogation or questioning by the prosecution or the defense is improper or the content is irrelevant to the case, the opposing party may raise an objection and apply to the presiding judge to stop it, and the presiding judge shall ascertain the circumstances and sustain or reject the objection; where the opposing party does not raise an objection, the presiding judge may also stop it according to the circumstances.

<a id="art-263"></a>第二百六十三条 审判人员认为必要时，可以询问证人、鉴定人、有专门知识的人、调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员。

**Article 263** Where the judicial personnel consider it necessary, they may question witnesses, expert witnesses, persons with specialized knowledge, investigators, investigation personnel, or other persons.

<a id="art-264"></a>第二百六十四条 向证人、调查人员、侦查人员发问应当分别进行。

**Article 264** Questioning of witnesses, investigators, and investigation personnel shall be conducted separately.

<a id="art-265"></a>第二百六十五条 证人、鉴定人、有专门知识的人、调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员不得旁听对本案的审理。有关人员作证或者发表意见后，审判长应当告知其退庭。

**Article 265** Witnesses, expert witnesses, persons with specialized knowledge, investigators, investigation personnel, or other persons shall not attend the hearing of the trial of the case. After the relevant person has testified or expressed opinions, the presiding judge shall inform him or her to withdraw from the courtroom.

<a id="art-266"></a>第二百六十六条 审理涉及未成年人的刑事案件，询问未成年被害人、证人，通知未成年被害人、证人出庭作证，适用本解释第二十二章的有关规定。

**Article 266** In the trial of criminal cases involving minors, the provisions of Chapter 22 of this Interpretation shall apply to the questioning of minor victims and witnesses and to the notification of minor victims and witnesses to appear in court to testify.

<a id="art-267"></a>第二百六十七条 举证方当庭出示证据后，由对方发表质证意见。

**Article 267** After the party presenting evidence produces the evidence in court, the opposing party shall give opinions on the examination of the evidence.

<a id="art-268"></a>第二百六十八条 对可能影响定罪量刑的关键证据和控辩双方存在争议的证据，一般应当单独举证、质证，充分听取质证意见。  
对控辩双方无异议的非关键证据，举证方可以仅就证据的名称及拟证明的事实作出说明。  
召开庭前会议的案件，举证、质证可以按照庭前会议确定的方式进行。  
根据案件和庭审情况，法庭可以对控辩双方的举证、质证方式进行必要的指引。

**Article 268** : For key evidence that may affect conviction and sentencing and evidence in dispute between the prosecution and the defense, presentation and cross-examination shall generally be conducted separately, and opinions on cross-examination shall be fully heard.  
For non-key evidence against which the prosecution and the defense have no objection, the party presenting the evidence may only explain the name of the evidence and the facts intended to be proven.  
For cases where a pre-trial meeting is held, evidence presentation and cross-examination may be conducted in the manner determined at the pre-trial meeting.  
Based on the circumstances of the case and the court session, the court may provide necessary guidance on the manner of evidence presentation and cross-examination by the prosecution and the defense.

<a id="art-269"></a>第二百六十九条 审理过程中，法庭认为有必要的，可以传唤同案被告人、分案审理的共同犯罪或者关联犯罪案件的被告人等到庭对质。

**Article 269** : During the trial, if the court deems it necessary, it may summon co-defendants of the same case, defendants of joint crimes or related crimes tried in separate cases, among others, to appear in court for confrontation.

<a id="art-270"></a>第二百七十条 当庭出示的证据，尚未移送人民法院的，应当在质证后当庭移交。

**Article 270** : Evidence presented in court that has not yet been transferred to the People's Court shall be handed over in court after cross-examination.

<a id="art-271"></a>第二百七十一条 法庭对证据有疑问的，可以告知公诉人、当事人及其法定代理人、辩护人、诉讼代理人补充证据或者作出说明；必要时，可以宣布休庭，对证据进行调查核实。  
对公诉人、当事人及其法定代理人、辩护人、诉讼代理人补充的和审判人员庭外调查核实取得的证据，应当经过当庭质证才能作为定案的根据。但是，对不影响定罪量刑的非关键证据、有利于被告人的量刑证据以及认定被告人有犯罪前科的裁判文书等证据，经庭外征求意见，控辩双方没有异议的除外。  
有关情况，应当记录在案。

**Article 271** : Where the court has doubts about evidence, it may inform the public prosecutor, the parties and their legal agents, defenders, and litigation agents to supplement evidence or provide explanations; where necessary, it may announce an adjournment to investigate and verify the evidence.  
Evidence supplemented by the public prosecutor, parties and their legal agents, defenders, and litigation agents, and evidence obtained by judicial personnel through out-of-court investigation and verification, shall be subject to in-court cross-examination before it may serve as the basis for deciding the case. However, this does not apply to non-key evidence that does not affect conviction and sentencing, sentencing evidence favorable to the defendant, and judicial documents determining that the defendant has a prior criminal record, where the prosecution and the defense have no objection after opinions are sought outside court.  
The relevant circumstances shall be recorded in the case file.

<a id="art-272"></a>第二百七十二条 公诉人申请出示开庭前未移送或者提交人民法院的证据，辩护方提出异议的，审判长应当要求公诉人说明理由；理由成立并确有出示必要的，应当准许。  
辩护方提出需要对新的证据作辩护准备的，法庭可以宣布休庭，并确定准备辩护的时间。  
辩护方申请出示开庭前未提交的证据，参照适用前两款规定。

**Article 272** : Where the public prosecutor applies to present evidence that was not transferred or submitted to the People's Court before the court session, and the defense raises an objection, the presiding judge shall require the public prosecutor to state the reasons; if the reasons are established and it is indeed necessary to present the evidence, permission shall be granted.  
Where the defense states that it needs to prepare a defense against new evidence, the court may announce an adjournment and determine the time for defense preparation.  
Where the defense applies to present evidence not submitted before the court session, the preceding two paragraphs shall apply by reference.

<a id="art-273"></a>第二百七十三条 法庭审理过程中，控辩双方申请通知新的证人到庭，调取新的证据，申请重新鉴定或者勘验的，应当提供证人的基本信息、证据的存放地点，说明拟证明的事项，申请重新鉴定或者勘验的理由。法庭认为有必要的，应当同意，并宣布休庭；根据案件情况，可以决定延期审理。  
人民法院决定重新鉴定的，应当及时委托鉴定，并将鉴定意见告知人民检察院、当事人及其辩护人、诉讼代理人。

**Article 273** : During the court trial, if the prosecution or the defense applies to summon new witnesses to appear in court, to obtain new evidence, or to apply for a new expert evaluation or inspection, the applicant shall provide the basic information of the witness, the location where the evidence is stored, explain the matters intended to be proven, and the reasons for applying for a new expert evaluation or inspection. If the court deems it necessary, it shall approve the application and announce an adjournment; based on the circumstances of the case, it may decide to postpone the trial.  
If the People's Court decides to conduct a new expert evaluation, it shall promptly commission the expert evaluation and notify the People's Procuratorate, the parties, their defenders, and litigation agents of the expert opinion.

<a id="art-274"></a>第二百七十四条 审判期间，公诉人发现案件需要补充侦查，建议延期审理的，合议庭可以同意，但建议延期审理不得超过两次。  
人民检察院将补充收集的证据移送人民法院的，人民法院应当通知辩护人、诉讼代理人查阅、摘抄、复制。  
补充侦查期限届满后，人民检察院未将补充的证据材料移送人民法院的，人民法院可以根据在案证据作出判决、裁定。

**Article 274** : During the trial, if the public prosecutor discovers that the case requires supplementary investigation and recommends postponement of the trial, the collegial panel may agree, but the recommendation for postponement shall not exceed two times.  
Where the People's Procuratorate transfers supplementary collected evidence to the People's Court, the People's Court shall notify the defenders and litigation agents to consult, extract, and copy it.  
After the time limit for supplementary investigation expires, if the People's Procuratorate has not transferred the supplementary evidence materials to the People's Court, the People's Court may make a judgment or ruling based on the evidence on file.

<a id="art-275"></a>第二百七十五条 人民法院向人民检察院调取需要调查核实的证据材料，或者根据被告人、辩护人的申请，向人民检察院调取在调查、侦查、审查起诉期间收集的有关被告人无罪或者罪轻的证据材料，应当通知人民检察院在收到调取证据材料决定书后三日以内移交。

**Article 275** : Where the People's Court retrieves evidence materials from the People's Procuratorate that need to be investigated and verified, or, upon the application of the defendant or the defender, retrieves from the People's Procuratorate evidence materials concerning the innocence or lesser guilt of the defendant collected during the periods of investigation, criminal investigation, and examination for prosecution, it shall notify the People's Procuratorate to hand them over within three days after receiving the Decision on Retrieving Evidence Materials.

<a id="art-276"></a>第二百七十六条 法庭审理过程中，对与量刑有关的事实、证据，应当进行调查。  
人民法院除应当审查被告人是否具有法定量刑情节外，还应当根据案件情况审查以下影响量刑的情节：  
（一）案件起因；  
（二）被害人有无过错及过错程度，是否对矛盾激化负有责任及责任大小；  
（三）被告人的近亲属是否协助抓获被告人；  
（四）被告人平时表现，有无悔罪态度；  
（五）退赃、退赔及赔偿情况；  
（六）被告人是否取得被害人或者其近亲属谅解；  
（七）影响量刑的其他情节。

**Article 276** : During the court trial, facts and evidence related to sentencing shall be investigated.  
In addition to reviewing whether the defendant has statutory sentencing circumstances, the People's Court shall also, based on the circumstances of the case, review the following circumstances affecting sentencing:  
(1) the cause of the case;  
(2) whether the victim was at fault and the degree of fault, and whether the victim bore responsibility for the aggravation of the conflict and the extent of such responsibility;  
(3) whether the defendant's close relatives assisted in capturing the defendant;  
(4) the defendant's usual behavior and whether the defendant has an attitude of repentance;  
(5) the circumstances of returning illegal gains, making restitution, and paying compensation;  
(6) whether the defendant has obtained the understanding of the victim or the victim's close relatives;  
(7) other circumstances affecting sentencing.

<a id="art-277"></a>第二百七十七条 审判期间，合议庭发现被告人可能有自首、坦白、立功等法定量刑情节，而人民检察院移送的案卷中没有相关证据材料的，应当通知人民检察院在指定时间内移送。  
审判期间，被告人提出新的立功线索的，人民法院可以建议人民检察院补充侦查。

**Article 277** : During the trial, if the collegial panel discovers that the defendant may have statutory sentencing circumstances such as voluntary surrender, truthful confession, or meritorious service, but the case file transferred by the People's Procuratorate contains no relevant evidence materials, it shall notify the People's Procuratorate to transfer them within a specified time.  
During the trial, if the defendant provides new clues for meritorious service, the People's Court may suggest that the People's Procuratorate conduct supplementary investigation.

<a id="art-278"></a>第二百七十八条 对被告人认罪的案件，在确认被告人了解起诉书指控的犯罪事实和罪名，自愿认罪且知悉认罪的法律后果后，法庭调查可以主要围绕量刑和其他有争议的问题进行。  
对被告人不认罪或者辩护人作无罪辩护的案件，法庭调查应当在查明定罪事实的基础上，查明有关量刑事实。

**Article 278** : For cases in which the defendant pleads guilty, after confirming that the defendant understands the criminal facts and charges alleged in the indictment, voluntarily pleads guilty, and is aware of the legal consequences of pleading guilty, the court investigation may mainly focus on sentencing and other disputed issues.  
For cases in which the defendant does not plead guilty or the defender makes a defense of not guilty, the court investigation shall, on the basis of establishing the facts for conviction, establish the relevant facts for sentencing.

<a id="art-279"></a>第二百七十九条 法庭审理过程中，应当对查封、扣押、冻结财物及其孳息的权属、来源等情况，是否属于违法所得或者依法应当追缴的其他涉案财物进行调查，由公诉人说明情况、出示证据、提出处理建议，并听取被告人、辩护人等诉讼参与人的意见。  
案外人对查封、扣押、冻结的财物及其孳息提出权属异议的，人民法院应当听取案外人的意见；必要时，可以通知案外人出庭。  
经审查，不能确认查封、扣押、冻结的财物及其孳息属于违法所得或者依法应当追缴的其他涉案财物的，不得没收。

**Article 279** : During the court trial, the court shall investigate the ownership, source, and other circumstances of the sealed-up, seized, or frozen property and its fruits, and whether it constitutes illegal gains or other property involved in the case that shall be recovered according to law. The public prosecutor shall explain the circumstances, present evidence, and put forward proposals for handling, and the opinions of litigation participants such as the defendant and the defender shall be heard.  
If a person other than a party raises an objection to the ownership of the sealed-up, seized, or frozen property and its fruits, the People's Court shall hear the opinion of that person; where necessary, it may notify that person to appear in court.  
After examination, if it cannot be confirmed that the sealed-up, seized, or frozen property and its fruits constitute illegal gains or other property involved in the case that shall be recovered according to law, they shall not be confiscated.

#### 第四节 法庭辩论与最后陈述

<a id="art-280"></a>第二百八十条 合议庭认为案件事实已经调查清楚的，应当由审判长宣布法庭调查结束，开始就定罪、量刑、涉案财物处理的事实、证据、适用法律等问题进行法庭辩论。

**Article 280** : Where the collegial panel considers that the facts of the case have been clearly investigated, the presiding judge shall announce the conclusion of the court investigation and commence court debate on issues such as the facts, evidence, and application of law concerning conviction, sentencing, and handling of property involved in the case.

<a id="art-281"></a>第二百八十一条 法庭辩论应当在审判长的主持下，按照下列顺序进行：  
（一）公诉人发言；  
（二）被害人及其诉讼代理人发言；  
（三）被告人自行辩护；  
（四）辩护人辩护；  
（五）控辩双方进行辩论。

**Article 281** : Court debate shall be conducted under the auspices of the presiding judge in the following order:  
(1) speech by the public prosecutor;  
(2) speech by the victim and the victim's litigation agent;  
(3) the defendant's self-defense;  
(4) defense by the defender;  
(5) debate between the prosecution and the defense.

<a id="art-282"></a>第二百八十二条 人民检察院可以提出量刑建议并说明理由；建议判处管制、宣告缓刑的，一般应当附有调查评估报告，或者附有委托调查函。  
当事人及其辩护人、诉讼代理人可以对量刑提出意见并说明理由。

**Article 282** : The People's Procuratorate may propose sentencing recommendations and state the reasons; where it recommends sentencing to public surveillance or probation, it shall generally attach an investigation and assessment report or a letter entrusting investigation.  
The parties, their defenders, and litigation agents may present opinions on sentencing and state the reasons.

<a id="art-283"></a>第二百八十三条 对被告人认罪的案件，法庭辩论时，应当指引控辩双方主要围绕量刑和其他有争议的问题进行。  
对被告人不认罪或者辩护人作无罪辩护的案件，法庭辩论时，可以指引控辩双方先辩论定罪问题，后辩论量刑和其他问题。

**Article 283** : For cases in which the defendant pleads guilty, during court debate, the prosecution and the defense shall be guided to mainly focus on sentencing and other disputed issues.  
For cases in which the defendant does not plead guilty or the defender makes a defense of not guilty, during court debate, the prosecution and the defense may be guided to first debate the issue of conviction and then debate sentencing and other issues.

<a id="art-284"></a>第二百八十四条 附带民事部分的辩论应当在刑事部分的辩论结束后进行，先由附带民事诉讼原告人及其诉讼代理人发言，后由附带民事诉讼被告人及其诉讼代理人答辩。

**Article 284** : The debate on the incidental civil part shall be conducted after the debate on the criminal part concludes, with the plaintiff of the incidental civil action and the plaintiff's litigation agent speaking first, and then the defendant of the incidental civil action and the defendant's litigation agent responding.

<a id="art-285"></a>第二百八十五条 法庭辩论过程中，审判长应当充分听取控辩双方的意见，对控辩双方与案件无关、重复或者指责对方的发言应当提醒、制止。

**Article 285** : During court debate, the presiding judge shall fully hear the opinions of the prosecution and the defense, and shall remind and stop statements made by the prosecution or the defense that are irrelevant to the case, repetitive, or accuse the other party.

<a id="art-286"></a>第二百八十六条 法庭辩论过程中，合议庭发现与定罪、量刑有关的新的事实，有必要调查的，审判长可以宣布恢复法庭调查，在对新的事实调查后，继续法庭辩论。

**Article 286** : During court debate, if the collegial panel discovers new facts related to conviction and sentencing that need to be investigated, the presiding judge may announce the resumption of court investigation, and after investigating the new facts, continue the court debate.

<a id="art-287"></a>第二百八十七条 审判长宣布法庭辩论终结后，合议庭应当保证被告人充分行使最后陈述的权利。  
被告人在最后陈述中多次重复自己的意见的，法庭可以制止；陈述内容蔑视法庭、公诉人，损害他人及社会公共利益，或者与本案无关的，应当制止。  
在公开审理的案件中，被告人最后陈述的内容涉及国家秘密、个人隐私或者商业秘密的，应当制止。

**Article 287** : After the presiding judge announces the conclusion of court debate, the collegial panel shall ensure that the defendant fully exercises the right to make a final statement.  
If the defendant repeatedly repeats his or her opinions in the final statement, the court may stop it; if the content of the statement shows contempt for the court or the public prosecutor, harms the interests of others or the public, or is unrelated to the case, it shall be stopped.  
In cases tried in public, if the content of the defendant's final statement involves state secrets, personal privacy, or trade secrets, it shall be stopped.

<a id="art-288"></a>第二百八十八条 被告人在最后陈述中提出新的事实、证据，合议庭认为可能影响正确裁判的，应当恢复法庭调查；被告人提出新的辩解理由，合议庭认为可能影响正确裁判的，应当恢复法庭辩论。

**Article 288** : If the defendant raises new facts or evidence in the final statement and the collegial panel considers that they may affect a correct adjudication, it shall resume court investigation; if the defendant raises new grounds for defense and the collegial panel considers that they may affect a correct adjudication, it shall resume court debate.

<a id="art-289"></a>第二百八十九条 公诉人当庭发表与起诉书不同的意见，属于变更、追加、补充或者撤回起诉的，人民法院应当要求人民检察院在指定时间内以书面方式提出；必要时，可以宣布休庭。人民检察院在指定时间内未提出的，人民法院应当根据法庭审理情况，就起诉书指控的犯罪事实依法作出判决、裁定。  
人民检察院变更、追加、补充起诉的，人民法院应当给予被告人及其辩护人必要的准备时间。

**Article 289** : If the public prosecutor expresses opinions in court different from the indictment, which constitute an alteration, addition, supplement, or withdrawal of the prosecution, the People's Court shall require the People's Procuratorate to submit them in writing within a specified time; where necessary, it may announce an adjournment. If the People's Procuratorate fails to submit within the specified time, the People's Court shall, based on the circumstances of the court trial, make a judgment or ruling according to law with respect to the criminal facts charged in the indictment.  
If the People's Procuratorate alters, adds, or supplements the prosecution, the People's Court shall grant the defendant and the defendant's defender the necessary preparation time.

<a id="art-290"></a>第二百九十条 辩护人应当及时将书面辩护意见提交人民法院。

**Article 290** : The defender shall promptly submit written defense opinions to the People's Court.

#### 第五节 评议案件与宣告判决

<a id="art-291"></a>第二百九十一条 被告人最后陈述后，审判长应当宣布休庭，由合议庭进行评议。

**Article 291** : After the defendant's final statement, the presiding judge shall announce an adjournment for the collegial panel to deliberate.

<a id="art-292"></a>第二百九十二条 开庭审理的全部活动，应当由书记员制作笔录；笔录经审判长审阅后，分别由审判长和书记员签名。

**Article 292** : The entire proceedings of the court hearing shall be recorded by the court clerk; after the record is reviewed by the presiding judge, it shall be signed by the presiding judge and the court clerk respectively.

<a id="art-293"></a>第二百九十三条 法庭笔录应当在庭审后交由当事人、法定代理人、辩护人、诉讼代理人阅读或者向其宣读。  
法庭笔录中的出庭证人、鉴定人、有专门知识的人、调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员的证言、意见部分，应当在庭审后分别交由有关人员阅读或者向其宣读。  
前两款所列人员认为记录有遗漏或者差错的，可以请求补充或者改正；确认无误后，应当签名；拒绝签名的，应当记录在案；要求改变庭审中陈述的，不予准许。

**Article 293** : The court record shall, after the court session, be handed to the parties, legal agents, defenders, and litigation agents for reading or be read aloud to them.  
The portions of the court record containing the testimony or opinions of witnesses appearing in court, expert witnesses, persons with specialized knowledge, investigators, criminal investigation personnel, or other persons shall, after the court session, be handed respectively to the relevant persons for reading or be read aloud to them.  
If the persons listed in the preceding two paragraphs consider that the record contains omissions or errors, they may request supplements or corrections; after confirming that the record is accurate, they shall sign it; if they refuse to sign, the refusal shall be recorded in the case file; requests to change statements made during the court session shall not be permitted.

<a id="art-294"></a>第二百九十四条 合议庭评议案件，应当根据已经查明的事实、证据和有关法律规定，在充分考虑控辩双方意见的基础上，确定被告人是否有罪、构成何罪，有无从重、从轻、减轻或者免除处罚情节，应否处以刑罚、判处何种刑罚，附带民事诉讼如何解决，查封、扣押、冻结的财物及其孳息如何处理等，并依法作出判决、裁定。

**Article 294** : When deliberating a case, the collegial panel shall, based on the established facts, evidence, and relevant legal provisions, and on the basis of fully considering the opinions of the prosecution and the defense, determine whether the defendant is guilty, what crime is constituted, whether there are circumstances for severe, light, mitigated, or exempted punishment, whether a penalty shall be imposed and what kind of penalty, how to resolve the incidental civil action, and how to handle the sealed-up, seized, or frozen property and its fruits, and shall make a judgment or ruling according to law.

<a id="art-295"></a>第二百九十五条 对第一审公诉案件，人民法院审理后，应当按照下列情形分别作出判决、裁定：  
（一）起诉指控的事实清楚，证据确实、充分，依据法律认定指控被告人的罪名成立的，应当作出有罪判决；  
（二）起诉指控的事实清楚，证据确实、充分，但指控的罪名不当的，应当依据法律和审理认定的事实作出有罪判决；  
（三）案件事实清楚，证据确实、充分，依据法律认定被告人无罪的，应当判决宣告被告人无罪；  
（四）证据不足，不能认定被告人有罪的，应当以证据不足、指控的犯罪不能成立，判决宣告被告人无罪；  
（五）案件部分事实清楚，证据确实、充分的，应当作出有罪或者无罪的判决；对事实不清、证据不足部分，不予认定；  
（六）被告人因未达到刑事责任年龄，不予刑事处罚的，应当判决宣告被告人不负刑事责任；  
（七）被告人是精神病人，在不能辨认或者不能控制自己行为时造成危害结果，不予刑事处罚的，应当判决宣告被告人不负刑事责任；被告人符合强制医疗条件的，应当依照本解释第二十六章的规定进行审理并作出判决；  
（八）犯罪已过追诉时效期限且不是必须追诉，或者经特赦令免除刑罚的，应当裁定终止审理；  
（九）属于告诉才处理的案件，应当裁定终止审理，并告知被害人有权提起自诉；  
（十）被告人死亡的，应当裁定终止审理；但有证据证明被告人无罪，经缺席审理确认无罪的，应当判决宣告被告人无罪。  
对涉案财物，人民法院应当根据审理查明的情况，依照本解释第十八章的规定作出处理。  
具有第一款第二项规定情形的，人民法院应当在判决前听取控辩双方的意见，保障被告人、辩护人充分行使辩护权。必要时，可以再次开庭，组织控辩双方围绕被告人的行为构成何罪及如何量刑进行辩论。

**Article 295** : For first-instance public prosecution cases, after trial, the People's Court shall make judgments or rulings separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) where the facts charged in the prosecution are clear, the evidence is reliable and sufficient, and the crime charged against the defendant is established according to law, a conviction judgment shall be made;  
(2) where the facts charged in the prosecution are clear, the evidence is reliable and sufficient, but the charge is improper, a conviction judgment shall be made in accordance with the law and the facts found in the trial;  
(3) where the facts of the case are clear, the evidence is reliable and sufficient, and the defendant is found not guilty according to law, a judgment shall be rendered declaring the defendant not guilty;  
(4) where evidence is insufficient and the defendant cannot be found guilty, a judgment shall be rendered declaring the defendant not guilty on the grounds that the evidence is insufficient and the charged crime cannot be established;  
(5) where part of the facts of the case are clear and the evidence is reliable and sufficient, a judgment of guilty or not guilty shall be made; the parts with unclear facts and insufficient evidence shall not be affirmed;  
(6) where the defendant is not subject to criminal punishment because he or she has not reached the age of criminal responsibility, a judgment shall be rendered declaring that the defendant bears no criminal liability;  
(7) where the defendant is a person with mental illness who causes harmful consequences when unable to recognize or control his or her own conduct and is exempted from criminal punishment, a judgment shall be rendered declaring that the defendant bears no criminal liability; where the defendant meets the conditions for compulsory medical treatment, the case shall be tried and a judgment shall be made in accordance with the provisions of Chapter 26 of this Interpretation;  
(8) where the crime has passed the time limit for prosecution and prosecution is not required, or where the punishment is exempted by a special pardon order, a ruling shall be made to terminate the trial;  
(9) where the case is handled only upon complaint, a ruling shall be made to terminate the trial, and the victim shall be informed of the right to bring a private prosecution;  
(10) where the defendant dies, a ruling shall be made to terminate the trial; however, if there is evidence proving that the defendant is not guilty and the non-guilt is confirmed through trial in absentia, a judgment shall be rendered declaring the defendant not guilty.  
With respect to the property involved in the case, the People's Court shall, according to the circumstances found in the trial, handle it in accordance with the provisions of Chapter 18 of this Interpretation.  
Where the circumstance provided in item (2) of the first paragraph exists, the People's Court shall, before the judgment, hear the opinions of the prosecution and the defense, and ensure that the defendant and the defender fully exercise the right to defense. Where necessary, it may hold court again to organize the prosecution and the defense to debate what crime the defendant's conduct constitutes and how to sentence.

<a id="art-296"></a>第二百九十六条 在开庭后、宣告判决前，人民检察院要求撤回起诉的，人民法院应当审查撤回起诉的理由，作出是否准许的裁定。

**Article 296** : If, after the court session begins and before the judgment is pronounced, the People's Procuratorate requests withdrawal of the prosecution, the People's Court shall review the reasons for the withdrawal and make a ruling on whether to permit it.

<a id="art-297"></a>第二百九十七条 审判期间，人民法院发现新的事实，可能影响定罪量刑的，或者需要补查补证的，应当通知人民检察院，由其决定是否补充、变更、追加起诉或者补充侦查。  
人民检察院不同意或者在指定时间内未回复书面意见的，人民法院应当就起诉指控的事实，依照[本解释第二百九十五条](#art-295)的规定作出判决、裁定。

**Article 297** : During the trial, if the People's Court discovers new facts that may affect conviction and sentencing, or if supplementary investigation and supplementary evidence are needed, it shall notify the People's Procuratorate, which shall decide whether to supplement, alter, or add the prosecution or conduct supplementary investigation.  
If the People's Procuratorate disagrees or fails to reply with a written opinion within the specified time, the People's Court shall make a judgment or ruling with respect to the facts charged in the prosecution in accordance with Article 295 of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-298"></a>第二百九十八条 对依照[本解释第二百一十九条](#art-219)第一款第五项规定受理的案件，人民法院应当在判决中写明被告人曾被人民检察院提起公诉，因证据不足，指控的犯罪不能成立，被人民法院依法判决宣告无罪的情况；前案依照[刑事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-200)第三项规定作出的判决不予撤销。

**Article 298** : For a case accepted in accordance with Article 219, paragraph 1, item (5) of this Interpretation, the People's Court shall, in its judgment, state the circumstances that the defendant was previously prosecuted by the People's Procuratorate and, because the evidence was insufficient and the charged crime could not be established, was declared not guilty by a judgment of the People's Court according to law; the judgment made in the previous case in accordance with Article 200, item (3) of the Criminal Procedure Law shall not be revoked.

<a id="art-299"></a>第二百九十九条 合议庭成员、法官助理、书记员应当在评议笔录上签名，在判决书、裁定书等法律文书上署名。

**Article 299** : The members of the collegial panel, judge assistants, and court clerks shall sign the deliberation record and affix their signatures to legal documents such as judgments and rulings.

<a id="art-300"></a>第三百条 裁判文书应当写明裁判依据，阐释裁判理由，反映控辩双方的意见并说明采纳或者不予采纳的理由。  
适用普通程序审理的被告人认罪的案件，裁判文书可以适当简化。

**Article 300** : Adjudication documents shall state the basis for adjudication, explain the reasons for adjudication, reflect the opinions of the prosecution and the defense, and explain the reasons for accepting or not accepting them.  
For cases in which the defendant pleads guilty and the ordinary procedure is applied, the adjudication documents may be appropriately simplified.

<a id="art-301"></a>第三百零一条 庭审结束后、评议前，部分合议庭成员不能继续履行审判职责的，人民法院应当依法更换合议庭组成人员，重新开庭审理。  
评议后、宣判前，部分合议庭成员因调动、退休等正常原因不能参加宣判，在不改变原评议结论的情况下，可以由审判本案的其他审判员宣判，裁判文书上仍署审判本案的合议庭成员的姓名。

**Article 301** : If, after the court session ends and before deliberation, some members of the collegial panel are unable to continue performing judicial duties, the People's Court shall replace the members of the collegial panel according to law and reopen the court trial.  
If, after deliberation and before pronouncement of judgment, some members of the collegial panel are unable to participate in the pronouncement due to normal reasons such as transfer or retirement, the judgment may be pronounced by other judges who tried the case, provided that the original deliberation conclusion is not changed, and the names of the members of the collegial panel who tried the case shall still be affixed to the adjudication document.

<a id="art-302"></a>第三百零二条 当庭宣告判决的，应当在五日以内送达判决书。定期宣告判决的，应当在宣判前，先期公告宣判的时间和地点，传唤当事人并通知公诉人、法定代理人、辩护人和诉讼代理人；判决宣告后，应当立即送达判决书。

**Article 302** : Where judgment is pronounced in court, the written judgment shall be served within five days. Where judgment is pronounced on a scheduled date, the time and place of the pronouncement shall be announced in advance before the pronouncement, the parties shall be summoned, and the public prosecutor, legal agents, defenders, and litigation agents shall be notified; after the judgment is pronounced, the written judgment shall be served immediately.

<a id="art-303"></a>第三百零三条 判决书应当送达人民检察院、当事人、法定代理人、辩护人、诉讼代理人，并可以送达被告人的近亲属。被害人死亡，其近亲属申请领取判决书的，人民法院应当及时提供。  
判决生效后，还应当送达被告人的所在单位或者户籍地的公安派出所，或者被告单位的注册登记机关。被告人系外国人，且在境内有居住地的，应当送达居住地的公安派出所。

**Article 303** : The written judgment shall be served on the People's Procuratorate, the parties, legal agents, defenders, and litigation agents, and may be served on the close relatives of the defendant. If the victim died and his or her close relatives apply to receive the written judgment, the People's Court shall provide it promptly.  
After the judgment takes effect, it shall also be served on the defendant's employer or the public security police station at the defendant's domicile, or on the registration authority of the defendant unit. If the defendant is a foreigner and has a place of residence within the territory, the written judgment shall be served on the public security police station at the place of residence.

<a id="art-304"></a>第三百零四条 宣告判决，一律公开进行。宣告判决结果时，法庭内全体人员应当起立。  
公诉人、辩护人、诉讼代理人、被害人、自诉人或者附带民事诉讼原告人未到庭的，不影响宣判的进行。

**Article 304** : Pronouncement of judgments shall in all cases be conducted in public. When the result of the judgment is pronounced, all persons in the courtroom shall stand.  
If the public prosecutor, defender, litigation agent, victim, private prosecutor, or plaintiff of an incidental civil action is not present in court, it shall not affect the conduct of the pronouncement.

#### 第六节 法庭纪律与其他规定

<a id="art-305"></a>第三百零五条 在押被告人出庭受审时，不着监管机构的识别服。  
庭审期间不得对被告人使用戒具，但法庭认为其人身危险性大，可能危害法庭安全的除外。

**Article 305** : When a defendant in custody appears in court for trial, he or she shall not wear the identifying uniform of the custodial institution.  
During the court session, restraints shall not be used on the defendant, except where the court considers that the defendant poses a high risk of personal danger and may endanger courtroom safety.

<a id="art-306"></a>第三百零六条 庭审期间，全体人员应当服从法庭指挥，遵守法庭纪律，尊重司法礼仪，不得实施下列行为：  
（一）鼓掌、喧哗、随意走动；  
（二）吸烟、进食；  
（三）拨打、接听电话，或者使用即时通讯工具；  
（四）对庭审活动进行录音、录像、拍照或者使用即时通讯工具等传播庭审活动；  
（五）其他危害法庭安全或者扰乱法庭秩序的行为。  
旁听人员不得进入审判活动区，不得随意站立、走动，不得发言和提问。  
记者经许可实施第一款第四项规定的行为，应当在指定的时间及区域进行，不得干扰庭审活动。

**Article 306** : During the court session, all persons shall obey the direction of the court, observe courtroom discipline, respect judicial etiquette, and shall not commit the following acts:  
(1) applauding, making noise, or walking around at will;  
(2) smoking or eating;  
(3) making or answering phone calls, or using instant messaging tools;  
(4) audio recording, video recording, photographing, or disseminating the court session by using instant messaging tools or otherwise;  
(5) other acts that endanger courtroom safety or disrupt courtroom order.  
Observers shall not enter the trial activity area, shall not stand up or walk around at will, and shall not speak or ask questions.  
Journalists who, with permission, engage in the conduct provided in item (4) of the first paragraph shall do so at the designated time and in the designated area, and shall not interfere with the court session.

<a id="art-307"></a>第三百零七条 有关人员危害法庭安全或者扰乱法庭秩序的，审判长应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）情节较轻的，应当警告制止；根据具体情况，也可以进行训诫；  
（二）训诫无效的，责令退出法庭；拒不退出的，指令法警强行带出法庭；  
（三）情节严重的，报经院长批准后，可以对行为人处一千元以下的罚款或者十五日以下的拘留。  
未经许可对庭审活动进行录音、录像、拍照或者使用即时通讯工具等传播庭审活动的，可以暂扣相关设备及存储介质，删除相关内容。  
有关人员对罚款、拘留的决定不服的，可以直接向上一级人民法院申请复议，也可以通过决定罚款、拘留的人民法院向上一级人民法院申请复议。通过决定罚款、拘留的人民法院申请复议的，该人民法院应当自收到复议申请之日起三日以内，将复议申请、罚款或者拘留决定书和有关事实、证据材料一并报上一级人民法院复议。复议期间，不停止决定的执行。

**Article 307** : Where a relevant person endangers courtroom safety or disrupts courtroom order, the presiding judge shall, respectively, handle the matter according to the following circumstances:  
(1) if the circumstances are relatively minor, the person shall be warned and stopped; depending on the specific circumstances, an admonition may also be given;  
(2) if the admonition is ineffective, the person shall be ordered to leave the courtroom; if the person refuses to leave, judicial police shall be instructed to forcefully remove the person from the courtroom;  
(3) if the circumstances are serious, after reporting to the president of the court for approval, the person may be imposed a fine of not more than 1,000 yuan or detention of not more than 15 days.  
Where, without permission, a person audio records, video records, photographs, or disseminates the court session by using instant messaging tools or otherwise, the relevant equipment and storage media may be temporarily seized and the relevant content deleted.  
Where a relevant person refuses to accept the decision on fine or detention, he or she may directly apply to the People's Court at the next higher level for reconsideration, or may apply to the People's Court at the next higher level for reconsideration through the People's Court that decided the fine or detention. Where an application for reconsideration is made through the People's Court that decided the fine or detention, that People's Court shall, within three days after receiving the application for reconsideration, submit the application for reconsideration, the decision on fine or detention, and the relevant facts and evidence materials to the People's Court at the next higher level for reconsideration. During the reconsideration period, the execution of the decision shall not be suspended.

<a id="art-308"></a>第三百零八条 担任辩护人、诉讼代理人的律师严重扰乱法庭秩序，被强行带出法庭或者被处以罚款、拘留的，人民法院应当通报司法行政机关，并可以建议依法给予相应处罚。

**Article 308** : Where a lawyer serving as a defender or litigation agent seriously disrupts courtroom order and is forcibly removed from the courtroom or is subjected to a fine or detention, the People's Court shall notify the judicial administrative organ and may recommend that corresponding punishment be imposed according to law.

<a id="art-309"></a>第三百零九条 实施下列行为之一，危害法庭安全或者扰乱法庭秩序，构成犯罪的，依法追究刑事责任：  
（一）非法携带枪支、弹药、管制刀具或者爆炸性、易燃性、毒害性、放射性以及传染病病原体等危险物质进入法庭；  
（二）哄闹、冲击法庭；  
（三）侮辱、诽谤、威胁、殴打司法工作人员或者诉讼参与人；  
（四）毁坏法庭设施，抢夺、损毁诉讼文书、证据；  
（五）其他危害法庭安全或者扰乱法庭秩序的行为。

**Article 309** : Whoever commits any of the following acts, endangering courtroom safety or disrupting courtroom order, and constituting a crime, shall be investigated for criminal liability according to law:  
(1) illegally carrying firearms, ammunition, controlled knives, or dangerous substances such as explosive, flammable, poisonous, radioactive, or infectious disease pathogen materials into the courtroom;  
(2) creating disturbances or storming the courtroom;  
(3) insulting, defaming, threatening, or assaulting judicial staff or litigation participants;  
(4) destroying courtroom facilities, or seizing or damaging litigation documents or evidence;  
(5) other acts endangering courtroom safety or disrupting courtroom order.

<a id="art-310"></a>第三百一十条 辩护人严重扰乱法庭秩序，被责令退出法庭、强行带出法庭或者被处以罚款、拘留，被告人自行辩护的，庭审继续进行；被告人要求另行委托辩护人，或者被告人属于应当提供法律援助情形的，应当宣布休庭。  
辩护人、诉讼代理人被责令退出法庭、强行带出法庭或者被处以罚款后，具结保证书，保证服从法庭指挥、不再扰乱法庭秩序的，经法庭许可，可以继续担任辩护人、诉讼代理人。  
辩护人、诉讼代理人具有下列情形之一的，不得继续担任同一案件的辩护人、诉讼代理人：  
（一）擅自退庭的；  
（二）无正当理由不出庭或者不按时出庭，严重影响审判顺利进行的；  
（三）被拘留或者具结保证书后再次被责令退出法庭、强行带出法庭的。

**Article 310** : Where a defender seriously disrupts the order of the courtroom and is ordered to withdraw from the courtroom, forcibly removed from the courtroom, or punished by fine or detention, and the defendant defends himself/herself, the courtroom trial shall continue; where the defendant requests to retain another defender, or the defendant falls under the circumstances where legal aid shall be provided, the court shall announce an adjournment.  
After a defender or litigation agent has been ordered to withdraw from the courtroom, forcibly removed from the courtroom, or punished by fine, if he/she submits a written guarantee ensuring obedience to the court's direction and no further disruption of courtroom order, with the permission of the court, he/she may continue to serve as defender or litigation agent.  
A defender or litigation agent shall not continue to serve as defender or litigation agent in the same case under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) leaving the courtroom without permission;  
(2) failing to appear in court or failing to appear on time without justifiable reasons, seriously affecting the smooth conduct of the trial;  
(3) being detained or, after submitting a written guarantee, again being ordered to withdraw from the courtroom or forcibly removed from the courtroom.

<a id="art-311"></a>第三百一十一条 被告人在一个审判程序中更换辩护人一般不得超过两次。  
被告人当庭拒绝辩护人辩护，要求另行委托辩护人或者指派律师的，合议庭应当准许。被告人拒绝辩护人辩护后，没有辩护人的，应当宣布休庭；仍有辩护人的，庭审可以继续进行。  
有多名被告人的案件，部分被告人拒绝辩护人辩护后，没有辩护人的，根据案件情况，可以对该部分被告人另案处理，对其他被告人的庭审继续进行。  
重新开庭后，被告人再次当庭拒绝辩护人辩护的，可以准许，但被告人不得再次另行委托辩护人或者要求另行指派律师，由其自行辩护。  
被告人属于应当提供法律援助的情形，重新开庭后再次当庭拒绝辩护人辩护的，不予准许。

**Article 311** : In a single trial procedure, a defendant shall generally not replace the defender more than twice.  
Where the defendant refuses the defender's representation in court and requests to retain another defender or assign a lawyer, the collegial panel shall permit it. Where, after the defendant refuses the defender's representation, the defendant has no defender, the court shall announce an adjournment; if there is still a defender, the trial may continue.  
In a case with multiple defendants, where some defendants refuse the defender's representation and thus have no defender, based on the circumstances of the case, the trial of those defendants may be handled separately, and the trial of the other defendants may continue.  
After the court session is reopened, where the defendant again refuses the defender's representation in court, it may be permitted, but the defendant shall not again request to retain another defender or to have another lawyer assigned, and the defendant shall defend himself/herself.
