Where the defendant falls under the circumstances where legal aid shall be provided, if after the court session is reopened, the defendant again refuses the defender's representation in court, it shall not be permitted.

<a id="art-312"></a>第三百一十二条 法庭审理过程中，辩护人拒绝为被告人辩护，有正当理由的，应当准许；是否继续庭审，参照适用前条规定。

**Article 312** : Where, during court trial, a defender refuses to defend the defendant and has justifiable reasons, it shall be permitted; whether to continue the trial shall be determined by reference to the provisions of the preceding article.

<a id="art-313"></a>第三百一十三条 依照前两条规定另行委托辩护人或者通知法律援助机构指派律师的，自案件宣布休庭之日起至第十五日止，由辩护人准备辩护，但被告人及其辩护人自愿缩短时间的除外。  
庭审结束后、判决宣告前另行委托辩护人的，可以不重新开庭；辩护人提交书面辩护意见的，应当接受。

**Article 313** : Where, pursuant to the provisions of the preceding two articles, another defender is retained or a legal aid institution is notified to assign a lawyer, the defender shall prepare the defense from the date the case is announced for adjournment until the fifteenth day, unless the defendant and the defender voluntarily shorten the time period.  
Where another defender is retained after the conclusion of the court trial but before the judgment is pronounced, the court may not re-open the court session; where the defender submits written defense opinions, they shall be accepted.

<a id="art-314"></a>第三百一十四条 有多名被告人的案件，部分被告人具有[刑事诉讼法第二百零六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-206)第一款规定情形的，人民法院可以对全案中止审理；根据案件情况，也可以对该部分被告人中止审理，对其他被告人继续审理。  
对中止审理的部分被告人，可以根据案件情况另案处理。

**Article 314** : In a case with multiple defendants, where some defendants fall under the circumstances provided in paragraph 1 of Article 206 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the People's Court may suspend the trial of the entire case; based on the circumstances of the case, it may also suspend the trial of those defendants only and continue the trial of the other defendants.  
The defendants whose trials are suspended may be handled in separate cases based on the circumstances of the case.

<a id="art-315"></a>第三百一十五条 人民检察院认为人民法院审理案件违反法定程序，在庭审后提出书面纠正意见，人民法院认为正确的，应当采纳。

**Article 315** : Where the People's Procuratorate considers that the People's Court has violated statutory procedures in trying a case and submits written correction opinions after the trial, if the People's Court considers them correct, it shall adopt them.

### 第十章 自诉案件第一审程序

<a id="art-316"></a>第三百一十六条 人民法院受理自诉案件必须符合下列条件：  
（一）符合[刑事诉讼法第二百一十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-210)、[本解释第一条](#art-1)的规定；  
（二）属于本院管辖；  
（三）被害人告诉；  
（四）有明确的被告人、具体的诉讼请求和证明被告人犯罪事实的证据。

**Article 316** : For the People's Court to accept a private prosecution case, the following conditions must be met:  
(1) it conforms to the provisions of Article 210 of the Criminal Procedure Law and Article 1 of this Interpretation;  
(2) it falls within the jurisdiction of this court;  
(3) the victim brings a complaint;  
(4) there is a clear defendant, specific claims, and evidence proving the defendant's criminal facts.

<a id="art-317"></a>第三百一十七条 [本解释第一条](#art-1)规定的案件，如果被害人死亡、丧失行为能力或者因受强制、威吓等无法告诉，或者是限制行为能力人以及因年老、患病、盲、聋、哑等不能亲自告诉，其法定代理人、近亲属告诉或者代为告诉的，人民法院应当依法受理。  
被害人的法定代理人、近亲属告诉或者代为告诉的，应当提供与被害人关系的证明和被害人不能亲自告诉的原因的证明。

**Article 317** : For cases provided in Article 1 of this Interpretation, if the victim has died, lost capacity, or is unable to bring a complaint due to coercion, intimidation, or other reasons, or is a person with limited capacity, or is unable to personally bring a complaint due to old age, illness, blindness, deafness, muteness, etc., and his/her legal representative or close relative brings the complaint or brings it on his/her behalf, the People's Court shall accept the case according to law.  
Where the victim's legal representative or close relative brings a complaint or brings it on his/her behalf, they shall provide proof of their relationship with the victim and proof of the reasons why the victim cannot personally bring the complaint.

<a id="art-318"></a>第三百一十八条 提起自诉应当提交刑事自诉状；同时提起附带民事诉讼的，应当提交刑事附带民事自诉状。

**Article 318** : To bring a private prosecution, a written criminal private prosecution complaint shall be submitted; where an incidental civil action is also brought, a written criminal incidental civil private prosecution complaint shall be submitted.

<a id="art-319"></a>第三百一十九条 自诉状一般应当包括以下内容：  
（一）自诉人（代为告诉人）、被告人的姓名、性别、年龄、民族、出生地、文化程度、职业、工作单位、住址、联系方式；  
（二）被告人实施犯罪的时间、地点、手段、情节和危害后果等；  
（三）具体的诉讼请求；  
（四）致送的人民法院和具状时间；  
（五）证据的名称、来源等；  
（六）证人的姓名、住址、联系方式等。  
对两名以上被告人提出告诉的，应当按照被告人的人数提供自诉状副本。

**Article 319** : A private prosecution complaint shall generally include the following contents:  
(1) the name, gender, age, ethnicity, place of birth, educational level, occupation, work unit, address, and contact information of the private prosecutor (person bringing the complaint on behalf of the victim) and the defendant;  
(2) the time, place, means, circumstances, harmful consequences, etc., of the defendant's commission of the crime;  
(3) the specific claims;  
(4) the People's Court to which it is submitted and the date of filing;  
(5) the names and sources of evidence, etc.;  
(6) the names, addresses, and contact information of witnesses, etc.  
Where a complaint is brought against two or more defendants, copies of the private prosecution complaint shall be provided in accordance with the number of defendants.

<a id="art-320"></a>第三百二十条 对自诉案件，人民法院应当在十五日以内审查完毕。经审查，符合受理条件的，应当决定立案，并书面通知自诉人或者代为告诉人。  
具有下列情形之一的，应当说服自诉人撤回起诉；自诉人不撤回起诉的，裁定不予受理：  
（一）不属于[本解释第一条](#art-1)规定的案件的；  
（二）缺乏罪证的；  
（三）犯罪已过追诉时效期限的；  
（四）被告人死亡的；  
（五）被告人下落不明的；  
（六）除因证据不足而撤诉的以外，自诉人撤诉后，就同一事实又告诉的；  
（七）经人民法院调解结案后，自诉人反悔，就同一事实再行告诉的；  
（八）属于[本解释第一条](#art-1)第二项规定的案件，公安机关正在立案侦查或者人民检察院正在审查起诉的；  
（九）不服人民检察院对未成年犯罪嫌疑人作出的附条件不起诉决定或者附条件不起诉考验期满后作出的不起诉决定，向人民法院起诉的。

**Article 320** : For a private prosecution case, the People's Court shall complete the review within fifteen days. After review, if the acceptance conditions are met, it shall decide to file the case and notify the private prosecutor or the person bringing the complaint on behalf of the victim in writing.  
Under any of the following circumstances, the court shall persuade the private prosecutor to withdraw the prosecution; if the private prosecutor does not withdraw the prosecution, it shall rule not to accept the case:  
(1) the case is not one provided in Article 1 of this Interpretation;  
(2) there is insufficient evidence of the crime;  
(3) the limitation period for prosecution has expired;  
(4) the defendant is deceased;  
(5) the defendant's whereabouts are unknown;  
(6) except where the prosecution was withdrawn due to insufficient evidence, after the private prosecutor withdraws the prosecution, he/she brings a complaint again on the same facts;  
(7) after the case is concluded through mediation by the People's Court, the private prosecutor reneges and brings a complaint again on the same facts;  
(8) the case belongs to the circumstances provided in Item 2 of Article 1 of this Interpretation, and a public security organ is placing the case on file and investigating it, or the People's Procuratorate is reviewing the prosecution;  
(9) the private prosecutor, being dissatisfied with the conditional non-prosecution decision made by the People's Procuratorate for a minor criminal suspect, or the non-prosecution decision made after the probation period for conditional non-prosecution expires, brings a lawsuit with the People's Court.

<a id="art-321"></a>第三百二十一条 对已经立案，经审查缺乏罪证的自诉案件，自诉人提不出补充证据的，人民法院应当说服其撤回起诉或者裁定驳回起诉；自诉人撤回起诉或者被驳回起诉后，又提出了新的足以证明被告人有罪的证据，再次提起自诉的，人民法院应当受理。

**Article 321** : For a private prosecution case that has been filed and is found after review to lack evidence of the crime, if the private prosecutor cannot provide supplementary evidence, the People's Court shall persuade him/her to withdraw the prosecution or rule to dismiss the prosecution; where, after withdrawing the prosecution or having the prosecution dismissed, the private prosecutor again brings a private prosecution after submitting new evidence sufficient to prove the defendant's guilt, the People's Court shall accept the case.

<a id="art-322"></a>第三百二十二条　自诉人对不予受理或者驳回起诉的裁定不服的，可以提起上诉。  
第二审人民法院查明第一审人民法院作出的不予受理裁定有错误的，应当在撤销原裁定的同时，指令第一审人民法院立案受理；查明第一审人民法院驳回起诉裁定有错误的，应当在撤销原裁定的同时，指令第一审人民法院进行审理。

**Article 322** : Where a private prosecutor is dissatisfied with a ruling of non-acceptance or dismissal of prosecution, he/she may file an appeal.  
Where the second-instance People's Court ascertains that the first-instance People's Court's ruling of non-acceptance is erroneous, it shall, while revoking the original ruling, instruct the first-instance People's Court to file the case for acceptance; where it ascertains that the first-instance People's Court's ruling dismissing the prosecution is erroneous, it shall, while revoking the original ruling, instruct the first-instance People's Court to conduct the trial.

<a id="art-323"></a>第三百二十三条 自诉人明知有其他共同侵害人，但只对部分侵害人提起自诉的，人民法院应当受理，并告知其放弃告诉的法律后果；自诉人放弃告诉，判决宣告后又对其他共同侵害人就同一事实提起自诉的，人民法院不予受理。  
共同被害人中只有部分人告诉的，人民法院应当通知其他被害人参加诉讼，并告知其不参加诉讼的法律后果。被通知人接到通知后表示不参加诉讼或者不出庭的，视为放弃告诉。第一审宣判后，被通知人就同一事实又提起自诉的，人民法院不予受理。但是，当事人另行提起民事诉讼的，不受本解释限制。

**Article 323** : Where a private prosecutor knows that there are other joint infringers but only brings a private prosecution against some of the infringers, the People's Court shall accept it and inform him/her of the legal consequences of abandoning the complaint; if the private prosecutor abandons the complaint and, after the judgment is pronounced, brings a private prosecution against the other joint infringers on the same facts, the People's Court shall not accept it.  
Where only some of the joint victims bring a complaint, the People's Court shall notify the other victims to participate in the proceedings and inform them of the legal consequences of non-participation. If the notified persons indicate after receiving the notice that they will not participate in the proceedings or will not appear in court, they are deemed to have abandoned the complaint. After the first-instance judgment is pronounced, if the notified persons bring a private prosecution again on the same facts, the People's Court shall not accept it. However, if the parties separately bring a civil action, it is not subject to the restrictions of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-324"></a>第三百二十四条 被告人实施两个以上犯罪行为，分别属于公诉案件和自诉案件，人民法院可以一并审理。对自诉部分的审理，适用本章的规定。

**Article 324** : Where a defendant commits two or more criminal acts, which belong respectively to public prosecution cases and private prosecution cases, the People's Court may try them together. The trial of the private prosecution part shall be governed by the provisions of this Chapter.

<a id="art-325"></a>第三百二十五条 自诉案件当事人因客观原因不能取得的证据，申请人民法院调取的，应当说明理由，并提供相关线索或者材料。人民法院认为有必要的，应当及时调取。  
对通过信息网络实施的侮辱、诽谤行为，被害人向人民法院告诉，但提供证据确有困难的，人民法院可以要求公安机关提供协助。

**Article 325** : Where a party to a private prosecution case cannot obtain evidence due to objective reasons and applies to the People's Court to obtain it, the party shall state the reasons and provide relevant leads or materials. If the People's Court deems it necessary, it shall obtain the evidence in a timely manner.  
For insult or defamation committed through information networks, if the victim brings a complaint to the People's Court but genuinely has difficulty providing evidence, the People's Court may request the public security organ to provide assistance.

<a id="art-326"></a>第三百二十六条 对犯罪事实清楚，有足够证据的自诉案件，应当开庭审理。

**Article 326** : Private prosecution cases where the criminal facts are clear and there is sufficient evidence shall be tried in court.

<a id="art-327"></a>第三百二十七条 自诉案件符合简易程序适用条件的，可以适用简易程序审理。  
不适用简易程序审理的自诉案件，参照适用公诉案件第一审普通程序的有关规定。

**Article 327** : Where a private prosecution case meets the conditions for the application of the summary procedure, it may be tried under the summary procedure.  
Private prosecution cases not tried under the summary procedure shall, by reference, apply the relevant provisions of the ordinary procedure for first-instance public prosecution cases.

<a id="art-328"></a>第三百二十八条 人民法院审理自诉案件，可以在查明事实、分清是非的基础上，根据自愿、合法的原则进行调解。调解达成协议的，应当制作刑事调解书，由审判人员、法官助理、书记员署名，并加盖人民法院印章。调解书经双方当事人签收后，即具有法律效力。调解没有达成协议，或者调解书签收前当事人反悔的，应当及时作出判决。  
[刑事诉讼法第二百一十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-210)第三项规定的案件不适用调解。

**Article 328** : In trying a private prosecution case, the People's Court may conduct mediation on the basis of ascertaining the facts and distinguishing right from wrong, in accordance with the principles of voluntariness and legality. Where a mediation agreement is reached, a written criminal mediation document shall be prepared, signed by the trial personnel, judge's assistant, and court clerk, and affixed with the seal of the People's Court. Once the mediation document is signed and received by both parties, it shall have legal effect. If no mediation agreement is reached, or if a party reneges before signing and receiving the mediation document, a judgment shall be rendered in a timely manner.  
For cases provided in Item 3 of Article 210 of the Criminal Procedure Law, mediation shall not apply.

<a id="art-329"></a>第三百二十九条 判决宣告前，自诉案件的当事人可以自行和解，自诉人可以撤回自诉。  
人民法院经审查，认为和解、撤回自诉确属自愿的，应当裁定准许；认为系被强迫、威吓等，并非自愿的，不予准许。

**Article 329** : Before the judgment is pronounced, the parties to a private prosecution case may reach a settlement on their own, and the private prosecutor may withdraw the private prosecution.  
After review, if the People's Court considers that the settlement or withdrawal of the private prosecution is genuinely voluntary, it shall rule to permit it; if it considers that it was caused by coercion, intimidation, or other means, and is not voluntary, it shall not permit it.

<a id="art-330"></a>第三百三十条 裁定准许撤诉的自诉案件，被告人被采取强制措施的，人民法院应当立即解除。

**Article 330** : For a private prosecution case where the withdrawal of the prosecution is ruled to be permitted, if compulsory measures have been taken against the defendant, the People's Court shall immediately lift them.

<a id="art-331"></a>第三百三十一条 自诉人经两次传唤，无正当理由拒不到庭，或者未经法庭准许中途退庭的，人民法院应当裁定按撤诉处理。  
部分自诉人撤诉或者被裁定按撤诉处理的，不影响案件的继续审理。

**Article 331** : Where a private prosecutor, after being summoned twice, refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons, or leaves the courtroom midway without the court's permission, the People's Court shall rule that the case be treated as withdrawn.  
Where some private prosecutors withdraw the prosecution or are ruled to be treated as withdrawn, it shall not affect the continued trial of the case.

<a id="art-332"></a>第三百三十二条 被告人在自诉案件审判期间下落不明的，人民法院可以裁定中止审理；符合条件的，可以对被告人依法决定逮捕。

**Article 332** : Where a defendant's whereabouts are unknown during the trial of a private prosecution case, the People's Court may rule to suspend the trial; where conditions are met, it may decide to arrest the defendant according to law.

<a id="art-333"></a>第三百三十三条 对自诉案件，应当参照[刑事诉讼法第二百条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-200)和[本解释第二百九十五条](#art-295)的有关规定作出判决。对依法宣告无罪的案件，有附带民事诉讼的，其附带民事部分可以依法进行调解或者一并作出判决，也可以告知附带民事诉讼原告人另行提起民事诉讼。

**Article 333** : For a private prosecution case, a judgment shall be rendered by reference to the relevant provisions of Article 200 of the Criminal Procedure Law and Article 295 of this Interpretation. For a case where the defendant is acquitted according to law, if there is an incidental civil action, the incidental civil part may be mediated according to law or adjudicated together, or the plaintiff in the incidental civil action may be informed to separately bring a civil action.

<a id="art-334"></a>第三百三十四条 告诉才处理和被害人有证据证明的轻微刑事案件的被告人或者其法定代理人在诉讼过程中，可以对自诉人提起反诉。反诉必须符合下列条件：  
（一）反诉的对象必须是本案自诉人；  
（二）反诉的内容必须是与本案有关的行为；  
（三）反诉的案件必须符合[本解释第一条](#art-1)第一项、第二项的规定。  
反诉案件适用自诉案件的规定，应当与自诉案件一并审理。自诉人撤诉的，不影响反诉案件的继续审理。

**Article 334** : In cases handled only upon complaint and minor criminal cases where the victim has evidence to prove the case, the defendant or his/her legal representative may, during the proceedings, file a counterclaim against the private prosecutor. A counterclaim must satisfy the following conditions:  
(1) the object of the counterclaim must be the private prosecutor in the present case;  
(2) the content of the counterclaim must be conduct related to the present case;  
(3) the counterclaim case must conform to the provisions of Items 1 and 2 of Article 1 of this Interpretation.  
Counterclaim cases shall be governed by the provisions on private prosecution cases and shall be tried together with the private prosecution case. If the private prosecutor withdraws the prosecution, it shall not affect the continued trial of the counterclaim case.

### 第十一章 单位犯罪案件的审理

<a id="art-335"></a>第三百三十五条 人民法院受理单位犯罪案件，除依照[本解释第二百一十八条](#art-218)的有关规定进行审查外，还应当审查起诉书是否列明被告单位的名称、住所地、联系方式，法定代表人、实际控制人、主要负责人以及代表被告单位出庭的诉讼代表人的姓名、职务、联系方式。需要人民检察院补充材料的，应当通知人民检察院在三日以内补送。

**Article 335** : When accepting a case of a unit crime, in addition to reviewing it in accordance with the relevant provisions of Article 218 of this Interpretation, the People's Court shall also review whether the indictment lists the name, domicile, and contact information of the defendant unit, and the names, positions, and contact information of its legal representative, actual controller, principal responsible person, and the litigation representative appearing in court on behalf of the defendant unit. Where the People's Procuratorate is required to supplement materials, it shall be notified to supplement and submit them within three days.

<a id="art-336"></a>第三百三十六条 被告单位的诉讼代表人，应当是法定代表人、实际控制人或者主要负责人；法定代表人、实际控制人或者主要负责人被指控为单位犯罪直接责任人员或者因客观原因无法出庭的，应当由被告单位委托其他负责人或者职工作为诉讼代表人。但是，有关人员被指控为单位犯罪直接责任人员或者知道案件情况、负有作证义务的除外。  
依据前款规定难以确定诉讼代表人的，可以由被告单位委托律师等单位以外的人员作为诉讼代表人。  
诉讼代表人不得同时担任被告单位或者被指控为单位犯罪直接责任人员的有关人员的辩护人。

**Article 336** : The litigation representative of a defendant unit shall be its legal representative, actual controller, or principal responsible person; where the legal representative, actual controller, or principal responsible person is charged as a person directly responsible for the unit crime, or is unable to appear in court due to objective reasons, the defendant unit shall entrust another responsible person or employee to serve as the litigation representative. However, this does not apply where such person is charged as a directly responsible person for the unit crime, or knows the circumstances of the case and has an obligation to testify.  
Where it is difficult to determine a litigation representative pursuant to the preceding paragraph, the defendant unit may entrust a lawyer or other person outside the unit to serve as the litigation representative.  
A litigation representative shall not simultaneously serve as the defender of the defendant unit or of the person charged as a directly responsible person for the unit crime.

<a id="art-337"></a>第三百三十七条 开庭审理单位犯罪案件，应当通知被告单位的诉讼代表人出庭；诉讼代表人不符合前条规定的，应当要求人民检察院另行确定。  
被告单位的诉讼代表人不出庭的，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）诉讼代表人系被告单位的法定代表人、实际控制人或者主要负责人，无正当理由拒不出庭的，可以拘传其到庭；因客观原因无法出庭，或者下落不明的，应当要求人民检察院另行确定诉讼代表人；  
（二）诉讼代表人系其他人员的，应当要求人民检察院另行确定诉讼代表人。

**Article 337** : When trying a unit crime case in court, the litigation representative of the defendant unit shall be notified to appear in court; if the litigation representative does not conform to the provisions of the preceding article, the People's Procuratorate shall be requested to determine another representative.  
Where the litigation representative of the defendant unit does not appear in court, the following circumstances shall be handled respectively:  
(1) if the litigation representative is the legal representative, actual controller, or principal responsible person of the defendant unit and refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons, he/she may be compelled to appear by summons; if he/she cannot appear due to objective reasons, or his/her whereabouts are unknown, the People's Procuratorate shall be requested to determine another litigation representative;  
(2) if the litigation representative is another person, the People's Procuratorate shall be requested to determine another litigation representative.

<a id="art-338"></a>第三百三十八条 被告单位的诉讼代表人享有刑事诉讼法规定的有关被告人的诉讼权利。开庭时，诉讼代表人席位置于审判台前左侧，与辩护人席并列。

**Article 338** : The litigation representative of a defendant unit shall enjoy the litigation rights of a defendant provided in the Criminal Procedure Law. At trial, the seat of the litigation representative shall be positioned on the left side in front of the judicial bench, side by side with the defender's seat.

<a id="art-339"></a>第三百三十九条 被告单位委托辩护人的，参照适用本解释的有关规定。

**Article 339** : Where a defendant unit retains a defender, the relevant provisions of this Interpretation shall apply by reference.

<a id="art-340"></a>第三百四十条 对应当认定为单位犯罪的案件，人民检察院只作为自然人犯罪起诉的，人民法院应当建议人民检察院对犯罪单位追加起诉。人民检察院仍以自然人犯罪起诉的，人民法院应当依法审理，按照单位犯罪直接负责的主管人员或者其他直接责任人员追究刑事责任，并援引刑法分则关于追究单位犯罪中直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员刑事责任的条款。

**Article 340** : For a case that should be determined as a unit crime, if the People's Procuratorate prosecutes it only as a crime committed by a natural person, the People's Court shall suggest that the People's Procuratorate additionally prosecute the criminal unit. If the People's Procuratorate still prosecutes it as a crime committed by a natural person, the People's Court shall try the case according to law, investigate the criminal liability of the directly responsible supervisors or other directly responsible personnel for the unit crime, and cite the provisions in the Specific Provisions of the Criminal Law concerning the investigation of criminal liability of the directly responsible supervisors and other directly responsible personnel in a unit crime.

<a id="art-341"></a>第三百四十一条 被告单位的违法所得及其他涉案财物，尚未被依法追缴或者查封、扣押、冻结的，人民法院应当决定追缴或者查封、扣押、冻结。

**Article 341** : Where the illegal gains and other property involved in the case of the defendant unit have not yet been recovered or sealed up, seized, or frozen according to law, the People's Court shall decide to recover or seal up, seize, or freeze them.

<a id="art-342"></a>第三百四十二条 为保证判决的执行，人民法院可以先行查封、扣押、冻结被告单位的财产，或者由被告单位提出担保。

**Article 342** : To ensure the enforcement of the judgment, the People's Court may seal up, seize, or freeze the property of the defendant unit in advance, or the defendant unit may provide security.

<a id="art-343"></a>第三百四十三条 采取查封、扣押、冻结等措施，应当严格依照法定程序进行，最大限度降低对被告单位正常生产经营活动的影响。

**Article 343** : Measures such as sealing up, seizing, or freezing shall be taken strictly in accordance with statutory procedures, so as to minimize the impact on the normal production and operation activities of the defendant unit.

<a id="art-344"></a>第三百四十四条 审判期间，被告单位被吊销营业执照、宣告破产但尚未完成清算、注销登记的，应当继续审理；被告单位被撤销、注销的，对单位犯罪直接负责的主管人员和其他直接责任人员应当继续审理。

**Article 344** : During the trial, if the defendant unit's business license is revoked, or it is declared bankrupt but has not yet completed liquidation or cancellation of registration, the trial shall continue; if the defendant unit is dissolved or cancelled, the trial of the directly responsible supervisors and other directly responsible personnel for the unit crime shall continue.

<a id="art-345"></a>第三百四十五条 审判期间，被告单位合并、分立的，应当将原单位列为被告单位，并注明合并、分立情况。对被告单位所判处的罚金以其在新单位的财产及收益为限。

**Article 345** : During the trial, if the defendant unit is merged or divided, the original unit shall be listed as the defendant unit, and the circumstances of the merger or division shall be noted. The fine imposed on the defendant unit shall be limited to its property and proceeds in the new unit.

<a id="art-346"></a>第三百四十六条 审理单位犯罪案件，本章没有规定的，参照适用本解释的有关规定。

**Article 346** : For trial of unit crime cases, where this Chapter has no provisions, the relevant provisions of this Interpretation shall apply by reference.

### 第十二章 认罪认罚案件的审理

<a id="art-347"></a>第三百四十七条 [刑事诉讼法第十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-15)规定的“认罪”，是指犯罪嫌疑人、被告人自愿如实供述自己的罪行，对指控的犯罪事实没有异议。  
[刑事诉讼法第十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-15)规定的“认罚”，是指犯罪嫌疑人、被告人真诚悔罪，愿意接受处罚。  
被告人认罪认罚的，可以依照[刑事诉讼法第十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-15)的规定，在程序上从简、实体上从宽处理。

**Article 347** : The 'admission of guilt' provided in Article 15 of the Criminal Procedure Law means that the criminal suspect or defendant voluntarily and truthfully confesses his/her crimes and has no objection to the charged criminal facts.  
The 'acceptance of punishment' provided in Article 15 of the Criminal Procedure Law means that the criminal suspect or defendant sincerely repents and is willing to accept punishment.  
Where a defendant admits guilt and accepts punishment, the case may, in accordance with Article 15 of the Criminal Procedure Law, be handled with simplified procedures in procedure and leniency in substance.

<a id="art-348"></a>第三百四十八条 对认罪认罚案件，应当根据案件情况，依法适用速裁程序、简易程序或者普通程序审理。

**Article 348** : For cases of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, the expedited procedure, summary procedure, or ordinary procedure shall be applied according to law based on the circumstances of the case.

<a id="art-349"></a>第三百四十九条 对人民检察院提起公诉的认罪认罚案件，人民法院应当重点审查以下内容：  
（一）人民检察院讯问犯罪嫌疑人时，是否告知其诉讼权利和认罪认罚的法律规定；  
（二）是否随案移送听取犯罪嫌疑人、辩护人或者值班律师、被害人及其诉讼代理人意见的笔录；  
（三）被告人与被害人达成调解、和解协议或者取得被害人谅解的，是否随案移送调解、和解协议、被害人谅解书等相关材料；  
（四）需要签署认罪认罚具结书的，是否随案移送具结书。  
未随案移送前款规定的材料的，应当要求人民检察院补充。

**Article 349** : For a case of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment for which the People's Procuratorate initiates a public prosecution, the People's Court shall focus on reviewing the following contents:  
(1) whether, when interrogating the criminal suspect, the People's Procuratorate informed him/her of his/her litigation rights and the legal provisions on admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment;  
(2) whether transcripts of opinions heard from the criminal suspect, defender, duty lawyer, victim, and the victim's litigation agent are transferred with the case file;  
(3) where the defendant and the victim have reached a mediation or settlement agreement or obtained the victim's understanding, whether relevant materials such as the mediation agreement, settlement agreement, and the victim's letter of understanding are transferred with the case file;  
(4) where an affidavit of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment is required to be signed, whether the affidavit is transferred with the case file.  
If the materials provided in the preceding paragraph are not transferred with the case file, the People's Procuratorate shall be requested to supplement them.

<a id="art-350"></a>第三百五十条 人民法院应当将被告人认罪认罚作为其是否具有社会危险性的重要考虑因素。被告人罪行较轻，采用非羁押性强制措施足以防止发生社会危险性的，应当依法适用非羁押性强制措施。

**Article 350** : The People's Court shall take the defendant's admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment as an important factor in determining whether he/she poses a social danger. Where the defendant's crime is relatively minor, and the application of non-custodial compulsory measures is sufficient to prevent the occurrence of social danger, non-custodial compulsory measures shall be applied according to law.

<a id="art-351"></a>第三百五十一条 对认罪认罚案件，法庭审理时应当告知被告人享有的诉讼权利和认罪认罚的法律规定，审查认罪认罚的自愿性和认罪认罚具结书内容的真实性、合法性。

**Article 351** : For cases of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, at the time of court trial, the defendant shall be informed of the litigation rights he/she enjoys and the legal provisions on admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, and the voluntariness of the admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment and the authenticity and legality of the contents of the affidavit of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment shall be reviewed.

<a id="art-352"></a>第三百五十二条 对认罪认罚案件，人民检察院起诉指控的事实清楚，但指控的罪名与审理认定的罪名不一致的，人民法院应当听取人民检察院、被告人及其辩护人对审理认定罪名的意见，依法作出判决。

**Article 352** : For a case of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, where the facts charged by the People's Procuratorate are clear, but the crime charged is inconsistent with the crime determined at trial, the People's Court shall hear the opinions of the People's Procuratorate, the defendant, and his/her defender on the crime determined at trial, and render a judgment according to law.

<a id="art-353"></a>第三百五十三条 对认罪认罚案件，人民法院经审理认为量刑建议明显不当，或者被告人、辩护人对量刑建议提出异议的，人民检察院可以调整量刑建议。人民检察院不调整或者调整后仍然明显不当的，人民法院应当依法作出判决。  
适用速裁程序审理认罪认罚案件，需要调整量刑建议的，应当在庭前或者当庭作出调整；调整量刑建议后，仍然符合速裁程序适用条件的，继续适用速裁程序审理。

**Article 353** : For a case of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, where, after trial, the People's Court considers that the sentencing recommendation is clearly inappropriate, or the defendant or his/her defender raises an objection to the sentencing recommendation, the People's Procuratorate may adjust the sentencing recommendation. If the People's Procuratorate does not adjust it, or if it remains clearly inappropriate after adjustment, the People's Court shall render a judgment according to law.  
Where the expedited procedure is applied to try a case of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, and the sentencing recommendation needs to be adjusted, the adjustment shall be made before trial or during trial; if the conditions for applying the expedited procedure are still met after the adjustment, the expedited procedure shall continue to be applied.

<a id="art-354"></a>第三百五十四条 对量刑建议是否明显不当，应当根据审理认定的犯罪事实、认罪认罚的具体情况，结合相关犯罪的法定刑、类似案件的刑罚适用等作出审查判断。

**Article 354** : Whether a sentencing recommendation is clearly inappropriate shall be reviewed and determined on the basis of the criminal facts determined at trial, the specific circumstances of the admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, in combination with the statutory penalty for the relevant crime, the application of penalties in similar cases, and other factors.

<a id="art-355"></a>第三百五十五条 对认罪认罚案件，人民法院一般应当对被告人从轻处罚；符合非监禁刑适用条件的，应当适用非监禁刑；具有法定减轻处罚情节的，可以减轻处罚。  
对认罪认罚案件，应当根据被告人认罪认罚的阶段早晚以及认罪认罚的主动性、稳定性、彻底性等，在从宽幅度上体现差异。  
共同犯罪案件，部分被告人认罪认罚的，可以依法对该部分被告人从宽处罚，但应当注意全案的量刑平衡。

**Article 355** : For a case of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, the People's Court shall generally impose a lighter punishment on the defendant; where the conditions for applying non-custodial sentences are met, a non-custodial sentence shall be applied; where there is a statutory circumstance for mitigated punishment, the punishment may be mitigated.  
For cases of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, differences in the extent of leniency shall be reflected according to the stage at which the defendant admitted guilt and accepted punishment, as well as the initiative, stability, and thoroughness of such admission and acceptance.  
In a joint crime case, where some defendants admit guilt and accept punishment, lenient punishment may be given to those defendants according to law, but attention shall be paid to the balance of sentencing for the entire case.

<a id="art-356"></a>第三百五十六条 被告人在人民检察院提起公诉前未认罪认罚，在审判阶段认罪认罚的，人民法院可以不再通知人民检察院提出或者调整量刑建议。  
对前款规定的案件，人民法院应当就定罪量刑听取控辩双方意见，根据[刑事诉讼法第十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-15)和[本解释第三百五十五条](#art-355)的规定作出判决。

**Article 356** : Where a defendant did not admit guilt and accept punishment before the People's Procuratorate initiated a public prosecution, but does so during the trial stage, the People's Court may no longer notify the People's Procuratorate to propose or adjust a sentencing recommendation.  
For a case provided in the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall hear the opinions of both the prosecution and the defense on conviction and sentencing, and render a judgment in accordance with Article 15 of the Criminal Procedure Law and Article 355 of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-357"></a>第三百五十七条 对被告人在第一审程序中未认罪认罚，在第二审程序中认罪认罚的案件，应当根据其认罪认罚的具体情况决定是否从宽，并依法作出裁判。确定从宽幅度时应当与第一审程序认罪认罚有所区别。

**Article 357** : For a case where the defendant did not admit guilt and accept punishment in the first-instance procedure, but admits guilt and accepts punishment in the second-instance procedure, whether to grant leniency shall be decided based on the specific circumstances of his/her admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, and a ruling shall be made according to law. When determining the extent of leniency, a distinction shall be made from cases where guilt is admitted and punishment is accepted in the first-instance procedure.

<a id="art-358"></a>第三百五十八条 案件审理过程中，被告人不再认罪认罚的，人民法院应当根据审理查明的事实，依法作出裁判。需要转换程序的，依照本解释的相关规定处理。

**Article 358** : During the trial, if the defendant no longer admits guilt and accepts punishment, the People's Court shall render a ruling according to law based on the facts found at trial. If the procedure needs to be changed, it shall be handled in accordance with the relevant provisions of this Interpretation.

### 第十三章 简易程序

<a id="art-359"></a>第三百五十九条 基层人民法院受理公诉案件后，经审查认为案件事实清楚、证据充分的，在将起诉书副本送达被告人时，应当询问被告人对指控的犯罪事实的意见，告知其适用简易程序的法律规定。被告人对指控的犯罪事实没有异议并同意适用简易程序的，可以决定适用简易程序，并在开庭前通知人民检察院和辩护人。  
对人民检察院建议或者被告人及其辩护人申请适用简易程序审理的案件，依照前款规定处理；不符合简易程序适用条件的，应当通知人民检察院或者被告人及其辩护人。

**Article 359** : After a primary people's court accepts a case of public prosecution, if upon examination it considers that the facts of the case are clear and the evidence is sufficient, when serving a copy of the indictment to the defendant, it shall ask the defendant for his opinion on the alleged criminal facts and inform him of the legal provisions on applying the simplified procedure. If the defendant has no objection to the alleged criminal facts and agrees to apply the simplified procedure, the court may decide to apply the simplified procedure and shall notify the people's procuratorate and the defender before the court session.  
For cases recommended by the people's procuratorate or applied for by the defendant or his defender to be tried under the simplified procedure, the provisions of the preceding paragraph shall be followed; if the conditions for applying the simplified procedure are not met, the people's procuratorate or the defendant or his defender shall be notified.

<a id="art-360"></a>第三百六十条 具有下列情形之一的，不适用简易程序：  
（一）被告人是盲、聋、哑人的；  
（二）被告人是尚未完全丧失辨认或者控制自己行为能力的精神病人的；  
（三）案件有重大社会影响的；  
（四）共同犯罪案件中部分被告人不认罪或者对适用简易程序有异议的；  
（五）辩护人作无罪辩护的；  
（六）被告人认罪但经审查认为可能不构成犯罪的；  
（七）不宜适用简易程序审理的其他情形。

**Article 360** : The simplified procedure shall not be applied under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the defendant is blind, deaf, or mute;  
(2) the defendant is a mental patient who has not completely lost the ability to recognize or control his own conduct;  
(3) the case has major social impact;  
(4) in a joint crime case, some defendants do not admit guilt or object to the application of the simplified procedure;  
(5) the defender makes a defense of not guilty;  
(6) the defendant admits guilt but upon examination it is considered that the act may not constitute a crime;  
(7) other circumstances where the simplified procedure is not suitable to be applied.

<a id="art-361"></a>第三百六十一条 适用简易程序审理的案件，符合[刑事诉讼法第三十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-35)第一款规定的，人民法院应当告知被告人及其近亲属可以申请法律援助。

**Article 361** : For a case tried under the simplified procedure, if the provisions of Article 35, paragraph 1 of the Criminal Procedure Law are satisfied, the people's court shall inform the defendant and his close relatives that they may apply for legal aid.

<a id="art-362"></a>第三百六十二条 适用简易程序审理案件，人民法院应当在开庭前将开庭的时间、地点通知人民检察院、自诉人、被告人、辩护人，也可以通知其他诉讼参与人。  
通知可以采用简便方式，但应当记录在案。

**Article 362** : When trying a case under the simplified procedure, the people's court shall notify the people's procuratorate, the private prosecutor, the defendant, and the defender of the time and place of the court session before the session, and may also notify other litigation participants.  
The notification may be made in a simplified manner, but it shall be recorded in the case file.

<a id="art-363"></a>第三百六十三条 适用简易程序审理案件，被告人有辩护人的，应当通知其出庭。

**Article 363** : When trying a case under the simplified procedure, if the defendant has a defender, the court shall notify the defender to appear in court.

<a id="art-364"></a>第三百六十四条 适用简易程序审理案件，审判长或者独任审判员应当当庭询问被告人对指控的犯罪事实的意见，告知被告人适用简易程序审理的法律规定，确认被告人是否同意适用简易程序。

**Article 364** : When trying a case under the simplified procedure, the presiding judge or the sole judge shall ask the defendant in court about his opinion on the alleged criminal facts, inform the defendant of the legal provisions on trial under the simplified procedure, and confirm whether the defendant agrees to apply the simplified procedure.

<a id="art-365"></a>第三百六十五条 适用简易程序审理案件，可以对庭审作如下简化：  
（一）公诉人可以摘要宣读起诉书；  
（二）公诉人、辩护人、审判人员对被告人的讯问、发问可以简化或者省略；  
（三）对控辩双方无异议的证据，可以仅就证据的名称及所证明的事项作出说明；对控辩双方有异议或者法庭认为有必要调查核实的证据，应当出示，并进行质证；  
（四）控辩双方对与定罪量刑有关的事实、证据没有异议的，法庭审理可以直接围绕罪名确定和量刑问题进行。  
适用简易程序审理案件，判决宣告前应当听取被告人的最后陈述。

**Article 365** : When trying a case under the simplified procedure, the court session may be simplified as follows:  
(1) the public prosecutor may read the indictment in an abridged form;  
(2) the questioning and examination of the defendant by the public prosecutor, defender, and judicial personnel may be simplified or omitted;  
(3) as to evidence on which both the prosecution and defense have no objection, a statement may be made only of the name of the evidence and the matters it proves; evidence on which the prosecution and defense have objections or which the court considers necessary to investigate and verify shall be produced and subjected to cross-examination;  
(4) if the prosecution and defense have no objection to the facts and evidence relevant to conviction and sentencing, the court trial may directly focus on the determination of the charge and the issue of sentencing.  
When trying a case under the simplified procedure, the final statement of the defendant shall be heard before the judgment is pronounced.

<a id="art-366"></a>第三百六十六条 适用简易程序独任审判过程中，发现对被告人可能判处的有期徒刑超过三年的，应当转由合议庭审理。

**Article 366** : During sole-judge trial under the simplified procedure, if it is discovered that the defendant may be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of more than three years, the case shall be transferred to a collegiate bench for trial.

<a id="art-367"></a>第三百六十七条 适用简易程序审理案件，裁判文书可以简化。  
适用简易程序审理案件，一般应当当庭宣判。

**Article 367** : For cases tried under the simplified procedure, the written judgment may be simplified.  
For cases tried under the simplified procedure, judgment shall generally be pronounced in court.

<a id="art-368"></a>第三百六十八条 适用简易程序审理案件，在法庭审理过程中，具有下列情形之一的，应当转为普通程序审理：  
（一）被告人的行为可能不构成犯罪的；  
（二）被告人可能不负刑事责任的；  
（三）被告人当庭对起诉指控的犯罪事实予以否认的；  
（四）案件事实不清、证据不足的；  
（五）不应当或者不宜适用简易程序的其他情形。  
决定转为普通程序审理的案件，审理期限应当从作出决定之日起计算。

**Article 368** : When trying a case under the simplified procedure, if during court trial any of the following circumstances exists, the case shall be transferred to the ordinary procedure:  
(1) the defendant's act may not constitute a crime;  
(2) the defendant may not bear criminal liability;  
(3) the defendant denies in court the criminal facts alleged in the prosecution;  
(4) the facts of the case are unclear or the evidence is insufficient;  
(5) other circumstances where the simplified procedure should not or is not suitable to be applied.  
For a case decided to be transferred to the ordinary procedure, the trial period shall be calculated from the date on which the decision is made.

### 第十四章 速裁程序

<a id="art-369"></a>第三百六十九条 对人民检察院在提起公诉时建议适用速裁程序的案件，基层人民法院经审查认为案件事实清楚，证据确实、充分，可能判处三年有期徒刑以下刑罚的，在将起诉书副本送达被告人时，应当告知被告人适用速裁程序的法律规定，询问其是否同意适用速裁程序。被告人同意适用速裁程序的，可以决定适用速裁程序，并在开庭前通知人民检察院和辩护人。  
对人民检察院未建议适用速裁程序的案件，人民法院经审查认为符合速裁程序适用条件的，可以决定适用速裁程序，并在开庭前通知人民检察院和辩护人。  
被告人及其辩护人可以向人民法院提出适用速裁程序的申请。

**Article 369** : For a case in which the people's procuratorate recommends applying the fast-track adjudication procedure when initiating public prosecution, if the primary people's court upon examination considers that the facts of the case are clear, the evidence is reliable and sufficient, and the possible punishment is fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years, when serving a copy of the indictment to the defendant, it shall inform the defendant of the legal provisions on applying the fast-track adjudication procedure and ask whether he agrees to apply the fast-track adjudication procedure. If the defendant agrees to apply the fast-track adjudication procedure, the court may decide to apply it and shall notify the people's procuratorate and the defender before the court session.  
For a case for which the people's procuratorate does not recommend applying the fast-track adjudication procedure, if the people's court upon examination considers that the conditions for applying the fast-track adjudication procedure are met, it may decide to apply the fast-track adjudication procedure and shall notify the people's procuratorate and the defender before the court session.  
The defendant and his defender may file an application with the people's court for applying the fast-track adjudication procedure.

<a id="art-370"></a>第三百七十条 具有下列情形之一的，不适用速裁程序：  
（一）被告人是盲、聋、哑人的；  
（二）被告人是尚未完全丧失辨认或者控制自己行为能力的精神病人的；  
（三）被告人是未成年人的；  
（四）案件有重大社会影响的；  
（五）共同犯罪案件中部分被告人对指控的犯罪事实、罪名、量刑建议或者适用速裁程序有异议的；  
（六）被告人与被害人或者其法定代理人没有就附带民事诉讼赔偿等事项达成调解、和解协议的；  
（七）辩护人作无罪辩护的；  
（八）其他不宜适用速裁程序的情形。

**Article 370** : The fast-track adjudication procedure shall not be applied under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the defendant is blind, deaf, or mute;  
(2) the defendant is a mental patient who has not completely lost the ability to recognize or control his own conduct;  
(3) the defendant is a minor;  
(4) the case has major social impact;  
(5) in a joint crime case, some defendants object to the alleged criminal facts, the charges, sentencing recommendations, or the application of the fast-track adjudication procedure;  
(6) the defendant and the victim or his legal representative have not reached a mediation or reconciliation agreement on matters such as compensation in collateral civil action;  
(7) the defender makes a defense of not guilty;  
(8) other circumstances where the fast-track adjudication procedure is not suitable to be applied.

<a id="art-371"></a>第三百七十一条 适用速裁程序审理案件，人民法院应当在开庭前将开庭的时间、地点通知人民检察院、被告人、辩护人，也可以通知其他诉讼参与人。  
通知可以采用简便方式，但应当记录在案。

**Article 371** : When trying a case under the fast-track adjudication procedure, the people's court shall notify the people's procuratorate, the defendant, and the defender of the time and place of the court session before the session, and may also notify other litigation participants.  
The notification may be made in a simplified manner, but it shall be recorded in the case file.

<a id="art-372"></a>第三百七十二条 适用速裁程序审理案件，可以集中开庭，逐案审理。公诉人简要宣读起诉书后，审判人员应当当庭询问被告人对指控事实、证据、量刑建议以及适用速裁程序的意见，核实具结书签署的自愿性、真实性、合法性，并核实附带民事诉讼赔偿等情况。

**Article 372** : When trying cases under the fast-track adjudication procedure, court sessions may be held collectively, with cases tried one by one. After the public prosecutor briefly reads the indictment, the judicial personnel shall ask the defendant in court for his opinions on the alleged facts, evidence, sentencing recommendation, and the application of the fast-track adjudication procedure; verify the voluntariness, authenticity, and legality of the signing of the plea affidavit; and verify compensation in the collateral civil action and other matters.

<a id="art-373"></a>第三百七十三条 适用速裁程序审理案件，一般不进行法庭调查、法庭辩论，但在判决宣告前应当听取辩护人的意见和被告人的最后陈述。

**Article 373** : When trying a case under the fast-track adjudication procedure, court investigation and court debate shall generally not be conducted, but before judgment is pronounced, the opinions of the defender and the final statement of the defendant shall be heard.

<a id="art-374"></a>第三百七十四条 适用速裁程序审理案件，裁判文书可以简化。  
适用速裁程序审理案件，应当当庭宣判。

**Article 374** : For cases tried under the fast-track adjudication procedure, the written judgment may be simplified.  
For cases tried under the fast-track adjudication procedure, judgment shall be pronounced in court.

<a id="art-375"></a>第三百七十五条 适用速裁程序审理案件，在法庭审理过程中，具有下列情形之一的，应当转为普通程序或者简易程序审理：  
（一）被告人的行为可能不构成犯罪或者不应当追究刑事责任的；  
（二）被告人违背意愿认罪认罚的；  
（三）被告人否认指控的犯罪事实的；  
（四）案件疑难、复杂或者对适用法律有重大争议的；  
（五）其他不宜适用速裁程序的情形。

**Article 375** : When trying a case under the fast-track adjudication procedure, if during court trial any of the following circumstances exists, the case shall be transferred to the ordinary procedure or the simplified procedure:  
(1) the defendant's act may not constitute a crime or the defendant should not be investigated for criminal liability;  
(2) the defendant admits guilt and accepts punishment against his will;  
(3) the defendant denies the alleged criminal facts;  
(4) the case is difficult or complex, or there is major controversy over the application of law;  
(5) other circumstances where the fast-track adjudication procedure is not suitable to be applied.

<a id="art-376"></a>第三百七十六条 决定转为普通程序或者简易程序审理的案件，审理期限应当从作出决定之日起计算。

**Article 376** : For a case decided to be transferred to the ordinary procedure or the simplified procedure, the trial period shall be calculated from the date on which the decision is made.

<a id="art-377"></a>第三百七十七条 适用速裁程序审理的案件，第二审人民法院依照[刑事诉讼法第二百三十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-236)第一款第三项的规定发回原审人民法院重新审判的，原审人民法院应当适用第一审普通程序重新审判。

**Article 377** : For a case tried under the fast-track adjudication procedure, if the second-instance people's court, pursuant to Article 236, paragraph 1, item (3) of the Criminal Procedure Law, remands the case to the original trial people's court for retrial, the original trial people's court shall apply the ordinary procedure of first instance for the retrial.

### 第十五章 第二审程序

<a id="art-378"></a>第三百七十八条 地方各级人民法院在宣告第一审判决、裁定时，应当告知被告人、自诉人及其法定代理人不服判决和准许撤回起诉、终止审理等裁定的，有权在法定期限内以书面或者口头形式，通过本院或者直接向上一级人民法院提出上诉；被告人的辩护人、近亲属经被告人同意，也可以提出上诉；附带民事诉讼当事人及其法定代理人，可以对判决、裁定中的附带民事部分提出上诉。  
被告人、自诉人、附带民事诉讼当事人及其法定代理人是否提出上诉，以其在上诉期满前最后一次的意思表示为准。

**Article 378** : When local people's courts at all levels pronounce first-instance judgments or rulings, they shall inform defendants, private prosecutors, and their legal representatives that, if they do not accept a judgment or a ruling such as one permitting withdrawal of prosecution or terminating the case, they have the right to file an appeal within the statutory time limit in writing or orally through the court itself or directly with the people's court at the next higher level; defenders and close relatives of the defendant, with the defendant's consent, may also file an appeal; parties to a collateral civil action and their legal representatives may file an appeal against the collateral civil part of a judgment or ruling.  
Whether the defendant, private prosecutor, party to a collateral civil action, or his legal representative files an appeal shall be determined by his last expression of intention before the expiration of the appeal period.

<a id="art-379"></a>第三百七十九条 人民法院受理的上诉案件，一般应当有上诉状正本及副本。  
上诉状内容一般包括：第一审判决书、裁定书的文号和上诉人收到的时间，第一审人民法院的名称，上诉的请求和理由，提出上诉的时间。被告人的辩护人、近亲属经被告人同意提出上诉的，还应当写明其与被告人的关系，并应当以被告人作为上诉人。

**Article 379** : An appeal case accepted by a people's court shall generally have an original and a copy of the written appeal.  
The contents of the written appeal shall generally include: the document number of the first-instance judgment or ruling and the time when the appellant received it, the name of the first-instance people's court, the request and reasons for the appeal, and the time when the appeal is filed. If the defendant's defender or close relative files an appeal with the defendant's consent, the relationship with the defendant shall also be stated, and the defendant shall be listed as the appellant.

<a id="art-380"></a>第三百八十条 上诉、抗诉必须在法定期限内提出。不服判决的上诉、抗诉的期限为十日；不服裁定的上诉、抗诉的期限为五日。上诉、抗诉的期限，从接到判决书、裁定书的第二日起计算。  
对附带民事判决、裁定的上诉、抗诉期限，应当按照刑事部分的上诉、抗诉期限确定。附带民事部分另行审判的，上诉期限也应当按照刑事诉讼法规定的期限确定。

**Article 380** : An appeal or protest must be filed within the statutory time limit. The time limit for appeal or protest against a judgment is ten days; the time limit for appeal or protest against a ruling is five days. The time limit for appeal or protest shall be calculated from the second day after receipt of the judgment or ruling.  
The time limit for appeal or protest against a collateral civil judgment or ruling shall be determined in accordance with the time limit for appeal or protest against the criminal part. Where the collateral civil part is tried separately, the appeal period shall also be determined in accordance with the time limit prescribed by the Criminal Procedure Law.

<a id="art-381"></a>第三百八十一条 上诉人通过第一审人民法院提出上诉的，第一审人民法院应当审查。上诉符合法律规定的，应当在上诉期满后三日以内将上诉状连同案卷、证据移送上一级人民法院，并将上诉状副本送交同级人民检察院和对方当事人。

**Article 381** : Where an appellant files an appeal through the first-instance people's court, the first-instance people's court shall examine it. If the appeal complies with the legal provisions, within three days after the expiration of the appeal period, it shall transfer the written appeal together with the case file and evidence to the people's court at the next higher level, and deliver a copy of the written appeal to the people's procuratorate at the same level and the opposing party.

<a id="art-382"></a>第三百八十二条 上诉人直接向第二审人民法院提出上诉的，第二审人民法院应当在收到上诉状后三日以内将上诉状交第一审人民法院。第一审人民法院应当审查上诉是否符合法律规定。符合法律规定的，应当在接到上诉状后三日以内将上诉状连同案卷、证据移送上一级人民法院，并将上诉状副本送交同级人民检察院和对方当事人。

**Article 382** : Where an appellant directly files an appeal with the second-instance people's court, the second-instance people's court shall, within three days after receiving the written appeal, deliver it to the first-instance people's court. The first-instance people's court shall examine whether the appeal complies with the legal provisions. If it does, within three days after receiving the written appeal, it shall transfer the written appeal together with the case file and evidence to the people's court at the next higher level, and deliver a copy of the written appeal to the people's procuratorate at the same level and the opposing party.

<a id="art-383"></a>第三百八十三条 上诉人在上诉期限内要求撤回上诉的，人民法院应当准许。  
上诉人在上诉期满后要求撤回上诉的，第二审人民法院经审查，认为原判认定事实和适用法律正确，量刑适当的，应当裁定准许；认为原判确有错误的，应当不予准许，继续按照上诉案件审理。  
被判处死刑立即执行的被告人提出上诉，在第二审开庭后宣告裁判前申请撤回上诉的，应当不予准许，继续按照上诉案件审理。

**Article 383** : Where an appellant requests to withdraw the appeal within the appeal period, the people's court shall permit it.  
Where an appellant requests to withdraw the appeal after the expiration of the appeal period, the second-instance people's court shall, upon examination, rule to permit the withdrawal if it considers that the facts found and the application of law in the original judgment are correct and the sentence is appropriate; if it considers that the original judgment is indeed erroneous, it shall not permit the withdrawal and shall continue to try the case as an appeal case.  
Where a defendant sentenced to death with immediate execution files an appeal and, after the second-instance court session but before the judgment is pronounced, applies to withdraw the appeal, the application shall not be permitted, and the case shall continue to be tried as an appeal case.

<a id="art-384"></a>第三百八十四条 地方各级人民检察院对同级人民法院第一审判决、裁定的抗诉，应当通过第一审人民法院提交抗诉书。第一审人民法院应当在抗诉期满后三日以内将抗诉书连同案卷、证据移送上一级人民法院，并将抗诉书副本送交当事人。

**Article 384** : A local people's procuratorate at any level that protests a first-instance judgment or ruling of a people's court at the corresponding level shall submit the written protest through the first-instance people's court. The first-instance people's court shall, within three days after the expiration of the protest period, transfer the written protest together with the case file and evidence to the people's court at the next higher level, and deliver a copy of the written protest to the parties.

<a id="art-385"></a>第三百八十五条 人民检察院在抗诉期限内要求撤回抗诉的，人民法院应当准许。  
人民检察院在抗诉期满后要求撤回抗诉的，第二审人民法院可以裁定准许，但是认为原判存在将无罪判为有罪、轻罪重判等情形的，应当不予准许，继续审理。  
上级人民检察院认为下级人民检察院抗诉不当，向第二审人民法院要求撤回抗诉的，适用前两款规定。

**Article 385** : Where a people's procuratorate requests to withdraw its protest within the protest period, the people's court shall permit it.  
Where a people's procuratorate requests to withdraw its protest after the expiration of the protest period, the second-instance people's court may rule to permit it; however, if it considers that the original judgment has circumstances such as finding a person guilty despite innocence or imposing a heavy sentence for a minor crime, it shall not permit the withdrawal and shall continue the trial.  
Where a higher people's procuratorate considers that the protest by a lower people's procuratorate is improper and requests the second-instance people's court to withdraw the protest, the preceding two paragraphs shall apply.

<a id="art-386"></a>第三百八十六条 在上诉、抗诉期满前撤回上诉、抗诉的，第一审判决、裁定在上诉、抗诉期满之日起生效。在上诉、抗诉期满后要求撤回上诉、抗诉，第二审人民法院裁定准许的，第一审判决、裁定应当自第二审裁定书送达上诉人或者抗诉机关之日起生效。

**Article 386** : Where an appeal or protest is withdrawn before the expiration of the appeal or protest period, the first-instance judgment or ruling shall take effect from the date on which the appeal or protest period expires. Where an appeal or protest is requested to be withdrawn after the expiration of the appeal or protest period and the second-instance people's court rules to permit it, the first-instance judgment or ruling shall take effect from the date on which the second-instance written ruling is served on the appellant or the protesting organ.

<a id="art-387"></a>第三百八十七条 第二审人民法院对第一审人民法院移送的上诉、抗诉案卷、证据，应当审查是否包括下列内容：  
（一）移送上诉、抗诉案件函；  
（二）上诉状或者抗诉书；  
（三）第一审判决书、裁定书八份（每增加一名被告人增加一份）及其电子文本；  
（四）全部案卷、证据，包括案件审理报告和其他应当移送的材料。  
前款所列材料齐全的，第二审人民法院应当收案；材料不全的，应当通知第一审人民法院及时补送。

**Article 387** : The second-instance people's court shall, with respect to the appeal or protest case file and evidence transferred by the first-instance people's court, examine whether they include the following contents:  
(1) a letter transferring the appeal or protest case;  
(2) the written appeal or written protest;  
(3) eight copies of the first-instance judgment or ruling (plus one copy for each additional defendant) and their electronic texts;  
(4) all case files and evidence, including the case trial report and other materials that should be transferred.  
If the materials listed in the preceding paragraph are complete, the second-instance people's court shall accept the case; if the materials are incomplete, it shall notify the first-instance people's court to make supplementary delivery in a timely manner.

<a id="art-388"></a>第三百八十八条 第二审人民法院审理上诉、抗诉案件，应当就第一审判决、裁定认定的事实和适用法律进行全面审查，不受上诉、抗诉范围的限制。

**Article 388** : When trying an appeal or protest case, the second-instance people's court shall conduct a comprehensive review of the facts found and the law applied in the first-instance judgment or ruling, and shall not be limited to the scope of the appeal or protest.

<a id="art-389"></a>第三百八十九条 共同犯罪案件，只有部分被告人提出上诉，或者自诉人只对部分被告人的判决提出上诉，或者人民检察院只对部分被告人的判决提出抗诉的，第二审人民法院应当对全案进行审查，一并处理。

**Article 389** : In a joint crime case, if only some defendants file appeals, or the private prosecutor appeals only against the judgments of some defendants, or the people's procuratorate protests only against the judgments of some defendants, the second-instance people's court shall review the entire case and dispose of it as a whole.

<a id="art-390"></a>第三百九十条 共同犯罪案件，上诉的被告人死亡，其他被告人未上诉的，第二审人民法院应当对死亡的被告人终止审理；但有证据证明被告人无罪，经缺席审理确认无罪的，应当判决宣告被告人无罪。  
具有前款规定的情形，第二审人民法院仍应对全案进行审查，对其他同案被告人作出判决、裁定。

**Article 390** : In a joint crime case, if a defendant who appeals dies and the other defendants do not appeal, the second-instance people's court shall terminate the trial of the deceased defendant; however, if there is evidence proving that the defendant is innocent and the innocence is confirmed after trial in absentia, the court shall pronounce a judgment declaring the defendant innocent.  
Where the circumstance provided in the preceding paragraph exists, the second-instance people's court shall still review the entire case and render judgments or rulings with respect to the other co-defendants.

<a id="art-391"></a>第三百九十一条 对上诉、抗诉案件，应当着重审查下列内容：  
（一）第一审判决认定的事实是否清楚，证据是否确实、充分；  
（二）第一审判决适用法律是否正确，量刑是否适当；  
（三）在调查、侦查、审查起诉、第一审程序中，有无违反法定程序的情形；  
（四）上诉、抗诉是否提出新的事实、证据；  
（五）被告人的供述和辩解情况；  
（六）辩护人的辩护意见及采纳情况；  
（七）附带民事部分的判决、裁定是否合法、适当；  
（八）对涉案财物的处理是否正确；  
（九）第一审人民法院合议庭、审判委员会讨论的意见。

**Article 391** : For appeal and protest cases, the following contents shall be examined with emphasis:  
(1) whether the facts found in the first-instance judgment are clear and whether the evidence is reliable and sufficient;  
(2) whether the law applied in the first-instance judgment is correct and whether the sentence is appropriate;  
(3) whether there were any circumstances violating statutory procedures during investigation, criminal investigation, examination and prosecution, and the first-instance proceedings;  
(4) whether the appeal or protest raises new facts or evidence;  
(5) the situation of the defendant's statements and defenses;  
(6) the defender's defense opinions and whether they were adopted;  
(7) whether the judgment or ruling on the collateral civil part is lawful and appropriate;  
(8) whether the disposition of property involved in the case is correct;  
(9) the opinions of the collegiate bench and the adjudication committee of the first-instance people's court.

<a id="art-392"></a>第三百九十二条 第二审期间，被告人除自行辩护外，还可以继续委托第一审辩护人或者另行委托辩护人辩护。  
共同犯罪案件，只有部分被告人提出上诉，或者自诉人只对部分被告人的判决提出上诉，或者人民检察院只对部分被告人的判决提出抗诉的，其他同案被告人也可以委托辩护人辩护。

**Article 392** : During the second instance, in addition to defending himself, the defendant may continue to retain the first-instance defender or separately retain another defender to defend him.  
In a joint crime case, if only some defendants file appeals, or the private prosecutor appeals only against the judgments of some defendants, or the people's procuratorate protests only against the judgments of some defendants, other co-defendants in the same case may also retain defenders to defend them.

<a id="art-393"></a>第三百九十三条 下列案件，根据[刑事诉讼法第二百三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-234)的规定，应当开庭审理：  
（一）被告人、自诉人及其法定代理人对第一审认定的事实、证据提出异议，可能影响定罪量刑的上诉案件；  
（二）被告人被判处死刑的上诉案件；  
（三）人民检察院抗诉的案件；  
（四）应当开庭审理的其他案件。  
被判处死刑的被告人没有上诉，同案的其他被告人上诉的案件，第二审人民法院应当开庭审理。

**Article 393** : The following cases shall, in accordance with Article 234 of the Criminal Procedure Law, be tried in court:  
(1) appeal cases in which the defendant, private prosecutor, or their legal representatives raise objections to the facts or evidence found in the first instance, which may affect conviction and sentencing;  
(2) appeal cases in which the defendant is sentenced to death;  
(3) cases protested by the people's procuratorate;  
(4) other cases that should be tried in court.  
Where a defendant sentenced to death does not appeal but other co-defendants in the same case appeal, the second-instance people's court shall try the case in court.

<a id="art-394"></a>第三百九十四条 对上诉、抗诉案件，第二审人民法院经审查，认为原判事实不清、证据不足，或者具有[刑事诉讼法第二百三十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-238)规定的违反法定诉讼程序情形，需要发回重新审判的，可以不开庭审理。

**Article 394** : With respect to appeal and protest cases, if the second-instance people's court upon examination considers that the facts of the original judgment are unclear, the evidence is insufficient, or there are circumstances of violation of statutory litigation procedures as prescribed in Article 238 of the Criminal Procedure Law, and the case needs to be remanded for retrial, the court may decide not to hold a court session.

<a id="art-395"></a>第三百九十五条 第二审期间，人民检察院或者被告人及其辩护人提交新证据的，人民法院应当及时通知对方查阅、摘抄或者复制。

**Article 395** : During the second instance, if the people's procuratorate or the defendant and his defender submit new evidence, the people's court shall promptly notify the other party to inspect, excerpt, or copy it.

<a id="art-396"></a>第三百九十六条 开庭审理第二审公诉案件，应当在决定开庭审理后及时通知人民检察院查阅案卷。自通知后的第二日起，人民检察院查阅案卷的时间不计入审理期限。

**Article 396** : When a second-instance public prosecution case is to be tried in court, the people's court shall promptly notify the people's procuratorate after deciding to hold a court session to examine the case file. From the second day after the notice, the time for the people's procuratorate to examine the case file shall not be counted into the trial period.

<a id="art-397"></a>第三百九十七条 开庭审理上诉、抗诉的公诉案件，应当通知同级人民检察院派员出庭。  
抗诉案件，人民检察院接到开庭通知后不派员出庭，且未说明原因的，人民法院可以裁定按人民检察院撤回抗诉处理。

**Article 397** : When an appeal or protest public prosecution case is tried in court, the people's court shall notify the people's procuratorate at the same level to send personnel to appear in court.  
In a protest case, if the people's procuratorate does not send personnel to appear in court after receiving the notice and fails to state reasons, the people's court may rule to treat it as a withdrawal of the protest by the people's procuratorate.

<a id="art-398"></a>第三百九十八条 开庭审理上诉、抗诉案件，除参照适用第一审程序的有关规定外，应当按照下列规定进行：  
（一）法庭调查阶段，审判人员宣读第一审判决书、裁定书后，上诉案件由上诉人或者辩护人先宣读上诉状或者陈述上诉理由，抗诉案件由检察员先宣读抗诉书；既有上诉又有抗诉的案件，先由检察员宣读抗诉书，再由上诉人或者辩护人宣读上诉状或者陈述上诉理由；  
（二）法庭辩论阶段，上诉案件，先由上诉人、辩护人发言，后由检察员、诉讼代理人发言；抗诉案件，先由检察员、诉讼代理人发言，后由被告人、辩护人发言；既有上诉又有抗诉的案件，先由检察员、诉讼代理人发言，后由上诉人、辩护人发言。

**Article 398** : When trying an appeal or protest case in court, in addition to mutatis mutandis application of the relevant provisions of the first-instance procedure, the trial shall be conducted in accordance with the following provisions:  
(1) at the stage of court investigation, after the judicial personnel read the first-instance judgment or ruling, in appeal cases, the appellant or the defender shall first read the written appeal or state the reasons for appeal; in protest cases, the prosecutor shall first read the written protest; in cases with both appeal and protest, the prosecutor shall first read the written protest, and then the appellant or the defender shall read the written appeal or state the reasons for appeal;  
(2) at the stage of court debate, in appeal cases, the appellant and the defender shall speak first, and then the prosecutor and the litigation agent shall speak; in protest cases, the prosecutor and the litigation agent shall speak first, and then the defendant and the defender shall speak; in cases with both appeal and protest, the prosecutor and the litigation agent shall speak first, and then the appellant and the defender shall speak.

<a id="art-399"></a>第三百九十九条 开庭审理上诉、抗诉案件，可以重点围绕对第一审判决、裁定有争议的问题或者有疑问的部分进行。根据案件情况，可以按照下列方式审理：  
（一）宣读第一审判决书，可以只宣读案由、主要事实、证据名称和判决主文等；  
（二）法庭调查应当重点围绕对第一审判决提出异议的事实、证据以及新的证据等进行；对没有异议的事实、证据和情节，可以直接确认；  
（三）对同案审理案件中未上诉的被告人，未被申请出庭或者人民法院认为没有必要到庭的，可以不再传唤到庭；  
（四）被告人犯有数罪的案件，对其中事实清楚且无异议的犯罪，可以不在庭审时审理。  
同案审理的案件，未提出上诉、人民检察院也未对其判决提出抗诉的被告人要求出庭的，应当准许。出庭的被告人可以参加法庭调查和辩论。

**Article 399** : When trying an appeal or protest case in court, the trial may focus on the issues disputed or the parts in doubt in the first-instance judgment or ruling. Depending on the circumstances of the case, the trial may be conducted in the following ways:  
(1) when reading the first-instance judgment, only the cause of action, main facts, names of evidence, and the main text of the judgment may be read;  
(2) court investigation shall focus on the facts, evidence, and new evidence with respect to which objections are raised against the first-instance judgment; facts, evidence, and circumstances to which no objection is raised may be directly confirmed;  
(3) for defendants in a jointly tried case who did not appeal, if they are not requested to appear in court or the people's court considers it unnecessary for them to appear in court, they may no longer be summoned to appear;  
(4) in a case where the defendant has committed several crimes, the crimes for which the facts are clear and no objection is raised may not be tried during the court session.  
In a jointly tried case, if a defendant who did not file an appeal and against whose judgment the people's procuratorate did not file a protest requests to appear in court, the request shall be permitted. The defendant appearing in court may participate in court investigation and debate.

<a id="art-400"></a>第四百条 第二审案件依法不开庭审理的，应当讯问被告人，听取其他当事人、辩护人、诉讼代理人的意见。合议庭全体成员应当阅卷，必要时应当提交书面阅卷意见。

**Article 400** : Where a second-instance case is not tried in court according to law, the defendant shall be interrogated, and the opinions of other parties, defenders, and litigation agents shall be heard. All members of the collegiate bench shall review the case file, and if necessary, submit written opinions on the case file review.

<a id="art-401"></a>第四百零一条 审理被告人或者其法定代理人、辩护人、近亲属提出上诉的案件，不得对被告人的刑罚作出实质不利的改判，并应当执行下列规定：  
（一）同案审理的案件，只有部分被告人上诉的，既不得加重上诉人的刑罚，也不得加重其他同案被告人的刑罚；  
（二）原判认定的罪名不当的，可以改变罪名，但不得加重刑罚或者对刑罚执行产生不利影响；  
（三）原判认定的罪数不当的，可以改变罪数，并调整刑罚，但不得加重决定执行的刑罚或者对刑罚执行产生不利影响；  
（四）原判对被告人宣告缓刑的，不得撤销缓刑或者延长缓刑考验期；  
（五）原判没有宣告职业禁止、禁止令的，不得增加宣告；原判宣告职业禁止、禁止令的，不得增加内容、延长期限；  
（六）原判对被告人判处死刑缓期执行没有限制减刑、决定终身监禁的，不得限制减刑、决定终身监禁；  
（七）原判判处的刑罚不当、应当适用附加刑而没有适用的，不得直接加重刑罚、适用附加刑。原判判处的刑罚畸轻，必须依法改判的，应当在第二审判决、裁定生效后，依照审判监督程序重新审判。  
人民检察院抗诉或者自诉人上诉的案件，不受前款规定的限制。

**Article 401** . Where a case is tried in which the defendant or his legal representative, defender, or close relative files an appeal, no judgment substantially unfavorable to the defendant's punishment shall be made on appeal, and the following provisions shall be complied with:  
(1) Where, in a case tried jointly, only some of the defendants appeal, the punishment of neither the appellant nor the other co-defendants in the same case may be aggravated;  
(2) Where the crime determined in the original judgment is improper, the crime may be changed, but the punishment shall not be aggravated or adversely affected in the execution of the punishment;  
(3) Where the number of crimes determined in the original judgment is improper, the number of crimes may be changed and the punishment adjusted, but the punishment decided to be executed shall not be aggravated or adversely affected in the execution of the punishment;  
(4) Where the original judgment granted probation to the defendant, the probation shall not be revoked or the probation period extended;  
(5) Where the original judgment did not impose an occupational prohibition or a prohibition order, no such imposition shall be added; where the original judgment imposed an occupational prohibition or a prohibition order, its content shall not be increased or its term extended;  
(6) Where the original judgment sentenced the defendant to death with a two-year reprieve without restricting commutation or deciding on life-long imprisonment, commutation shall not be restricted or life-long imprisonment decided;  
(7) Where the punishment imposed in the original judgment is improper, or a supplementary punishment should have been imposed but was not, the punishment shall not be directly aggravated or a supplementary punishment imposed. Where the punishment imposed in the original judgment is obviously light and must be corrected according to law, a retrial shall be conducted under the procedure for trial supervision after the judgment or ruling of second instance takes effect.  
Cases in which the People's Procuratorate files a protest or the private prosecutor files an appeal shall not be subject to the restrictions in the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-402"></a>第四百零二条 人民检察院只对部分被告人的判决提出抗诉，或者自诉人只对部分被告人的判决提出上诉的，第二审人民法院不得对其他同案被告人加重刑罚。

**Article 402** . Where the People's Procuratorate files a protest only against the judgments concerning some of the defendants, or the private prosecutor files an appeal only against the judgments concerning some of the defendants, the People's Court of Second Instance shall not aggravate the punishment of the other co-defendants in the same case.

<a id="art-403"></a>第四百零三条 被告人或者其法定代理人、辩护人、近亲属提出上诉，人民检察院未提出抗诉的案件，第二审人民法院发回重新审判后，除有新的犯罪事实且人民检察院补充起诉的以外，原审人民法院不得加重被告人的刑罚。  
对前款规定的案件，原审人民法院对上诉发回重新审判的案件依法作出判决后，人民检察院抗诉的，第二审人民法院不得改判为重于原审人民法院第一次判处的刑罚。

**Article 403** . Where, in a case in which the defendant or his legal representative, defender, or close relative files an appeal and the People's Procuratorate does not file a protest, the People's Court of Second Instance remands the case for retrial, the original trial People's Court shall not aggravate the defendant's punishment, unless there are new criminal facts and the People's Procuratorate supplements the prosecution.  
With respect to the case specified in the preceding paragraph, where the original trial People's Court renders a judgment according to law on the case remanded for retrial upon appeal, and the People's Procuratorate then files a protest, the People's Court of Second Instance shall not change the judgment to a punishment heavier than that imposed in the first instance by the original trial People's Court.

<a id="art-404"></a>第四百零四条 第二审人民法院认为第一审判决事实不清、证据不足的，可以在查清事实后改判，也可以裁定撤销原判，发回原审人民法院重新审判。  
有多名被告人的案件，部分被告人的犯罪事实不清、证据不足或者有新的犯罪事实需要追诉，且有关犯罪与其他同案被告人没有关联的，第二审人民法院根据案件情况，可以对该部分被告人分案处理，将该部分被告人发回原审人民法院重新审判。原审人民法院重新作出判决后，被告人上诉或者人民检察院抗诉，其他被告人的案件尚未作出第二审判决、裁定的，第二审人民法院可以并案审理。

**Article 404** . Where the People's Court of Second Instance considers that the facts in the first-instance judgment are unclear or the evidence is insufficient, it may modify the judgment after ascertaining the facts, or it may rule to quash the original judgment and remand the case to the original trial People's Court for retrial.  
Where, in a case with multiple defendants, the criminal facts concerning some defendants are unclear or the evidence is insufficient, or there are new criminal facts to be prosecuted, and the relevant crimes are not related to the other co-defendants in the same case, the People's Court of Second Instance may, according to the circumstances of the case, handle the cases of such defendants separately and remand the cases of such defendants to the original trial People's Court for retrial. After the original trial People's Court renders a new judgment, if the defendants appeal or the People's Procuratorate files a protest, and the cases of the other defendants have not yet been decided by a judgment or ruling of second instance, the People's Court of Second Instance may consolidate the cases for trial.

<a id="art-405"></a>第四百零五条 原判事实不清、证据不足，第二审人民法院发回重新审判的案件，原审人民法院重新作出判决后，被告人上诉或者人民检察院抗诉的，第二审人民法院应当依法作出判决、裁定，不得再发回重新审判。

**Article 405** . Where a case is remanded for retrial by the People's Court of Second Instance because the facts in the original judgment are unclear or the evidence is insufficient, and after the original trial People's Court renders a new judgment, the defendant appeals or the People's Procuratorate files a protest, the People's Court of Second Instance shall render a judgment or ruling according to law and shall not remand the case for retrial again.

<a id="art-406"></a>第四百零六条 第二审人民法院发现原审人民法院在重新审判过程中，有[刑事诉讼法第二百三十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-238)规定的情形之一，或者违反第二百三十九条规定的，应当裁定撤销原判，发回重新审判。

**Article 406** . Where the People's Court of Second Instance finds that, during the retrial by the original trial People's Court, there is any circumstance provided for in Article 238 of the Criminal Procedure Law, or the provisions of Article 239 are violated, it shall rule to quash the original judgment and remand the case for retrial.

<a id="art-407"></a>第四百零七条 第二审人民法院审理对刑事部分提出上诉、抗诉，附带民事部分已经发生法律效力的案件，发现第一审判决、裁定中的附带民事部分确有错误的，应当依照审判监督程序对附带民事部分予以纠正。

**Article 407** . Where the People's Court of Second Instance tries a case in which an appeal or protest is filed against the criminal part and the incidental civil part has already taken legal effect, if it finds that the incidental civil part in the first-instance judgment or ruling is indeed erroneous, it shall correct the incidental civil part in accordance with the procedure for trial supervision.

<a id="art-408"></a>第四百零八条 刑事附带民事诉讼案件，只有附带民事诉讼当事人及其法定代理人上诉的，第一审刑事部分的判决在上诉期满后即发生法律效力。  
应当送监执行的第一审刑事被告人是第二审附带民事诉讼被告人的，在第二审附带民事诉讼案件审结前，可以暂缓送监执行。

**Article 408** . In a criminal case with an incidental civil action, if only the parties to the incidental civil action and their legal representatives appeal, the judgment on the criminal part of the first instance shall take legal effect upon expiration of the appeal period.  
Where a first-instance criminal defendant who should be delivered to prison for execution is also a defendant in the second-instance incidental civil action, the delivery to prison for execution may be suspended until the conclusion of the second-instance incidental civil action.

<a id="art-409"></a>第四百零九条 第二审人民法院审理对附带民事部分提出上诉，刑事部分已经发生法律效力的案件，应当对全案进行审查，并按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）第一审判决的刑事部分并无不当的，只需就附带民事部分作出处理；  
（二）第一审判决的刑事部分确有错误的，依照审判监督程序对刑事部分进行再审，并将附带民事部分与刑事部分一并审理。

**Article 409** . Where the People's Court of Second Instance tries a case in which an appeal is filed against the incidental civil part and the criminal part has already taken legal effect, it shall review the entire case and handle it separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the criminal part of the first-instance judgment is not improper, it is only necessary to make a disposition on the incidental civil part;  
(2) If the criminal part of the first-instance judgment is indeed erroneous, a retrial of the criminal part shall be conducted under the procedure for trial supervision, and the incidental civil part shall be tried together with the criminal part.

<a id="art-410"></a>第四百一十条 第二审期间，第一审附带民事诉讼原告人增加独立的诉讼请求或者第一审附带民事诉讼被告人提出反诉的，第二审人民法院可以根据自愿、合法的原则进行调解；调解不成的，告知当事人另行起诉。

**Article 410** . During the second instance, if the plaintiff of the first-instance incidental civil action adds independent claims or the defendant of the first-instance incidental civil action files a counterclaim, the People's Court of Second Instance may conduct mediation in accordance with the principles of voluntariness and legality; if mediation fails, the parties shall be informed that they may bring a separate action.

<a id="art-411"></a>第四百一十一条 对第二审自诉案件，必要时可以调解，当事人也可以自行和解。调解结案的，应当制作调解书，第一审判决、裁定视为自动撤销。当事人自行和解的，依照[本解释第三百二十九条](#art-329)的规定处理；裁定准许撤回自诉的，应当撤销第一审判决、裁定。

**Article 411** . With respect to a second-instance private prosecution case, mediation may be conducted when necessary, and the parties may also reach a settlement on their own. Where a case is concluded by mediation, a mediation document shall be prepared, and the first-instance judgment or ruling shall be deemed automatically revoked. Where the parties reach a settlement on their own, the matter shall be handled in accordance with Article 329 of this Interpretation; where a ruling is made to permit withdrawal of the private prosecution, the first-instance judgment or ruling shall be quashed.

<a id="art-412"></a>第四百一十二条 第二审期间，自诉案件的当事人提出反诉的，应当告知其另行起诉。

**Article 412** . During the second instance, if a party to a private prosecution case files a counterclaim, the party shall be informed that he or she may bring a separate action.

<a id="art-413"></a>第四百一十三条 第二审人民法院可以委托第一审人民法院代为宣判，并向当事人送达第二审判决书、裁定书。第一审人民法院应当在代为宣判后五日以内将宣判笔录送交第二审人民法院，并在送达完毕后及时将送达回证送交第二审人民法院。  
委托宣判的，第二审人民法院应当直接向同级人民检察院送达第二审判决书、裁定书。  
第二审判决、裁定是终审的判决、裁定的，自宣告之日起发生法律效力。

**Article 413** . The People's Court of Second Instance may entrust the People's Court of First Instance to pronounce the judgment on its behalf and serve the second-instance judgment or ruling on the parties. The People's Court of First Instance shall, within five days after pronouncing the judgment on its behalf, deliver the record of the judgment pronouncement to the People's Court of Second Instance, and shall, after service is completed, promptly deliver the proof of service to the People's Court of Second Instance.  
Where judgment is pronounced by entrustment, the People's Court of Second Instance shall directly serve the second-instance judgment or ruling on the People's Procuratorate at the same level.  
Where the second-instance judgment or ruling is a final judgment or ruling, it shall take legal effect as of the date of pronouncement.

### 第十六章 在法定刑以下判处刑罚和特殊假释的核准

<a id="art-414"></a>第四百一十四条 报请最高人民法院核准在法定刑以下判处刑罚的案件，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）被告人未上诉、人民检察院未抗诉的，在上诉、抗诉期满后三日以内报请上一级人民法院复核。上级人民法院同意原判的，应当书面层报最高人民法院核准；不同意的，应当裁定发回重新审判，或者按照第二审程序提审；  
（二）被告人上诉或者人民检察院抗诉的，上一级人民法院维持原判，或者改判后仍在法定刑以下判处刑罚的，应当依照前项规定层报最高人民法院核准。

**Article 414** . A case in which approval is requested from the Supreme People's Court for imposing a punishment below the statutory sentencing range shall be handled separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the defendant does not appeal and the People's Procuratorate does not file a protest, the case shall be submitted to the people's court at the next higher level for review within three days after the expiration of the appeal or protest period. If the higher people's court agrees with the original judgment, it shall report in writing through the levels to the Supreme People's Court for approval; if it disagrees, it shall rule to remand the case for retrial, or bring the case up for trial under the second-instance procedure;  
(2) If the defendant appeals or the People's Procuratorate files a protest, and the people's court at the next higher level upholds the original judgment, or after modifying the judgment still imposes a punishment below the statutory sentencing range, it shall, in accordance with the provisions of the preceding item, report through the levels to the Supreme People's Court for approval.

<a id="art-415"></a>第四百一十五条 对符合[刑法第六十三条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法.md#art-63)第二款规定的案件，第一审人民法院未在法定刑以下判处刑罚的，第二审人民法院可以在法定刑以下判处刑罚，并层报最高人民法院核准。

**Article 415** . For a case that meets the provisions of Article 63, paragraph 2, of the Criminal Law, if the People's Court of First Instance does not impose a punishment below the statutory sentencing range, the People's Court of Second Instance may impose a punishment below the statutory sentencing range and report through the levels to the Supreme People's Court for approval.

<a id="art-416"></a>第四百一十六条 报请最高人民法院核准在法定刑以下判处刑罚的案件，应当报送判决书、报请核准的报告各五份，以及全部案卷、证据。

**Article 416** . For a case submitted to the Supreme People's Court for approval of a punishment below the statutory sentencing range, five copies each of the judgment and the report requesting approval, as well as all case files and evidence, shall be submitted.

<a id="art-417"></a>第四百一十七条 对在法定刑以下判处刑罚的案件，最高人民法院予以核准的，应当作出核准裁定书;不予核准的，应当作出不核准裁定书，并撤销原判决、裁定，发回原审人民法院重新审判或者指定其他下级人民法院重新审判。

**Article 417** . Where the Supreme People's Court approves a case of imposing a punishment below the statutory sentencing range, it shall make a written ruling of approval; where it does not approve, it shall make a written ruling of non-approval, quash the original judgment or ruling, and remand the case to the original trial People's Court for retrial or designate another lower People's Court to retry the case.

<a id="art-418"></a>第四百一十八条 依照[本解释第四百一十四条](#art-414)、第四百一十七条规定发回第二审人民法院重新审判的案件，第二审人民法院可以直接改判；必须通过开庭查清事实、核实证据或者纠正原审程序违法的，应当开庭审理。

**Article 418** . For a case remanded to the People's Court of Second Instance for retrial in accordance with Articles 414 and 417 of this Interpretation, the People's Court of Second Instance may directly modify the judgment; where it is necessary to ascertain facts, verify evidence, or correct illegal procedures in the original trial through a court session, the case shall be heard in court.

<a id="art-419"></a>第四百一十九条 最高人民法院和上级人民法院复核在法定刑以下判处刑罚案件的审理期限，参照适用[刑事诉讼法第二百四十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-243)的规定。

**Article 419** . The time limit for review by the Supreme People's Court and the people's courts at higher levels of cases in which a punishment is imposed below the statutory sentencing range shall apply mutatis mutandis the provisions of Article 243 of the Criminal Procedure Law.

<a id="art-420"></a>第四百二十条 报请最高人民法院核准因罪犯具有特殊情况，不受执行刑期限制的假释案件，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）中级人民法院依法作出假释裁定后，应当报请高级人民法院复核。高级人民法院同意的，应当书面报请最高人民法院核准；不同意的，应当裁定撤销中级人民法院的假释裁定；  
（二）高级人民法院依法作出假释裁定的，应当报请最高人民法院核准。

**Article 420** . Cases of parole submitted to the Supreme People's Court for approval because the criminal has special circumstances and is not subject to the restriction of the executed term of imprisonment shall be handled separately according to the following circumstances:
