(1) After the Intermediate People's Court makes a parole ruling according to law, it shall submit the ruling to the Higher People's Court for review. If the Higher People's Court agrees, it shall submit a written request to the Supreme People's Court for approval; if it disagrees, it shall rule to quash the parole ruling of the Intermediate People's Court;  
(2) Where the Higher People's Court makes a parole ruling according to law, it shall submit the ruling to the Supreme People's Court for approval.

<a id="art-421"></a>第四百二十一条 报请最高人民法院核准因罪犯具有特殊情况，不受执行刑期限制的假释案件，应当报送报请核准的报告、罪犯具有特殊情况的报告、假释裁定书各五份，以及全部案卷。

**Article 421** . For a case of parole submitted to the Supreme People's Court for approval because the criminal has special circumstances and is not subject to the restriction of the executed term of imprisonment, five copies each of the report requesting approval, the report on the criminal's special circumstances, and the parole ruling, as well as all case files, shall be submitted.

<a id="art-422"></a>第四百二十二条 对因罪犯具有特殊情况，不受执行刑期限制的假释案件，最高人民法院予以核准的，应当作出核准裁定书；不予核准的，应当作出不核准裁定书，并撤销原裁定。

**Article 422** . For a case of parole in which the criminal has special circumstances and is not subject to the restriction of the executed term of imprisonment, if the Supreme People's Court approves the case, it shall make a written ruling of approval; if it does not approve, it shall make a written ruling of non-approval and quash the original ruling.

### 第十七章 死刑复核程序

<a id="art-423"></a>第四百二十三条 报请最高人民法院核准死刑的案件，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）中级人民法院判处死刑的第一审案件，被告人未上诉、人民检察院未抗诉的，在上诉、抗诉期满后十日以内报请高级人民法院复核。高级人民法院同意判处死刑的，应当在作出裁定后十日以内报请最高人民法院核准；认为原判认定的某一具体事实或者引用的法律条款等存在瑕疵，但判处被告人死刑并无不当的，可以在纠正后作出核准的判决、裁定；不同意判处死刑的，应当依照第二审程序提审或者发回重新审判；  
（二）中级人民法院判处死刑的第一审案件，被告人上诉或者人民检察院抗诉，高级人民法院裁定维持的，应当在作出裁定后十日以内报请最高人民法院核准；  
（三）高级人民法院判处死刑的第一审案件，被告人未上诉、人民检察院未抗诉的，应当在上诉、抗诉期满后十日以内报请最高人民法院核准。  
高级人民法院复核死刑案件，应当讯问被告人。

**Article 423** . Cases submitted to the Supreme People's Court for approval of the death penalty shall be handled separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) For a first-instance case in which the Intermediate People's Court imposes the death penalty and the defendant does not appeal and the People's Procuratorate does not file a protest, the case shall be submitted to the Higher People's Court for review within ten days after the expiration of the appeal or protest period. If the Higher People's Court agrees to imposing the death penalty, it shall submit the case to the Supreme People's Court for approval within ten days after making its ruling; if it considers that defects exist in a specific fact found in the original judgment or the legal provisions cited, but sentencing the defendant to death is not improper, it may, after correcting the defects, render a judgment or ruling of approval; if it disagrees with imposing the death penalty, it shall bring the case up for trial under the second-instance procedure or remand it for retrial;  
(2) For a first-instance case in which the Intermediate People's Court imposes the death penalty and the defendant appeals or the People's Procuratorate files a protest, and the Higher People's Court rules to affirm the judgment, the case shall be submitted to the Supreme People's Court for approval within ten days after making the ruling;  
(3) For a first-instance case in which the Higher People's Court imposes the death penalty and the defendant does not appeal and the People's Procuratorate does not file a protest, the case shall be submitted to the Supreme People's Court for approval within ten days after the expiration of the appeal or protest period.  
When reviewing a death penalty case, the Higher People's Court shall interrogate the defendant.

<a id="art-424"></a>第四百二十四条 中级人民法院判处死刑缓期执行的第一审案件，被告人未上诉、人民检察院未抗诉的，应当报请高级人民法院核准。  
高级人民法院复核死刑缓期执行案件，应当讯问被告人。

**Article 424** . For a first-instance case in which the Intermediate People's Court imposes a death sentence with a two-year reprieve and the defendant does not appeal and the People's Procuratorate does not file a protest, the case shall be submitted to the Higher People's Court for approval.  
When reviewing a case of death sentence with a two-year reprieve, the Higher People's Court shall interrogate the defendant.

<a id="art-425"></a>第四百二十五条 报请复核的死刑、死刑缓期执行案件，应当一案一报。报送的材料包括报请复核的报告，第一、二审裁判文书，案件综合报告各五份以及全部案卷、证据。案件综合报告，第一、二审裁判文书和审理报告应当附送电子文本。  
同案审理的案件应当报送全案案卷、证据。  
曾经发回重新审判的案件，原第一、二审案卷应当一并报送。

**Article 425** . Cases of death penalty and death sentence with a two-year reprieve submitted for review shall be reported case by case. The materials submitted shall include five copies each of the report requesting review, the judgment documents of the first and second instances, and the comprehensive case report, as well as all case files and evidence. Electronic copies of the comprehensive case report, the judgment documents of the first and second instances, and the trial report shall be attached.  
For cases tried jointly, the case files and evidence of the entire case shall be submitted.  
Where a case has previously been remanded for retrial, the original case files of the first and second instances shall be submitted together.

<a id="art-426"></a>第四百二十六条 报请复核死刑、死刑缓期执行的报告，应当写明案由、简要案情、审理过程和判决结果。  
案件综合报告应当包括以下内容：  
（一）被告人、被害人的基本情况。被告人有前科或者曾受过行政处罚、处分的，应当写明；  
（二）案件的由来和审理经过。案件曾经发回重新审判的，应当写明发回重新审判的原因、时间、案号等；  
（三）案件侦破情况。通过技术调查、侦查措施抓获被告人、侦破案件，以及与自首、立功认定有关的情况，应当写明；  
（四）第一审审理情况。包括控辩双方意见，第一审认定的犯罪事实，合议庭和审判委员会意见；  
（五）第二审审理或者高级人民法院复核情况。包括上诉理由、人民检察院的意见，第二审审理或者高级人民法院复核认定的事实，证据采信情况及理由，控辩双方意见及采纳情况；  
（六）需要说明的问题。包括共同犯罪案件中另案处理的同案犯的处理情况，案件有无重大社会影响，以及当事人的反应等情况；  
（七）处理意见。写明合议庭和审判委员会的意见。

**Article 426** . A report submitted for review of a death penalty or death sentence with a two-year reprieve case shall state the charge, a brief summary of the facts, the trial process, and the result of the judgment.  
The comprehensive case report shall include the following contents:  
(1) Basic information about the defendant and the victim. If the defendant has a criminal record or has previously been subject to administrative penalty or disciplinary sanction, such facts shall be stated;  
(2) The origin and trial process of the case. If the case was previously remanded for retrial, the reasons, time, and case number of the remand for retrial shall be stated;  
(3) The circumstances of solving the case. The circumstances in which the defendant was captured and the case was solved through technical investigation or investigative measures, as well as the circumstances relating to the determination of voluntary surrender or meritorious service, shall be stated;  
(4) The circumstances of the first-instance trial. Including the opinions of the prosecution and the defense, the criminal facts determined in the first instance, and the opinions of the collegial panel and the judicial committee;  
(5) The circumstances of the second-instance trial or the review by the Higher People's Court. Including the reasons for appeal, the opinions of the People's Procuratorate, the facts determined in the second-instance trial or the review by the Higher People's Court, the circumstances of and reasons for the admission of evidence, and the opinions of the prosecution and the defense and whether such opinions were adopted;  
(6) Issues that need to be explained. Including the handling of co-offenders in a joint crime case who were dealt with in separate cases, whether the case has major social impact, and the reactions of the parties;  
(7) Proposed handling opinions. Stating the opinions of the collegial panel and the judicial committee.

<a id="art-427"></a>第四百二十七条 复核死刑、死刑缓期执行案件，应当全面审查以下内容：  
（一）被告人的年龄，被告人有无刑事责任能力、是否系怀孕的妇女；  
（二）原判认定的事实是否清楚，证据是否确实、充分；  
（三）犯罪情节、后果及危害程度；  
（四）原判适用法律是否正确，是否必须判处死刑，是否必须立即执行；  
（五）有无法定、酌定从重、从轻或者减轻处罚情节；  
（六）诉讼程序是否合法；  
（七）应当审查的其他情况。  
复核死刑、死刑缓期执行案件，应当重视审查被告人及其辩护人的辩解、辩护意见。

**Article 427** . In reviewing a case of death penalty or death sentence with a two-year reprieve, the following contents shall be comprehensively examined:  
(1) The age of the defendant, whether the defendant has criminal responsibility capacity, and whether the defendant is a pregnant woman;  
(2) Whether the facts determined in the original judgment are clear and whether the evidence is reliable and sufficient;  
(3) The circumstances, consequences, and degree of harm of the crime;  
(4) Whether the law applied in the original judgment is correct, whether the death penalty is necessary, and whether immediate execution is necessary;  
(5) Whether there are statutory or discretionary circumstances for heavier, lighter, or mitigated punishment;  
(6) Whether the litigation procedure is lawful;  
(7) Other circumstances that shall be examined.  
In reviewing a case of death penalty or death sentence with a two-year reprieve, attention shall be paid to examining the exculpatory statements and defense opinions of the defendant and his defender.

<a id="art-428"></a>第四百二十八条 高级人民法院复核死刑缓期执行案件，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）原判认定事实和适用法律正确、量刑适当、诉讼程序合法的，应当裁定核准；  
（二）原判认定的某一具体事实或者引用的法律条款等存在瑕疵，但判处被告人死刑缓期执行并无不当的，可以在纠正后作出核准的判决、裁定；  
（三）原判认定事实正确，但适用法律有错误，或者量刑过重的，应当改判；  
（四）原判事实不清、证据不足的，可以裁定不予核准，并撤销原判，发回重新审判，或者依法改判；  
（五）复核期间出现新的影响定罪量刑的事实、证据的，可以裁定不予核准，并撤销原判，发回重新审判，或者依照[本解释第二百七十一条](#art-271)的规定审理后依法改判；  
（六）原审违反法定诉讼程序，可能影响公正审判的，应当裁定不予核准，并撤销原判，发回重新审判。  
复核死刑缓期执行案件，不得加重被告人的刑罚。

**Article 428** . When the Higher People's Court reviews a case of death sentence with a two-year reprieve, it shall handle the case separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the facts determined and the law applied in the original judgment are correct, the sentence is appropriate, and the litigation procedure is lawful, it shall rule to approve;  
(2) If defects exist in a specific fact determined in the original judgment or the legal provisions cited, but sentencing the defendant to death with a two-year reprieve is not improper, it may, after correcting the defects, render a judgment or ruling of approval;  
(3) If the facts determined in the original judgment are correct but the law applied is erroneous, or the sentence is excessively heavy, it shall modify the judgment;  
(4) If the facts in the original judgment are unclear or the evidence is insufficient, it may rule not to approve, quash the original judgment, and remand the case for retrial, or modify the judgment according to law;  
(5) If new facts or evidence affecting conviction and sentencing appear during the review period, it may rule not to approve, quash the original judgment, and remand the case for retrial, or after trial in accordance with the provisions of Article 271 of this Interpretation, modify the judgment according to law;  
(6) If the original trial violated the statutory litigation procedure and may affect a fair trial, it shall rule not to approve, quash the original judgment, and remand the case for retrial.  
When reviewing a case of death sentence with a two-year reprieve, the punishment of the defendant shall not be aggravated.

<a id="art-429"></a>第四百二十九条 最高人民法院复核死刑案件，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）原判认定事实和适用法律正确、量刑适当、诉讼程序合法的，应当裁定核准；  
（二）原判认定的某一具体事实或者引用的法律条款等存在瑕疵，但判处被告人死刑并无不当的，可以在纠正后作出核准的判决、裁定；  
（三）原判事实不清、证据不足的，应当裁定不予核准，并撤销原判，发回重新审判；  
（四）复核期间出现新的影响定罪量刑的事实、证据的，应当裁定不予核准，并撤销原判，发回重新审判；  
（五）原判认定事实正确、证据充分，但依法不应当判处死刑的，应当裁定不予核准，并撤销原判，发回重新审判；根据案件情况，必要时，也可以依法改判；  
（六）原审违反法定诉讼程序，可能影响公正审判的，应当裁定不予核准，并撤销原判，发回重新审判。

**Article 429** . When the Supreme People's Court reviews a death penalty case, it shall handle the case separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the facts determined and the law applied in the original judgment are correct, the sentence is appropriate, and the litigation procedure is lawful, it shall rule to approve;  
(2) If defects exist in a specific fact determined in the original judgment or the legal provisions cited, but sentencing the defendant to death is not improper, it may, after correcting the defects, render a judgment or ruling of approval;  
(3) If the facts in the original judgment are unclear or the evidence is insufficient, it shall rule not to approve, quash the original judgment, and remand the case for retrial;  
(4) If new facts or evidence affecting conviction and sentencing appear during the review period, it shall rule not to approve, quash the original judgment, and remand the case for retrial;  
(5) If the facts determined in the original judgment are correct and the evidence is sufficient, but the defendant shall not be sentenced to death according to law, it shall rule not to approve, quash the original judgment, and remand the case for retrial; where necessary according to the circumstances of the case, it may also modify the judgment according to law;  
(6) If the original trial violated the statutory litigation procedure and may affect a fair trial, it shall rule not to approve, quash the original judgment, and remand the case for retrial.

<a id="art-430"></a>第四百三十条 最高人民法院裁定不予核准死刑的，根据案件情况，可以发回第二审人民法院或者第一审人民法院重新审判。  
对最高人民法院发回第二审人民法院重新审判的案件，第二审人民法院一般不得发回第一审人民法院重新审判。  
第一审人民法院重新审判的，应当开庭审理。第二审人民法院重新审判的，可以直接改判；必须通过开庭查清事实、核实证据或者纠正原审程序违法的，应当开庭审理。

**Article 430** . Where the Supreme People's Court rules not to approve a death penalty, it may, according to the circumstances of the case, remand the case to the People's Court of Second Instance or the People's Court of First Instance for retrial.  
For a case remanded by the Supreme People's Court to the People's Court of Second Instance for retrial, the People's Court of Second Instance shall generally not remand the case to the People's Court of First Instance for retrial.  
Where the People's Court of First Instance conducts the retrial, it shall hold a court session for trial. Where the People's Court of Second Instance conducts the retrial, it may directly modify the judgment; where it is necessary to ascertain facts, verify evidence, or correct illegal procedures in the original trial through a court session, it shall hold a court session for trial.

<a id="art-431"></a>第四百三十一条 高级人民法院依照复核程序审理后报请最高人民法院核准死刑，最高人民法院裁定不予核准，发回高级人民法院重新审判的，高级人民法院可以依照第二审程序提审或者发回重新审判。

**Article 431** . Where the Higher People's Court, after trial under the review procedure, submits a death penalty case to the Supreme People's Court for approval, and the Supreme People's Court rules not to approve and remands the case to the Higher People's Court for retrial, the Higher People's Court may bring the case up for trial under the second-instance procedure or remand it for retrial.

<a id="art-432"></a>第四百三十二条 最高人民法院裁定不予核准死刑，发回重新审判的案件，原审人民法院应当另行组成合议庭审理，但[本解释第四百二十九条](#art-429)第四项、第五项规定的案件除外。

**Article 432** . Where the Supreme People's Court rules not to approve a death penalty and remands the case for retrial, the original trial People's Court shall form a new collegial panel for trial, except for the cases provided for in items (4) and (5) of Article 429 of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-433"></a>第四百三十三条 依照[本解释第四百三十条](#art-430)、第四百三十一条发回重新审判的案件，第一审人民法院判处死刑、死刑缓期执行的，上一级人民法院依照第二审程序或者复核程序审理后，应当依法作出判决或者裁定，不得再发回重新审判。但是，第一审人民法院有[刑事诉讼法第二百三十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-238)规定的情形或者违反[刑事诉讼法第二百三十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-239) 规定的除外。

**Article 433** . For a case remanded for retrial in accordance with Articles 430 and 431 of this Interpretation, if the People's Court of First Instance imposes a death penalty or a death sentence with a two-year reprieve, the people's court at the next higher level shall, after trial under the second-instance procedure or the review procedure, render a judgment or ruling according to law and shall not remand the case for retrial again, unless the People's Court of First Instance has any circumstance provided for in Article 238 of the Criminal Procedure Law or violates the provisions of Article 239 of the Criminal Procedure Law.

<a id="art-434"></a>第四百三十四条 死刑复核期间，辩护律师要求当面反映意见的，最高人民法院有关合议庭应当在办公场所听取其意见，并制作笔录；辩护律师提出书面意见的，应当附卷。

**Article 434** . During the death penalty review period, if the defense lawyer requests to present opinions in person, the relevant collegial panel of the Supreme People's Court shall listen to his opinions in the office premises and make a record; if the defense lawyer submits written opinions, they shall be attached to the case file.

<a id="art-435"></a>第四百三十五条 死刑复核期间，最高人民检察院提出意见的，最高人民法院应当审查，并将采纳情况及理由反馈最高人民检察院。

**Article 435** . During the death penalty review period, if the Supreme People's Procuratorate raises opinions, the Supreme People's Court shall review them and feed back to the Supreme People's Procuratorate the circumstances of adoption and the reasons therefor.

<a id="art-436"></a>第四百三十六条 最高人民法院应当根据有关规定向最高人民检察院通报死刑案件复核结果。

**Article 436** . The Supreme People's Court shall, in accordance with relevant provisions, notify the Supreme People's Procuratorate of the review results of death penalty cases.

### 第十八章 涉案财物处理

<a id="art-437"></a>第四百三十七条 人民法院对查封、扣押、冻结的涉案财物及其孳息，应当妥善保管，并制作清单，附卷备查；对人民检察院随案移送的实物，应当根据清单核查后妥善保管。任何单位和个人不得挪用或者自行处理。  
查封不动产、车辆、船舶、航空器等财物，应当扣押其权利证书，经拍照或者录像后原地封存，或者交持有人、被告人的近亲属保管，登记并写明财物的名称、型号、权属、地址等详细信息，并通知有关财物的登记、管理部门办理查封登记手续。  
扣押物品，应当登记并写明物品名称、型号、规格、数量、重量、质量、成色、纯度、颜色、新旧程度、缺损特征和来源等。扣押货币、有价证券，应当登记并写明货币、有价证券的名称、数额、面额等，货币应当存入银行专门账户，并登记银行存款凭证的名称、内容。扣押文物、金银、珠宝、名贵字画等贵重物品以及违禁品，应当拍照，需要鉴定的，应当及时鉴定。对扣押的物品应当根据有关规定及时估价。  
冻结存款、汇款、债券、股票、基金份额等财产，应当登记并写明编号、种类、面值、张数、金额等。

**Article 437** . The People's Court shall properly keep the property involved in the case and its fruits that have been sealed up, seized, or frozen, and shall prepare an inventory list to be attached to the case file for future inspection. Any unit or individual shall not misappropriate or dispose of such property without authorization.  
Where immovable property, vehicles, ships, aircraft, or other property is sealed up, the certificate of title shall be seized and the property shall be sealed up on the spot after photographs are taken or video recordings are made, or shall be delivered to the holder or a close relative of the defendant for safekeeping, with detailed information such as the name, model, ownership, and address of the property registered and stated, and the relevant registration and administrative department for the property shall be notified to handle the seal-up registration procedures.  
Where articles are seized, the name, model, specification, quantity, weight, quality, fineness, purity, color, degree of newness, damage characteristics, and source of the articles shall be registered and stated. Where currency or securities are seized, the name, amount, and denomination of the currency or securities shall be registered and stated; currency shall be deposited in a special bank account, and the name and content of the bank deposit certificate shall be registered. Where cultural relics, gold and silver, jewelry, precious calligraphy and paintings, and other valuables, as well as prohibited articles, are seized, photographs shall be taken, and if appraisal is needed, appraisal shall be conducted promptly. The seized articles shall be appraised for value promptly in accordance with relevant provisions.  
Where deposits, remittances, bonds, stocks, fund shares, or other property are frozen, the serial number, type, face value, number of sheets, and amount shall be registered and stated.

<a id="art-438"></a>第四百三十八条 对被害人的合法财产，权属明确的，应当依法及时返还，但须经拍照、鉴定、估价，并在案卷中注明返还的理由，将原物照片、清单和被害人的领取手续附卷备查；权属不明的，应当在人民法院判决、裁定生效后，按比例返还被害人，但已获退赔的部分应予扣除。

**Article 438** : Where the lawful property of a victim has clear ownership, it shall be returned in a timely manner according to law, provided that it is photographed, appraised, and valued, and the reasons for the return shall be noted in the case file, with photographs of the original property, a list, and the victim's receipt formalities attached to the file for future reference. Where ownership is unclear, the property shall be returned to the victim on a pro rata basis after the judgment or ruling of the People's Court becomes effective, but the portion already returned as compensation shall be deducted.

<a id="art-439"></a>第四百三十九条 审判期间，对不宜长期保存、易贬值或者市场价格波动大的财产，或者有效期即将届满的票据等，经权利人申请或者同意，并经院长批准，可以依法先行处置，所得款项由人民法院保管。  
涉案财物先行处置应当依法、公开、公平。

**Article 439** : During the trial, where property is unsuitable for long-term preservation, prone to depreciation, or subject to significant market price fluctuations, or bills, notes, or other instruments whose validity period is about to expire, upon application or consent of the right holder and with approval of the president of the court, such property may be disposed of in advance according to law, and the proceeds shall be kept by the People's Court.  
Advance disposal of property involved in a case shall be conducted according to law, openly, and fairly.

<a id="art-440"></a>第四百四十条 对作为证据使用的实物，应当随案移送。第一审判决、裁定宣告后，被告人上诉或者人民检察院抗诉的，第一审人民法院应当将上述证据移送第二审人民法院。

**Article 440** : Physical evidence used as evidence shall be transferred with the case file. After a judgment or ruling of first instance is pronounced, if the defendant files an appeal or the People's Procuratorate files a protest, the People's Court of first instance shall transfer the said evidence to the People's Court of second instance.

<a id="art-441"></a>第四百四十一条 对实物未随案移送的，应当根据情况，分别审查以下内容：  
（一）大宗的、不便搬运的物品，是否随案移送查封、扣押清单，并附原物照片和封存手续，注明存放地点等；  
（二）易腐烂、霉变和不易保管的物品，查封、扣押机关变卖处理后，是否随案移送原物照片、清单、变价处理的凭证（复印件）等；  
（三）枪支弹药、剧毒物品、易燃易爆物品以及其他违禁品、危险物品，查封、扣押机关根据有关规定处理后，是否随案移送原物照片和清单等。  
上述未随案移送的实物，应当依法鉴定、估价的，还应当审查是否附有鉴定、估价意见。  
对查封、扣押的货币、有价证券等，未移送实物的，应当审查是否附有原物照片、清单或者其他证明文件。

**Article 441** : Where physical evidence has not been transferred with the case file, the following contents shall be examined separately according to the circumstances:  
(1) For bulk items or items inconvenient to transport, whether the list of sealing and seizure has been transferred with the case file, together with photographs of the original items and sealing formalities, indicating the storage location, etc.;  
(2) For items prone to decay, mildew, or difficult to preserve, after the sealing and seizure organ has sold or disposed of them, whether photographs of the original items, lists, and vouchers (copies) for the disposal have been transferred with the case file;  
(3) For firearms, ammunition, highly toxic substances, flammable and explosive articles, and other contraband or dangerous articles, after the sealing and seizure organ has disposed of them in accordance with relevant provisions, whether photographs of the original items and lists have been transferred with the case file.  
Where the above-mentioned physical evidence not transferred with the case file should be appraised and valued according to law, it shall also be examined whether expert evaluation opinions and valuation opinions are attached.  
Where the currency, negotiable securities, etc., that have been sealed and seized have not been transferred as physical evidence, it shall be examined whether photographs of the original items, lists, or other certifying documents are attached.

<a id="art-442"></a>第四百四十二条 法庭审理过程中，应当依照[本解释第二百七十九条](#art-279)的规定，依法对查封、扣押、冻结的财物及其孳息进行审查。

**Article 442** : During court trial, the property and its fruits that have been sealed, seized, or frozen shall be examined according to law in accordance with Article 279 of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-443"></a>第四百四十三条 被告人将依法应当追缴的涉案财物用于投资或者置业的，对因此形成的财产及其收益，应当追缴。  
被告人将依法应当追缴的涉案财物与其他合法财产共同用于投资或者置业的，对因此形成的财产中与涉案财物对应的份额及其收益，应当追缴。

**Article 443** : Where a defendant uses property involved in a case that shall be recovered according to law for investment or real estate purchase, the property and its proceeds formed thereby shall be recovered.  
Where a defendant uses property involved in a case that shall be recovered according to law together with other lawful property for investment or real estate purchase, the share corresponding to the property involved in the case in the property formed thereby, and its proceeds, shall be recovered.

<a id="art-444"></a>第四百四十四条 对查封、扣押、冻结的财物及其孳息，应当在判决书中写明名称、金额、数量、存放地点及其处理方式等。涉案财物较多，不宜在判决主文中详细列明的，可以附清单。  
判决追缴违法所得或者责令退赔的，应当写明追缴、退赔的金额或者财物的名称、数量等情况；已经发还的，应当在判决书中写明。

**Article 444** : For property and its fruits that have been sealed, seized, or frozen, the judgment shall specify their names, amounts, quantities, storage locations, and methods of disposal, etc. Where there is a large amount of property involved in the case and it is inappropriate to list them in detail in the main text of the judgment, a list may be attached.  
Where the judgment orders the recovery of illegal gains or orders restitution, the amounts of recovery or restitution, or the names and quantities of property, etc., shall be specified; where the property has already been returned, it shall be stated in the judgment.

<a id="art-445"></a>第四百四十五条 查封、扣押、冻结的财物及其孳息，经审查，确属违法所得或者依法应当追缴的其他涉案财物的，应当判决返还被害人，或者没收上缴国库，但法律另有规定的除外。  
对判决时尚未追缴到案或者尚未足额退赔的违法所得，应当判决继续追缴或者责令退赔。  
判决返还被害人的涉案财物，应当通知被害人认领；无人认领的，应当公告通知；公告满一年无人认领的，应当上缴国库；上缴国库后有人认领，经查证属实的，应当申请退库予以返还；原物已经拍卖、变卖的，应当返还价款。  
对侵犯国有财产的案件，被害单位已经终止且没有权利义务继受人，或者损失已经被核销的，查封、扣押、冻结的财物及其孳息应当上缴国库。

**Article 445** : Where property and its fruits that have been sealed, seized, or frozen are, upon examination, confirmed to be illegal gains or other property involved in a case that shall be recovered according to law, the judgment shall order the return of such property to the victim, or confiscation and turning over to the State Treasury, unless otherwise provided by law.  
Where illegal gains have not yet been recovered or have not been fully restituted at the time of judgment, the judgment shall order continued recovery or order restitution.  
Where the judgment orders return of property involved in the case to the victim, the victim shall be notified to claim it; if no one claims it, a public notice shall be issued; if after one year from the public notice no one claims it, the property shall be turned over to the State Treasury; if after being turned over to the State Treasury someone claims it and the claim is verified, an application shall be made for withdrawal from the treasury to return it; if the original property has already been auctioned or sold, the proceeds shall be returned.  
For cases involving infringement of State-owned property, where the victim entity has terminated and has no successor of rights and obligations, or the losses have already been written off, the property and its fruits that have been sealed, seized, or frozen shall be turned over to the State Treasury.

<a id="art-446"></a>第四百四十六条 第二审期间，发现第一审判决未对随案移送的涉案财物及其孳息作出处理的，可以裁定撤销原判，发回原审人民法院重新审判，由原审人民法院依法对涉案财物及其孳息一并作出处理。  
判决生效后，发现原判未对随案移送的涉案财物及其孳息作出处理的，由原审人民法院依法对涉案财物及其孳息另行作出处理。

**Article 446** : During the second instance, if it is found that the judgment of first instance failed to address the property involved in the case and its fruits transferred with the case file, a ruling may be made to vacate the original judgment and remand the case to the original People's Court for retrial, and the original People's Court shall, according to law, address the property involved in the case and its fruits together.  
After the judgment takes effect, if it is found that the original judgment failed to address the property involved in the case and its fruits transferred with the case file, the original People's Court shall separately address the property involved in the case and its fruits according to law.

<a id="art-447"></a>第四百四十七条 随案移送的或者人民法院查封、扣押的财物及其孳息，由第一审人民法院在判决生效后负责处理。  
实物未随案移送、由扣押机关保管的，人民法院应当在判决生效后十日以内，将判决书、裁定书送达扣押机关，并告知其在一个月以内将执行回单送回，确因客观原因无法按时完成的，应当说明原因。

**Article 447** : Property and its fruits transferred with the case file or sealed and seized by the People's Court shall be handled by the People's Court of first instance after the judgment takes effect.  
Where the physical evidence has not been transferred with the case file and is kept by the seizure organ, the People's Court shall, within ten days after the judgment takes effect, serve the judgment and ruling on the seizure organ and notify it to return the enforcement receipt within one month; if it is truly unable to complete this on time due to objective reasons, it shall explain the reasons.

<a id="art-448"></a>第四百四十八条 对冻结的存款、汇款、债券、股票、基金份额等财产判决没收的，第一审人民法院应当在判决生效后，将判决书、裁定书送达相关金融机构和财政部门，通知相关金融机构依法上缴国库并在接到执行通知书后十五日以内，将上缴国库的凭证、执行回单送回。

**Article 448** : Where a judgment orders confiscation of frozen deposits, remittances, bonds, stocks, fund shares, or other property, the People's Court of first instance shall, after the judgment takes effect, serve the judgment and ruling on the relevant financial institutions and financial departments, notify the relevant financial institutions to turn over the property to the State Treasury according to law and, within fifteen days after receiving the enforcement notice, return the vouchers for turning over to the State Treasury and the enforcement receipt.

<a id="art-449"></a>第四百四十九条 查封、扣押、冻结的财物与本案无关但已列入清单的，应当由查封、扣押、冻结机关依法处理。  
查封、扣押、冻结的财物属于被告人合法所有的，应当在赔偿被害人损失、执行财产刑后及时返还被告人。

**Article 449** : Where property that has been sealed, seized, or frozen is unrelated to the case but has been included in the list, it shall be handled by the sealing, seizure, or freezing organ according to law.  
Where the property that has been sealed, seized, or frozen is lawfully owned by the defendant, it shall be returned to the defendant promptly after compensation for the victim's losses and execution of the property penalty.

<a id="art-450"></a>第四百五十条 查封、扣押、冻结财物及其处理，本解释没有规定的，参照适用其他司法解释的有关规定。

**Article 450** : Where this Interpretation has no provisions on the sealing, seizure, freezing, and disposal of property, the relevant provisions of other judicial interpretations shall apply by reference.

### 第十九章 审判监督程序

<a id="art-451"></a>第四百五十一条 当事人及其法定代理人、近亲属对已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定提出申诉的，人民法院应当审查处理。  
案外人认为已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定侵害其合法权益，提出申诉的，人民法院应当审查处理。  
申诉可以委托律师代为进行。

**Article 451** : Where a party or his or her legal representative or close relative files a petition against a legally effective judgment or ruling, the People's Court shall review and handle it.  
Where a person other than a party believes that a legally effective judgment or ruling infringes upon his or her lawful rights and interests and files a petition, the People's Court shall review and handle it.  
A petitioner may entrust a lawyer to file the petition on his or her behalf.

<a id="art-452"></a>第四百五十二条 向人民法院申诉，应当提交以下材料：  
（一）申诉状。应当写明当事人的基本情况、联系方式以及申诉的事实与理由；  
（二）原一、二审判决书、裁定书等法律文书。经过人民法院复查或者再审的，应当附有驳回申诉通知书、再审决定书、再审判决书、裁定书；  
（三）其他相关材料。以有新的证据证明原判决、裁定认定的事实确有错误为由申诉的，应当同时附有相关证据材料；申请人民法院调查取证的，应当附有相关线索或者材料。  
申诉符合前款规定的，人民法院应当出具收到申诉材料的回执。申诉不符合前款规定的，人民法院应当告知申诉人补充材料；申诉人拒绝补充必要材料且无正当理由的，不予审查。

**Article 452** : To file a petition with the People's Court, the following materials shall be submitted:  
(1) A petition letter, which shall state the basic information of the party, contact information, and the facts and reasons for the petition;  
(2) The original legal documents of first and second instance, such as judgments and rulings. Where the case has been reexamined or retried by the People's Court, a notice of rejection of the petition, a retrial decision, a retrial judgment, and a ruling shall be attached;  
(3) Other relevant materials. Where the petition is based on new evidence proving that the facts determined by the original judgment or ruling are indeed erroneous, the relevant evidence materials shall be attached at the same time; where application is made to the People's Court for investigation and evidence collection, relevant clues or materials shall be attached.  
Where the petition complies with the provisions of the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall issue a receipt for receipt of the petition materials. Where the petition does not comply with the provisions of the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall notify the petitioner to supplement the materials; if the petitioner refuses to supplement the necessary materials without justifiable reasons, the petition shall not be reviewed.

<a id="art-453"></a>第四百五十三条 申诉由终审人民法院审查处理。但是，第二审人民法院裁定准许撤回上诉的案件，申诉人对第一审判决提出申诉的，可以由第一审人民法院审查处理。  
上一级人民法院对未经终审人民法院审查处理的申诉，可以告知申诉人向终审人民法院提出申诉，或者直接交终审人民法院审查处理，并告知申诉人；案件疑难、复杂、重大的，也可以直接审查处理。  
对未经终审人民法院及其上一级人民法院审查处理，直接向上级人民法院申诉的，上级人民法院应当告知申诉人向下级人民法院提出。

**Article 453** : A petition shall be reviewed and handled by the People's Court of final instance. However, where in a case the People's Court of second instance rules to permit withdrawal of an appeal, and the petitioner files a petition against the judgment of first instance, the petition may be reviewed and handled by the People's Court of first instance.  
Where a petition has not been reviewed and handled by the People's Court of final instance, the People's Court at the next higher level may inform the petitioner to file the petition with the People's Court of final instance, or directly refer it to the People's Court of final instance for review and handling, and inform the petitioner; where the case is difficult, complex, or major, the higher court may also directly review and handle it.  
Where a petition is filed directly with a higher People's Court without having been reviewed and handled by the People's Court of final instance or the People's Court at the next higher level, the higher People's Court shall inform the petitioner to file it with the lower People's Court.

<a id="art-454"></a>第四百五十四条 最高人民法院或者上级人民法院可以指定终审人民法院以外的人民法院对申诉进行审查。被指定的人民法院审查后，应当制作审查报告，提出处理意见，层报最高人民法院或者上级人民法院审查处理。

**Article 454** : The Supreme People's Court or a higher people's court may designate a people's court other than the People's Court of final instance to review the petition. After review, the designated People's Court shall prepare a review report, put forward handling opinions, and report them level by level to the Supreme People's Court or the higher people's court for review and handling.

<a id="art-455"></a>第四百五十五条 对死刑案件的申诉，可以由原核准的人民法院直接审查处理，也可以交由原审人民法院审查。原审人民法院应当制作审查报告，提出处理意见，层报原核准的人民法院审查处理。

**Article 455** : A petition in a death penalty case may be directly reviewed and handled by the People's Court that originally approved the death penalty, or may be referred to the original trial People's Court for review. The original trial People's Court shall prepare a review report, put forward handling opinions, and report them level by level to the People's Court that originally approved the death penalty for review and handling.

<a id="art-456"></a>第四百五十六条 对立案审查的申诉案件，人民法院可以听取当事人和原办案单位的意见，也可以对原判据以定罪量刑的证据和新的证据进行核实。必要时，可以进行听证。

**Article 456** : For a petition case placed on file for review, the People's Court may hear the opinions of the parties and the original case-handling unit, and may also verify the evidence on which the original judgment was based for conviction and sentencing and the new evidence. When necessary, a hearing may be held.

<a id="art-457"></a>第四百五十七条 对立案审查的申诉案件，应当在三个月以内作出决定，至迟不得超过六个月。因案件疑难、复杂、重大或者其他特殊原因需要延长审查期限的，参照[本解释第二百一十条](#art-210)的规定处理。  
经审查，具有下列情形之一的，应当根据[刑事诉讼法第二百五十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-253)的规定，决定重新审判：  
（一）有新的证据证明原判决、裁定认定的事实确有错误，可能影响定罪量刑的；  
（二）据以定罪量刑的证据不确实、不充分、依法应当排除的；  
（三）证明案件事实的主要证据之间存在矛盾的；  
（四）主要事实依据被依法变更或者撤销的；  
（五）认定罪名错误的；  
（六）量刑明显不当的；  
（七）对违法所得或者其他涉案财物的处理确有明显错误的；  
（八）违反法律关于溯及力规定的；  
（九）违反法定诉讼程序，可能影响公正裁判的；  
（十）审判人员在审理该案件时有贪污受贿、徇私舞弊、枉法裁判行为的。  
申诉不具有上述情形的，应当说服申诉人撤回申诉；对仍然坚持申诉的，应当书面通知驳回。

**Article 457** : For a petition case placed on file for review, a decision shall be made within three months, and in no case later than six months. Where it is necessary to extend the review period because the case is difficult, complex, major, or for other special reasons, the provisions of Article 210 of this Interpretation shall be applied by reference.  
Upon review, if any of the following circumstances exists, it shall be decided to retry the case in accordance with Article 253 of the Criminal Procedure Law:  
(1) There is new evidence proving that the facts determined in the original judgment or ruling are indeed erroneous and may affect conviction and sentencing;  
(2) The evidence on which the conviction and sentencing were based is not reliable or insufficient and shall be excluded according to law;  
(3) There are contradictions among the primary evidence proving the facts of the case;  
(4) The primary factual basis has been changed or revoked according to law;  
(5) The determination of the charge is erroneous;  
(6) The sentencing is obviously inappropriate;  
(7) There are obvious errors in the handling of illegal gains or other property involved in the case;  
(8) Violating the legal provisions on retroactive effect;  
(9) Violating the statutory litigation procedures, which may affect a fair judgment;  
(10) The judicial officers committed acts of embezzlement, bribery, engaging in malpractices for personal gain, or perverting the law in adjudicating the case.  
Where the petition does not have any of the above circumstances, the petitioner shall be persuaded to withdraw the petition; if the petitioner still insists on the petition, a written notice of rejection shall be given.

<a id="art-458"></a>第四百五十八条 具有下列情形之一，可能改变原判决、裁定据以定罪量刑的事实的证据，应当认定为[刑事诉讼法第二百五十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-253)第一项规定的“新的证据”：  
（一）原判决、裁定生效后新发现的证据；  
（二）原判决、裁定生效前已经发现，但未予收集的证据；  
（三）原判决、裁定生效前已经收集，但未经质证的证据；  
（四）原判决、裁定所依据的鉴定意见，勘验、检查等笔录被改变或者否定的；  
（五）原判决、裁定所依据的被告人供述、证人证言等证据发生变化，影响定罪量刑，且有合理理由的。

**Article 458** : Evidence that falls under any of the following circumstances and may change the facts on which the original judgment or ruling was based for conviction and sentencing shall be determined as 'new evidence' as provided in Item (1) of Article 253 of the Criminal Procedure Law:  
(1) Evidence discovered after the original judgment or ruling took effect;  
(2) Evidence discovered before the original judgment or ruling took effect but not collected;  
(3) Evidence collected before the original judgment or ruling took effect but not cross-examined;  
(4) The expert evaluation opinions, records of inspection, examination, etc., on which the original judgment or ruling was based have been changed or denied;  
(5) Evidence such as the defendant's confession and witness testimony on which the original judgment or ruling was based has changed, affecting conviction and sentencing, and there are reasonable grounds for such change.

<a id="art-459"></a>第四百五十九条 申诉人对驳回申诉不服的，可以向上一级人民法院申诉。上一级人民法院经审查认为申诉不符合[刑事诉讼法第二百五十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-253)和[本解释第四百五十七条](#art-457)第二款规定的，应当说服申诉人撤回申诉；对仍然坚持申诉的，应当驳回或者通知不予重新审判。

**Article 459** : Where a petitioner refuses to accept the rejection of the petition, he or she may file a petition with the People's Court at the next higher level. Where the People's Court at the next higher level, upon review, considers that the petition does not comply with the provisions of Article 253 of the Criminal Procedure Law and paragraph 2 of Article 457 of this Interpretation, it shall persuade the petitioner to withdraw the petition; if the petitioner still insists on the petition, the petition shall be rejected or the petitioner shall be notified that no retrial will be granted.

<a id="art-460"></a>第四百六十条 各级人民法院院长发现本院已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定确有错误的，应当提交审判委员会讨论决定是否再审。

**Article 460** : Where the president of a people's court at any level finds that a legally effective judgment or ruling made by that court is indeed erroneous, he or she shall submit it to the judicial committee for discussion and decision on whether to retry the case.

<a id="art-461"></a>第四百六十一条 上级人民法院发现下级人民法院已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定确有错误的，可以指令下级人民法院再审；原判决、裁定认定事实正确但适用法律错误，或者案件疑难、复杂、重大，或者有不宜由原审人民法院审理情形的，也可以提审。  
上级人民法院指令下级人民法院再审的，一般应当指令原审人民法院以外的下级人民法院审理；由原审人民法院审理更有利于查明案件事实、纠正裁判错误的，可以指令原审人民法院审理。

**Article 461** : Where a higher people's court finds that a legally effective judgment or ruling made by a lower people's court is indeed erroneous, it may instruct the lower people's court to retry the case; where the original judgment or ruling correctly determined the facts but erred in applying the law, or the case is difficult, complex, or major, or there are circumstances making it inappropriate for the original trial people's court to hear the case, the higher people's court may also bring the case up for trial.  
Where a higher people's court instructs a lower people's court to retry the case, it shall generally instruct a lower people's court other than the original trial people's court to hear the case; where trial by the original trial people's court is more conducive to ascertaining the facts of the case and correcting judicial errors, the original trial people's court may be instructed to hear the case.

<a id="art-462"></a>第四百六十二条 对人民检察院依照审判监督程序提出抗诉的案件，人民法院应当在收到抗诉书后一个月以内立案。但是，有下列情形之一的，应当区别情况予以处理：  
（一）不属于本院管辖的，应当将案件退回人民检察院；  
（二）按照抗诉书提供的住址无法向被抗诉的原审被告人送达抗诉书的，应当通知人民检察院在三日以内重新提供原审被告人的住址；逾期未提供的，将案件退回人民检察院；  
（三）以有新的证据为由提出抗诉，但未附相关证据材料或者有关证据不是指向原起诉事实的，应当通知人民检察院在三日以内补送相关材料；逾期未补送的，将案件退回人民检察院。  
决定退回的抗诉案件，人民检察院经补充相关材料后再次抗诉，经审查符合受理条件的，人民法院应当受理。

**Article 462** : For a case protested by the People's Procuratorate in accordance with the trial supervision procedure, the People's Court shall place the case on file within one month after receiving the written protest. However, under any of the following circumstances, the case shall be handled differently according to the circumstances:  
(1) If the case is not within the jurisdiction of that court, the case shall be returned to the People's Procuratorate;  
(2) If the written protest cannot be served on the protested original defendant according to the address provided in the written protest, the People's Procuratorate shall be notified to provide the address of the original defendant within three days; if not provided upon expiration of the time limit, the case shall be returned to the People's Procuratorate;  
(3) If the protest is based on new evidence, but the relevant evidence materials are not attached, or the relevant evidence does not point to the facts of the original prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall be notified to supplement the relevant materials within three days; if not supplemented upon expiration of the time limit, the case shall be returned to the People's Procuratorate.  
For a protested case decided to be returned, if the People's Procuratorate protests again after supplementing the relevant materials, and upon examination the case meets the acceptance conditions, the People's Court shall accept it.

<a id="art-463"></a>第四百六十三条 对人民检察院依照审判监督程序提出抗诉的案件，接受抗诉的人民法院应当组成合议庭审理。对原判事实不清、证据不足，包括有新的证据证明原判可能有错误，需要指令下级人民法院再审的，应当在立案之日起一个月以内作出决定，并将指令再审决定书送达抗诉的人民检察院。

**Article 463** : For a case protested by the People's Procuratorate in accordance with the trial supervision procedure, the People's Court that accepts the protest shall form a collegial panel to hear the case. Where the facts of the original judgment are unclear or the evidence is insufficient, including where there is new evidence proving that the original judgment may be erroneous, and it is necessary to instruct a lower people's court to retry the case, a decision shall be made within one month from the date of placing the case on file, and the written decision to instruct retrial shall be served on the People's Procuratorate that filed the protest.

<a id="art-464"></a>第四百六十四条 对决定依照审判监督程序重新审判的案件，人民法院应当制作再审决定书。再审期间不停止原判决、裁定的执行，但被告人可能经再审改判无罪，或者可能经再审减轻原判刑罚而致刑期届满的，可以决定中止原判决、裁定的执行，必要时，可以对被告人采取取保候审、监视居住措施。

**Article 464** : For a case decided to be retried in accordance with the trial supervision procedure, the People's Court shall make a written retrial decision. During the retrial, the execution of the original judgment or ruling shall not be suspended; however, if the defendant may be acquitted through retrial, or may have the original sentence reduced through retrial resulting in the expiration of the term of imprisonment, a decision may be made to suspend the execution of the original judgment or ruling; when necessary, compulsory measures such as release on bail pending investigation or trial and residential surveillance may be taken against the defendant.

<a id="art-465"></a>第四百六十五条 依照审判监督程序重新审判的案件，人民法院应当重点针对申诉、抗诉和决定再审的理由进行审理。必要时，应当对原判决、裁定认定的事实、证据和适用法律进行全面审查。

**Article 465** : For a case retried in accordance with the trial supervision procedure, the People's Court shall focus on the reasons for the petition, the protest, and the decision to retry. When necessary, it shall conduct a comprehensive review of the facts, evidence, and application of law determined in the original judgment or ruling.

<a id="art-466"></a>第四百六十六条 原审人民法院审理依照审判监督程序重新审判的案件，应当另行组成合议庭。  
原来是第一审案件，应当依照第一审程序进行审判，所作的判决、裁定可以上诉、抗诉；原来是第二审案件，或者是上级人民法院提审的案件，应当依照第二审程序进行审判，所作的判决、裁定是终审的判决、裁定。  
符合[刑事诉讼法第二百九十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-296)、第二百九十七条规定的，可以缺席审判。

**Article 466** : Where the original trial People's Court hears a case retried in accordance with the trial supervision procedure, it shall form a new collegial panel.  
If the case was originally a case of first instance, it shall be tried in accordance with the procedure of first instance, and the judgment or ruling rendered may be appealed or protested; if the case was originally a case of second instance, or is a case brought up for trial by a higher people's court, it shall be tried in accordance with the procedure of second instance, and the judgment or ruling rendered shall be final.  
Where the provisions of Articles 296 and 297 of the Criminal Procedure Law are met, trial by default may be conducted.

<a id="art-467"></a>第四百六十七条 对依照审判监督程序重新审判的案件，人民法院在依照第一审程序进行审判的过程中，发现原审被告人还有其他犯罪的，一般应当并案审理，但分案审理更为适宜的，可以分案审理。

**Article 467** : For a case retried in accordance with the trial supervision procedure, if during the trial in accordance with the procedure of first instance, the People's Court discovers that the original defendant has other crimes, the cases shall generally be tried jointly; however, where separate trial is more appropriate, the cases may be tried separately.

<a id="art-468"></a>第四百六十八条 开庭审理再审案件，再审决定书或者抗诉书只针对部分原审被告人，其他同案原审被告人不出庭不影响审理的，可以不出庭参加诉讼。

**Article 468** : Where a retrial case is heard in court, if the retrial decision or written protest is only directed at some of the original defendants, and the failure of other original defendants in the same case to appear in court does not affect the trial, such other original defendants may decline to appear in court to participate in the proceedings.

<a id="art-469"></a>第四百六十九条 除人民检察院抗诉的以外，再审一般不得加重原审被告人的刑罚。再审决定书或者抗诉书只针对部分原审被告人的，不得加重其他同案原审被告人的刑罚。

**Article 469** : Except for cases protested by the People's Procuratorate, a retrial shall generally not increase the punishment of the original defendant. Where the retrial decision or written protest is only directed at some of the original defendants, the punishment of other co-defendants in the original trial shall not be increased.

<a id="art-470"></a>第四百七十条 人民法院审理人民检察院抗诉的再审案件，人民检察院在开庭审理前撤回抗诉的，应当裁定准许；人民检察院接到出庭通知后不派员出庭，且未说明原因的，可以裁定按撤回抗诉处理，并通知诉讼参与人。  
人民法院审理申诉人申诉的再审案件，申诉人在再审期间撤回申诉的，可以裁定准许；但认为原判确有错误的，应当不予准许，继续按照再审案件审理。申诉人经依法通知无正当理由拒不到庭，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭的，可以裁定按撤回申诉处理，但申诉人不是原审当事人的除外。

**Article 470** : Where the People's Court tries a retrial case protested by the People's Procuratorate, if the People's Procuratorate withdraws the protest before the court hearing begins, a ruling shall be made to permit the withdrawal; if the People's Procuratorate, after receiving the notice to appear in court, fails to send a representative to appear in court without stating reasons, it may be ruled that the protest is withdrawn, and the litigation participants shall be notified.  
Where the People's Court tries a retrial case based on a petitioner's petition, if the petitioner withdraws the petition during the retrial, a ruling may be made to permit the withdrawal; however, if the court considers that the original judgment is indeed erroneous, the withdrawal shall not be permitted, and the case shall continue to be tried as a retrial case. If the petitioner, after being notified according to law, refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons, or withdraws from the courtroom midway without the permission of the court, a ruling may be made to treat the petition as withdrawn, unless the petitioner is not a party to the original trial.

<a id="art-471"></a>第四百七十一条 开庭审理的再审案件，系人民法院决定再审的，由合议庭组成人员宣读再审决定书；系人民检察院抗诉的，由检察员宣读抗诉书；系申诉人申诉的，由申诉人或者其辩护人、诉讼代理人陈述申诉理由。

**Article 471** : For a retrial case heard in court, if the retrial was decided by the People's Court, a member of the collegial panel shall read the retrial decision; if it is protested by the People's Procuratorate, the procurator shall read the written protest; if it is based on a petitioner's petition, the petitioner or his or her defender or litigation agent shall state the reasons for the petition.

<a id="art-472"></a>第四百七十二条 再审案件经过重新审理后，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）原判决、裁定认定事实和适用法律正确、量刑适当的，应当裁定驳回申诉或者抗诉，维持原判决、裁定；  
（二）原判决、裁定定罪准确、量刑适当，但在认定事实、适用法律等方面有瑕疵的，应当裁定纠正并维持原判决、裁定；  
（三）原判决、裁定认定事实没有错误，但适用法律错误或者量刑不当的，应当撤销原判决、裁定，依法改判；  
（四）依照第二审程序审理的案件，原判决、裁定事实不清、证据不足的，可以在查清事实后改判，也可以裁定撤销原判，发回原审人民法院重新审判。  
原判决、裁定事实不清或者证据不足，经审理事实已经查清的，应当根据查清的事实依法裁判；事实仍无法查清，证据不足，不能认定被告人有罪的，应当撤销原判决、裁定，判决宣告被告人无罪。

**Article 472** : After a retrial case has been retried, it shall be handled separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the facts determined and the law applied in the original judgment or ruling are correct and the sentencing is appropriate, a ruling shall be made to reject the petition or protest and affirm the original judgment or ruling;  
(2) If the conviction in the original judgment or ruling is accurate and the sentencing is appropriate, but there are defects in determining facts or applying law, a ruling shall be made to correct such defects and affirm the original judgment or ruling;  
(3) If the facts determined in the original judgment or ruling are not erroneous, but the law applied is erroneous or the sentencing is inappropriate, the original judgment or ruling shall be revoked and a new judgment shall be rendered according to law;  
(4) For a case tried in accordance with the procedure of second instance, if the facts in the original judgment or ruling are unclear or the evidence is insufficient, a new judgment may be rendered after the facts are clarified, or a ruling may be made to revoke the original judgment and remand the case to the original trial People's Court for retrial.  
If the facts in the original judgment or ruling are unclear or the evidence is insufficient, and the facts have been clarified upon trial, judgment shall be rendered according to law based on the clarified facts; if the facts still cannot be clarified and the evidence is insufficient to establish the defendant's guilt, the original judgment or ruling shall be revoked and a judgment shall be rendered acquitting the defendant.

<a id="art-473"></a>第四百七十三条 原判决、裁定认定被告人姓名等身份信息有误，但认定事实和适用法律正确、量刑适当的，作出生效判决、裁定的人民法院可以通过裁定对有关信息予以更正。

**Article 473** : Where the original judgment or ruling contains errors in the defendant's name or other identity information, but the facts determined and the law applied are correct and the sentencing is appropriate, the People's Court that rendered the effective judgment or ruling may correct the relevant information by ruling.

<a id="art-474"></a>第四百七十四条 对再审改判宣告无罪并依法享有申请国家赔偿权利的当事人，人民法院宣判时，应当告知其在判决发生法律效力后可以依法申请国家赔偿。

**Article 474** : For a party who is acquitted by a judgment upon retrial and enjoys the right to apply for state compensation according to law, the People's Court shall, when pronouncing judgment, inform the party that he or she may apply for state compensation according to law after the judgment becomes legally effective.

### 第二十章 涉外刑事案件的审理和刑事司法协助

#### 第一节 涉外刑事案件的审理

<a id="art-475"></a>第四百七十五条 本解释所称的涉外刑事案件是指：  
（一）在中华人民共和国领域内，外国人犯罪或者我国公民对外国、外国人犯罪的案件；  
（二）符合[刑法第七条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法.md#art-7)、第十条规定情形的我国公民在中华人民共和国领域外犯罪的案件；  
（三）符合[刑法第八条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法.md#art-8)、第十条规定情形的外国人犯罪的案件；  
（四）符合[刑法第九条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法.md#art-9)规定情形的中华人民共和国在所承担国际条约义务范围内行使管辖权的案件。

**Article 475** : For the purposes of this Interpretation, 'foreign-related criminal cases' mean:  
(1) Cases in which a foreigner commits a crime within the territory of the People's Republic of China, or a Chinese citizen commits a crime against a foreign country or a foreigner;  
(2) Cases in which a Chinese citizen commits a crime outside the territory of the People's Republic of China under the circumstances provided for in Articles 7 and 10 of the Criminal Law;  
(3) Cases in which a foreigner commits a crime under the circumstances provided for in Articles 8 and 10 of the Criminal Law;  
(4) Cases in which the People's Republic of China exercises jurisdiction within the scope of international treaty obligations it undertakes under the circumstances provided for in Article 9 of the Criminal Law.

<a id="art-476"></a>第四百七十六条 第一审涉外刑事案件，除[刑事诉讼法第二十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-21)至第二十三条规定的以外，由基层人民法院管辖。必要时，中级人民法院可以指定辖区内若干基层人民法院集中管辖第一审涉外刑事案件，也可以依照[刑事诉讼法第二十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-24)的规定，审理基层人民法院管辖的第一审涉外刑事案件。

**Article 476** : First-instance foreign-related criminal cases shall be under the jurisdiction of primary people's courts, except those provided for in Articles 21 through 23 of the Criminal Procedure Law. When necessary, an intermediate people's court may designate several primary people's courts within its jurisdiction to centrally handle first-instance foreign-related criminal cases, and may also, in accordance with Article 24 of the Criminal Procedure Law, try first-instance foreign-related criminal cases under the jurisdiction of primary people's courts.

<a id="art-477"></a>第四百七十七条 外国人的国籍，根据其入境时持用的有效证件确认；国籍不明的，根据公安机关或者有关国家驻华使领馆出具的证明确认。  
国籍无法查明的，以无国籍人对待，适用本章有关规定，在裁判文书中写明“国籍不明”。

**Article 477** : The nationality of a foreigner shall be determined according to the valid documents held at the time of entry; if the nationality is unknown, it shall be determined according to the certificates issued by the public security organ or the relevant embassy or consulate of the foreign country in China.  
If the nationality cannot be ascertained, the person shall be treated as a stateless person, and the relevant provisions of this Chapter shall apply, with 'nationality unknown' stated in the adjudication document.

<a id="art-478"></a>第四百七十八条 在刑事诉讼中，外国籍当事人享有我国法律规定的诉讼权利并承担相应义务。

**Article 478** : In criminal proceedings, parties of foreign nationality enjoy the litigation rights provided by Chinese law and bear corresponding obligations.

<a id="art-479"></a>第四百七十九条 涉外刑事案件审判期间，人民法院应当将下列事项及时通报同级人民政府外事主管部门，并依照有关规定通知有关国家驻华使领馆：  
（一）人民法院决定对外国籍被告人采取强制措施的情况，包括外国籍当事人的姓名（包括译名）、性别、入境时间、护照或者证件号码、采取的强制措施及法律依据、羁押地点等；  
（二）开庭的时间、地点、是否公开审理等事项；  
（三）宣判的时间、地点。  
涉外刑事案件宣判后，应当将处理结果及时通报同级人民政府外事主管部门。  
对外国籍被告人执行死刑的，死刑裁决下达后执行前，应当通知其国籍国驻华使领馆。  
外国籍被告人在案件审理中死亡的，应当及时通报同级人民政府外事主管部门，并通知有关国家驻华使领馆。

**Article 479** During the trial of a foreign-related criminal case, the People's Court shall promptly notify the competent foreign affairs department of the people's government at the same level of the following matters, and notify the relevant embassy or consulate of the country concerned stationed in China in accordance with relevant provisions:  
(1) The circumstance that the People's Court decides to take compulsory measures against a foreign national defendant, including the name (including translation), gender, time of entry into China, passport or certificate number, compulsory measures taken and legal basis, place of custody, etc.;  
(2) The time and place of court session, whether the hearing is public, and other matters;  
(3) The time and place of pronouncement of judgment.  
After pronouncement of judgment in a foreign-related criminal case, the handling result shall be promptly notified to the competent foreign affairs department of the people's government at the same level.  
Where the death penalty is to be executed against a foreign national defendant, after the death penalty ruling is issued and before execution, the embassy or consulate of his or her country of nationality in China shall be notified.  
Where a foreign national defendant dies during the trial, the competent foreign affairs department of the people's government at the same level shall be promptly notified, and the relevant embassy or consulate of the country concerned stationed in China shall be notified.

<a id="art-480"></a>第四百八十条 需要向有关国家驻华使领馆通知有关事项的，应当层报高级人民法院，由高级人民法院按照下列规定通知：  
（一）外国籍当事人国籍国与我国签订有双边领事条约的，根据条约规定办理；未与我国签订双边领事条约，但参加《维也纳领事关系公约》的，根据公约规定办理；未与我国签订领事条约，也未参加《维也纳领事关系公约》，但与我国有外交关系的，可以根据外事主管部门的意见，按照互惠原则，根据有关规定和国际惯例办理；  
（二）在外国驻华领馆领区内发生的涉外刑事案件，通知有关外国驻该地区的领馆；在外国领馆领区外发生的涉外刑事案件，通知有关外国驻华使馆；与我国有外交关系，但未设使领馆的国家，可以通知其代管国家驻华使领馆；无代管国家、代管国家不明的，可以不通知；  
（三）双边领事条约规定通知时限的，应当在规定的期限内通知；没有规定的，应当根据或者参照《维也纳领事关系公约》和国际惯例尽快通知，至迟不得超过七日；  
（四）双边领事条约没有规定必须通知，外国籍当事人要求不通知其国籍国驻华使领馆的，可以不通知，但应当由其本人出具书面声明。  
高级人民法院向外国驻华使领馆通知有关事项，必要时，可以请人民政府外事主管部门协助。

**Article 480** Where it is necessary to notify the relevant embassy or consulate of the country concerned stationed in China of relevant matters, the matter shall be reported level by level to the Higher People's Court, which shall make the notification in accordance with the following provisions:  
(1) Where the country of nationality of the foreign national party has concluded a bilateral consular treaty with China, the matter shall be handled in accordance with the treaty provisions; where no bilateral consular treaty has been concluded with China, but the country is a party to the Vienna Convention on Consular Relations, the matter shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of the Convention; where no consular treaty has been concluded with China and the country is not a party to the Vienna Convention on Consular Relations, but has diplomatic relations with China, the matter may be handled in accordance with the opinions of the foreign affairs department, the principle of reciprocity, and relevant provisions and international practice;  
(2) For a foreign-related criminal case occurring within the consular district of a foreign consulate in China, the relevant foreign consulate in that region shall be notified; for a foreign-related criminal case occurring outside the consular district of a foreign consulate, the relevant foreign embassy in China shall be notified; where a country has diplomatic relations with China but has not established an embassy or consulate, the embassy or consulate in China of the state representing its interests may be notified; where there is no representing state, or the representing state is unknown, notification may be omitted;  
(3) Where a bilateral consular treaty stipulates a time limit for notification, notification shall be made within the prescribed time limit; where there is no such stipulation, notification shall be made as soon as possible in accordance with or by reference to the Vienna Convention on Consular Relations and international practice, and shall not exceed seven days at the latest;  
(4) Where a bilateral consular treaty does not stipulate that notification is mandatory, and the foreign national party requests that his or her country of nationality's embassy or consulate in China not be notified, notification may be omitted, but the party shall personally issue a written statement.  
Where the Higher People's Court notifies the relevant foreign embassy or consulate in China of relevant matters, it may, if necessary, request the assistance of the foreign affairs department of the people's government.

<a id="art-481"></a>第四百八十一条 人民法院受理涉外刑事案件后，应当告知在押的外国籍被告人享有与其国籍国驻华使领馆联系，与其监护人、近亲属会见、通信，以及请求人民法院提供翻译的权利。

**Article 481** After accepting a foreign-related criminal case, the People's Court shall inform the foreign national defendant in custody of the rights to contact the embassy or consulate of his or her country of nationality stationed in China, to meet and correspond with his or her guardian or close relatives, and to request the People's Court to provide translation.

<a id="art-482"></a>第四百八十二条 涉外刑事案件审判期间，外国籍被告人在押，其国籍国驻华使领馆官员要求探视的，可以向受理案件的人民法院所在地的高级人民法院提出。人民法院应当根据我国与被告人国籍国签订的双边领事条约规定的时限予以安排；没有条约规定的，应当尽快安排。必要时，可以请人民政府外事主管部门协助。  
涉外刑事案件审判期间，外国籍被告人在押，其监护人、近亲属申请会见的，可以向受理案件的人民法院所在地的高级人民法院提出，并依照[本解释第四百八十六条](#art-486)的规定提供与被告人关系的证明。人民法院经审查认为不妨碍案件审判的，可以批准。  
被告人拒绝接受探视、会见的，应当由其本人出具书面声明。拒绝出具书面声明的，应当记录在案；必要时，应当录音录像。  
探视、会见被告人应当遵守我国法律规定。

**Article 482** During the trial of a foreign-related criminal case, where a foreign national defendant is in custody and officials of the embassy or consulate of his or her country of nationality stationed in China request to visit him or her, they may make a request to the Higher People's Court at the place where the court accepting the case is located. The People's Court shall arrange the visit in accordance with the time limit stipulated in the bilateral consular treaty concluded between China and the defendant's country of nationality; where there is no treaty stipulation, the visit shall be arranged as soon as possible. If necessary, the assistance of the foreign affairs department of the people's government may be requested.  
During the trial of a foreign-related criminal case, where a foreign national defendant is in custody and his or her guardian or close relatives apply for a meeting, they may make an application to the Higher People's Court at the place where the court accepting the case is located, and provide proof of the relationship with the defendant in accordance with the provisions of Article 486 of this Interpretation. Where the People's Court, upon examination, considers that the meeting will not impede the trial of the case, it may approve it.  
Where the defendant refuses to receive visits or meetings, he or she shall personally issue a written statement. Where he or she refuses to issue a written statement, it shall be recorded in the case file; if necessary, audio and video recording shall be made.  
Visits and meetings with the defendant shall comply with the laws of China.

<a id="art-483"></a>第四百八十三条 人民法院审理涉外刑事案件，应当公开进行，但依法不应公开审理的除外。  
公开审理的涉外刑事案件，外国籍当事人国籍国驻华使领馆官员要求旁听的，可以向受理案件的人民法院所在地的高级人民法院提出申请，人民法院应当安排。

**Article 483** The People's Court shall hear foreign-related criminal cases in public, except where the case shall not be heard in public according to law.  
For a foreign-related criminal case heard in public, where officials of the embassy or consulate of the country of nationality of the foreign national party stationed in China request to observe the hearing, they may apply to the Higher People's Court at the place where the court accepting the case is located, and the People's Court shall make arrangements.

<a id="art-484"></a>第四百八十四条 人民法院审判涉外刑事案件，使用中华人民共和国通用的语言、文字，应当为外国籍当事人提供翻译。翻译人员应当在翻译文件上签名。  
人民法院的诉讼文书为中文本。外国籍当事人不通晓中文的，应当附有外文译本，译本不加盖人民法院印章，以中文本为准。  
外国籍当事人通晓中国语言、文字，拒绝他人翻译，或者不需要诉讼文书外文译本的，应当由其本人出具书面声明。拒绝出具书面声明的，应当记录在案；必要时，应当录音录像。

**Article 484** When the People's Court adjudicates foreign-related criminal cases, it shall use the common spoken and written languages of the People's Republic of China, and shall provide translation for foreign national parties. The interpreter shall sign the translation documents.  
The litigation documents of the People's Court shall be in Chinese. Where a foreign national party does not understand Chinese, a foreign language translation shall be attached; the translation shall not be affixed with the seal of the People's Court, and the Chinese text shall prevail.  
Where a foreign national party understands the Chinese language and script, refuses translation by others, or does not need a foreign language translation of the litigation documents, he or she shall personally issue a written statement. Where he or she refuses to issue a written statement, it shall be recorded in the case file; if necessary, audio and video recording shall be made.

<a id="art-485"></a>第四百八十五条 外国籍被告人委托律师辩护，或者外国籍附带民事诉讼原告人、自诉人委托律师代理诉讼的，应当委托具有中华人民共和国律师资格并依法取得执业证书的律师。  
外国籍被告人在押的，其监护人、近亲属或者其国籍国驻华使领馆可以代为委托辩护人。其监护人、近亲属代为委托的，应当提供与被告人关系的有效证明。  
外国籍当事人委托其监护人、近亲属担任辩护人、诉讼代理人的，被委托人应当提供与当事人关系的有效证明。经审查，符合刑事诉讼法、有关司法解释规定的，人民法院应当准许。  
外国籍被告人没有委托辩护人的，人民法院可以通知法律援助机构为其指派律师提供辩护。被告人拒绝辩护人辩护的，应当由其出具书面声明，或者将其口头声明记录在案；必要时，应当录音录像。被告人属于应当提供法律援助情形的，依照[本解释第五十条](#art-50)规定处理。

**Article 485** Where a foreign national defendant entrusts a lawyer to defend, or a foreign national plaintiff in an incidental civil action or private prosecutor entrusts a lawyer to act as agent ad litem, a lawyer who possesses the qualifications of a lawyer of the People's Republic of China and has lawfully obtained a practice certificate shall be entrusted.  
Where a foreign national defendant is in custody, his or her guardian, close relatives, or the embassy or consulate of his or her country of nationality stationed in China may entrust a defender on his or her behalf. Where the guardian or close relatives entrust a defender on his or her behalf, they shall provide valid proof of the relationship with the defendant.  
Where a foreign national party entrusts his or her guardian or close relatives to serve as defender or litigation agent, the entrusted person shall provide valid proof of the relationship with the party. Upon examination, if it conforms to the Criminal Procedure Law and relevant judicial interpretations, the People's Court shall permit it.  
Where a foreign national defendant has not entrusted a defender, the People's Court may notify a legal aid institution to assign a lawyer to provide defense for him or her. Where the defendant refuses the defense of the defender, he or she shall issue a written statement, or his or her oral statement shall be recorded in the case file; if necessary, audio and video recording shall be made. Where the defendant falls under the circumstances where legal aid shall be provided, the matter shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of Article 50 of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-486"></a>第四百八十六条 外国籍当事人从中华人民共和国领域外寄交或者托交给中国律师或者中国公民的委托书，以及外国籍当事人的监护人、近亲属提供的与当事人关系的证明，必须经所在国公证机关证明，所在国中央外交主管机关或者其授权机关认证，并经中华人民共和国驻该国使领馆认证，或者履行中华人民共和国与该所在国订立的有关条约中规定的证明手续，但我国与该国之间有互免认证协定的除外。

**Article 486** A power of attorney sent or entrusted by a foreign national party from outside the territory of the People's Republic of China to a Chinese lawyer or Chinese citizen, and the proof of relationship with the party provided by the guardian or close relatives of the foreign national party, must be notarized by a notarial organ of the country where the party is located, authenticated by the central competent foreign affairs authority of that country or its authorized organ, and authenticated by the embassy or consulate of the People's Republic of China in that country, or undergo the certification formalities prescribed in the relevant treaties concluded between the People's Republic of China and that country, except where an agreement on mutual exemption of authentication exists between China and that country.

<a id="art-487"></a>第四百八十七条 对涉外刑事案件的被告人，可以决定限制出境；对开庭审理案件时必须到庭的证人，可以要求暂缓出境。限制外国人出境的，应当通报同级人民政府外事主管部门和当事人国籍国驻华使领馆。  
人民法院决定限制外国人和中国公民出境的，应当书面通知被限制出境的人在案件审理终结前不得离境，并可以采取扣留护照或者其他出入境证件的办法限制其出境；扣留证件的，应当履行必要手续，并发给本人扣留证件的证明。  
需要对外国人和中国公民在口岸采取边控措施的，受理案件的人民法院应当按照规定制作边控对象通知书，并附有关法律文书，层报高级人民法院办理交控手续。紧急情况下，需要采取临时边控措施的，受理案件的人民法院可以先向有关口岸所在地出入境边防检查机关交控，但应当在七日以内按照规定层报高级人民法院办理手续。

**Article 487** With respect to a defendant in a foreign-related criminal case, a decision may be made to restrict him or her from leaving the country; with respect to a witness who must be present in court when the case is heard, he or she may be required to postpone leaving the country. Where the exit of a foreigner is restricted, the competent foreign affairs department of the people's government at the same level and the embassy or consulate of the party's country of nationality stationed in China shall be notified.  
Where the People's Court decides to restrict a foreigner or a Chinese citizen from leaving the country, it shall notify in writing the person whose exit is restricted that he or she shall not leave the country before the conclusion of the trial of the case, and may take measures to detain his or her passport or other exit-entry documents to restrict his or her exit; where documents are detained, necessary formalities shall be completed, and a certificate of detention of the documents shall be issued to the person.  
Where border control measures are required to be taken against a foreigner or a Chinese citizen at a port, the People's Court accepting the case shall, in accordance with the provisions, prepare a notice of the subject of border control, attach relevant legal documents, and report level by level to the Higher People's Court for handling the control procedures. In an emergency, where temporary border control measures need to be taken, the People's Court accepting the case may first deliver the control to the exit-entry border inspection authority at the place of the relevant port, but shall, within seven days, report level by level to the Higher People's Court to complete the procedures in accordance with the provisions.

<a id="art-488"></a>第四百八十八条 涉外刑事案件，符合[刑事诉讼法第二百零八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-208)第一款、第二百四十三条规定的，经有关人民法院批准或者决定，可以延长审理期限。

**Article 488** For a foreign-related criminal case, if the provisions of paragraph 1 of Article 208 and Article 243 of the Criminal Procedure Law are satisfied, the trial period may be extended upon approval or decision by the relevant People's Court.

<a id="art-489"></a>第四百八十九条 涉外刑事案件宣判后，外国籍当事人国籍国驻华使领馆要求提供裁判文书的，可以向受理案件的人民法院所在地的高级人民法院提出，人民法院可以提供。

**Article 489** After judgment is pronounced in a foreign-related criminal case, if the embassy or consulate of the country of nationality of the foreign national party stationed in China requests provision of the judgment document, it may make a request to the Higher People's Court at the place where the court accepting the case is located, and the People's Court may provide it.

<a id="art-490"></a>第四百九十条 涉外刑事案件审理过程中的其他事项，依照法律、司法解释和其他有关规定办理。

**Article 490** Other matters in the course of trial of foreign-related criminal cases shall be handled in accordance with laws, judicial interpretations, and other relevant provisions.

#### 第二节 刑事司法协助

<a id="art-491"></a>第四百九十一条 请求和提供司法协助，应当依照《中华人民共和国国际刑事司法协助法》、我国与有关国家、地区签订的刑事司法协助条约、移管被判刑人条约和有关法律规定进行。  
对请求书的签署机关、请求书及所附材料的语言文字、有关办理期限和具体程序等事项，在不违反中华人民共和国法律的基本原则的情况下，可以按照刑事司法协助条约规定或者双方协商办理。

**Article 491** Requests for and provision of judicial assistance shall be made in accordance with the International Criminal Judicial Assistance Law of the People's Republic of China, the treaties on criminal judicial assistance and treaties on the transfer of sentenced persons concluded between China and the relevant countries and regions, and relevant legal provisions.  
With respect to matters such as the organ signing the request, the language used in the request and attached materials, the relevant time limits for handling, and specific procedures, where the fundamental principles of the laws of the People's Republic of China are not violated, they may be handled in accordance with the provisions of the criminal judicial assistance treaty or through consultation between the parties.

<a id="art-492"></a>第四百九十二条 外国法院请求的事项有损中华人民共和国的主权、安全、社会公共利益以及违反中华人民共和国法律的基本原则的，人民法院不予协助；属于有关法律规定的可以拒绝提供刑事司法协助情形的，可以不予协助。

**Article 492** Where the matter requested by a foreign court is detrimental to the sovereignty, security, or public interests of the People's Republic of China, or violates the fundamental principles of the laws of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court shall not provide assistance; where it falls under the circumstances provided by relevant laws under which criminal judicial assistance may be refused, assistance may be refused.

<a id="art-493"></a>第四百九十三条 人民法院请求外国提供司法协助的，应当层报最高人民法院，经最高人民法院审核同意后交由有关对外联系机关及时向外国提出请求。  
外国法院请求我国提供司法协助，有关对外联系机关认为属于人民法院职权范围的，经最高人民法院审核同意后转有关人民法院办理。

**Article 493** Where the People's Court requests a foreign country to provide judicial assistance, the request shall be reported level by level to the Supreme People's Court, and after examination and approval by the Supreme People's Court, it shall be submitted to the relevant organ for foreign contacts to promptly make the request to the foreign country.  
Where a foreign court requests China to provide judicial assistance, and the relevant organ for foreign contacts considers that the matter falls within the functions and powers of the People's Court, after examination and approval by the Supreme People's Court, the matter shall be transferred to the relevant People's Court for handling.

<a id="art-494"></a>第四百九十四条 人民法院请求外国提供司法协助的请求书，应当依照刑事司法协助条约的规定提出；没有条约或者条约没有规定的，应当载明法律规定的相关信息并附相关材料。请求书及其所附材料应当以中文制作,并附有被请求国官方文字的译本。  
外国请求我国法院提供司法协助的请求书，应当依照刑事司法协助条约的规定提出；没有条约或者条约没有规定的，应当载明我国法律规定的相关信息并附相关材料。请求书及所附材料应当附有中文译本。

**Article 494** A request by the People's Court for a foreign country to provide judicial assistance shall be made in accordance with the provisions of the criminal judicial assistance treaty; where there is no treaty or the treaty has no provisions, the request shall contain the relevant information prescribed by law and be accompanied by relevant materials. The request and its attached materials shall be made in Chinese, with a translation attached in the official language of the requested country.  
A request by a foreign country for judicial assistance from the courts of China shall be made in accordance with the provisions of the criminal judicial assistance treaty; where there is no treaty or the treaty has no provisions, the request shall contain the relevant information prescribed by the laws of China and be accompanied by relevant materials. The request and its attached materials shall be accompanied by a Chinese translation.

<a id="art-495"></a>第四百九十五条 人民法院向在中华人民共和国领域外居住的当事人送达刑事诉讼文书，可以采用下列方式：  
（一）根据受送达人所在国与中华人民共和国缔结或者共同参加的国际条约规定的方式送达；  
（二）通过外交途径送达;  
（三）对中国籍当事人，所在国法律允许或者经所在国同意的，可以委托我国驻受送达人所在国的使领馆代为送达；  
（四）当事人是自诉案件的自诉人或者附带民事诉讼原告人的，可以向有权代其接受送达的诉讼代理人送达；  
（五）当事人是外国单位的，可以向其在中华人民共和国领域内设立的代表机构或者有权接受送达的分支机构、业务代办人送达；  
（六）受送达人所在国法律允许的，可以邮寄送达；自邮寄之日起满三个月，送达回证未退回，但根据各种情况足以认定已经送达的，视为送达；  
（七）受送达人所在国法律允许的，可以采用传真、电子邮件等能够确认受送达人收悉的方式送达。

**Article 495** Where the People's Court serves criminal procedural documents on a party residing outside the territory of the People's Republic of China, the following methods may be used:  
(1) Service in accordance with the method prescribed by the international treaty concluded between the country where the addressee is located and the People's Republic of China or to which both countries are parties;  
(2) Service through diplomatic channels;  
(3) For a party of Chinese nationality, where the law of the country where the party is located permits or the country agrees, the embassy or consulate of China in the country where the addressee is located may be entrusted to serve the documents on his or her behalf;  
(4) Where the party is a private prosecutor in a case of private prosecution or a plaintiff in an incidental civil action, service may be made on a litigation agent who is authorized to accept service on his or her behalf;  
(5) Where the party is a foreign entity, service may be made on its representative office established within the territory of the People's Republic of China or its branch or business agent authorized to accept service;  
(6) Where the law of the country where the addressee is located permits, service may be made by mail; where three months have elapsed since the date of mailing and the receipt of service has not been returned, but it is sufficient to determine that service has been effected based on the various circumstances, it shall be deemed served;  
(7) Where the law of the country where the addressee is located permits, service may be made by fax, e-mail, or other means capable of confirming receipt by the addressee.

<a id="art-496"></a>第四百九十六条 人民法院通过外交途径向在中华人民共和国领域外居住的受送达人送达刑事诉讼文书的，所送达的文书应当经高级人民法院审查后报最高人民法院审核。最高人民法院认为可以发出的，由最高人民法院交外交部主管部门转递。  
外国法院通过外交途径请求人民法院送达刑事诉讼文书的，由该国驻华使馆将法律文书交我国外交部主管部门转最高人民法院。最高人民法院审核后认为属于人民法院职权范围，且可以代为送达的，应当转有关人民法院办理。

**Article 496** Where the People's Court serves criminal procedural documents through diplomatic channels on an addressee residing outside the territory of the People's Republic of China, the documents to be served shall be examined by the Higher People's Court and then reported to the Supreme People's Court for review. Where the Supreme People's Court considers that they may be sent out, the Supreme People's Court shall deliver them to the competent department of the Ministry of Foreign Affairs for transmission.  
Where a foreign court requests the People's Court through diplomatic channels to serve criminal procedural documents, the embassy of that country in China shall deliver the legal documents to the competent department of the Ministry of Foreign Affairs of China for transmission to the Supreme People's Court. Where the Supreme People's Court, after review, considers that the matter falls within the functions and powers of the People's Court and that service may be effected on its behalf, it shall transfer the matter to the relevant People's Court for handling.

### 第二十一章 执行程序

#### 第一节 死刑的执行

<a id="art-497"></a>第四百九十七条 被判处死刑缓期执行的罪犯，在死刑缓期执行期间犯罪的，应当由罪犯服刑地的中级人民法院依法审判，所作的判决可以上诉、抗诉。  
认定故意犯罪，情节恶劣，应当执行死刑的，在判决、裁定发生法律效力后，应当层报最高人民法院核准执行死刑。  
对故意犯罪未执行死刑的，不再报高级人民法院核准，死刑缓期执行的期间重新计算，并层报最高人民法院备案。备案不影响判决、裁定的生效和执行。  
最高人民法院经备案审查，认为原判不予执行死刑错误，确需改判的，应当依照审判监督程序予以纠正。

**Article 497** Where a criminal sentenced to death with a two-year reprieve commits a crime during the period of suspension of execution, the case shall be tried according to law by the Intermediate People's Court at the place where the criminal serves his or her sentence, and the judgment rendered may be appealed or protested.  
Where it is determined that the crime was intentional, the circumstances are flagrant, and the death penalty shall be executed, after the judgment or ruling becomes legally effective, the matter shall be reported level by level to the Supreme People's Court for approval of execution of the death penalty.  
Where the death penalty is not executed for the intentional crime, it shall no longer be reported to the Higher People's Court for approval, the period of suspension of execution of the death sentence shall be recalculated, and the matter shall be reported level by level to the Supreme People's Court for record. The record shall not affect the taking effect and execution of the judgment or ruling.  
Where the Supreme People's Court, upon review of the record, considers that the original judgment not to execute the death penalty is erroneous and a change of sentence is indeed necessary, it shall correct it in accordance with the trial supervision procedure.

<a id="art-498"></a>第四百九十八条 死刑缓期执行的期间，从判决或者裁定核准死刑缓期执行的法律文书宣告或者送达之日起计算。  
死刑缓期执行期满，依法应当减刑的，人民法院应当及时减刑。死刑缓期执行期满减为无期徒刑、有期徒刑的，刑期自死刑缓期执行期满之日起计算。

**Article 498** The period of suspension of execution of a death sentence shall be calculated from the date on which the legal document approving the suspension of execution of the death sentence is pronounced or served.  
Where the period of suspension of execution of a death sentence expires and commutation of sentence shall be granted according to law, the People's Court shall promptly commute the sentence. Where the sentence is commuted to life imprisonment or fixed-term imprisonment upon expiration of the period of suspension of execution of the death sentence, the term of imprisonment shall be calculated from the date on which the period of suspension of execution of the death sentence expires.

<a id="art-499"></a>第四百九十九条 最高人民法院的执行死刑命令，由高级人民法院交付第一审人民法院执行。第一审人民法院接到执行死刑命令后，应当在七日以内执行。  
在死刑缓期执行期间故意犯罪，最高人民法院核准执行死刑的，由罪犯服刑地的中级人民法院执行。
