**Article 499** The execution order of the Supreme People's Court for the death penalty shall be delivered by the Higher People's Court to the court of first instance for execution. After receiving the execution order for the death penalty, the court of first instance shall execute it within seven days.  
Where a criminal commits an intentional crime during the period of suspension of execution of a death sentence and the Supreme People's Court approves the execution of the death penalty, the execution shall be carried out by the Intermediate People's Court at the place where the criminal serves his or her sentence.

<a id="art-500"></a>第五百条 下级人民法院在接到执行死刑命令后、执行前，发现有下列情形之一的，应当暂停执行，并立即将请求停止执行死刑的报告和相关材料层报最高人民法院：  
（一）罪犯可能有其他犯罪的；  
（二）共同犯罪的其他犯罪嫌疑人到案，可能影响罪犯量刑的；  
（三）共同犯罪的其他罪犯被暂停或者停止执行死刑，可能影响罪犯量刑的；  
（四）罪犯揭发重大犯罪事实或者有其他重大立功表现，可能需要改判的；  
（五）罪犯怀孕的；  
（六）判决、裁定可能有影响定罪量刑的其他错误的。  
最高人民法院经审查，认为可能影响罪犯定罪量刑的，应当裁定停止执行死刑；认为不影响的，应当决定继续执行死刑。

**Article 500** Where the lower People's Court, after receiving the execution order for the death penalty and before execution, discovers that any of the following circumstances exists, it shall suspend the execution and immediately report the report requesting suspension of execution of the death penalty and relevant materials level by level to the Supreme People's Court:  
(1) The criminal may have committed other crimes;  
(2) Other criminal suspects in the joint crime have been brought to justice, which may affect the sentencing of the criminal;  
(3) Other criminals in the joint crime have been suspended or stopped from execution of the death penalty, which may affect the sentencing of the criminal;  
(4) The criminal has exposed major criminal facts or has other major meritorious service, which may necessitate a change of sentence;  
(5) The criminal is pregnant;  
(6) There may be other errors in the judgment or ruling affecting the conviction and sentencing.  
Where the Supreme People's Court, upon examination, considers that it may affect the conviction and sentencing of the criminal, it shall rule to stop the execution of the death penalty; where it considers that it does not affect, it shall decide to continue the execution of the death penalty.

<a id="art-501"></a>第五百零一条 最高人民法院在执行死刑命令签发后、执行前，发现有前条第一款规定情形的，应当立即裁定停止执行死刑，并将有关材料移交下级人民法院。

**Article 501** Where the Supreme People's Court, after issuing the execution order for the death penalty and before execution, discovers any circumstance provided in paragraph 1 of the preceding article, it shall immediately rule to stop the execution of the death penalty and transfer the relevant materials to the lower People's Court.

<a id="art-502"></a>第五百零二条 下级人民法院接到最高人民法院停止执行死刑的裁定后，应当会同有关部门调查核实停止执行死刑的事由，并及时将调查结果和意见层报最高人民法院审核。

**Article 502** After receiving the ruling of the Supreme People's Court to stop the execution of the death penalty, the lower People's Court shall, together with the relevant departments, investigate and verify the grounds for stopping the execution of the death penalty, and promptly report the investigation results and opinions level by level to the Supreme People's Court for review.

<a id="art-503"></a>第五百零三条 对下级人民法院报送的停止执行死刑的调查结果和意见，由最高人民法院原作出核准死刑判决、裁定的合议庭负责审查；必要时，另行组成合议庭进行审查。

**Article 503** The investigation results and opinions submitted by the lower People's Court regarding the suspension of execution of the death penalty shall be reviewed by the collegiate bench of the Supreme People's Court that originally rendered the judgment or ruling approving the death penalty; if necessary, a separate collegiate bench shall be formed for review.

<a id="art-504"></a>第五百零四条 最高人民法院对停止执行死刑的案件，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）确认罪犯怀孕的，应当改判；  
（二）确认罪犯有其他犯罪，依法应当追诉的，应当裁定不予核准死刑，撤销原判，发回重新审判；  
（三）确认原判决、裁定有错误或者罪犯有重大立功表现，需要改判的，应当裁定不予核准死刑，撤销原判，发回重新审判；  
（四）确认原判决、裁定没有错误，罪犯没有重大立功表现，或者重大立功表现不影响原判决、裁定执行的，应当裁定继续执行死刑，并由院长重新签发执行死刑的命令。

**Article 504** The Supreme People's Court shall handle a case in which the execution of the death penalty has been stopped in accordance with the following circumstances respectively:  
(1) Where it is confirmed that the criminal is pregnant, the sentence shall be changed;  
(2) Where it is confirmed that the criminal has committed other crimes and shall be prosecuted according to law, it shall rule not to approve the death penalty, revoke the original judgment, and remand the case for retrial;  
(3) Where it is confirmed that the original judgment or ruling is erroneous or the criminal has major meritorious service and a change of sentence is necessary, it shall rule not to approve the death penalty, revoke the original judgment, and remand the case for retrial;  
(4) Where it is confirmed that the original judgment or ruling is not erroneous, the criminal has no major meritorious service, or the major meritorious service does not affect the execution of the original judgment or ruling, it shall rule to continue the execution of the death penalty, and the president shall reissue the execution order for the death penalty.

<a id="art-505"></a>第五百零五条 第一审人民法院在执行死刑前，应当告知罪犯有权会见其近亲属。罪犯申请会见并提供具体联系方式的，人民法院应当通知其近亲属。确实无法与罪犯近亲属取得联系，或者其近亲属拒绝会见的，应当告知罪犯。罪犯申请通过录音录像等方式留下遗言的，人民法院可以准许。  
罪犯近亲属申请会见的，人民法院应当准许并及时安排，但罪犯拒绝会见的除外。罪犯拒绝会见的，应当记录在案并及时告知其近亲属；必要时，应当录音录像。  
罪犯申请会见近亲属以外的亲友，经人民法院审查，确有正当理由的，在确保安全的情况下可以准许。  
罪犯申请会见未成年子女的，应当经未成年子女的监护人同意；会见可能影响未成年人身心健康的，人民法院可以通过视频方式安排会见，会见时监护人应当在场。  
会见一般在罪犯羁押场所进行。  
会见情况应当记录在案，附卷存档。

**Article 505** Before executing the death penalty, the court of first instance shall inform the criminal of his or her right to meet with close relatives. Where the criminal applies for a meeting and provides specific contact information, the People's Court shall notify his or her close relatives. Where it is truly impossible to contact the criminal's close relatives, or the close relatives refuse to meet, the criminal shall be informed. Where the criminal applies to leave last words by means of audio or video recording, the People's Court may permit it.  
Where close relatives of the criminal apply for a meeting, the People's Court shall permit it and arrange it promptly, except where the criminal refuses to meet. Where the criminal refuses to meet, the refusal shall be recorded in the case file and promptly notified to his or her close relatives; if necessary, audio or video recording shall be made.  
Where the criminal applies to meet with friends or relatives other than close relatives, and upon examination by the People's Court there are indeed legitimate reasons, it may be permitted on the premise of ensuring safety.  
Where the criminal applies to meet with his or her minor children, the consent of the guardian of the minor children shall be obtained; where the meeting may affect the physical and mental health of the minors, the People's Court may arrange the meeting by video, and the guardian shall be present at the meeting.  
Meetings shall generally be held at the place where the criminal is in custody.  
The circumstances of the meeting shall be recorded in the case file and kept in the file for archival purposes.

<a id="art-506"></a>第五百零六条 第一审人民法院在执行死刑三日以前，应当通知同级人民检察院派员临场监督。

**Article 506** The court of first instance shall, three days before the execution of the death penalty, notify the People's Procuratorate at the same level to send personnel to supervise on the scene.

<a id="art-507"></a>第五百零七条 死刑采用枪决或者注射等方法执行。  
采用注射方法执行死刑的，应当在指定的刑场或者羁押场所内执行。  
采用枪决、注射以外的其他方法执行死刑的，应当事先层报最高人民法院批准。

**Article 507** The death penalty shall be executed by shooting or injection, among other methods.  
Where the death penalty is executed by injection, it shall be carried out at a designated execution ground or place of custody.  
Where the death penalty is executed by a method other than shooting or injection, prior approval shall be obtained from the Supreme People's Court through level-by-level reporting.

<a id="art-508"></a>第五百零八条 执行死刑前，指挥执行的审判人员应当对罪犯验明正身，讯问有无遗言、信札，并制作笔录，再交执行人员执行死刑。  
执行死刑应当公布，禁止游街示众或者其他有辱罪犯人格的行为。

**Article 508** Before the execution of the death penalty, the judicial officer directing the execution shall verify the identity of the criminal, interrogate whether he or she has last words or letters, and make a record, and then hand the criminal over to the execution personnel for execution of the death penalty.  
The execution of the death penalty shall be announced publicly. Parading the criminal in public or other acts that humiliate the criminal's personality are prohibited.

<a id="art-509"></a>第五百零九条 执行死刑后，应当由法医验明罪犯确实死亡，在场书记员制作笔录。负责执行的人民法院应当在执行死刑后十五日以内将执行情况，包括罪犯被执行死刑前后的照片，上报最高人民法院。

**Article 509** After the execution of the death penalty, a forensic doctor shall verify that the criminal is indeed dead, and the court clerk present shall make a record. The People's Court responsible for the execution shall, within fifteen days after the execution of the death penalty, report the execution circumstances, including photographs of the criminal before and after the execution, to the Supreme People's Court.

<a id="art-510"></a>第五百一十条 执行死刑后，负责执行的人民法院应当办理以下事项：  
（一）对罪犯的遗书、遗言笔录，应当及时审查；涉及财产继承、债务清偿、家事嘱托等内容的，将遗书、遗言笔录交给家属，同时复制附卷备查；涉及案件线索等问题的，抄送有关机关；  
（二）通知罪犯家属在限期内领取罪犯骨灰；没有火化条件或者因民族、宗教等原因不宜火化的，通知领取尸体；过期不领取的，由人民法院通知有关单位处理，并要求有关单位出具处理情况的说明；对罪犯骨灰或者尸体的处理情况，应当记录在案；  
（三）对外国籍罪犯执行死刑后，通知外国驻华使领馆的程序和时限，根据有关规定办理。

**Article 510** After the execution of the death penalty, the People's Court responsible for the execution shall handle the following matters:  
(1) The criminal's suicide note and record of last words shall be promptly examined; where they involve property succession, debt settlement, family matters entrusted, etc., the suicide note and record of last words shall be handed over to the family members, with copies kept in the file for future reference; where they involve case clues or other issues, copies shall be sent to the relevant organs;  
(2) The criminal's family members shall be notified to collect the criminal's ashes within a time limit; where there are no cremation conditions or cremation is inappropriate due to ethnic, religious, or other reasons, the family members shall be notified to collect the body; where the ashes or body are not collected upon expiration of the time limit, the People's Court shall notify the relevant unit to handle the matter and require the relevant unit to issue an explanation of the handling; the handling of the criminal's ashes or body shall be recorded in the case file;  
(3) After the execution of the death penalty on a foreign national criminal, the procedures and time limits for notifying the foreign embassy or consulate in China shall be handled in accordance with relevant provisions.

#### 第二节 死刑缓期执行、无期徒刑、有期徒刑、拘役的交付执行

<a id="art-511"></a>第五百一十一条 被判处死刑缓期执行、无期徒刑、有期徒刑、拘役的罪犯，第一审人民法院应当在判决、裁定生效后十日以内，将判决书、裁定书、起诉书副本、自诉状复印件、执行通知书、结案登记表送达公安机关、监狱或者其他执行机关。

**Article 511** For a criminal sentenced to death with a two-year reprieve, life imprisonment, fixed-term imprisonment, or criminal detention, the court of first instance shall, within ten days after the judgment or ruling takes effect, serve the judgment, ruling, copy of the indictment, copy of the private complaint, execution notice, and case closure registration form on the public security organ, prison, or other executing organ.

<a id="art-512"></a>第五百一十二条 同案审理的案件中，部分被告人被判处死刑，对未被判处死刑的同案被告人需要羁押执行刑罚的，应当根据前条规定及时交付执行。但是，该同案被告人参与实施有关死刑之罪的，应当在复核讯问被判处死刑的被告人后交付执行。

**Article 512** In a case tried jointly, where some defendants are sentenced to death and co-defendants not sentenced to death need to be detained to serve their sentences, they shall be delivered for execution promptly in accordance with the preceding article. However, where such co-defendants participated in committing the crime related to the death penalty, they shall be delivered for execution after the review interrogation of the defendant sentenced to death.

<a id="art-513"></a>第五百一十三条 执行通知书回执经看守所盖章后，应当附卷备查。

**Article 513** After the receipt of the execution notice is stamped by the detention house, it shall be attached to the case file for future reference.

<a id="art-514"></a>第五百一十四条 罪犯在被交付执行前，因有严重疾病、怀孕或者正在哺乳自己婴儿的妇女、生活不能自理的原因，依法提出暂予监外执行的申请的，有关病情诊断、妊娠检查和生活不能自理的鉴别，由人民法院负责组织进行。

**Article 514** Where, before being delivered for execution, a criminal applies pursuant to law for temporary service of sentence outside prison because of serious illness, pregnancy, being a woman nursing her own infant, or inability to take care of himself or herself, the People's Court shall be responsible for organizing the diagnosis of the illness, the pregnancy examination, and the appraisal of inability to take care of oneself.

<a id="art-515"></a>第五百一十五条 被判处无期徒刑、有期徒刑或者拘役的罪犯，符合[刑事诉讼法第二百六十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-265)第一款、第二款的规定，人民法院决定暂予监外执行的，应当制作暂予监外执行决定书，写明罪犯基本情况、判决确定的罪名和刑罚、决定暂予监外执行的原因、依据等。  
人民法院在作出暂予监外执行决定前，应当征求人民检察院的意见。  
人民检察院认为人民法院的暂予监外执行决定不当，在法定期限内提出书面意见的，人民法院应当立即对该决定重新核查，并在一个月以内作出决定。  
对暂予监外执行的罪犯，适用[本解释第五百一十九条](#art-519)的有关规定，依法实行社区矫正。  
人民法院决定暂予监外执行的，由看守所或者执行取保候审、监视居住的公安机关自收到决定之日起十日以内将罪犯移送社区矫正机构。

**Article 515** Where a criminal sentenced to life imprisonment, fixed-term imprisonment or criminal detention meets the provisions of Article 265, paragraphs 1 and 2, of the Criminal Procedure Law, and the People's Court decides to allow temporary service of sentence outside prison, it shall issue a written decision on temporary service of sentence outside prison, specifying the basic information of the criminal, the crime and penalty determined by the judgment, and the reasons and basis for deciding temporary service of sentence outside prison.  
Before making a decision on temporary service of sentence outside prison, the People's Court shall seek the opinions of the People's Procuratorate.  
Where the People's Procuratorate considers that the People's Court's decision on temporary service of sentence outside prison is improper and submits written opinions within the statutory period, the People's Court shall immediately re-examine the decision and make a decision within one month.  
For criminals serving sentences temporarily outside prison, the relevant provisions of Article 519 of this Interpretation shall apply, and community correction shall be implemented according to law.  
Where the People's Court decides on temporary service of sentence outside prison, the detention house or the public security organ executing release on bail pending investigation or trial or residential surveillance shall transfer the criminal to the community correction institution within ten days from receipt of the decision.

<a id="art-516"></a>第五百一十六条 人民法院收到社区矫正机构的收监执行建议书后，经审查，确认暂予监外执行的罪犯具有下列情形之一的，应当作出收监执行的决定：  
（一）不符合暂予监外执行条件的；  
（二）未经批准离开所居住的市、县，经警告拒不改正，或者拒不报告行踪，脱离监管的；  
（三）因违反监督管理规定受到治安管理处罚，仍不改正的；  
（四）受到执行机关两次警告，仍不改正的；  
（五）保外就医期间不按规定提交病情复查情况，经警告拒不改正的；  
（六）暂予监外执行的情形消失后，刑期未满的；  
（七）保证人丧失保证条件或者因不履行义务被取消保证人资格，不能在规定期限内提出新的保证人的；  
（八）违反法律、行政法规和监督管理规定，情节严重的其他情形。

**Article 516** After receiving the written recommendation for execution of imprisonment from the community correction institution, the People's Court shall, upon examination, make a decision to execute imprisonment if it confirms that a criminal serving a sentence temporarily outside prison falls under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) failing to meet the conditions for temporary service of sentence outside prison;  
(2) leaving the city or county where he or she resides without approval and, after being warned, refusing to correct, or refusing to report whereabouts and evading supervision;  
(3) having been subjected to public security administration penalty for violating supervision and administration regulations and still refusing to correct;  
(4) having received two warnings from the executing organ and still refusing to correct;  
(5) during the period of medical parole, failing to submit the re-examination of the illness in accordance with the provisions and, after being warned, refusing to correct;  
(6) after the circumstances for temporary service of sentence outside prison disappear, the term of imprisonment has not expired;  
(7) the surety loses the qualifications for guarantee or is disqualified as a surety due to failure to perform obligations, and cannot propose a new surety within the prescribed time limit;  
(8) other circumstances where laws, administrative regulations, and supervision and administration regulations are violated and the circumstances are serious.

<a id="art-517"></a>第五百一十七条 人民法院应当在收到社区矫正机构的收监执行建议书后三十日以内作出决定。收监执行决定书一经作出，立即生效。  
人民法院应当将收监执行决定书送达社区矫正机构和公安机关，并抄送人民检察院，由公安机关将罪犯交付执行。

**Article 517** The People's Court shall make a decision within thirty days from receipt of the written recommendation for execution of imprisonment from the community correction institution. The written decision on execution of imprisonment shall take effect immediately upon being made.  
The People's Court shall serve the written decision on execution of imprisonment on the community correction institution and the public security organ, and send a copy to the People's Procuratorate; the public security organ shall deliver the criminal for execution.

<a id="art-518"></a>第五百一十八条 被收监执行的罪犯有不计入执行刑期情形的，人民法院应当在作出收监决定时，确定不计入执行刑期的具体时间。

**Article 518** Where a criminal executed in prison has circumstances in which time is not counted into the term of imprisonment, the People's Court shall, when making the decision on execution of imprisonment, determine the specific period of time that is not counted into the term of imprisonment.

#### 第三节 管制、缓刑、剥夺政治权利的交付执行

<a id="art-519"></a>第五百一十九条 对被判处管制、宣告缓刑的罪犯，人民法院应当依法确定社区矫正执行地。社区矫正执行地为罪犯的居住地；罪犯在多个地方居住的，可以确定其经常居住地为执行地；罪犯的居住地、经常居住地无法确定或者不适宜执行社区矫正的，应当根据有利于罪犯接受矫正、更好地融入社会的原则，确定执行地。  
宣判时，应当告知罪犯自判决、裁定生效之日起十日以内到执行地社区矫正机构报到，以及不按期报到的后果。  
人民法院应当自判决、裁定生效之日起五日以内通知执行地社区矫正机构，并在十日以内将判决书、裁定书、执行通知书等法律文书送达执行地社区矫正机构，同时抄送人民检察院和执行地公安机关。人民法院与社区矫正执行地不在同一地方的，由执行地社区矫正机构将法律文书转送所在地的人民检察院和公安机关。

**Article 519** For a criminal sentenced to public surveillance or granted probation, the People's Court shall determine the place for community correction according to law. The place for community correction shall be the criminal's place of residence; if the criminal resides in multiple places, his or her habitual residence may be determined as the place of execution; if the criminal's place of residence or habitual residence cannot be determined or is unsuitable for community correction, the place of execution shall be determined in accordance with the principle of facilitating the criminal's acceptance of correction and better integration into society.  
At the time of pronouncement of judgment, the criminal shall be informed that he or she shall report to the community correction institution at the place of execution within ten days from the effective date of the judgment or ruling, as well as the consequences of failing to report on time.  
The People's Court shall notify the community correction institution at the place of execution within five days from the effective date of the judgment or ruling, and shall serve legal documents such as the judgment, ruling, and execution notice on the community correction institution at the place of execution within ten days, with copies to the People's Procuratorate and the public security organ at the place of execution. If the People's Court is not in the same place as the place of community correction, the community correction institution at the place of execution shall forward the legal documents to the local People's Procuratorate and public security organ.

<a id="art-520"></a>第五百二十条 对单处剥夺政治权利的罪犯，人民法院应当在判决、裁定生效后十日以内，将判决书、裁定书、执行通知书等法律文书送达罪犯居住地的县级公安机关，并抄送罪犯居住地的县级人民检察院。

**Article 520** For a criminal sentenced solely to deprivation of political rights, the People's Court shall, within ten days after the judgment or ruling takes effect, serve legal documents such as the judgment, ruling, and execution notice on the county-level public security organ at the criminal's place of residence, and send a copy to the county-level People's Procuratorate at the criminal's place of residence.

#### 第四节 刑事裁判涉财产部分和附带民事裁判的执行

<a id="art-521"></a>第五百二十一条 刑事裁判涉财产部分的执行，是指发生法律效力的刑事裁判中下列判项的执行：  
（一）罚金、没收财产；  
（二）追缴、责令退赔违法所得；  
（三）处置随案移送的赃款赃物；  
（四）没收随案移送的供犯罪所用本人财物；  
（五）其他应当由人民法院执行的相关涉财产的判项。

**Article 521** Enforcement of the property-related part of a criminal judgment refers to the enforcement of the following items in a criminal judgment that has taken legal effect:  
(1) fines and confiscation of property;  
(2) recovery and ordering of restitution of illegal gains;  
(3) disposition of illicit money and goods transferred with the case;  
(4) confiscation of the criminal's own property used for the crime and transferred with the case;  
(5) other property-related judgments that shall be enforced by the People's Court.

<a id="art-522"></a>第五百二十二条 刑事裁判涉财产部分和附带民事裁判应当由人民法院执行的，由第一审人民法院负责裁判执行的机构执行。

**Article 522** Where the property-related part of a criminal judgment and any incidental civil judgment are to be enforced by the People's Court, the organ responsible for enforcement of judgments of the first-instance People's Court shall execute them.

<a id="art-523"></a>第五百二十三条 罚金在判决规定的期限内一次或者分期缴纳。期满无故不缴纳或者未足额缴纳的，人民法院应当强制缴纳。经强制缴纳仍不能全部缴纳的，在任何时候，包括主刑执行完毕后，发现被执行人有可供执行的财产的，应当追缴。  
行政机关对被告人就同一事实已经处以罚款的，人民法院判处罚金时应当折抵，扣除行政处罚已执行的部分。

**Article 523** A fine shall be paid in a lump sum or in installments within the time limit prescribed by the judgment. If the fine is not paid or not paid in full within the time limit without justifiable reasons, the People's Court shall enforce payment. If the fine still cannot be paid in full after compulsory enforcement, the fine shall be recovered whenever it is discovered that the person subject to enforcement has property available for enforcement, including after the execution of the principal punishment has been completed.  
Where an administrative organ has already imposed a fine on the defendant for the same facts, the People's Court shall, when imposing a fine, offset it by deducting the portion already executed as an administrative penalty.

<a id="art-524"></a>第五百二十四条 因遭遇不能抗拒的灾祸等原因缴纳罚金确有困难，被执行人申请延期缴纳、酌情减少或者免除罚金的，应当提交相关证明材料。人民法院应当在收到申请后一个月以内作出裁定。符合法定条件的，应当准许；不符合条件的，驳回申请。

**Article 524** Where the person subject to enforcement has genuine difficulty in paying a fine due to irresistible disasters or other reasons, and applies for an extension of the time limit for payment, a discretionary reduction, or exemption from the fine, he or she shall submit relevant evidentiary materials. The People's Court shall make a ruling within one month after receiving the application. If the statutory conditions are met, the application shall be granted; if not, the application shall be rejected.

<a id="art-525"></a>第五百二十五条 判处没收财产的，判决生效后，应当立即执行。

**Article 525** Where confiscation of property is imposed, the enforcement shall be carried out immediately after the judgment takes effect.

<a id="art-526"></a>第五百二十六条 执行财产刑，应当参照被扶养人住所地政府公布的上年度当地居民最低生活费标准，保留被执行人及其所扶养人的生活必需费用。

**Article 526** In enforcing a property-related punishment, the necessary living expenses of the person subject to enforcement and of the persons he or she supports shall be reserved, with reference to the local minimum living expense standard for residents published by the government of the place where the supported persons reside.

<a id="art-527"></a>第五百二十七条 被判处财产刑，同时又承担附带民事赔偿责任的被执行人，应当先履行民事赔偿责任。

**Article 527** A person subject to enforcement who is sentenced to a property-related punishment and also bears liability for incidental civil compensation shall first perform the civil compensation liability.

<a id="art-528"></a>第五百二十八条 执行刑事裁判涉财产部分、附带民事裁判过程中，当事人、利害关系人认为执行行为违反法律规定，或者案外人对被执行标的书面提出异议的，人民法院应当参照民事诉讼法的有关规定处理。

**Article 528** In the process of enforcing the property-related part of a criminal judgment or an incidental civil judgment, if a party or an interested person considers that the enforcement act violates the provisions of law, or a person other than the party raises a written objection to the subject matter being enforced, the People's Court shall handle the matter by referring to the relevant provisions of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-529"></a>第五百二十九条 执行刑事裁判涉财产部分、附带民事裁判过程中，具有下列情形之一的，人民法院应当裁定终结执行：  
（一）据以执行的判决、裁定被撤销的；  
（二）被执行人死亡或者被执行死刑，且无财产可供执行的；  
（三）被判处罚金的单位终止，且无财产可供执行的；  
（四）依照[刑法第五十三条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国刑法.md#art-53)规定免除罚金的；  
（五）应当终结执行的其他情形。  
裁定终结执行后，发现被执行人的财产有被隐匿、转移等情形的，应当追缴。

**Article 529** In the process of enforcing the property-related part of a criminal judgment or an incidental civil judgment, if any of the following circumstances exists, the People's Court shall rule to terminate the enforcement:  
(1) the judgment or ruling based on which enforcement is conducted has been revoked;  
(2) the person subject to enforcement dies or is executed with the death penalty and has no property available for enforcement;  
(3) the entity sentenced to a fine terminates and has no property available for enforcement;  
(4) the fine is exempted in accordance with Article 53 of the Criminal Law;  
(5) other circumstances under which enforcement shall be terminated.  
After a ruling to terminate enforcement, if it is discovered that the property of the person subject to enforcement has been concealed or transferred, the same shall be recovered.

<a id="art-530"></a>第五百三十条 被执行财产在外地的，第一审人民法院可以委托财产所在地的同级人民法院执行。

**Article 530** If the property to be enforced is located elsewhere, the first-instance People's Court may entrust the People's Court at the same level where the property is located to enforce it.

<a id="art-531"></a>第五百三十一条 刑事裁判涉财产部分、附带民事裁判全部或者部分被撤销的，已经执行的财产应当全部或者部分返还被执行人；无法返还的，应当依法赔偿。

**Article 531** If the property-related part of a criminal judgment or an incidental civil judgment is revoked in whole or in part, the property already enforced shall be returned to the person subject to enforcement in whole or in part; if return is impossible, compensation shall be made according to law.

<a id="art-532"></a>第五百三十二条 刑事裁判涉财产部分、附带民事裁判的执行，刑事诉讼法及有关刑事司法解释没有规定的，参照适用民事执行的有关规定。

**Article 532** Where the Criminal Procedure Law and relevant criminal judicial interpretations contain no provisions on the enforcement of the property-related part of a criminal judgment or an incidental civil judgment, the relevant provisions on civil enforcement shall apply by reference.

#### 第五节 减刑、假释案件的审理

<a id="art-533"></a>第五百三十三条 被判处死刑缓期执行的罪犯，在死刑缓期执行期间，没有故意犯罪的，死刑缓期执行期满后，应当裁定减刑；死刑缓期执行期满后，尚未裁定减刑前又犯罪的，应当在依法减刑后，对其所犯新罪另行审判。

**Article 533** Where a criminal sentenced to death with a two-year reprieve commits no intentional crime during the period of reprieve, a ruling shall be made to commute the punishment after the period of reprieve expires; if the criminal commits another crime after the period of the death sentence with a two-year reprieve expires but before a ruling on commutation is made, the new crime shall be tried separately after the punishment is commuted according to law.

<a id="art-534"></a>第五百三十四条 对减刑、假释案件，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）对被判处死刑缓期执行的罪犯的减刑，由罪犯服刑地的高级人民法院在收到同级监狱管理机关审核同意的减刑建议书后一个月以内作出裁定；  
（二）对被判处无期徒刑的罪犯的减刑、假释，由罪犯服刑地的高级人民法院在收到同级监狱管理机关审核同意的减刑、假释建议书后一个月以内作出裁定，案情复杂或者情况特殊的，可以延长一个月；  
（三）对被判处有期徒刑和被减为有期徒刑的罪犯的减刑、假释，由罪犯服刑地的中级人民法院在收到执行机关提出的减刑、假释建议书后一个月以内作出裁定，案情复杂或者情况特殊的，可以延长一个月；  
（四）对被判处管制、拘役的罪犯的减刑，由罪犯服刑地的中级人民法院在收到同级执行机关审核同意的减刑建议书后一个月以内作出裁定。  
对社区矫正对象的减刑，由社区矫正执行地的中级以上人民法院在收到社区矫正机构减刑建议书后三十日以内作出裁定。

**Article 534** Cases of commutation of punishment and parole shall be handled separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) Commutation of punishment for a criminal sentenced to death with a two-year reprieve shall be ruled by the Higher People's Court at the place where the criminal serves his or her sentence within one month after receiving the written recommendation for commutation that has been examined and approved by the prison administration organ at the same level;  
(2) Commutation of punishment and parole for a criminal sentenced to life imprisonment shall be ruled by the Higher People's Court at the place where the criminal serves his or her sentence within one month after receiving the written recommendation for commutation or parole that has been examined and approved by the prison administration organ at the same level, which may be extended by one month if the case is complex or the circumstances are special;  
(3) Commutation of punishment and parole for a criminal sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment and for a criminal whose sentence has been commuted to fixed-term imprisonment shall be ruled by the Intermediate People's Court at the place where the criminal serves his or her sentence within one month after receiving the written recommendation for commutation or parole proposed by the executing organ, which may be extended by one month if the case is complex or the circumstances are special;  
(4) Commutation of punishment for a criminal sentenced to public surveillance or criminal detention shall be ruled by the Intermediate People's Court at the place where the criminal serves his or her sentence within one month after receiving the written recommendation for commutation that has been examined and approved by the executing organ at the same level.  
Commutation of punishment for a person under community correction shall be ruled by an Intermediate People's Court or a higher People's Court at the place where community correction is executed within thirty days after receiving the written recommendation for commutation from the community correction institution.

<a id="art-535"></a>第五百三十五条 受理减刑、假释案件，应当审查执行机关移送的材料是否包括下列内容：  
（一）减刑、假释建议书；  
（二）原审法院的裁判文书、执行通知书、历次减刑裁定书的复制件；  
（三）证明罪犯确有悔改、立功或者重大立功表现具体事实的书面材料；  
（四）罪犯评审鉴定表、奖惩审批表等；  
（五）罪犯假释后对所居住社区影响的调查评估报告；  
（六）刑事裁判涉财产部分、附带民事裁判的执行、履行情况；  
（七）根据案件情况需要移送的其他材料。  
人民检察院对报请减刑、假释案件提出意见的，执行机关应当一并移送受理减刑、假释案件的人民法院。  
经审查，材料不全的，应当通知提请减刑、假释的执行机关在三日以内补送；逾期未补送的，不予立案。

**Article 535** When accepting a case of commutation of punishment or parole, it shall be examined whether the materials transferred by the executing organ include the following:  
(1) the written recommendation for commutation or parole;  
(2) copies of the judgment documents of the original trial court, the execution notice, and previous rulings on commutation of punishment;  
(3) written materials proving the specific facts of the criminal's genuine repentance, meritorious service, or major meritorious service;  
(4) the criminal's evaluation and appraisal form, and forms for approval of rewards and punishments;  
(5) an investigation and evaluation report on the impact of the criminal's parole on the community where he or she will reside;  
(6) the enforcement and performance of the property-related part of the criminal judgment and the incidental civil judgment;  
(7) other materials that need to be transferred according to the circumstances of the case.  
Where the People's Procuratorate submits opinions on a case submitted for commutation or parole, the executing organ shall transfer such opinions together to the People's Court accepting the case.  
Upon examination, if the materials are incomplete, the executing organ that proposed the commutation or parole shall be notified to supplement the materials within three days; if not supplemented within the time limit, the case shall not be placed on file.

<a id="art-536"></a>第五百三十六条 审理减刑、假释案件，对罪犯积极履行刑事裁判涉财产部分、附带民事裁判确定的义务的，可以认定有悔改表现，在减刑、假释时从宽掌握；对确有履行能力而不履行或者不全部履行的，在减刑、假释时从严掌握。

**Article 536** In hearing a case of commutation of punishment or parole, where a criminal actively performs the obligations determined in the property-related part of a criminal judgment or an incidental civil judgment, he or she may be found to have shown repentance, and lenient discretion may be exercised in commutation of punishment or parole; where the criminal has the ability to perform but fails or fails in full to perform, strict discretion shall be exercised in commutation of punishment or parole.

<a id="art-537"></a>第五百三十七条 审理减刑、假释案件，应当在立案后五日以内对下列事项予以公示：  
（一）罪犯的姓名、年龄等个人基本情况；  
（二）原判认定的罪名和刑期；  
（三）罪犯历次减刑情况；  
（四）执行机关的减刑、假释建议和依据。  
公示应当写明公示期限和提出意见的方式。

**Article 537** In hearing a case of commutation of punishment or parole, the following matters shall be publicized within five days after the case is placed on file:  
(1) the basic personal information of the criminal, such as name and age;  
(2) the crime and term of sentence determined by the original judgment;  
(3) the criminal's previous commutations of punishment;  
(4) the executing organ's recommendation for commutation or parole and the basis therefor.  
The publicity notice shall state the publicity period and the manner of submitting opinions.

<a id="art-538"></a>第五百三十八条 审理减刑、假释案件，应当组成合议庭，可以采用书面审理的方式，但下列案件应当开庭审理：  
（一）因罪犯有重大立功表现提请减刑的；  
（二）提请减刑的起始时间、间隔时间或者减刑幅度不符合一般规定的；  
（三）被提请减刑、假释罪犯系职务犯罪罪犯，组织、领导、参加、包庇、纵容黑社会性质组织罪犯，破坏金融管理秩序罪犯或者金融诈骗罪犯的；  
（四）社会影响重大或者社会关注度高的；  
（五）公示期间收到不同意见的；  
（六）人民检察院提出异议的；  
（七）有必要开庭审理的其他案件。

**Article 538** In hearing a case of commutation of punishment or parole, a collegial panel shall be formed, and written examination may be used; however, the following cases shall be heard in court:  
(1) cases where commutation of punishment is proposed because the criminal has performed major meritorious service;  
(2) cases where the starting time, interval, or extent of commutation proposed does not conform to the general provisions;  
(3) cases where the criminal for whom commutation of punishment or parole is proposed is a criminal of duty-related crime, a criminal who organizes, leads, participates in, shields, or connives at an organization of a mafia-type nature, a criminal who disrupts financial management order, or a criminal of financial fraud;  
(4) cases with major social impact or high social concern;  
(5) cases where different opinions are received during the publicity period;  
(6) cases where the People's Procuratorate raises an objection;  
(7) other cases in which it is necessary to hold a hearing in court.

<a id="art-539"></a>第五百三十九条 人民法院作出减刑、假释裁定后，应当在七日以内送达提请减刑、假释的执行机关、同级人民检察院以及罪犯本人。人民检察院认为减刑、假释裁定不当，在法定期限内提出书面纠正意见的，人民法院应当在收到意见后另行组成合议庭审理，并在一个月以内作出裁定。  
对假释的罪犯，适用[本解释第五百一十九条](#art-519)的有关规定，依法实行社区矫正。

**Article 539** After the People's Court makes a ruling on commutation of punishment or parole, it shall serve the ruling on the executing organ that proposed the commutation or parole, the People's Procuratorate at the same level, and the criminal himself or herself within seven days. Where the People's Procuratorate considers the ruling on commutation of punishment or parole improper and submits written corrective opinions within the statutory period, the People's Court shall, after receiving the opinions, form a new collegial panel to hear the case and make a ruling within one month.  
For a criminal released on parole, the relevant provisions of Article 519 of this Interpretation shall apply, and community correction shall be implemented according to law.

<a id="art-540"></a>第五百四十条 减刑、假释裁定作出前，执行机关书面提请撤回减刑、假释建议的，人民法院可以决定是否准许。

**Article 540** Where, before a ruling on commutation of punishment or parole is made, the executing organ submits a written request to withdraw its recommendation for commutation or parole, the People's Court may decide whether to permit it.

<a id="art-541"></a>第五百四十一条 人民法院发现本院已经生效的减刑、假释裁定确有错误的，应当另行组成合议庭审理；发现下级人民法院已经生效的减刑、假释裁定确有错误的，可以指令下级人民法院另行组成合议庭审理，也可以自行组成合议庭审理。

**Article 541** Where the People's Court discovers that its own effective ruling on commutation of punishment or parole is indeed erroneous, it shall form a new collegial panel to hear the case; where it discovers that an effective ruling on commutation of punishment or parole made by a lower people's court is indeed erroneous, it may instruct the lower people's court to form a new collegial panel to hear the case, or may itself form a collegial panel to hear the case.

#### 第六节 缓刑、假释的撤销

<a id="art-542"></a>第五百四十二条 罪犯在缓刑、假释考验期限内犯新罪或者被发现在判决宣告前还有其他罪没有判决，应当撤销缓刑、假释的，由审判新罪的人民法院撤销原判决、裁定宣告的缓刑、假释，并书面通知原审人民法院和执行机关。

**Article 542** Where a criminal commits a new crime during the probation or parole period, or it is discovered that there is another crime for which no judgment has been rendered before the judgment was pronounced, and probation or parole shall be revoked, the People's Court trying the new crime shall revoke the probation or parole pronounced in the original judgment or ruling, and shall notify in writing the original trial court and the executing organ.

<a id="art-543"></a>第五百四十三条 人民法院收到社区矫正机构的撤销缓刑建议书后，经审查，确认罪犯在缓刑考验期限内具有下列情形之一的，应当作出撤销缓刑的裁定：  
（一）违反禁止令，情节严重的；  
（二）无正当理由不按规定时间报到或者接受社区矫正期间脱离监管，超过一个月的；  
（三）因违反监督管理规定受到治安管理处罚，仍不改正的；  
（四）受到执行机关二次警告，仍不改正的；  
（五）违反法律、行政法规和监督管理规定，情节严重的其他情形。  
人民法院收到社区矫正机构的撤销假释建议书后，经审查，确认罪犯在假释考验期限内具有前款第二项、第四项规定情形之一，或者有其他违反监督管理规定的行为，尚未构成新的犯罪的，应当作出撤销假释的裁定。

**Article 543** After receiving the written recommendation for revocation of probation from the community correction institution, the People's Court shall, upon examination, make a ruling to revoke probation if it confirms that the criminal falls under any of the following circumstances during the probation period:  
(1) violating a prohibition order with serious circumstances;  
(2) without justifiable reasons, failing to report within the prescribed time or evading supervision during community correction for more than one month;  
(3) having been subjected to public security administration penalty for violating supervision and administration regulations and still refusing to correct;  
(4) having received two warnings from the executing organ and still refusing to correct;  
(5) other circumstances where laws, administrative regulations, and supervision and administration regulations are violated and the circumstances are serious.  
After receiving the written recommendation for revocation of parole from the community correction institution, the People's Court shall, upon examination, make a ruling to revoke parole if it confirms that the criminal falls under any of the circumstances provided in items 2 and 4 of the preceding paragraph during the parole period, or has other acts violating supervision and administration regulations that do not yet constitute a new crime.

<a id="art-544"></a>第五百四十四条 被提请撤销缓刑、假释的罪犯可能逃跑或者可能发生社会危险，社区矫正机构在提出撤销缓刑、假释建议的同时，提请人民法院决定对其予以逮捕的，人民法院应当在四十八小时以内作出是否逮捕的决定。决定逮捕的，由公安机关执行。逮捕后的羁押期限不得超过三十日。

**Article 544** Where a criminal for whom revocation of probation or parole is proposed may escape or may cause danger to society, and the community correction institution, while making the recommendation for revocation of probation or parole, requests the People's Court to decide on arresting him or her, the People's Court shall make a decision on whether to arrest within forty-eight hours. If arrest is decided, the public security organ shall execute it. The period of custody after arrest shall not exceed thirty days.

<a id="art-545"></a>第五百四十五条 人民法院应当在收到社区矫正机构的撤销缓刑、假释建议书后三十日以内作出裁定。撤销缓刑、假释的裁定一经作出，立即生效。  
人民法院应当将撤销缓刑、假释裁定书送达社区矫正机构和公安机关，并抄送人民检察院，由公安机关将罪犯送交执行。执行以前被逮捕的，羁押一日折抵刑期一日。

**Article 545** The People's Court shall make a ruling within thirty days after receiving the written recommendation for revocation of probation or parole from the community correction institution. The ruling revoking probation or parole shall take effect immediately upon being made.  
The People's Court shall serve the written ruling revoking probation or parole on the community correction institution and the public security organ, and send a copy to the People's Procuratorate; the public security organ shall deliver the criminal for execution. If the criminal was arrested before execution, one day in custody shall be offset against one day of the term of imprisonment.

### 第二十二章 未成年人刑事案件诉讼程序

#### 第一节 一般规定

<a id="art-546"></a>第五百四十六条 人民法院审理未成年人刑事案件，应当贯彻教育、感化、挽救的方针，坚持教育为主、惩罚为辅的原则，加强对未成年人的特殊保护。

**Article 546** In hearing criminal cases involving minors, the People's Court shall implement the policy of education, rehabilitation, and rescue, adhere to the principle of education as the primary approach and punishment as supplementary, and strengthen special protection for minors.

<a id="art-547"></a>第五百四十七条 人民法院应当加强同政府有关部门、人民团体、社会组织等的配合，推动未成年人刑事案件人民陪审、情况调查、安置帮教等工作的开展，充分保障未成年人的合法权益，积极参与社会治安综合治理。

**Article 547** The People's Court shall strengthen cooperation with relevant government departments, people's organizations, social organizations, and others, promote the development of people's assessors, situation investigation, placement, assistance, and education in criminal cases involving minors, fully protect the lawful rights and interests of minors, and actively participate in comprehensive governance of social security.

<a id="art-548"></a>第五百四十八条 人民法院应当加强同政府有关部门、人民团体、社会组织等的配合，对遭受性侵害或者暴力伤害的未成年被害人及其家庭实施必要的心理干预、经济救助、法律援助、转学安置等保护措施。

**Article 548** The People's Court shall strengthen cooperation with relevant government departments, people's organizations, social organizations, and others, and implement necessary protective measures such as psychological intervention, financial assistance, legal aid, and school transfer and placement for minor victims who have suffered sexual assault or violent harm and their families.

<a id="art-549"></a>第五百四十九条 人民法院应当确定专门机构或者指定专门人员，负责审理未成年人刑事案件。审理未成年人刑事案件的人员应当经过专门培训，熟悉未成年人身心特点、善于做未成年人思想教育工作。  
参加审理未成年人刑事案件的人民陪审员，可以从熟悉未成年人身心特点、关心未成年人保护工作的人民陪审员名单中随机抽取确定。

**Article 549** The People's Court shall establish a special organ or designate special personnel to be responsible for hearing criminal cases involving minors. Personnel hearing criminal cases involving minors shall receive special training, be familiar with the physical and psychological characteristics of minors, and be good at conducting ideological education work with minors.  
People's assessors participating in the hearing of criminal cases involving minors may be determined by random selection from the list of people's assessors who are familiar with the physical and psychological characteristics of minors and care about the protection of minors.

<a id="art-550"></a>第五百五十条 被告人实施被指控的犯罪时不满十八周岁、人民法院立案时不满二十周岁的案件，由未成年人案件审判组织审理。  
下列案件可以由未成年人案件审判组织审理：  
（一）人民法院立案时不满二十二周岁的在校学生犯罪案件；  
（二）强奸、猥亵、虐待、遗弃未成年人等侵害未成年人人身权利的犯罪案件；  
（三）由未成年人案件审判组织审理更为适宜的其他案件。  
共同犯罪案件有未成年被告人的或者其他涉及未成年人的刑事案件，是否由未成年人案件审判组织审理，由院长根据实际情况决定。

**Article 550** Cases in which the defendant was under the age of eighteen when committing the alleged crime and under the age of twenty when the People's Court placed the case on file shall be heard by an adjudication organization for cases involving minors.  
The following cases may be heard by an adjudication organization for cases involving minors:  
(1) criminal cases of enrolled students under the age of twenty-two when the People's Court places the case on file;  
(2) criminal cases infringing upon the personal rights of minors, such as rape, indecent assault, abuse, and abandonment of minors;  
(3) other cases more suitable to be heard by an adjudication organization for cases involving minors.  
In a joint crime case involving a minor defendant or in other criminal cases involving minors, whether to be heard by an adjudication organization for cases involving minors shall be decided by the president of the court according to the actual circumstances.

<a id="art-551"></a>第五百五十一条 对分案起诉至同一人民法院的未成年人与成年人共同犯罪案件，可以由同一个审判组织审理；不宜由同一个审判组织审理的，可以分别审理。  
未成年人与成年人共同犯罪案件，由不同人民法院或者不同审判组织分别审理的，有关人民法院或者审判组织应当互相了解共同犯罪被告人的审判情况，注意全案的量刑平衡。

**Article 551** For a joint crime case involving minors and adults that is prosecuted in separate indictments to the same People's Court, the same adjudication organization may hear the case; where it is not appropriate for the same adjudication organization to hear the case, the cases may be heard separately.  
Where a joint crime case involving minors and adults is heard separately by different People's Courts or different adjudication organizations, the relevant People's Courts or adjudication organizations shall keep informed of each other's trial circumstances of the co-defendants in the joint crime and pay attention to the sentencing balance of the whole case.

<a id="art-552"></a>第五百五十二条 对未成年人刑事案件，必要时，上级人民法院可以根据[刑事诉讼法第二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-27)的规定，指定下级人民法院将案件移送其他人民法院审判。

**Article 552** For criminal cases involving minors, when necessary, a higher People's Court may, in accordance with Article 27 of the Criminal Procedure Law, designate a lower people's court to transfer the case to another People's Court for adjudication.

<a id="art-553"></a>第五百五十三条 对未成年被告人应当严格限制适用逮捕措施。  
人民法院决定逮捕，应当讯问未成年被告人，听取辩护律师的意见。  
对被逮捕且没有完成义务教育的未成年被告人，人民法院应当与教育行政部门互相配合，保证其接受义务教育。

**Article 553** Arrest measures shall be strictly restricted in application to minor defendants.  
When the People's Court decides to arrest, it shall interrogate the minor defendant and hear the opinions of the defense lawyer.  
For a minor defendant who has been arrested and has not completed compulsory education, the People's Court shall cooperate with the administrative department of education to ensure that he or she receives compulsory education.

<a id="art-554"></a>第五百五十四条 人民法院对无固定住所、无法提供保证人的未成年被告人适用取保候审的，应当指定合适成年人作为保证人，必要时可以安排取保候审的被告人接受社会观护。

**Article 554** Where the People's Court applies release on bail pending investigation or trial to a minor defendant who has no fixed residence and cannot provide a surety, it shall designate an appropriate adult as the surety, and when necessary, may arrange for the defendant released on bail to receive social guardianship.

<a id="art-555"></a>第五百五十五条 人民法院审理未成年人刑事案件，在讯问和开庭时，应当通知未成年被告人的法定代理人到场。法定代理人无法通知、不能到场或者是共犯的，也可以通知合适成年人到场,并将有关情况记录在案。  
到场的法定代理人或者其他人员，除依法行使[刑事诉讼法第二百八十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-281)第二款规定的权利外，经法庭同意，可以参与对未成年被告人的法庭教育等工作。  
适用简易程序审理未成年人刑事案件，适用前两款规定。

**Article 555** When the People's Court hears a criminal case involving a minor, it shall notify the legal representative of the minor defendant to be present during interrogation and court sessions. Where the legal representative cannot be notified, cannot be present, or is a co-offender, an appropriate adult may be notified to be present, and the relevant circumstances shall be recorded in the case file.  
The legal representative or other persons present may, in addition to exercising the rights provided in Article 281, paragraph 2, of the Criminal Procedure Law according to law, participate in work such as courtroom education of the minor defendant with the consent of the court.  
Where the summary procedure is applied to a criminal case involving a minor, the provisions of the preceding two paragraphs shall apply.

<a id="art-556"></a>第五百五十六条 询问未成年被害人、证人，适用前条规定。  
审理未成年人遭受性侵害或者暴力伤害案件，在询问未成年被害人、证人时，应当采取同步录音录像等措施，尽量一次完成；未成年被害人、证人是女性的，应当由女性工作人员进行。

**Article 556** : The provisions of the preceding article shall apply to the questioning of minor victims and witnesses.  
When trying a case involving a minor victim of sexual assault or violent injury, measures such as synchronous audio and video recording shall be adopted when questioning the minor victim or witness, and the questioning shall, as far as possible, be completed in one session; if the minor victim or witness is female, the questioning shall be conducted by female staff.

<a id="art-557"></a>第五百五十七条 开庭审理时被告人不满十八周岁的案件，一律不公开审理。经未成年被告人及其法定代理人同意，未成年被告人所在学校和未成年人保护组织可以派代表到场。到场代表的人数和范围，由法庭决定。经法庭同意，到场代表可以参与对未成年被告人的法庭教育工作。  
对依法公开审理，但可能需要封存犯罪记录的案件，不得组织人员旁听；有旁听人员的，应当告知其不得传播案件信息。

**Article 557** : Cases in which the defendant is under the age of 18 at the time of court hearing shall in all cases be heard in camera. With the consent of the minor defendant and his or her legal representative, the school attended by the minor defendant and the organization for the protection of minors may send representatives to be present. The number and scope of the representatives present shall be determined by the court. With the permission of the court, the representatives present may participate in the court's educational work directed at the minor defendant.  
For a case that is lawfully heard in public but may require sealing of criminal records, no personnel shall be organized to attend the hearing; if there are attendees, they shall be informed that they shall not disseminate case information.

<a id="art-558"></a>第五百五十八条 开庭审理涉及未成年人的刑事案件，未成年被害人、证人一般不出庭作证；必须出庭的，应当采取保护其隐私的技术手段和心理干预等保护措施。

**Article 558** : In criminal cases involving minors tried in court, minor victims and witnesses shall, as a general rule, not appear in court to testify; where they must appear in court, protective measures such as technical means to protect their privacy and psychological intervention shall be adopted.

<a id="art-559"></a>第五百五十九条 审理涉及未成年人的刑事案件，不得向外界披露未成年人的姓名、住所、照片以及可能推断出未成年人身份的其他资料。  
查阅、摘抄、复制的案卷材料，涉及未成年人的，不得公开和传播。

**Article 559** : In criminal cases involving minors, the name, domicile, photograph, and other materials from which the identity of a minor may be inferred shall not be disclosed to the outside world.  
Case file materials consulted, excerpted, or copied that involve minors shall not be made public or disseminated.

<a id="art-560"></a>第五百六十条 人民法院发现有关单位未尽到未成年人教育、管理、救助、看护等保护职责的，应当向该单位提出司法建议。

**Article 560** : Where the People's Court discovers that a relevant unit has failed to fulfill its protective duties such as education, management, assistance, and care of minors, it shall make a judicial recommendation to that unit.

<a id="art-561"></a>第五百六十一条 人民法院应当结合实际，根据涉及未成年人刑事案件的特点，开展未成年人法治宣传教育工作。

**Article 561** : The People's Court shall, in light of actual conditions and the characteristics of criminal cases involving minors, carry out publicity and education on the rule of law for minors.

<a id="art-562"></a>第五百六十二条 审理未成年人刑事案件，本章没有规定的，适用本解释的有关规定。

**Article 562** : In the trial of criminal cases involving minors, where this Chapter has no provisions, the relevant provisions of this Interpretation shall apply.

#### 第二节 开庭准备

<a id="art-563"></a>第五百六十三条 人民法院向未成年被告人送达起诉书副本时，应当向其讲明被指控的罪行和有关法律规定，并告知其审判程序和诉讼权利、义务。

**Article 563** : When serving a copy of the indictment on a minor defendant, the People's Court shall explain to him or her the charged crime and the relevant legal provisions, and inform him or her of the trial procedure and litigation rights and obligations.

<a id="art-564"></a>第五百六十四条 审判时不满十八周岁的未成年被告人没有委托辩护人的，人民法院应当通知法律援助机构指派熟悉未成年人身心特点的律师为其提供辩护。

**Article 564** : Where a minor defendant who is under the age of 18 at the time of trial has not entrusted a defender, the People's Court shall notify a legal aid institution to appoint a lawyer familiar with the physical and mental characteristics of minors to provide defense for him or her.

<a id="art-565"></a>第五百六十五条 未成年被害人及其法定代理人因经济困难或者其他原因没有委托诉讼代理人的，人民法院应当帮助其申请法律援助。

**Article 565** : Where a minor victim or his or her legal representative has not entrusted a litigation agent due to financial difficulty or other reasons, the People's Court shall assist him or her in applying for legal aid.

<a id="art-566"></a>第五百六十六条 对未成年人刑事案件，人民法院决定适用简易程序审理的，应当征求未成年被告人及其法定代理人、辩护人的意见。上述人员提出异议的，不适用简易程序。

**Article 566** : For a criminal case involving a minor, where the People's Court decides to apply the summary procedure for trial, it shall seek the opinions of the minor defendant, his or her legal representative, and defender. If the above-mentioned persons raise an objection, the summary procedure shall not apply.

<a id="art-567"></a>第五百六十七条 被告人实施被指控的犯罪时不满十八周岁，开庭时已满十八周岁、不满二十周岁的，人民法院开庭时，一般应当通知其近亲属到庭。经法庭同意，近亲属可以发表意见。近亲属无法通知、不能到场或者是共犯的，应当记录在案。

**Article 567** : Where the defendant was under the age of 18 when committing the charged crime, and has reached 18 but is under 20 at the time of court hearing, the People's Court shall, as a general rule, notify his or her close relatives to be present in court. With the permission of the court, the close relatives may present opinions. Where the close relatives cannot be notified, cannot be present, or are co-offenders, this shall be recorded in the case file.

<a id="art-568"></a>第五百六十八条 对人民检察院移送的关于未成年被告人性格特点、家庭情况、社会交往、成长经历、犯罪原因、犯罪前后的表现、监护教育等情况的调查报告，以及辩护人提交的反映未成年被告人上述情况的书面材料，法庭应当接受。  
必要时，人民法院可以委托社区矫正机构、共青团、社会组织等对未成年被告人的上述情况进行调查，或者自行调查。

**Article 568** : The court shall accept the investigation reports transferred by the People's Procuratorate concerning the personality characteristics, family circumstances, social interactions, growth experience, causes of crime, conduct before and after the crime, guardianship and education of the minor defendant, as well as the written materials submitted by the defender reflecting the above circumstances of the minor defendant.  
Where necessary, the People's Court may entrust a community correction institution, the Communist Youth League, social organizations, or other entities to investigate the above circumstances of the minor defendant, or conduct the investigation on its own.

<a id="art-569"></a>第五百六十九条 人民法院根据情况，可以对未成年被告人、被害人、证人进行心理疏导；根据实际需要并经未成年被告人及其法定代理人同意，可以对未成年被告人进行心理测评。  
心理疏导、心理测评可以委托专门机构、专业人员进行。  
心理测评报告可以作为办理案件和教育未成年人的参考。

**Article 569** : The People's Court may, according to the circumstances, provide psychological counseling to the minor defendant, victim, or witness; where actually needed and with the consent of the minor defendant and his or her legal representative, it may conduct a psychological assessment of the minor defendant.  
Psychological counseling and psychological assessment may be entrusted to specialized institutions or professionals.  
The psychological assessment report may serve as a reference for handling the case and educating minors.

<a id="art-570"></a>第五百七十条 开庭前和休庭时，法庭根据情况，可以安排未成年被告人与其法定代理人或者合适成年人会见。

**Article 570** : Before the hearing and during adjournment, the court may, according to the circumstances, arrange for the minor defendant to meet with his or her legal representative or an appropriate adult.

#### 第三节 审判

<a id="art-571"></a>第五百七十一条 人民法院应当在辩护台靠近旁听区一侧为未成年被告人的法定代理人或者合适成年人设置席位。  
审理可能判处五年有期徒刑以下刑罚或者过失犯罪的未成年人刑事案件，可以采取适合未成年人特点的方式设置法庭席位。

**Article 571** : The People's Court shall set up seats for the legal representative or appropriate adult of the minor defendant on the side of the defense podium adjacent to the audience area.  
For criminal cases involving minors where the minor may be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of five years or less or the case is a negligent crime, court seats may be arranged in a manner suitable to the characteristics of minors.

<a id="art-572"></a>第五百七十二条 未成年被告人或者其法定代理人当庭拒绝辩护人辩护的，适用[本解释第三百一十一条](#art-311)第二款、第三款的规定。  
重新开庭后，未成年被告人或者其法定代理人再次当庭拒绝辩护人辩护的，不予准许。重新开庭时被告人已满十八周岁的，可以准许，但不得再另行委托辩护人或者要求另行指派律师，由其自行辩护。

**Article 572** : Where a minor defendant or his or her legal representative refuses defense by the defender in court, the provisions of Article 311, paragraphs 2 and 3 of this Interpretation shall apply.  
Where, after the hearing is reconvened, the minor defendant or his or her legal representative again refuses defense by the defender in court, such refusal shall not be permitted. If the defendant has reached the age of 18 when the hearing is reconvened, it may be permitted, but he or she may not again entrust another defender or request the appointment of another lawyer, and shall defend himself or herself.

<a id="art-573"></a>第五百七十三条 法庭审理过程中，审判人员应当根据未成年被告人的智力发育程度和心理状态，使用适合未成年人的语言表达方式。  
发现有对未成年被告人威胁、训斥、诱供或者讽刺等情形的，审判长应当制止。

**Article 573** : During court proceedings, judicial personnel shall use language suitable for minors based on the minor defendant's level of intellectual development and psychological state.  
Where it is discovered that there are circumstances such as threatening, reprimanding, inducing a confession from, or ridiculing the minor defendant, the presiding judge shall stop such conduct.

<a id="art-574"></a>第五百七十四条 控辩双方提出对未成年被告人判处管制、宣告缓刑等量刑建议的，应当向法庭提供有关未成年被告人能够获得监护、帮教以及对所居住社区无重大不良影响的书面材料。

**Article 574** : Where the prosecution and the defense propose sentencing recommendations such as public surveillance or probation for a minor defendant, they shall provide the court with written materials showing that the minor defendant can obtain guardianship and assistance in education and will not have a major adverse impact on the community in which he or she resides.

<a id="art-575"></a>第五百七十五条 对未成年被告人情况的调查报告，以及辩护人提交的有关未成年被告人情况的书面材料，法庭应当审查并听取控辩双方意见。上述报告和材料可以作为办理案件和教育未成年人的参考。  
人民法院可以通知作出调查报告的人员出庭说明情况，接受控辩双方和法庭的询问。

**Article 575** : The court shall review the investigation reports on the circumstances of the minor defendant and the written materials submitted by the defender concerning the circumstances of the minor defendant, and shall hear the opinions of both the prosecution and the defense. The above reports and materials may serve as a reference for handling the case and educating minors.  
The People's Court may notify the persons who produced the investigation reports to appear in court to explain the circumstances and to accept questioning by the prosecution, the defense, and the court.

<a id="art-576"></a>第五百七十六条 法庭辩论结束后，法庭可以根据未成年人的生理、心理特点和案件情况，对未成年被告人进行法治教育；判决未成年被告人有罪的，宣判后，应当对未成年被告人进行法治教育。  
对未成年被告人进行教育，其法定代理人以外的成年亲属或者教师、辅导员等参与有利于感化、挽救未成年人的，人民法院应当邀请其参加有关活动。  
适用简易程序审理的案件，对未成年被告人进行法庭教育，适用前两款规定。

**Article 576** : After the court debate concludes, the court may, based on the minor's physical and psychological characteristics and the circumstances of the case, conduct rule-of-law education for the minor defendant; if the minor defendant is convicted, the court shall conduct rule-of-law education for the minor defendant after the pronouncement of judgment.  
Where, in educating the minor defendant, the participation of adult relatives other than his or her legal representative, or teachers, counselors, or other persons is conducive to the rehabilitation and redemption of the minor, the People's Court shall invite them to participate in the relevant activities.  
For cases tried under the summary procedure, the provisions of the preceding two paragraphs shall apply to courtroom education for the minor defendant.

<a id="art-577"></a>第五百七十七条 未成年被告人最后陈述后，法庭应当询问其法定代理人是否补充陈述。

**Article 577** : After the minor defendant makes the final statement, the court shall ask his or her legal representative whether he or she wishes to make a supplementary statement.

<a id="art-578"></a>第五百七十八条 对未成年人刑事案件，宣告判决应当公开进行。  
对依法应当封存犯罪记录的案件，宣判时，不得组织人员旁听；有旁听人员的，应当告知其不得传播案件信息。

**Article 578** : For criminal cases involving minors, the pronouncement of judgment shall be conducted in public.  
For cases where criminal records shall be sealed according to law, no personnel shall be organized to attend the pronouncement; if there are attendees, they shall be informed that they shall not disseminate case information.

<a id="art-579"></a>第五百七十九条 定期宣告判决的未成年人刑事案件，未成年被告人的法定代理人无法通知、不能到场或者是共犯的，法庭可以通知合适成年人到庭，并在宣判后向未成年被告人的成年亲属送达判决书。

**Article 579** : For a criminal case involving a minor in which judgment is pronounced on a scheduled date, where the legal representative of the minor defendant cannot be notified, cannot be present, or is a co-offender, the court may notify an appropriate adult to appear in court, and shall serve the judgment on the adult relatives of the minor defendant after the pronouncement.

#### 第四节 执行

<a id="art-580"></a>第五百八十条 将未成年罪犯送监执行刑罚或者送交社区矫正时，人民法院应当将有关未成年罪犯的调查报告及其在案件审理中的表现材料，连同有关法律文书，一并送达执行机关。

**Article 580** : When a minor offender is sent to prison to serve a sentence or placed under community correction, the People's Court shall serve the relevant investigation reports on the minor offender and the materials on his or her conduct during the trial, together with the relevant legal documents, on the executing organ at the same time.

<a id="art-581"></a>第五百八十一条 犯罪时不满十八周岁，被判处五年有期徒刑以下刑罚以及免予刑事处罚的未成年人的犯罪记录，应当封存。  
司法机关或者有关单位向人民法院申请查询封存的犯罪记录的，应当提供查询的理由和依据。对查询申请，人民法院应当及时作出是否同意的决定。

**Article 581** : The criminal records of minors who were under the age of 18 when committing the crime and who were sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of five years or less or exempted from criminal punishment shall be sealed.  
Where a judicial organ or a relevant unit applies to the People's Court for inquiry into sealed criminal records, it shall provide the reasons and basis for the inquiry. With respect to the inquiry application, the People's Court shall promptly decide whether to approve it.

<a id="art-582"></a>第五百八十二条 人民法院可以与未成年犯管教所等服刑场所建立联系，了解未成年罪犯的改造情况，协助做好帮教、改造工作，并可以对正在服刑的未成年罪犯进行回访考察。

**Article 582** : The People's Court may establish contacts with juvenile offender correctional institutions and other places of imprisonment to learn about the rehabilitation of minor offenders, assist in the work of assistance, education, and rehabilitation, and may conduct return visits and inspections of minor offenders currently serving sentences.

<a id="art-583"></a>第五百八十三条 人民法院认为必要时，可以督促被收监服刑的未成年罪犯的父母或者其他监护人及时探视。

**Article 583** : Where the People's Court deems it necessary, it may urge the parents or other guardians of a minor offender serving a sentence in prison to visit him or her in a timely manner.

<a id="art-584"></a>第五百八十四条 对被判处管制、宣告缓刑、裁定假释、决定暂予监外执行的未成年罪犯，人民法院可以协助社区矫正机构制定帮教措施。

**Article 584** : For minor offenders sentenced to public surveillance, granted probation, released on parole, or placed under temporary service of sentence outside prison, the People's Court may assist the community correction institution in formulating measures for assistance and education.

<a id="art-585"></a>第五百八十五条 人民法院可以适时走访被判处管制、宣告缓刑、免予刑事处罚、裁定假释、决定暂予监外执行等的未成年罪犯及其家庭，了解未成年罪犯的管理和教育情况，引导未成年罪犯的家庭承担管教责任，为未成年罪犯改过自新创造良好环境。

**Article 585** : The People's Court may, at appropriate times, visit minor offenders who were sentenced to public surveillance, granted probation, exempted from criminal punishment, released on parole, or placed under temporary service of sentence outside prison, and their families, to learn about the management and education of the minor offenders, guide the families of minor offenders to assume responsibility for discipline and education, and create a favorable environment for the minor offenders to turn over a new leaf.

<a id="art-586"></a>第五百八十六条 被判处管制、宣告缓刑、免予刑事处罚、裁定假释、决定暂予监外执行等的未成年罪犯，具备就学、就业条件的，人民法院可以就其安置问题向有关部门提出建议，并附送必要的材料。

**Article 586** : Where a minor offender who was sentenced to public surveillance, granted probation, exempted from criminal punishment, released on parole, or placed under temporary service of sentence outside prison meets the conditions for schooling or employment, the People's Court may make recommendations to the relevant departments on the arrangement of the minor offender, and attach necessary materials.

### 第二十三章 当事人和解的公诉案件诉讼程序

<a id="art-587"></a>第五百八十七条 对符合[刑事诉讼法第二百八十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-288)规定的公诉案件，事实清楚、证据充分的，人民法院应当告知当事人可以自行和解；当事人提出申请的，人民法院可以主持双方当事人协商以达成和解。  
根据案件情况，人民法院可以邀请人民调解员、辩护人、诉讼代理人、当事人亲友等参与促成双方当事人和解。

**Article 587** : For a public prosecution case that meets the provisions of Article 288 of the Criminal Procedure Law, if the facts are clear and the evidence is sufficient, the People's Court shall inform the parties that they may settle the case by themselves; where a party applies, the People's Court may preside over negotiation between the parties to reach a settlement.  
According to the circumstances of the case, the People's Court may invite people's mediators, defenders, litigation agents, relatives and friends of the parties, and others to participate in facilitating a settlement between the parties.

<a id="art-588"></a>第五百八十八条 符合[刑事诉讼法第二百八十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-288)规定的公诉案件，被害人死亡的，其近亲属可以与被告人和解。近亲属有多人的，达成和解协议，应当经处于最先继承顺序的所有近亲属同意。  
被害人系无行为能力或者限制行为能力人的，其法定代理人、近亲属可以代为和解。

**Article 588** : For a public prosecution case that meets the provisions of Article 288 of the Criminal Procedure Law, where the victim dies, his or her close relatives may reach a settlement with the defendant. Where there are multiple close relatives, reaching a settlement agreement shall require the consent of all close relatives in the first inheritance order.  
Where the victim is a person without capacity for civil conduct or with limited capacity for civil conduct, his or her legal representative or close relatives may reach a settlement on his or her behalf.

<a id="art-589"></a>第五百八十九条 被告人的近亲属经被告人同意，可以代为和解。  
被告人系限制行为能力人的，其法定代理人可以代为和解。  
被告人的法定代理人、近亲属依照前两款规定代为和解的，和解协议约定的赔礼道歉等事项，应当由被告人本人履行。

**Article 589** : The close relatives of the defendant, with the consent of the defendant, may reach a settlement on his or her behalf.  
Where the defendant is a person with limited capacity for civil conduct, his or her legal representative may reach a settlement on his or her behalf.  
Where the legal representative or close relatives of the defendant reach a settlement on the defendant's behalf in accordance with the preceding two paragraphs, matters such as making an apology as agreed in the settlement agreement shall be performed by the defendant personally.

<a id="art-590"></a>第五百九十条 对公安机关、人民检察院主持制作的和解协议书，当事人提出异议的，人民法院应当审查。经审查，和解自愿、合法的，予以确认，无需重新制作和解协议书；和解违反自愿、合法原则的，应当认定无效。和解协议被认定无效后，双方当事人重新达成和解的，人民法院应当主持制作新的和解协议书。

**Article 590** : Where a party raises an objection to a settlement agreement produced under the auspices of a public security organ or the People's Procuratorate, the People's Court shall review it. Upon review, if the settlement is voluntary and lawful, it shall be confirmed, and there is no need to produce a new settlement agreement; if the settlement violates the principles of voluntariness and lawfulness, it shall be determined to be invalid. After the settlement agreement is determined to be invalid, if the parties reach a new settlement, the People's Court shall preside over the production of a new settlement agreement.

<a id="art-591"></a>第五百九十一条 审判期间，双方当事人和解的，人民法院应当听取当事人及其法定代理人等有关人员的意见。双方当事人在庭外达成和解的，人民法院应当通知人民检察院，并听取其意见。经审查，和解自愿、合法的，应当主持制作和解协议书。

**Article 591** : During trial, where the parties reach a settlement, the People's Court shall hear the opinions of the parties and their legal representatives and other relevant persons. Where the parties reach a settlement outside court, the People's Court shall notify the People's Procuratorate and hear its opinions. Upon review, if the settlement is voluntary and lawful, the court shall preside over the production of a settlement agreement.

<a id="art-592"></a>第五百九十二条 和解协议书应当包括以下内容：  
（一）被告人承认自己所犯罪行，对犯罪事实没有异议，并真诚悔罪；  
（二）被告人通过向被害人赔礼道歉、赔偿损失等方式获得被害人谅解；涉及赔偿损失的，应当写明赔偿的数额、方式等；提起附带民事诉讼的，由附带民事诉讼原告人撤回起诉；  
（三）被害人自愿和解，请求或者同意对被告人依法从宽处罚。  
和解协议书应当由双方当事人和审判人员签名，但不加盖人民法院印章。  
和解协议书一式三份，双方当事人各持一份，另一份交人民法院附卷备查。  
对和解协议中的赔偿损失内容，双方当事人要求保密的，人民法院应当准许，并采取相应的保密措施。

**Article 592** : A settlement agreement shall include the following contents:  
(1) The defendant admits the crime committed, has no objection to the criminal facts, and sincerely repents;  
(2) The defendant obtains the victim's forgiveness by apologizing to the victim, compensating for losses, or other means; where compensation for losses is involved, the amount and method of compensation, etc. shall be specified; if an incidental civil action has been initiated, the plaintiff of the incidental civil action shall withdraw the prosecution;  
(3) The victim voluntarily settles and requests or agrees to a lenient punishment of the defendant according to law.  
The settlement agreement shall be signed by both parties and the judicial personnel, but shall not be affixed with the seal of the People's Court.  
The settlement agreement shall be made in triplicate, with each party holding one copy and the other copy delivered to the People's Court to be attached to the case file for future reference.  
Where both parties request confidentiality of the compensation for losses content in the settlement agreement, the People's Court shall permit it and adopt corresponding confidentiality measures.

<a id="art-593"></a>第五百九十三条 和解协议约定的赔偿损失内容，被告人应当在协议签署后即时履行。  
和解协议已经全部履行，当事人反悔的，人民法院不予支持，但有证据证明和解违反自愿、合法原则的除外。

**Article 593** : The defendant shall perform the compensation for losses agreed in the settlement agreement immediately after the agreement is signed.  
Where the settlement agreement has been fully performed and a party repudiates it, the People's Court shall not support the repudiation, unless there is evidence proving that the settlement violates the principles of voluntariness and lawfulness.

<a id="art-594"></a>第五百九十四条 双方当事人在侦查、审查起诉期间已经达成和解协议并全部履行，被害人或者其法定代理人、近亲属又提起附带民事诉讼的，人民法院不予受理，但有证据证明和解违反自愿、合法原则的除外。

**Article 594** : Where the parties have already reached a settlement agreement and fully performed it during investigation and examination for prosecution, and the victim or his or her legal representative or close relatives again file an incidental civil action, the People's Court shall not accept the case, unless there is evidence proving that the settlement violates the principles of voluntariness and lawfulness.

<a id="art-595"></a>第五百九十五条 被害人或者其法定代理人、近亲属提起附带民事诉讼后，双方愿意和解，但被告人不能即时履行全部赔偿义务的，人民法院应当制作附带民事调解书。

**Article 595** : After the victim or his or her legal representative or close relatives file an incidental civil action, where both parties are willing to settle but the defendant cannot immediately perform all compensation obligations, the People's Court shall produce an incidental civil mediation document.

<a id="art-596"></a>第五百九十六条 对达成和解协议的案件，人民法院应当对被告人从轻处罚；符合非监禁刑适用条件的，应当适用非监禁刑；判处法定最低刑仍然过重的，可以减轻处罚；综合全案认为犯罪情节轻微不需要判处刑罚的，可以免予刑事处罚。  
共同犯罪案件，部分被告人与被害人达成和解协议的，可以依法对该部分被告人从宽处罚，但应当注意全案的量刑平衡。

**Article 596** : For a case in which a settlement agreement has been reached, the People's Court shall impose a lighter punishment on the defendant; where the conditions for applying a non-custodial sentence are met, a non-custodial sentence shall be applied; if even the statutory minimum punishment is still too heavy, the punishment may be mitigated; where, upon comprehensive consideration of the whole case, the circumstances of the crime are minor and no punishment is necessary, the defendant may be exempted from criminal punishment.  
In a joint crime case, where some defendants have reached settlement agreements with the victim, those defendants may be given lenient punishment according to law, but attention shall be paid to the balance of sentencing in the whole case.

<a id="art-597"></a>第五百九十七条 达成和解协议的，裁判文书应当叙明，并援引刑事诉讼法的相关条文。

**Article 597** : Where a settlement agreement has been reached, the judgment document shall state this and cite the relevant provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law.

### 第二十四章 缺席审判程序

<a id="art-598"></a>第五百九十八条 对人民检察院依照[刑事诉讼法第二百九十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-291)第一款的规定提起公诉的案件，人民法院应当重点审查以下内容：  
（一）是否属于可以适用缺席审判程序的案件范围；  
（二）是否属于本院管辖；  
（三）是否写明被告人的基本情况，包括明确的境外居住地、联系方式等；  
（四）是否写明被告人涉嫌有关犯罪的主要事实，并附证据材料；  
（五）是否写明被告人有无近亲属以及近亲属的姓名、身份、住址、联系方式等情况；  
（六）是否列明违法所得及其他涉案财产的种类、数量、价值、所在地等，并附证据材料；  
（七）是否附有查封、扣押、冻结违法所得及其他涉案财产的清单和相关法律手续。  
前款规定的材料需要翻译件的，人民法院应当要求人民检察院一并移送。

**Article 598** : For a case prosecuted by the People's Procuratorate in accordance with the provisions of Article 291, paragraph 1 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the People's Court shall focus on reviewing the following contents:  
(1) Whether it falls within the scope of cases to which the trial by default procedure may be applied;  
(2) Whether it is within the jurisdiction of this court;  
(3) Whether the basic information of the defendant is stated, including the definite overseas residence, contact information, etc.;  
(4) Whether the main facts of the crime suspected by the defendant are stated, with evidentiary materials attached;  
(5) Whether it is stated whether the defendant has close relatives and the names, identities, addresses, contact information, etc. of the close relatives;  
(6) Whether the types, quantities, values, locations, etc. of illegal gains and other property involved in the case are listed, with evidentiary materials attached;  
(7) Whether a list and relevant legal formalities for sealing, seizing, and freezing illegal gains and other property involved in the case are attached.  
Where the materials specified in the preceding paragraph require translations, the People's Court shall require the People's Procuratorate to transfer them together.

<a id="art-599"></a>第五百九十九条 对人民检察院依照[刑事诉讼法第二百九十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-291)第一款的规定提起公诉的案件，人民法院审查后，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）符合缺席审判程序适用条件，属于本院管辖，且材料齐全的，应当受理；  
（二）不属于可以适用缺席审判程序的案件范围、不属于本院管辖或者不符合缺席审判程序的其他适用条件的，应当退回人民检察院；  
（三）材料不全的，应当通知人民检察院在三十日以内补送；三十日以内不能补送的，应当退回人民检察院。

**Article 599** : After review of a case prosecuted by the People's Procuratorate in accordance with Article 291, paragraph 1 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the People's Court shall handle it separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If it meets the conditions for application of the trial by default procedure, is within the jurisdiction of this court, and the materials are complete, it shall accept the case;  
(2) If it does not fall within the scope of cases to which the trial by default procedure may be applied, is not within the jurisdiction of this court, or does not meet other conditions for application of the trial by default procedure, it shall return the case to the People's Procuratorate;  
(3) If the materials are incomplete, it shall notify the People's Procuratorate to supplement them within thirty days; if they cannot be supplemented within thirty days, it shall return the case to the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-600"></a>第六百条 对人民检察院依照[刑事诉讼法第二百九十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-291)第一款的规定提起公诉的案件，人民法院立案后，应当将传票和起诉书副本送达被告人，传票应当载明被告人到案期限以及不按要求到案的法律后果等事项；应当将起诉书副本送达被告人近亲属，告知其有权代为委托辩护人，并通知其敦促被告人归案。

**Article 600** : For a case prosecuted by the People's Procuratorate in accordance with Article 291, paragraph 1 of the Criminal Procedure Law, after placing the case on file, the People's Court shall serve the summons and a copy of the indictment on the defendant. The summons shall specify the time limit for the defendant to appear and the legal consequences of failing to appear as required; it shall serve a copy of the indictment on the close relatives of the defendant, inform them that they have the right to entrust a defender on the defendant's behalf, and notify them to urge the defendant to surrender.

<a id="art-601"></a>第六百零一条 人民法院审理人民检察院依照[刑事诉讼法第二百九十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-291)第一款的规定提起公诉的案件，被告人有权委托或者由近亲属代为委托一至二名辩护人。委托律师担任辩护人的，应当委托具有中华人民共和国律师资格并依法取得执业证书的律师；在境外委托的，应当依照[本解释第四百八十六条](#art-486)的规定对授权委托进行公证、认证。  
被告人及其近亲属没有委托辩护人的，人民法院应当通知法律援助机构指派律师为被告人提供辩护。  
被告人及其近亲属拒绝法律援助机构指派的律师辩护的，依照[本解释第五十条](#art-50)第二款的规定处理。

**Article 601** : Where the People's Court tries a case prosecuted by the People's Procuratorate in accordance with Article 291, paragraph 1 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the defendant has the right to entrust one to two defenders, or to have them entrusted by his or her close relatives on his or her behalf. Where a lawyer is entrusted to serve as a defender, a lawyer who possesses the qualification of lawyers of the People's Republic of China and has lawfully obtained a practicing certificate shall be entrusted; where the entrustment is made outside the territory of China, the power of attorney shall be notarized and authenticated in accordance with Article 486 of this Interpretation.  
Where the defendant and his or her close relatives have not entrusted a defender, the People's Court shall notify a legal aid institution to appoint a lawyer to provide defense for the defendant.  
Where the defendant and his or her close relatives refuse the defense by the lawyer appointed by the legal aid institution, the matter shall be handled in accordance with Article 50, paragraph 2 of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-602"></a>第六百零二条 人民法院审理人民检察院依照[刑事诉讼法第二百九十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-291)第一款的规定提起公诉的案件，被告人的近亲属申请参加诉讼的，应当在收到起诉书副本后、第一审开庭前提出，并提供与被告人关系的证明材料。有多名近亲属的，应当推选一至二人参加诉讼。  
对被告人的近亲属提出申请的，人民法院应当及时审查决定。

**Article 602** : Where, in a case tried by the People's Court that is prosecuted by the People's Procuratorate in accordance with Article 291, paragraph 1 of the Criminal Procedure Law, a close relative of the defendant applies to participate in the proceedings, the application shall be made after receipt of the copy of the indictment and before the first-instance court hearing, and evidentiary materials proving the relationship with the defendant shall be provided. Where there are multiple close relatives, one to two persons shall be selected to participate in the proceedings.  
Where a close relative of the defendant makes an application, the People's Court shall promptly review and decide on it.

<a id="art-603"></a>第六百零三条 人民法院审理人民检察院依照[刑事诉讼法第二百九十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-291)第一款的规定提起公诉的案件，参照适用公诉案件第一审普通程序的有关规定。被告人的近亲属参加诉讼的，可以发表意见，出示证据，申请法庭通知证人、鉴定人等出庭，进行辩论。

**Article 603** : Where the People's Court tries a case prosecuted by the People's Procuratorate in accordance with Article 291, paragraph 1 of the Criminal Procedure Law, it shall, by reference, apply the relevant provisions of the ordinary procedure for first instance of public prosecution cases. Where the close relatives of the defendant participate in the proceedings, they may present opinions, produce evidence, apply to the court to notify witnesses, expert witnesses, and others to appear in court, and conduct debate.

<a id="art-604"></a>第六百零四条 对人民检察院依照[刑事诉讼法第二百九十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-291)第一款的规定提起公诉的案件，人民法院审理后应当参照[本解释第二百九十五条](#art-295)的规定作出判决、裁定。  
作出有罪判决的，应当达到证据确实、充分的证明标准。  
经审理认定的罪名不属于[刑事诉讼法第二百九十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-291)第一款规定的罪名的，应当终止审理。  
适用缺席审判程序审理案件，可以对违法所得及其他涉案财产一并作出处理。

**Article 604** : For a case prosecuted by the People's Procuratorate in accordance with Article 291, paragraph 1 of the Criminal Procedure Law, after trial, the People's Court shall render a judgment or ruling by reference to Article 295 of this Interpretation.  
A conviction shall be made only upon meeting the standard of proof that the evidence is reliable and sufficient.  
Where the crime determined upon trial does not fall within the crimes specified in Article 291, paragraph 1 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the trial shall be terminated.  
Where a case is tried under the trial by default procedure, the illegal gains and other property involved in the case may be disposed of together.

<a id="art-605"></a>第六百零五条 因被告人患有严重疾病导致缺乏受审能力，无法出庭受审，中止审理超过六个月，被告人仍无法出庭，被告人及其法定代理人、近亲属申请或者同意恢复审理的，人民法院可以根据[刑事诉讼法第二百九十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-296)的规定缺席审判。  
符合前款规定的情形，被告人无法表达意愿的，其法定代理人、近亲属可以代为申请或者同意恢复审理。

**Article 605** : Where the defendant suffers from a serious illness resulting in lack of capacity to stand trial and is unable to appear in court for trial, and the trial has been suspended for more than six months, and the defendant is still unable to appear in court, and the defendant and his or her legal representative or close relatives apply for or consent to the resumption of the trial, the People's Court may, in accordance with Article 296 of the Criminal Procedure Law, hold a trial by default.  
Where the circumstances specified in the preceding paragraph exist and the defendant is unable to express his or her will, his or her legal representative or close relatives may apply for or consent to the resumption of the trial on his or her behalf.

<a id="art-606"></a>第六百零六条 人民法院受理案件后被告人死亡的，应当裁定终止审理；但有证据证明被告人无罪，经缺席审理确认无罪的，应当判决宣告被告人无罪。  
前款所称“有证据证明被告人无罪，经缺席审理确认无罪”，包括案件事实清楚，证据确实、充分，依据法律认定被告人无罪的情形，以及证据不足，不能认定被告人有罪的情形。

**Article 606** : Where the defendant dies after the People's Court accepts a case, it shall rule to terminate the trial; however, where there is evidence proving that the defendant is innocent, and after trial by default it is confirmed that the defendant is innocent, the court shall render a judgment declaring the defendant not guilty.
