The phrase "there is evidence proving that the defendant is innocent, and after trial by default it is confirmed that the defendant is innocent" as mentioned in the preceding paragraph includes the circumstances where the facts of the case are clear, the evidence is reliable and sufficient, and the defendant is determined to be innocent according to law, as well as the circumstances where the evidence is insufficient and the defendant cannot be found guilty.

<a id="art-607"></a>第六百零七条 人民法院按照审判监督程序重新审判的案件，被告人死亡的，可以缺席审理。有证据证明被告人无罪，经缺席审理确认被告人无罪的，应当判决宣告被告人无罪；虽然构成犯罪，但原判量刑畸重的，应当依法作出判决。

**Article 607** : For a case retried by the People's Court under the trial supervision procedure, where the defendant dies, trial by default may be conducted. Where there is evidence proving that the defendant is innocent, and after trial by default it is confirmed that the defendant is innocent, a judgment shall be rendered declaring the defendant not guilty; where the conduct constitutes a crime, but the original sentence was obviously excessive, a judgment shall be rendered according to law.

<a id="art-608"></a>第六百零八条 人民法院缺席审理案件，本章没有规定的，参照适用本解释的有关规定。

**Article 608** : Where the People's Court conducts a trial by default, and this Chapter has no provisions, the relevant provisions of this Interpretation shall apply by reference.

### 第二十五章 犯罪嫌疑人、被告人逃匿、死亡案件违法所得的没收程序

<a id="art-609"></a>第六百零九条 [刑事诉讼法第二百九十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-298)规定的“贪污贿赂犯罪、恐怖活动犯罪等”犯罪案件，是指下列案件：  
（一）贪污贿赂、失职渎职等职务犯罪案件；  
（二）刑法分则第二章规定的相关恐怖活动犯罪案件，以及恐怖活动组织、恐怖活动人员实施的杀人、爆炸、绑架等犯罪案件；  
（三）危害国家安全、走私、洗钱、金融诈骗、黑社会性质组织、毒品犯罪案件；  
（四）电信诈骗、网络诈骗犯罪案件。

**Article 609** . The criminal cases of "crimes of embezzlement and bribery, terrorist activities, and the like" as prescribed in Article 298 of the Criminal Procedure Law refer to the following cases:  
(1) cases of duty-related crimes such as embezzlement, bribery, dereliction of duty, and malfeasance;  
(2) cases of crimes related to terrorist activities as prescribed in Chapter II of the Specific Provisions of the Criminal Law, as well as criminal cases such as homicide, explosion, and kidnapping committed by terrorist organizations or terrorist personnel;  
(3) cases of crimes endangering national security, smuggling, money laundering, financial fraud, crimes committed by mafia-type organizations, and drug-related crimes;  
(4) cases of telecom fraud and internet fraud.

<a id="art-610"></a>第六百一十条 在省、自治区、直辖市或者全国范围内具有较大影响的犯罪案件，或者犯罪嫌疑人、被告人逃匿境外的犯罪案件，应当认定为[刑事诉讼法第二百九十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-298)第一款规定的“重大犯罪案件”。

**Article 610** . Criminal cases that have a relatively significant impact within a province, autonomous region, municipality directly under the Central Government, or nationwide, or criminal cases in which the criminal suspect or defendant flees abroad, shall be determined as "major criminal cases" as prescribed in paragraph 1 of Article 298 of the Criminal Procedure Law.

<a id="art-611"></a>第六百一十一条 犯罪嫌疑人、被告人死亡，依照刑法规定应当追缴其违法所得及其他涉案财产，人民检察院提出没收违法所得申请的，人民法院应当依法受理。

**Article 611** . Where a criminal suspect or defendant dies and, according to the provisions of the Criminal Law, his or her illegal gains and other property involved in the case shall be recovered, and the People's Procuratorate files an application for confiscation of illegal gains, the People's Court shall accept the application according to law.

<a id="art-612"></a>第六百一十二条 对人民检察院提出的没收违法所得申请，人民法院应当审查以下内容：  
（一）是否属于可以适用违法所得没收程序的案件范围；  
（二）是否属于本院管辖；  
（三）是否写明犯罪嫌疑人、被告人基本情况，以及涉嫌有关犯罪的情况，并附证据材料；  
（四）是否写明犯罪嫌疑人、被告人逃匿、被通缉、脱逃、下落不明、死亡等情况，并附证据材料；  
（五）是否列明违法所得及其他涉案财产的种类、数量、价值、所在地等，并附证据材料；  
（六）是否附有查封、扣押、冻结违法所得及其他涉案财产的清单和法律手续；  
（七）是否写明犯罪嫌疑人、被告人有无利害关系人，利害关系人的姓名、身份、住址、联系方式及其要求等情况；  
（八）是否写明申请没收的理由和法律依据；  
（九）其他依法需要审查的内容和材料。  
前款规定的材料需要翻译件的，人民法院应当要求人民检察院一并移送。

**Article 612** . With respect to an application for confiscation of illegal gains filed by the People's Procuratorate, the People's Court shall examine the following contents:  
(1) whether the case falls within the scope of cases to which the procedure for confiscation of illegal gains is applicable;  
(2) whether it is within the jurisdiction of this court;  
(3) whether the basic information of the criminal suspect or defendant and the circumstances of the alleged crimes are stated, and evidence materials are attached;  
(4) whether the circumstances such as the criminal suspect's or defendant's fleeing, being wanted, escaping, disappearance, or death are stated, and evidence materials are attached;  
(5) whether the types, quantities, values, locations, and the like of the illegal gains and other property involved in the case are listed, and evidence materials are attached;  
(6) whether a list and legal formalities for sealing, seizing, and freezing the illegal gains and other property involved in the case are attached;  
(7) whether it is stated whether the criminal suspect or defendant has interested parties, and the names, identities, addresses, contact information, and claims of the interested parties, and the like;  
(8) whether the reasons and legal basis for the application for confiscation are stated;  
(9) other contents and materials that need to be examined according to law.  
Where the materials specified in the preceding paragraph require translation, the People's Court shall require the People's Procuratorate to submit the translations together.

<a id="art-613"></a>第六百一十三条 对没收违法所得的申请，人民法院应当在三十日以内审查完毕，并按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）属于没收违法所得申请受案范围和本院管辖，且材料齐全、有证据证明有犯罪事实的，应当受理；  
（二）不属于没收违法所得申请受案范围或者本院管辖的，应当退回人民检察院；  
（三）没收违法所得申请不符合“有证据证明有犯罪事实”标准要求的，应当通知人民检察院撤回申请；  
（四）材料不全的，应当通知人民检察院在七日以内补送；七日以内不能补送的，应当退回人民检察院。  
人民检察院尚未查封、扣押、冻结申请没收的财产或者查封、扣押、冻结期限即将届满，涉案财产有被隐匿、转移或者毁损、灭失危险的，人民法院可以查封、扣押、冻结申请没收的财产。

**Article 613** . The People's Court shall complete the examination of an application for confiscation of illegal gains within thirty days and handle it separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) where the application falls within the scope of acceptance of applications for confiscation of illegal gains and the jurisdiction of this court, and the materials are complete and there is evidence proving the existence of criminal facts, the application shall be accepted;  
(2) where the application does not fall within the scope of acceptance of applications for confiscation of illegal gains or the jurisdiction of this court, the application shall be returned to the People's Procuratorate;  
(3) where the application for confiscation of illegal gains does not meet the standard requirement of "there is evidence proving the existence of criminal facts," the People's Procuratorate shall be notified to withdraw the application;  
(4) where the materials are incomplete, the People's Procuratorate shall be notified to submit supplementary materials within seven days; if the materials cannot be supplemented within seven days, the application shall be returned to the People's Procuratorate.  
Where the People's Procuratorate has not yet sealed, seized, or frozen the property for which confiscation is applied, or the duration of sealing, seizure, or freezing is about to expire, and there is a risk that the property involved in the case may be concealed, transferred, damaged, or destroyed, the People's Court may seal, seize, or freeze the property for which confiscation is applied.

<a id="art-614"></a>第六百一十四条 人民法院受理没收违法所得的申请后，应当在十五日以内发布公告。公告应当载明以下内容：  
（一）案由、案件来源；  
（二）犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的基本情况；  
（三）犯罪嫌疑人、被告人涉嫌犯罪的事实；  
（四）犯罪嫌疑人、被告人逃匿、被通缉、脱逃、下落不明、死亡等情况；  
（五）申请没收的财产的种类、数量、价值、所在地等以及已查封、扣押、冻结财产的清单和法律手续；  
（六）申请没收的财产属于违法所得及其他涉案财产的相关事实；  
（七）申请没收的理由和法律依据；  
（八）利害关系人申请参加诉讼的期限、方式以及未按照该期限、方式申请参加诉讼可能承担的不利法律后果；  
（九）其他应当公告的情况。  
公告期为六个月，公告期间不适用中止、中断、延长的规定。

**Article 614** . After accepting an application for confiscation of illegal gains, the People's Court shall publish an announcement within fifteen days. The announcement shall specify the following contents:  
(1) the cause of action and the source of the case;  
(2) the basic information of the criminal suspect or defendant;  
(3) the facts of the crime of which the criminal suspect or defendant is suspected;  
(4) the circumstances such as the criminal suspect's or defendant's fleeing, being wanted, escaping, disappearance, or death;  
(5) the types, quantities, values, locations, and the like of the property for which confiscation is applied, as well as the list and legal formalities for the property already sealed, seized, or frozen;  
(6) the relevant facts that the property for which confiscation is applied constitutes illegal gains and other property involved in the case;  
(7) the reasons and legal basis for the application for confiscation;  
(8) the time limit and method for interested parties to apply to participate in the proceedings, and the adverse legal consequences that may be borne if they fail to apply for participation within such time limit or in such manner;  
(9) other circumstances that shall be announced.  
The announcement period is six months, and the provisions on suspension, interruption, and extension shall not apply during the announcement period.

<a id="art-615"></a>第六百一十五条 公告应当在全国公开发行的报纸、信息网络媒体、最高人民法院的官方网站发布，并在人民法院公告栏发布。必要时，公告可以在犯罪地、犯罪嫌疑人、被告人居住地或者被申请没收财产所在地发布。最后发布的公告的日期为公告日期。发布公告的，应当采取拍照、录像等方式记录发布过程。  
人民法院已经掌握境内利害关系人联系方式的，应当直接送达含有公告内容的通知；直接送达有困难的，可以委托代为送达、邮寄送达。经受送达人同意的，可以采用传真、电子邮件等能够确认其收悉的方式告知公告内容，并记录在案。  
人民法院已经掌握境外犯罪嫌疑人、被告人、利害关系人联系方式，经受送达人同意的，可以采用传真、电子邮件等能够确认其收悉的方式告知公告内容，并记录在案；受送达人未表示同意，或者人民法院未掌握境外犯罪嫌疑人、被告人、利害关系人联系方式，其所在国、地区的主管机关明确提出应当向受送达人送达含有公告内容的通知的，人民法院可以决定是否送达。决定送达的，应当依照[本解释第四百九十三条](#art-493)的规定请求所在国、地区提供司法协助。

**Article 615** . The announcement shall be published in newspapers publicly distributed nationwide, on information network media, and on the official website of the Supreme People's Court, and shall be posted in the announcement column of the People's Court. When necessary, the announcement may be published at the place where the crime was committed, the place of residence of the criminal suspect or defendant, or the place where the property for which confiscation is applied is located. The date of the last publication of the announcement is the date of the announcement. Where an announcement is published, the publication process shall be recorded by means of photographing, videotaping, or the like.  
Where the People's Court has already obtained the contact information of interested parties within the territory, it shall directly serve a notice containing the contents of the announcement; where direct service is difficult, service may be entrusted to another person or service may be made by mail. With the consent of the person to be served, the contents of the announcement may be notified by fax, e-mail, or other means capable of confirming receipt, and a record shall be made in the case file.  
Where the People's Court has already obtained the contact information of criminal suspects, defendants, or interested parties outside the territory, with the consent of the person to be served, the contents of the announcement may be notified by fax, e-mail, or other means capable of confirming receipt, and a record shall be made in the case file; where the person to be served does not indicate consent, or the People's Court has not obtained the contact information of the criminal suspect, defendant, or interested party outside the territory, and the competent authority of the country or region in which such person is located explicitly proposes that a notice containing the contents of the announcement shall be served on the person to be served, the People's Court may decide whether to serve it. Where it decides to serve, it shall request judicial assistance from the country or region in which such person is located in accordance with Article 493 of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-616"></a>第六百一十六条 [刑事诉讼法第二百九十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-299)第二款、第三百条第二款规定的“其他利害关系人”，是指除犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的近亲属以外的，对申请没收的财产主张权利的自然人和单位。

**Article 616** . The "other interested parties" as prescribed in paragraph 2 of Article 299 and paragraph 2 of Article 300 of the Criminal Procedure Law refer to natural persons and units, other than the close relatives of the criminal suspect or defendant, that claim rights over the property for which confiscation is applied.

<a id="art-617"></a>第六百一十七条 犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的近亲属和其他利害关系人申请参加诉讼的，应当在公告期间内提出。犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的近亲属应当提供其与犯罪嫌疑人、被告人关系的证明材料，其他利害关系人应当提供证明其对违法所得及其他涉案财产主张权利的证据材料。  
利害关系人可以委托诉讼代理人参加诉讼。委托律师担任诉讼代理人的，应当委托具有中华人民共和国律师资格并依法取得执业证书的律师；在境外委托的，应当依照[本解释第四百八十六条](#art-486)的规定对授权委托进行公证、认证。  
利害关系人在公告期满后申请参加诉讼，能够合理说明理由的，人民法院应当准许。

**Article 617** . Where close relatives of the criminal suspect or defendant and other interested parties apply to participate in the proceedings, they shall file the application within the announcement period. The close relatives of the criminal suspect or defendant shall provide evidentiary materials proving their relationship with the criminal suspect or defendant, and other interested parties shall provide evidentiary materials proving their claims to rights over the illegal gains and other property involved in the case.  
Interested parties may entrust litigation agents to participate in the proceedings. Where a lawyer is entrusted as a litigation agent, a lawyer who possesses the qualification of a lawyer of the People's Republic of China and has lawfully obtained a practice certificate shall be entrusted; where the entrustment is made outside the territory, the power of attorney shall be notarized and authenticated in accordance with Article 486 of this Interpretation.  
Where an interested party applies to participate in the proceedings after the expiration of the announcement period and can reasonably explain the reasons, the People's Court shall permit it.

<a id="art-618"></a>第六百一十八条 犯罪嫌疑人、被告人逃匿境外，委托诉讼代理人申请参加诉讼，且违法所得或者其他涉案财产所在国、地区主管机关明确提出意见予以支持的，人民法院可以准许。  
人民法院准许参加诉讼的，犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的诉讼代理人依照本解释关于利害关系人的诉讼代理人的规定行使诉讼权利。

**Article 618** . Where a criminal suspect or defendant flees abroad, entrusts a litigation agent to apply to participate in the proceedings, and the competent authority of the country or region where the illegal gains or other property involved in the case is located explicitly expresses support, the People's Court may permit the participation.  
Where the People's Court permits participation in the proceedings, the litigation agent of the criminal suspect or defendant shall exercise litigation rights in accordance with the provisions of this Interpretation concerning litigation agents of interested parties.

<a id="art-619"></a>第六百一十九条 公告期满后，人民法院应当组成合议庭对申请没收违法所得的案件进行审理。  
利害关系人申请参加或者委托诉讼代理人参加诉讼的，应当开庭审理。没有利害关系人申请参加诉讼的，或者利害关系人及其诉讼代理人无正当理由拒不到庭的，可以不开庭审理。  
人民法院确定开庭日期后，应当将开庭的时间、地点通知人民检察院、利害关系人及其诉讼代理人、证人、鉴定人、翻译人员。通知书应当依照[本解释第六百一十五条](#art-615)第二款、第三款规定的方式，至迟在开庭审理三日以前送达；受送达人在境外的，至迟在开庭审理三十日以前送达。

**Article 619** . After the expiration of the announcement period, the People's Court shall form a collegiate panel to hear the case involving the application for confiscation of illegal gains.  
Where an interested party applies to participate in the proceedings or entrusts a litigation agent to participate in the proceedings, the case shall be heard in court. Where no interested party applies to participate in the proceedings, or the interested party and his or her litigation agent refuse to appear in court without justifiable reasons, the case may be tried without a court session.  
After the People's Court determines the date of the court session, it shall notify the People's Procuratorate, the interested parties and their litigation agents, witnesses, expert witnesses, and interpreters of the time and place of the court session. The notice shall be served in accordance with the methods specified in paragraphs 2 and 3 of Article 615 of this Interpretation, no later than three days before the court session; where the person to be served is outside the territory, the notice shall be served no later than thirty days before the court session.

<a id="art-620"></a>第六百二十条 开庭审理申请没收违法所得的案件，按照下列程序进行：  
（一）审判长宣布法庭调查开始后，先由检察员宣读申请书，后由利害关系人、诉讼代理人发表意见；  
（二）法庭应当依次就犯罪嫌疑人、被告人是否实施了贪污贿赂犯罪、恐怖活动犯罪等重大犯罪并已经通缉一年不能到案，或者是否已经死亡，以及申请没收的财产是否依法应当追缴进行调查；调查时，先由检察员出示证据，后由利害关系人、诉讼代理人出示证据，并进行质证；  
（三）法庭辩论阶段，先由检察员发言，后由利害关系人、诉讼代理人发言，并进行辩论。  
利害关系人接到通知后无正当理由拒不到庭，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭的，可以转为不开庭审理，但还有其他利害关系人参加诉讼的除外。

**Article 620** . A case involving an application for confiscation of illegal gains heard in court shall proceed according to the following procedures:  
(1) after the chief judge announces the commencement of the court investigation, the prosecutor shall first read the application, and then the interested parties and litigation agents shall present their opinions;  
(2) the court shall, in turn, investigate whether the criminal suspect or defendant committed major crimes such as embezzlement, bribery, or terrorist activities and had been wanted for one year without appearing, or whether he or she has died, and whether the property for which confiscation is applied shall be recovered according to law; during the investigation, the prosecutor shall first present evidence, and then the interested parties and litigation agents shall present evidence and conduct cross-examination;  
(3) during the court debate stage, the prosecutor shall first speak, and then the interested parties and litigation agents shall speak and debate.  
Where an interested party, after receiving the notice, refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons, or withdraws from the court session midway without the permission of the court, the case may be converted to a trial without a court session, except where other interested parties participate in the proceedings.

<a id="art-621"></a>第六百二十一条 对申请没收违法所得的案件，人民法院审理后，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）申请没收的财产属于违法所得及其他涉案财产的，除依法返还被害人的以外，应当裁定没收；  
（二）不符合[刑事诉讼法第二百九十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-298)第一款规定的条件的，应当裁定驳回申请，解除查封、扣押、冻结措施。  
申请没收的财产具有高度可能属于违法所得及其他涉案财产的，应当认定为前款规定的“申请没收的财产属于违法所得及其他涉案财产”。巨额财产来源不明犯罪案件中，没有利害关系人对违法所得及其他涉案财产主张权利，或者利害关系人对违法所得及其他涉案财产虽然主张权利但提供的证据没有达到相应证明标准的，应当视为“申请没收的财产属于违法所得及其他涉案财产”。

**Article 621** . After hearing a case involving an application for confiscation of illegal gains, the People's Court shall handle it separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) where the property for which confiscation is applied constitutes illegal gains and other property involved in the case, a ruling of confiscation shall be made, except where the property shall be returned to the victim according to law;  
(2) where the conditions prescribed in paragraph 1 of Article 298 of the Criminal Procedure Law are not met, a ruling shall be made to reject the application and lift the measures of sealing, seizure, and freezing.  
Where the property for which confiscation is applied is highly likely to constitute illegal gains and other property involved in the case, it shall be determined as "the property for which confiscation is applied constitutes illegal gains and other property involved in the case" as prescribed in the preceding paragraph. In a criminal case involving property of huge amounts with unidentified sources, if no interested party claims rights over the illegal gains and other property involved in the case, or although an interested party claims rights over the illegal gains and other property involved in the case, the evidence provided does not meet the corresponding standard of proof, the property shall be deemed as "the property for which confiscation is applied constitutes illegal gains and other property involved in the case."

<a id="art-622"></a>第六百二十二条 对没收违法所得或者驳回申请的裁定，犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的近亲属和其他利害关系人或者人民检察院可以在五日以内提出上诉、抗诉。

**Article 622** . With respect to a ruling to confiscate illegal gains or to reject the application, close relatives of the criminal suspect or defendant, other interested parties, or the People's Procuratorate may file an appeal or protest within five days.

<a id="art-623"></a>第六百二十三条 对不服第一审没收违法所得或者驳回申请裁定的上诉、抗诉案件，第二审人民法院经审理，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）第一审裁定认定事实清楚和适用法律正确的，应当驳回上诉或者抗诉，维持原裁定；  
（二）第一审裁定认定事实清楚，但适用法律有错误的，应当改变原裁定；  
（三）第一审裁定认定事实不清的，可以在查清事实后改变原裁定，也可以撤销原裁定，发回原审人民法院重新审判；  
（四）第一审裁定违反法定诉讼程序，可能影响公正审判的，应当撤销原裁定，发回原审人民法院重新审判。  
第一审人民法院对发回重新审判的案件作出裁定后，第二审人民法院对不服第一审人民法院裁定的上诉、抗诉，应当依法作出裁定，不得再发回原审人民法院重新审判；但是，第一审人民法院在重新审判过程中违反法定诉讼程序，可能影响公正审判的除外。

**Article 623** . For appeal or protest cases against a first-instance ruling to confiscate illegal gains or to reject the application, the second-instance People's Court shall, after trial, handle them separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) where the facts found in the first-instance ruling are clear and the law applied is correct, the appeal or protest shall be dismissed and the original ruling affirmed;  
(2) where the facts found in the first-instance ruling are clear but the law applied is erroneous, the original ruling shall be modified;  
(3) where the facts found in the first-instance ruling are unclear, the original ruling may be modified after the facts are ascertained, or the original ruling may be revoked and the case remanded to the original trial court for retrial;  
(4) where the first-instance ruling violates the statutory litigation procedures and may affect an impartial trial, the original ruling shall be revoked and the case remanded to the original trial court for retrial.  
After the first-instance People's Court renders a ruling on a remanded case, the second-instance People's Court shall, for an appeal or protest against the ruling of the first-instance People's Court, render a ruling according to law and shall not remand the case to the original trial court for retrial again, except where the first-instance People's Court violates the statutory litigation procedures during the retrial and may affect an impartial trial.

<a id="art-624"></a>第六百二十四条 利害关系人非因故意或者重大过失在第一审期间未参加诉讼，在第二审期间申请参加诉讼的，人民法院应当准许，并撤销原裁定，发回原审人民法院重新审判。

**Article 624** . Where an interested party fails to participate in the proceedings during the first instance not due to intent or gross negligence, and applies to participate in the proceedings during the second instance, the People's Court shall permit it, revoke the original ruling, and remand the case to the original trial court for retrial.

<a id="art-625"></a>第六百二十五条 在审理申请没收违法所得的案件过程中，在逃的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人到案的，人民法院应当裁定终止审理。人民检察院向原受理申请的人民法院提起公诉的，可以由同一审判组织审理。

**Article 625** . Where, during the trial of a case involving an application for confiscation of illegal gains, a fleeing criminal suspect or defendant appears before the court, the People's Court shall rule to terminate the trial. Where the People's Procuratorate initiates a public prosecution with the People's Court that originally accepted the application, the case may be tried by the same adjudication panel.

<a id="art-626"></a>第六百二十六条 在审理案件过程中，被告人脱逃或者死亡，符合[刑事诉讼法第二百九十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-298)第一款规定的，人民检察院可以向人民法院提出没收违法所得的申请；符合[刑事诉讼法第二百九十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-291)第一款规定的，人民检察院可以按照缺席审判程序向人民法院提起公诉。  
人民检察院向原受理案件的人民法院提出没收违法所得申请的，可以由同一审判组织审理。

**Article 626** . Where, during the trial of a case, the defendant escapes or dies, and the conditions prescribed in paragraph 1 of Article 298 of the Criminal Procedure Law are met, the People's Procuratorate may file an application with the People's Court for confiscation of illegal gains; where the conditions prescribed in paragraph 1 of Article 291 of the Criminal Procedure Law are met, the People's Procuratorate may initiate a public prosecution with the People's Court in accordance with the procedure for trial in absentia.  
Where the People's Procuratorate files an application for confiscation of illegal gains with the People's Court that originally accepted the case, the case may be tried by the same adjudication panel.

<a id="art-627"></a>第六百二十七条 审理申请没收违法所得案件的期限，参照公诉案件第一审普通程序和第二审程序的审理期限执行。  
公告期间和请求刑事司法协助的时间不计入审理期限。

**Article 627** . The time limit for hearing a case involving an application for confiscation of illegal gains shall be implemented by reference to the time limits for the first-instance ordinary procedure and the second-instance procedure of public prosecution cases.  
The announcement period and the time for requesting criminal judicial assistance shall not be included in the trial time limit.

<a id="art-628"></a>第六百二十八条 没收违法所得裁定生效后，犯罪嫌疑人、被告人到案并对没收裁定提出异议，人民检察院向原作出裁定的人民法院提起公诉的，可以由同一审判组织审理。  
人民法院经审理，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）原裁定正确的，予以维持，不再对涉案财产作出判决；  
（二）原裁定确有错误的，应当撤销原裁定，并在判决中对有关涉案财产一并作出处理。  
人民法院生效的没收裁定确有错误的，除第一款规定的情形外，应当依照审判监督程序予以纠正。

**Article 628** . After a ruling to confiscate illegal gains takes effect, where the criminal suspect or defendant appears before the court and raises an objection to the confiscation ruling, and the People's Procuratorate initiates a public prosecution with the People's Court that originally rendered the ruling, the case may be tried by the same adjudication panel.  
After trial, the People's Court shall handle the case separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) where the original ruling is correct, the original ruling shall be affirmed and no judgment shall be made on the property involved in the case;  
(2) where the original ruling is indeed erroneous, the original ruling shall be revoked, and the relevant property involved in the case shall be disposed of together in the judgment.  
Where an effective confiscation ruling of the People's Court is indeed erroneous, except for the circumstances specified in paragraph 1, it shall be corrected in accordance with the trial supervision procedure.

<a id="art-629"></a>第六百二十九条 人民法院审理申请没收违法所得的案件，本章没有规定的，参照适用本解释的有关规定。

**Article 629** . Where, in the trial of a case involving an application for confiscation of illegal gains, the provisions of this Chapter have no provisions on a matter, the relevant provisions of this Interpretation shall be applied by reference.

### 第二十六章 依法不负刑事责任的精神病人的强制医疗程序

<a id="art-630"></a>第六百三十条 实施暴力行为，危害公共安全或者严重危害公民人身安全，社会危害性已经达到犯罪程度，但经法定程序鉴定依法不负刑事责任的精神病人，有继续危害社会可能的，可以予以强制医疗。

**Article 630** . Where a mental patient who commits violent conduct, endangers public safety or seriously endangers the personal safety of citizens, and whose social harmfulness has reached the degree of a crime, but who, upon identification through statutory procedures, is not criminally liable according to law, and who may continue to endanger society, compulsory medical treatment may be applied.

<a id="art-631"></a>第六百三十一条 人民检察院申请对依法不负刑事责任的精神病人强制医疗的案件，由被申请人实施暴力行为所在地的基层人民法院管辖；由被申请人居住地的人民法院审判更为适宜的，可以由被申请人居住地的基层人民法院管辖。

**Article 631** . A case in which the People's Procuratorate applies for compulsory medical treatment for a mental patient who is not criminally liable according to law shall be under the jurisdiction of the Primary People's Court at the place where the respondent committed the violent conduct; where it is more appropriate for the People's Court at the place of the respondent's residence to adjudicate the case, the Primary People's Court at the place of the respondent's residence may have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-632"></a>第六百三十二条 对人民检察院提出的强制医疗申请，人民法院应当审查以下内容：  
（一）是否属于本院管辖；  
（二）是否写明被申请人的身份，实施暴力行为的时间、地点、手段、所造成的损害等情况，并附证据材料；  
（三）是否附有法医精神病鉴定意见和其他证明被申请人属于依法不负刑事责任的精神病人的证据材料；  
（四）是否列明被申请人的法定代理人的姓名、住址、联系方式；  
（五）需要审查的其他事项。

**Article 632** . With respect to an application for compulsory medical treatment filed by the People's Procuratorate, the People's Court shall examine the following contents:  
(1) whether it is within the jurisdiction of this court;  
(2) whether the identity of the respondent, the time, place, means, and resulting harm of the violent conduct, and other circumstances are stated, and evidence materials are attached;  
(3) whether an expert opinion on forensic psychiatric evaluation and other evidence materials proving that the respondent is a mental patient who is not criminally liable according to law are attached;  
(4) whether the name, address, and contact information of the respondent's legal representative are listed;  
(5) other matters that need to be examined.

<a id="art-633"></a>第六百三十三条 对人民检察院提出的强制医疗申请，人民法院应当在七日以内审查完毕，并按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）属于强制医疗程序受案范围和本院管辖，且材料齐全的，应当受理；  
（二）不属于本院管辖的，应当退回人民检察院；  
（三）材料不全的，应当通知人民检察院在三日以内补送；三日以内不能补送的，应当退回人民检察院。

**Article 633** . The People's Court shall complete the examination of an application for compulsory medical treatment filed by the People's Procuratorate within seven days and handle it separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) where the application falls within the scope of acceptance of the compulsory medical treatment procedure and the jurisdiction of this court, and the materials are complete, the application shall be accepted;  
(2) where the application is not within the jurisdiction of this court, it shall be returned to the People's Procuratorate;  
(3) where the materials are incomplete, the People's Procuratorate shall be notified to submit supplementary materials within three days; if the materials cannot be supplemented within three days, the application shall be returned to the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-634"></a>第六百三十四条 审理强制医疗案件，应当通知被申请人或者被告人的法定代理人到场；被申请人或者被告人的法定代理人经通知未到场的，可以通知被申请人或者被告人的其他近亲属到场。  
被申请人或者被告人没有委托诉讼代理人的，应当自受理强制医疗申请或者发现被告人符合强制医疗条件之日起三日以内，通知法律援助机构指派律师担任其诉讼代理人，为其提供法律帮助。

**Article 634** . When trying a compulsory medical treatment case, the legal representative of the respondent or defendant shall be notified to be present; where the legal representative of the respondent or defendant fails to appear after being notified, other close relatives of the respondent or defendant may be notified to be present.  
Where the respondent or defendant has not entrusted a litigation agent, the legal aid institution shall be notified within three days from the date of accepting the compulsory medical treatment application or discovering that the defendant meets the conditions for compulsory medical treatment, to assign a lawyer to serve as his or her litigation agent and provide him or her with legal assistance.

<a id="art-635"></a>第六百三十五条 审理强制医疗案件，应当组成合议庭，开庭审理。但是，被申请人、被告人的法定代理人请求不开庭审理，并经人民法院审查同意的除外。  
审理强制医疗案件，应当会见被申请人，听取被害人及其法定代理人的意见。

**Article 635** . A compulsory medical treatment case shall be tried by a collegiate panel in a court session, except where the legal representative of the respondent or defendant requests that the case not be tried in court and the People's Court approves the request after examination.  
When trying a compulsory medical treatment case, the respondent shall be met with, and the opinions of the victim and his or her legal representative shall be heard.

<a id="art-636"></a>第六百三十六条 开庭审理申请强制医疗的案件，按照下列程序进行：  
（一）审判长宣布法庭调查开始后，先由检察员宣读申请书，后由被申请人的法定代理人、诉讼代理人发表意见；  
（二）法庭依次就被申请人是否实施了危害公共安全或者严重危害公民人身安全的暴力行为、是否属于依法不负刑事责任的精神病人、是否有继续危害社会的可能进行调查；调查时，先由检察员出示证据，后由被申请人的法定代理人、诉讼代理人出示证据，并进行质证；必要时，可以通知鉴定人出庭对鉴定意见作出说明；  
（三）法庭辩论阶段，先由检察员发言，后由被申请人的法定代理人、诉讼代理人发言，并进行辩论。  
被申请人要求出庭，人民法院经审查其身体和精神状态，认为可以出庭的，应当准许。出庭的被申请人，在法庭调查、辩论阶段，可以发表意见。  
检察员宣读申请书后，被申请人的法定代理人、诉讼代理人无异议的，法庭调查可以简化。

**Article 636** . A case involving an application for compulsory medical treatment tried in court shall proceed according to the following procedures:  
(1) after the chief judge announces the commencement of the court investigation, the prosecutor shall first read the application, and then the legal representative and litigation agent of the respondent shall present their opinions;  
(2) the court shall, in turn, investigate whether the respondent committed violent conduct endangering public safety or seriously endangering the personal safety of citizens, whether the respondent is a mental patient who is not criminally liable according to law, and whether the respondent is likely to continue to endanger society; during the investigation, the prosecutor shall first present evidence, and then the legal representative and litigation agent of the respondent shall present evidence and conduct cross-examination; where necessary, an expert witness may be notified to appear in court to explain the expert opinion;  
(3) during the court debate stage, the prosecutor shall first speak, and then the legal representative and litigation agent of the respondent shall speak and debate.  
Where the respondent requests to appear in court, and the People's Court, after examining his or her physical and mental condition, considers that it is appropriate for the respondent to appear, the court shall permit it. A respondent who appears in court may present opinions during the court investigation and debate stages.  
After the prosecutor reads the application, if the legal representative and litigation agent of the respondent raise no objection, the court investigation may be simplified.

<a id="art-637"></a>第六百三十七条 对申请强制医疗的案件，人民法院审理后，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）符合[刑事诉讼法第三百零二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-302)规定的强制医疗条件的，应当作出对被申请人强制医疗的决定；  
（二）被申请人属于依法不负刑事责任的精神病人，但不符合强制医疗条件的，应当作出驳回强制医疗申请的决定；被申请人已经造成危害结果的，应当同时责令其家属或者监护人严加看管和医疗；  
（三）被申请人具有完全或者部分刑事责任能力，依法应当追究刑事责任的，应当作出驳回强制医疗申请的决定，并退回人民检察院依法处理。

**Article 637** . After hearing a case involving an application for compulsory medical treatment, the People's Court shall handle it separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) where the conditions for compulsory medical treatment prescribed in Article 302 of the Criminal Procedure Law are met, a decision shall be made to subject the respondent to compulsory medical treatment;  
(2) where the respondent is a mental patient who is not criminally liable according to law but does not meet the conditions for compulsory medical treatment, a decision shall be made to reject the application for compulsory medical treatment; where the respondent has already caused harmful consequences, his or her family members or guardians shall, at the same time, be ordered to keep strict custody and provide medical treatment;  
(3) where the respondent has full or partial criminal liability capacity and criminal liability shall be investigated according to law, a decision shall be made to reject the application for compulsory medical treatment, and the case shall be returned to the People's Procuratorate for handling according to law.

<a id="art-638"></a>第六百三十八条 第一审人民法院在审理刑事案件过程中，发现被告人可能符合强制医疗条件的，应当依照法定程序对被告人进行法医精神病鉴定。经鉴定，被告人属于依法不负刑事责任的精神病人的，应当适用强制医疗程序，对案件进行审理。  
开庭审理前款规定的案件，应当先由合议庭组成人员宣读对被告人的法医精神病鉴定意见，说明被告人可能符合强制医疗的条件，后依次由公诉人和被告人的法定代理人、诉讼代理人发表意见。经审判长许可，公诉人和被告人的法定代理人、诉讼代理人可以进行辩论。

**Article 638** . Where, during the trial of a criminal case, the first-instance People's Court discovers that the defendant may meet the conditions for compulsory medical treatment, it shall, in accordance with statutory procedures, conduct a forensic psychiatric evaluation of the defendant. Where, upon evaluation, the defendant is determined to be a mental patient who is not criminally liable according to law, the compulsory medical treatment procedure shall be applied to try the case.  
Where a case specified in the preceding paragraph is tried in court, a member of the collegiate panel shall first read the forensic psychiatric evaluation opinion on the defendant, explaining that the defendant may meet the conditions for compulsory medical treatment; then the public prosecutor and the defendant's legal representative and litigation agent shall present their opinions in turn. With the permission of the chief judge, the public prosecutor and the defendant's legal representative and litigation agent may debate.

<a id="art-639"></a>第六百三十九条 对前条规定的案件，人民法院审理后，应当按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）被告人符合强制医疗条件的，应当判决宣告被告人不负刑事责任，同时作出对被告人强制医疗的决定；  
（二）被告人属于依法不负刑事责任的精神病人，但不符合强制医疗条件的，应当判决宣告被告人无罪或者不负刑事责任；被告人已经造成危害结果的，应当同时责令其家属或者监护人严加看管和医疗；  
（三）被告人具有完全或者部分刑事责任能力，依法应当追究刑事责任的，应当依照普通程序继续审理。

**Article 639** : For a case specified in the preceding Article, after trial, the People's Court shall, according to the following circumstances, handle it separately:  
(1) Where the defendant meets the conditions for compulsory medical treatment, it shall pronounce a judgment declaring that the defendant bears no criminal liability, and at the same time make a decision on compulsory medical treatment for the defendant;  
(2) Where the defendant is a mental patient who bears no criminal liability according to law but does not meet the conditions for compulsory medical treatment, it shall pronounce a judgment declaring the defendant not guilty or bearing no criminal liability; if the defendant has already caused harmful consequences, it shall at the same time order his or her family members or guardian to keep strict watch and provide medical treatment;  
(3) Where the defendant has full or partial criminal capacity and shall be investigated for criminal liability according to law, the trial shall continue in accordance with the ordinary procedure.

<a id="art-640"></a>第六百四十条 第二审人民法院在审理刑事案件过程中，发现被告人可能符合强制医疗条件的，可以依照强制医疗程序对案件作出处理，也可以裁定发回原审人民法院重新审判。

**Article 640** : Where, during the trial of a criminal case, the People's Court of second instance discovers that the defendant may meet the conditions for compulsory medical treatment, it may handle the case in accordance with the compulsory medical treatment procedure, or may rule to remand the case to the original trial People's Court for retrial.

<a id="art-641"></a>第六百四十一条 人民法院决定强制医疗的，应当在作出决定后五日以内，向公安机关送达强制医疗决定书和强制医疗执行通知书，由公安机关将被决定强制医疗的人送交强制医疗。

**Article 641** : Where a People's Court decides to impose compulsory medical treatment, it shall, within five days after making the decision, serve the written decision on compulsory medical treatment and the notice of execution of compulsory medical treatment on the public security organ, and the public security organ shall deliver the person subject to compulsory medical treatment to the compulsory medical treatment institution.

<a id="art-642"></a>第六百四十二条 被决定强制医疗的人、被害人及其法定代理人、近亲属对强制医疗决定不服的，可以自收到决定书第二日起五日以内向上一级人民法院申请复议。复议期间不停止执行强制医疗的决定。

**Article 642** : Where the person subject to compulsory medical treatment, the victim, or their legal representatives or close relatives are not satisfied with the compulsory medical treatment decision, they may, within five days from the second day after receiving the written decision, apply to the People's Court at the next higher level for reconsideration. The enforcement of the compulsory medical treatment decision shall not be suspended during the reconsideration period.

<a id="art-643"></a>第六百四十三条 对不服强制医疗决定的复议申请，上一级人民法院应当组成合议庭审理，并在一个月以内，按照下列情形分别作出复议决定：  
（一）被决定强制医疗的人符合强制医疗条件的，应当驳回复议申请，维持原决定；  
（二）被决定强制医疗的人不符合强制医疗条件的，应当撤销原决定；  
（三）原审违反法定诉讼程序，可能影响公正审判的，应当撤销原决定，发回原审人民法院重新审判。

**Article 643** : For an application for reconsideration of a compulsory medical treatment decision, the People's Court at the next higher level shall form a collegial panel to hear the case, and shall, within one month, make a reconsideration decision according to the following circumstances:  
(1) Where the person subject to compulsory medical treatment meets the conditions for compulsory medical treatment, the reconsideration application shall be rejected and the original decision upheld;  
(2) Where the person subject to compulsory medical treatment does not meet the conditions for compulsory medical treatment, the original decision shall be revoked;  
(3) Where the original trial violated the statutory litigation procedures and may affect the impartiality of the trial, the original decision shall be revoked and the case remanded to the original trial People's Court for retrial.

<a id="art-644"></a>第六百四十四条 对[本解释第六百三十九条](#art-639)第一项规定的判决、决定，人民检察院提出抗诉，同时被决定强制医疗的人、被害人及其法定代理人、近亲属申请复议的，上一级人民法院应当依照第二审程序一并处理。

**Article 644** : Where, with respect to the judgment and decision specified in item (1) of Article 639 of this Interpretation, the People's Procuratorate files a protest, and at the same time the person subject to compulsory medical treatment, the victim, or their legal representatives or close relatives apply for reconsideration, the People's Court at the next higher level shall handle the matters jointly in accordance with the procedure for second instance.

<a id="art-645"></a>第六百四十五条 被强制医疗的人及其近亲属申请解除强制医疗的，应当向决定强制医疗的人民法院提出。  
被强制医疗的人及其近亲属提出的解除强制医疗申请被人民法院驳回，六个月后再次提出申请的，人民法院应当受理。

**Article 645** : Where a person subject to compulsory medical treatment or his or her close relatives apply for termination of compulsory medical treatment, the application shall be submitted to the People's Court that made the compulsory medical treatment decision.  
Where the application for termination of compulsory medical treatment submitted by the person subject to compulsory medical treatment or his or her close relatives is rejected by the People's Court, and an application is submitted again after six months, the People's Court shall accept it.

<a id="art-646"></a>第六百四十六条 强制医疗机构提出解除强制医疗意见，或者被强制医疗的人及其近亲属申请解除强制医疗的，人民法院应当审查是否附有对被强制医疗的人的诊断评估报告。  
强制医疗机构提出解除强制医疗意见，未附诊断评估报告的，人民法院应当要求其提供。  
被强制医疗的人及其近亲属向人民法院申请解除强制医疗，强制医疗机构未提供诊断评估报告的，申请人可以申请人民法院调取。必要时，人民法院可以委托鉴定机构对被强制医疗的人进行鉴定。

**Article 646** : Where a compulsory medical treatment institution submits an opinion on termination of compulsory medical treatment, or a person subject to compulsory medical treatment or his or her close relatives apply for termination of compulsory medical treatment, the People's Court shall examine whether a diagnostic assessment report on the person subject to compulsory medical treatment is attached.  
Where a compulsory medical treatment institution submits an opinion on termination of compulsory medical treatment without attaching a diagnostic assessment report, the People's Court shall require it to provide one.  
Where a person subject to compulsory medical treatment or his or her close relatives apply to the People's Court for termination of compulsory medical treatment, and the compulsory medical treatment institution fails to provide a diagnostic assessment report, the applicant may apply to the People's Court to obtain it. Where necessary, the People's Court may entrust an expert evaluation institution to conduct an expert evaluation on the person subject to compulsory medical treatment.

<a id="art-647"></a>第六百四十七条 强制医疗机构提出解除强制医疗意见，或者被强制医疗的人及其近亲属申请解除强制医疗的，人民法院应当组成合议庭进行审查，并在一个月以内，按照下列情形分别处理：  
（一）被强制医疗的人已不具有人身危险性，不需要继续强制医疗的，应当作出解除强制医疗的决定，并可责令被强制医疗的人的家属严加看管和医疗；  
（二）被强制医疗的人仍具有人身危险性，需要继续强制医疗的，应当作出继续强制医疗的决定。  
对前款规定的案件，必要时，人民法院可以开庭审理，通知人民检察院派员出庭。  
人民法院应当在作出决定后五日以内，将决定书送达强制医疗机构、申请解除强制医疗的人、被决定强制医疗的人和人民检察院。决定解除强制医疗的，应当通知强制医疗机构在收到决定书的当日解除强制医疗。

**Article 647** : Where a compulsory medical treatment institution submits an opinion on termination of compulsory medical treatment, or a person subject to compulsory medical treatment or his or her close relatives apply for termination of compulsory medical treatment, the People's Court shall form a collegial panel to conduct review, and shall, within one month, handle the case according to the following circumstances:  
(1) Where the person subject to compulsory medical treatment no longer poses a danger to personal safety and does not need to continue compulsory medical treatment, a decision shall be made to terminate compulsory medical treatment, and the family members of the person subject to compulsory medical treatment may be ordered to keep strict watch and provide medical treatment;  
(2) Where the person subject to compulsory medical treatment still poses a danger to personal safety and needs to continue compulsory medical treatment, a decision shall be made to continue compulsory medical treatment.  
For the cases specified in the preceding paragraph, if necessary, the People's Court may hold a hearing in court and notify the People's Procuratorate to send personnel to appear in court.  
Within five days after making the decision, the People's Court shall serve the written decision on the compulsory medical treatment institution, the person who applied for termination of compulsory medical treatment, the person subject to compulsory medical treatment, and the People's Procuratorate. Where a decision is made to terminate compulsory medical treatment, the compulsory medical treatment institution shall be notified to terminate compulsory medical treatment on the day it receives the written decision.

<a id="art-648"></a>第六百四十八条 人民检察院认为强制医疗决定或者解除强制医疗决定不当，在收到决定书后二十日以内提出书面纠正意见的，人民法院应当另行组成合议庭审理，并在一个月以内作出决定。

**Article 648** : Where the People's Procuratorate considers that the compulsory medical treatment decision or the decision to terminate compulsory medical treatment is improper and submits written corrective opinions within twenty days after receiving the written decision, the People's Court shall form a separate collegial panel to hear the case and make a decision within one month.

<a id="art-649"></a>第六百四十九条 审理强制医疗案件，本章没有规定的，参照适用本解释的有关规定。

**Article 649** : For the trial of compulsory medical treatment cases, where this Chapter has no provisions, the relevant provisions of this Interpretation shall apply mutatis mutandis.

### 第二十七章 附则

<a id="art-650"></a>第六百五十条 人民法院讯问被告人，宣告判决，审理减刑、假释案件等，可以根据情况采取视频方式。

**Article 650** : Where a People's Court interrogates a defendant, pronounces a judgment, or hears cases concerning commutation of punishment or parole, it may, as circumstances require, adopt a video method.

<a id="art-651"></a>第六百五十一条 向人民法院提出自诉、上诉、申诉、申请等的，应当以书面形式提出。书写有困难的，除另有规定的以外，可以口头提出，由人民法院工作人员制作笔录或者记录在案，并向口述人宣读或者交其阅读。

**Article 651** : A private prosecution, appeal, petition, application, or the like submitted to a People's Court shall be made in writing. Where a person has difficulty writing, unless otherwise provided, it may be made orally, and a staff member of the People's Court shall make a record or note it in the case file, and read it to or have it read by the person making the oral statement.

<a id="art-652"></a>第六百五十二条 诉讼期间制作、形成的工作记录、告知笔录等材料，应当由制作人员和其他有关人员签名、盖章。宣告或者送达裁判文书、通知书等诉讼文书的，应当由接受宣告或者送达的人在诉讼文书、送达回证上签名、盖章。  
诉讼参与人未签名、盖章的，应当捺指印；刑事被告人除签名、盖章外，还应当捺指印。  
当事人拒绝签名、盖章、捺指印的，办案人员应当在诉讼文书或者笔录材料中注明情况，有见证人见证或者有录音录像证明的，不影响相关诉讼文书或者笔录材料的效力。

**Article 652** : Work records, notification transcripts, and other materials produced or formed during the litigation period shall be signed and sealed by the personnel who produced them and other relevant personnel. Where litigation documents such as judgment documents and notices are declared or served, the person receiving the declaration or service shall sign and seal the litigation document or the service receipt.  
Where a litigation participant fails to sign or seal, he or she shall affix a fingerprint; a criminal defendant shall, in addition to signing and sealing, also affix a fingerprint.  
Where a party refuses to sign, seal, or affix a fingerprint, the case-handling personnel shall note the circumstances in the litigation document or transcript materials; if there is a witness to attest or an audio-video recording to prove it, the validity of the relevant litigation document or transcript materials shall not be affected.

<a id="art-653"></a>第六百五十三条 本解释的有关规定适用于军事法院等专门人民法院。

**Article 653** : The relevant provisions of this Interpretation shall apply to special people's courts such as military courts.

<a id="art-654"></a>第六百五十四条 本解释有关公安机关的规定，依照刑事诉讼法的有关规定，适用于国家安全机关、军队保卫部门、中国海警局和监狱。

**Article 654** : The provisions of this Interpretation concerning public security organs shall, in accordance with the relevant provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law, apply to state security organs, military security departments, China Coast Guard, and prisons.

<a id="art-655"></a>第六百五十五条 本解释自2021年3月1日起施行。最高人民法院2012年12月20日发布的《关于适用〈中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法〉的解释》（法释〔2012〕21号）同时废止。最高人民法院以前发布的司法解释和规范性文件，与本解释不一致的，以本解释为准。  
<div

**Article 655** : This Interpretation shall come into force on March 1, 2021. The Interpretation of the Supreme People's Court on the Application of the Criminal Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China (Fa Shi [2012] No. 21) issued by the Supreme People's Court on December 20, 2012 shall be repealed simultaneously. Where the judicial interpretations and normative documents previously issued by the Supreme People's Court are inconsistent with this Interpretation, this Interpretation shall prevail.
